### [1] [An exposition on some viscosity dependent issues in refined potential flow theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10649)
*Taofiq Omoniyi Amoloye,Leke Thaddeus Oladimeji,Mahmoud A. Hayajnh,Olalekan Adebayo Olayemi*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究评估了改进势流理论（RPT）在黏性效应下求解脉冲启动圆柱绕流的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典势流理论忽略黏性，无法满足无滑移边界条件，因此需要评估RPT在黏性依赖限制下的适用性。

Method: RPT通过引入准无旋黏性流函数，修正经典势流理论以纳入黏性效应、流动脉动与三维变量，并求解连续性方程与Navier-Stokes方程，同时满足自由来流与无滑移边界条件。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅说明该框架能够同时满足自由来流与无滑移边界条件。

Conclusion: RPT为脉冲启动圆柱绕流提供了一种兼顾黏性与无滑移条件的求解思路，但其黏性相关局限性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: This study evaluates viscosity-dependent limitations in refined potential flow theory (RPT) applied to incompressible flow over an impulsively started circular cylinder. RPT adapts classical potential flow theory to account for viscous effects, flow fluctuations, and three-dimensional variables. By utilizing a quasi-irrotational viscous stream function, the framework solves the continuity and Navier-Stokes equations while satisfying both free-stream and no-slip boundary conditions.

</details>


### [2] [A Robust All-Mach Six-Equation Diffuse-Interface Method for Multiphase Flows with Surface Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10727)
*Ghanshyam Bharate,J. C. Mandal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个鲁棒的有限体积框架，用于模拟跨宽马赫数范围、带表面张力的可压缩多相流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统近似黎曼求解器在低马赫区数值耗散过大，且六方程弥散界面模型在相平衡时难以保持毛细压力跳跃，需发展兼顾低马赫精度与稳定性的方法。

Method: 基于双压力、六方程弥散界面模型，通过连续表面力公式纳入粘性、重力与毛细效应；利用广义黎曼不变量分析构造HLLC黎曼求解器以捕捉毛细诱导压力跳跃；修正瞬时压力松弛过程以保持拉普拉斯压力跳跃；并提出修正缩放策略的低马赫校正。

Result: 在涉及表面张力、粘性、重力与可压缩性的基准算例中，准确预测了界面动力学、毛细压力及低马赫流动特征，同时显著降低数值耗散且未损失稳定性。

Conclusion: 该方法从近不可压到可压缩区均保持精度与六方程框架的鲁棒性，为宽流区复杂多相流模拟提供了高效可靠途径。

Abstract: A robust finite-volume framework is presented for the simulation of compressible multiphase flows with surface tension across a wide range of Mach numbers. The method is based on a two-pressure, six-equation diffuse interface model incorporating viscous, gravitational, and capillary effects through the continuum surface force formulation. To consistently account for capillary-induced pressure jumps, an HLLC Riemann solver is developed using generalized Riemann invariant analysis, and the instantaneous pressure relaxation procedure is modified to preserve the Laplace pressure jump during phase equilibration. To overcome the excessive numerical diffusion of conventional approximate Riemann solvers in the low-Mach regime, a robust low-Mach correction is proposed by extending our previous formulation with a modified scaling strategy that remains stable in regions of strong pressure variation. The resulting method retains accuracy from nearly incompressible flows to compressible regimes while preserving the robustness of the six-equation formulation. The numerical framework is validated using a series of benchmark problems involving surface tension, viscosity, gravity, and compressibility. The results demonstrate accurate prediction of interface dynamics, capillary pressure, and low-Mach flow features, while significantly reducing numerical dissipation without compromising stability. The proposed methodology provides an efficient and reliable approach for the simulation of complex multiphase flows spanning a broad range of flow regimes.

</details>


### [3] [Mixing dynamics and transport mechanisms during laminar stirring flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10773)
*Mohammad Reza Daneshvar Garmroodi,Ida Karimfazli*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过二维数值模拟研究圆柱搅拌器沿圆轨迹旋转对被动染料的层流混合，揭示搅拌雷诺数控制三种混合区制，并阐明流动拓扑与混合增强之间的机制联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解层流条件下搅拌产生的流动拓扑如何决定被动标量的混合效率，特别是为何增大搅拌强度并不总能提升每周期混合效果，需要建立流动特征与混合机制之间的因果联系。

Method: 在无限二维牛顿流体域中，模拟圆柱搅拌器以恒定转速沿圆形路径运动，初始流体静止，下半域用被动染料标记；在层流范围内改变搅拌雷诺数，分析染料界面演化与混合动力学，并将混合事件与具体流动特征（搅拌器与界面的直接作用、涡脱落）关联。

Result: 识别出三种混合区制：扩散主导型表现为形成良好混合的中心区和近似自相似的螺旋染料图案；对流混合以染料界面在中心区外显著变形为标志。当涡结构能逃逸中心区并非局域输运标量梯度时混合增强；若涡活动仅局限于搅拌器路径附近，则每搅拌周期的混合量对搅拌速度仅有弱依赖，仅当逃逸涡引入新输运机制时每周期混合才显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过识别流动特征控制标量演化的输运机制，建立了流动拓扑与混合之间的机制联系，在混合运动学与底层流体动力学之间架起桥梁，解释了搅拌强度与混合效率非单调关系的原因。

Abstract: We investigate laminar mixing of a passive dye in an infinite, two-dimensional domain filled with a Newtonian fluid by simulating a cylindrical stirrer that rotates at constant speed along a circular path, stirring an initially quiescent fluid. The fluid is marked by a passive dye in the lower half of the domain, enabling a systematic analysis of dye-interface evolution and mixing dynamics. By varying the stirring Reynolds number within the laminar regime, we identify how transitions in flow topology govern mixing and distinguish three mixing regimes spanning diffusion-dominated and advective mixing. In the diffusion-dominated regime, mixing is characterized by the formation of a well-mixed central region and the development of a spiral dye pattern that evolves in an approximately self-similar manner. Advective mixing is marked by substantial deformation of the dye interface beyond the central region.
  We provide a mechanistic interpretation of advective mixing by relating mixing events to specific flow features: (i) direct interaction between the stirrer and the dye interface, and (ii) vortex shedding near the stirrer and away from the central region. Enhanced mixing occurs when vortical structures are able to escape the central region and transport scalar gradients nonlocally. When vortical activity remains confined near the stirrer's path, mixing per stirrer period exhibits only weak dependence on stirring speed despite increasing stirring intensity. Significant enhancement of mixing per period occurs only when escaping vortices introduce a new transport mechanism that carries scalar gradients far beyond the stirrer's path.
  Overall, we present a mechanistic link between flow topology and mixing by identifying the transport mechanisms through which flow features govern scalar evolution, providing a bridge between the kinematics of mixing and the underlying fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [4] [Long interfacial internal waves with currents: current profiles with quadratic depth-dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10820)
*Conor T. Curtin,Rossen I. Ivanov*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究了赤道内波在赤道潜流（EUC）背景流中的传播，将EUC流速剖面设为二次函数，导出可积的非线性Gardner方程，并推广至变底地形下的广义Gardner方程，指出方程系数对EUC参数高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内波常在有流环境中传播，赤道内波与赤道潜流（EUC）是这一现象的典型代表，因此有必要建立能反映EUC背景流影响的内波传播模型。

Method: 假设EUC流速剖面为深度的二次函数（与已有EUC观测结果一致），针对密度略有差异的两层流体间温跃层的波运动，推导出可积的非线性Gardner方程；进一步在变底地形情形下导出广义Gardner方程，并分析方程系数对EUC参数的敏感性。

Result: 得到了可积的Gardner方程作为温跃层波运动模型，并在变底地形下得到广义Gardner方程；结果表明方程系数对EUC参数非常敏感。

Conclusion: 二次流速剖面假设下的Gardner方程可有效刻画EUC背景流中的赤道内波运动，且系数对EUC参数的高度敏感性意味着背景流特征的变化会显著影响内波传播特性。

Abstract: Internal waves often propagate in the presence of currents. Internal waves and currents are widespread in the world's oceans. The equatorial internal waves and the equatorial undercurrent (EUC) are perhaps the most representative illustration of this phenomenon. The current profile in this study is a quadratic function of depth, which is in agreement with the known results of the EUC. With this assumption the integrable nonlinear Gardner's equation is derived as a model for the wave motion of the thermocline, separating the two layers of slightly different densities. In addition, we derive the generalised Gardner's equation in the case of variable bottom. We demonstrate that the coefficients of the equation are very sensitive with respect to the parameters of the EUC.

</details>


### [5] [Giesekus Stick-Slip Singularity: Asymptotic Theory in the Log-Conformation Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10836)
*Florian Becker,Philipp Knechtges*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文应用对数构象重构方法解析研究了Giesekus流体在平面黏滑奇点附近的渐近行为，验证并拓展了已有理论，得到了统一的复合渐近解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对数构象重构此前主要作为数值计算工具，作者希望探索其解析用途，并借助该重构深化对Giesekus流体黏滑奇点附近应力渐近结构的理解。

Method: 在给定牛顿速度场假设下，采用Giesekus流体的对数构象重构方程，对平面黏滑奇点进行解析渐近分析，涵盖黏着区、滑移区与核心区，并研究边界层与核心区之间的过渡行为。

Result: 所得结果与Evans (JNNFM 222 (2015) 24-33)的渐近应力分析一致；进一步证明构象张量的行列式渐近为常数，并完整刻画了边界层与核心区之间的过渡行为。

Conclusion: 结合上述结果，作者获得了对数构象方程的复合渐近解，该解在奇点邻近区域内一致有效。

Abstract: In this paper, the log-conformation reformulation of a Giesekus fluid near a planar stick-slip singularity is investigated analytically. In what is, to our knowledge, the first use of the log-conformation formulation beyond its numerical purpose, we present results that match the asymptotic stress analysis of Evans [JNNFM 222 (2015) 24-33] for the stick, slip, and core regions under the assumption of a given Newtonian velocity field. Furthermore, the logarithmic reformulation allows us to extend the existing asymptotic theory substantially, showing, e.g., that the determinant of the conformation tensor is asymptotically constant, as well as thoroughly characterizing the transition behavior between the boundary layers and the core region. Combining these results, we obtain a composite asymptotic solution of the log-conformation equation that is valid uniformly throughout a close neighborhood of the singularity.

</details>


### [6] [Correlative effects of induced magnetic field-buoyancy on reactive solute dispersion dynamics in couple-stress fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11099)
*Aritra Roy,Debabrata Das,Pranab Kumar Mondal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究在两平行板间流动的 couple-stress 流体中，受压力驱动、浮力与诱导磁场共同作用的反应性溶质弥散，基于 Mei 三阶多尺度均匀化建立高阶渐近理论，并用 Brownian 动力学、有限差分与 Aris 矩方法验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: couple-stress 流体兼具非牛顿微极效应与磁流体特性，其耦合传质-反应-磁场弥散机理尚不清楚，需要给出可解析刻画的长期弥散规律并与数值/实验对照。

Method: Mei 多尺度均匀化技术（三阶精度）推导有效纵向弥散系数与浓度场；Aris 矩方法求瞬态平均位移、空间方差与有效弥散率；辅以 Brownian 动力学模拟与有限差分求解；壁面一阶非均相反应加体相反应；考察 Hartmann 数、couple-stress 参数、浮力及壁面吸收不对称性的影响。

Result: 速度解在 Hartmann 数等于 couple-stress 参数一半时出现奇异分支，且二者间存在特征四分之一次幂标度，区分 couple-stress 主导与磁主导区制；非反应 Newton 极限下退化为经典 Taylor 弥散并与实验吻合。couple-stress 流变与磁阻尼抑制剪切诱导弥散，浮力经附加横向速度梯度增强弥散；弥散系数随 couple-stress 参数增大出现饱和区制，壁面吸收不等导致持续横向不对称，强吸收增强近源区溶质移除。

Conclusion: 建立的高阶渐近框架经数值与随机模拟验证，能够解析高阶浓度结构与颗粒尺度壁面吸附，为磁场与 couple-stress 耦合下的反应性弥散提供统一预测工具。

Abstract: We investigate the dispersion of a reactive solute in a couple-stress fluid flowing between two parallel plates under the combined effects of pressure-driven flow, buoyancy, and an induced magnetic field. The model incorporates first-order heterogeneous reactions at both channel walls alongside a bulk reaction. Using Mei's multiscale homogenization technique accurate to third order, we develop a higher-order asymptotic formulation to determine the effective longitudinal dispersion coefficient and concentration field. Analytical predictions are complemented by Brownian dynamics simulations and finite-difference solutions, while the Aris method of moments quantifies transient mean displacement, spatial variance, and effective dispersivity. The hydrodynamic analysis reveals a singular branch in the velocity solution when the Hartmann number equals half the couple-stress parameter and identifies a characteristic quarter-power scaling between the Hartmann number and couple-stress parameter, separating couple-stress- and magnetically dominated regimes. The model recovers classical Taylor-dispersion behavior in the non-reactive Newtonian limit and agrees well with experimental measurements. Couple-stress rheology and magnetic damping suppress shear-induced dispersion, whereas buoyancy enhances dispersion through additional transverse velocity gradients. A distinct saturation regime of the dispersion coefficient emerges with an increasing couple-stress parameter, while unequal wall absorption induces persistent transverse asymmetry, and stronger absorption enhances solute removal near the source. Numerical and stochastic results validate the analytical framework while resolving higher-order concentration structures and particle-scale wall adsorption.

</details>


### [7] [A Novel Multi-fidelity Surrogate for Turbomachinery Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11111)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Zhendong Guo,Jun Li,Zhenping Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 提出多-单保真度优化（MSFO）算法，利用DBSCAN识别低保真失效区域，融合全局多保真与局部单保真代理模型，在涡轮气动与端壁气膜冷却设计中优于传统SFO和MFO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮机械设计优化属于昂贵的黑箱问题，多保真优化（MFO）本应借助低保真样本减少高保真样本需求。但在序贯采样场景下，MFO早期表现更优，最终最优解却常劣于单保真优化（SFO）。根本原因在于传统MFO假设样本均匀且嵌套分布，而序贯加点策略下该假设不成立，导致低保真数据误导优化过程。

Method: 提出MSFO算法：在代理模型构建中，采用基于密度的带噪声空间聚类（DBSCAN）检测低保真样本不再有效的局部区域；将全局多保真代理模型与仅用高保真样本构建的局部单保真代理模型组合成集成模型，以增强算法对误导性低保真数据的抗干扰能力。先在数值基准函数上验证，再用于涡轮气动叶型与涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局优化，其中高/低保真样本分别来自细网格与粗网格CFD仿真。

Result: 数值基准函数验证了MSFO的有效性；在涡轮气动叶型和端壁气膜冷却布局两个工程优化问题中，MSFO显著优于传统SFO和MFO方法，并具有更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSFO通过DBSCAN局部检测与多保真/单保真集成建模，克服了传统MFO在序贯采样下样本非嵌套分布带来的局限，为涡轮机械昂贵黑箱设计优化提供了更高效、更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Turbomachinery design optimization involves expensive black-box problems. Sample-efficient multi-fidelity optimization (MFO) offers an efficient solution. By utilizing multi-fidelity surrogates (MFS), the MFO algorithm can use fewer high-fidelity samples aided by low-fidelity samples to establish an accurate surrogate model. However, when MFS is used in sequential sampling optimization, it has been observed that the final optimal solution obtained by single-fidelity optimization (SFO) is better than that of MFO, even though MFO performs better at the early stages. This can be attributed to the assumption of an even and nested distribution of samples, which is incorrect when using a sequential adding strategy. To address these issues, we propose a novel algorithm called multi-single-fidelity optimization (MSFO) to overcome the limitations of the conventional MFO procedures. In the surrogate establishment of MSFO, we use the density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) method to detect local areas where low-fidelity samples are no longer effective. A combination of both global MFS and local single-fidelity surrogate model, built using high-fidelity samples alone, is used to establish an ensemble, which improves the anti-interference ability of the algorithm against misleading low-fidelity data. The effectiveness of the MSFO algorithm is verified first on numerical benchmark functions. Then, the algorithm is used to optimize the aerodynamic profile of a turbine and the film cooling layout design of a turbine endwall. Here, high-fidelity sample sources are obtained from fine-mesh CFD simulations, whereas low-fidelity sample sources are obtained from the same simulations run on a coarser mesh. The results demonstrate that our MSFO algorithm performs significantly better than the conventional SFO and MFO processes, with a higher level of robustness.

</details>


### [8] [A Novel Multi-fidelity Surrogate for Efficient Turbine Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11112)
*Qineng Wang,Liming Song,Zhendong Guo,Jun Li,Zhenping Feng*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出集成加权多保真度代理模型（EMFS）及融合优化算法MSFO，通过DBSCAN识别多保真代理失效区域并构建局部单保真代理，以自适应权重融合两者，解决多保真优化后期精度退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多保真代理优化（MFS）利用大量廉价低保真样本辅助少量高保真样本，以降低涡轮设计成本。但观察发现，随着优化过程中高保真样本增多，低保真样本会产生负面影响，导致MFS仅在优化早期收敛更快，最终解却劣于仅使用高保真样本的单保真代理优化（SFS）。

Method: 提出集成加权多保真代理模型（EMFS）：先用基于密度的噪声应用空间聚类（DBSCAN）检测MFS无法构建更精确代理的区域，在该区域构建局部SFS；再以自适应权重将MFS与SFS组合成EMFS并用于引导优化，相应算法命名为多保真与单保真代理融合优化（MSFO）。

Result: 在GE-E3叶片优化和涡轮端壁气膜冷却布局设计两个案例上进行了测试，验证了所提MSFO算法的有效性。

Conclusion: EMFS通过局部SFS补偿与自适应加权融合，克服了MFS后期受低保真样本误导的问题，在涡轮设计优化中兼顾收敛速度与最终解质量，MSFO具有良好的工程适用性。

Abstract: To solve the turbine design optimization problems efficiently, surrogate-based optimization (SBO) algorithms are frequently used. To further reduce the cost of turbine design, the multi-fidelity surrogate (MFS) based optimization is proposed by the researchers, who resort to augmenting the small number of expensive high-fidelity (HF) samples by a large portion of low-fidelity (LF) but cheap samples in surrogate modeling and optimization process. Nonetheless, according to our observations, the MFS based optimization sometimes can only have better convergence rate at the early stage of optimization process, but yielding worse final solution than the single-fidelity surrogate (SFS) based optimization that uses high-fidelity samples alone. The reason behind can be explained as follows. With the increase of HF samples in the optimization process, the LF samples can cause negative effect and therefore misleading the optimization search. To address the above issue, an ensemble weighted multi-fidelity surrogate (EMFS) is proposed. Specifically, the density-based spatial clustering of applications with noise (DBSCAN) is used to detect the region where the MFS cannot build a more accurate surrogate, and a local SFS is built there. Then, an EMFS is built by combining the MFS and SFS with adaptive weights, which is used to guide the optimization process. The related algorithm is named as multi- and single-fidelity surrogate fused optimization, i.e., MSFO. Through tests on GE-E3 blade optimization and the film cooling layout design of a turbine endwall, the effectiveness of proposed MSFO is well demonstrated.

</details>


### [9] [DiffSWE2d: a differentiable Shallow Water Equations solver for end-to-end flood and tsunami modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11426)
*Zhonghou Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffSWE2d，一个基于PyTorch的开源可微分二维浅水方程求解器，通过自动微分实现端到端洪水与海啸建模及梯度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统浅水方程求解器在求解反问题和优化问题时计算代价高昂，尤其当需要通过对模型输入或参数进行重复前向模拟来估计梯度时。

Method: 将时间推进的物理过程表示为可微分计算图，利用自动微分使梯度直接通过数值求解器反向传播，并在PyTorch中实现。

Abstract: Solving inverse and optimisation problems with traditional shallow water equations (SWE) solvers can be computationally expensive, particularly when gradients with respect to model inputs or parameters must be estimated through repeated forward simulations. In this paper, we introduce DiffSWE2d, an open-source differentiable shallow water equations solver for end-to-end flood and tsunami modelling implemented in PyTorch. By leveraging automatic differentiation, DiffSWE2d represents the time-marching physics as a differentiable computational graph, enabling gradients to be propagated directly through the numerical solver. We validate the solver against two established benchmark cases and demonstrate its application to tsunami waveform inversion, showing its ability to infer model inputs through gradient-based optimisation. DiffSWE2d provides a flexible framework for integrating physics-based hydrodynamic modelling with modern optimisation and machine learning methods. Abstract: Pipe and channel flows of viscoelastic fluids display chaotic dynamics at unusually low speeds, a phenomenon referred to as elasto-inertial turbulence, EIT. First reported in experiments a century ago, recent theoretical studies and model computations predict a variety of scenarios for the phenomenon's origin, ranging from hoop stress modes to center modes and to Tollmien-Schlichting waves. Lacking experimental confirmation, the relevant scenario in actual flows of polymer solutions remains unknown. We here determine the transition threshold of EIT in pipe experiments, covering three decades in elasticity number. Across this entire parameter range, the transition features center mode structures at onset. Eventually the instability diverges at a lower inertia (upper elasticity) limit, which is a robust signature of this center mode scenario. Finally, we report the first experimental observation of a traveling wave in viscoelastic pipe flow, and the sequences of localized structures found, are in excellent agreement with a center mode traveling wave, the "arrowhead" solution, discovered in model simulations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入"等效可解性条件"（equivalent solvability condition）：在多时间尺度展开下，要求约化方程与原系统具有相同的主阶解和一阶可解性条件。据此构建约化模型，分别针对(1)深度不变背景流上的宽带波、(2)完全三维背景流上的窄带波导出2D约化方程。后者化为含背景流近表面涡度的薛定谔方程。

Result: 宽带/深度不变背景流情形得到2D约化方程；三维背景流窄带情形得到薛定谔型方程，仅涉及近表面涡度，近表面水平流散度贡献为次阶。该方程在背景流的慢平流时间尺度上描述波演化，消除解析快波周期的计算负担。通过组织流斑块弱散射的解析解与无序流斑块强散射的数值解验证了约化方程能力。

Abstract: We consider deep-water surface gravity waves propagating above a background flow whose spatial scale is comparable to the wavelength, focusing on the regime where the flow is slow compared to the group velocity of the waves. We introduce an "equivalent solvability condition" method to construct reduced equations, demanding that, upon multiple-timescale expansion, the reduced equations share the same leading-order solution and first solvability condition as the original system. This approach turns the full 3D problem into a 2D reduced equation for the wave field. We derive such reduced equations for broad-band waves above a depth-invariant background flow, and for narrow-band waves above a fully 3D background flow. In the latter case the reduced equation takes the form of a Schrodinger equation involving the near-surface vorticity of the background flow only, with the impact of the near-surface horizontal flow divergence shown to be subdominant. Beyond the reduction in spatial dimensionality, the latter reduced equation describes the evolution of the wave field over the slow advective timescale of the background flow, thereby eliminating the computational burden of time-resolving the fast wave period. We illustrate the capabilities of the reduced equations through an analytical solution for the weak scattering of a wave packet by a patch of organized flow, followed by numerical solutions for stronger scattering of a wave packet by a patch of disorganized flow.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 背景流空间尺度与波长相当、流速与波Stokes漂移相当时，波与流的相互作用不能被忽略，且非线性波相互作用与双向耦合同阶，需要建立自洽的双向耦合模型。

Method: 采用渐近分析/多尺度方法，针对深水窄带表面重力波，推导与part I中约化波方程的非线性版本与Craik-Leibovich背景流演化方程双向耦合的系统；耦合项写成紧凑形式。

Conclusion: 该双向耦合模型在保持守恒性质的同时降低计算成本，耦合项形式紧凑，便于物理解释与数值实现，适用于背景流与波同阶耦合的情形。

Abstract: We consider narrow-band deep-water surface gravity waves propagating above a background flow whose spatial scale is comparable to the wavelength. We focus on the regime where the background-flow speed is comparable to the Stokes drift of the waves to derive a two-way coupled model between the fast waves and the slow background flow. Nonlinear wave interactions arise at the same asymptotic order as the two-way coupling and must therefore be included in the derivation. The resulting model consists of a nonlinear version of the reduced wave equation obtained in part I, coupled to a Craik-Leibovich equation governing the evolution of the background flow. The model describes the evolution of the wave field and background flow over the slow turnover frequency of the background flow, thus saving the computational burden of temporally resolving the fast wave frequency. The inviscid model exactly conserves wave action, mechanical energy and horizontal momentum, while the viscous model exactly conserves horizontal momentum. The coupling terms are cast in compact form for ease of physical interpretation and numerical implementation.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用连通分量标记检测已解析蒸汽结构，并通过确定性并行过程跨处理器边界进行协调；利用物理信息预测结构位置和尺寸来关联连续观测，从而在生灭、破碎、合并及表示转换过程中保持持久身份与历史；保留的历史支持历史感知的欧拉到拉格朗日转换判据，并为拉格朗日气泡初始化提供所需半径和半径变化率信息；采用保守双向传递算子保持蒸汽质量和线性动量并防止同一蒸汽体积被同时表示。

Conclusion: 嵌入在线持久结构追踪的保守混合欧拉-拉格朗日框架能够有效利用时间历史改进表示转换，并保证守恒性和并行确定性。

Abstract: Hybrid Eulerian Lagrangian methods for multiscale cavitation represent grid resolved vapor structures in an Eulerian formulation and unresolved bubbles in a Lagrangian description, requiring transitions between the two representations as structures evolve. Existing approaches typically treat these transitions as local geometric operations, while resolved structures are identified independently at each time step, limiting the use of temporal history in representation changes. This work presents a conservative hybrid Eulerian Lagrangian framework with embedded, on the fly persistent structure tracking. Resolved vapor structures are detected using connected component labeling and reconciled across processor boundaries through a deterministic parallel procedure. Successive observations are associated using physics informed predictions of structure position and size, maintaining persistent identities and histories through birth, death, breakup, coalescence, and representation transitions. The retained history enables history aware Eulerian to Lagrangian transition criteria and initialization of Lagrangian bubbles with the radius and radius rate information required by the bubble dynamics model. Conservative bidirectional transfer operators preserve vapor mass and linear momentum while preventing simultaneous representation of the same vapor volume. Canonical tests verify persistent tracking through fragmentation, coalescence, and close crossings, repeated representation switching, conservation of mass and momentum, and deterministic parallel behavior.

</details>

Abstract: We develop a high-order, fully explicit operator-splitting method for 2.5D ideal magnetohydrodynamics on Cartesian meshes. The magnetic--velocity subflow uses compatible finite elements and mass-lumped reconstructions of electric field and current density. Its discrete-curl update exactly preserves the global $H(\mathrm{div})$ divergence-free subspace, while the ideal semidiscretization balances kinetic, magnetic, and internal energy. Curl-form artificial resistivity and a direction-resolved velocity filter return removed magnetic and kinetic energy to internal energy; consequently, the stabilized magnetic stage preserves positive internal energy and satisfies a discrete entropy inequality. The two solvers are composed by a second-order hydrodynamic--magnetic--hydrodynamic Strang splitting, yielding a matrix-free scheme that retains global mass, nodal admissibility, and magnetic divergence on accepted steps. Smooth Alfvén-wave and advected-vortex tests reveal an even--odd convergence pattern in the magnetic polynomial degree, verify second-order temporal accuracy, and show that stabilization preserves high-order accuracy. Field-loop, Orszag--Tang, rotor, MHD blast-wave, and Kelvin--Helmholtz calculations demonstrate robust performance for nonsmooth multidimensional flows and show that artificial resistivity suppresses grid-scale magnetic oscillations while retaining the resolved structures.

Abstract: Evaluating layer potentials with rapidly decaying kernels becomes computationally challenging in the presence of boundary layers, as occurs for Helmholtz layer potentials with large imaginary wavenumbers. We introduce Quadrature by Matched Asymptotic Expansion (QBMAX), a high-order method that extends the Quadrature by Expansion (QBX) framework by incorporating the kernel's asymptotic decay behavior directly into the Taylor expansion of the potential. Numerical results in both 2D and 3D show improvements of up to four digits over QBX for the tested large-parameter cases, while the methods can perform similarly for smaller parameters or more strongly curved geometries. The Taylor expansions have the same formal order, while QBMAX reduces the truncation-error constants in the flat-boundary model and generally exhibits less convergence degradation in the reported experiments.

Abstract: Exploring high dimensional energy landscape is a challenging problem in many applications where both ground states and transition states offer important information about the underlying physical systems. At each step of the iteration, the proposed method involves several ingredients, such as updating the eigen-directions of the Hessian according to the prescribed index, reflecting the gradient and the Hessian along selected unstable eigen-directions, if any, and constructing the next step by solving a cubic-regularized subproblem. Abstract: We study computational trade-offs between two solvers for the same semi-implicit finite-difference discretization of forward-backward partial differential equation (PDE) systems arising in mean field games (MFGs). Abstract: We study early stopping for a continuous-time model of stochastic gradient descent (SGD) in ill-posed linear inverse problems. We consider an a posteriori stopping rule based on the discrepancy principle, which stops the dynamics once the residual reaches the noise level. Unlike deterministic gradient flow, the stochastic dynamics exhibits persistent multiplicative fluctuations whose amplitude scales with the residual itself.

Our results therefore establish the discrepancy principle as an adaptive regularization strategy for continuous-time SGD that is minimax-optimal up to a logarithmic factor, despite the persistent fluctuations induced by stochastic sampling. Abstract: We discuss some theoretical results and their applications to specific practical problems from wireless communications. We assume that we know the noisy version of the signal, which is sparse with respect to a given system of elements (dictionary), at a finite number of points and we want to approximately recover it. Under certain conditions on a dictionary (RIP-type condition, coherence condition) we obtain different kinds of the Lebesgue-type inequalities for the OMP and its version WOMP algorithms. The most important feature of our new theoretical result is the assumption that the dictionary has the RIP-type property instead of the assumption that it is the Riesz basis, which was used in the previous results. Our approach allows us to treat redundant (overcomplete) systems, which is important in applications.

Our main example is the oversampled Fourier dictionary and the recovery of the frequency response of a sparse channel. We develop a general approach to such problems and we prove that under certain conditions the OMP algorithm recovers all dictionary elements with large coefficients, and estimate its accuracy (in NMSE metric) in terms of signal-to-noise ratio.

Abstract: We consider boundary integral methods for Stokes mobility and resistance problems involving smooth axisymmetric particles. A primary numerical challenge is the accurate and efficient evaluation of layer potentials at off-surface points close to particle surfaces. We present a tolerance-driven workflow for evaluating the Stokes double layer potential at such evaluation points (targets) to prescribed accuracy while avoiding unnecessary computational cost. For each target--particle interaction, a fast classifier selects the least costly option estimated to meet the tolerance among standard, upsampled, and special quadrature. Geometry-dependent unit-density error indicators are precomputed and tabulated in reduced cylindrical coordinates, then combined on the fly with a local layer-density modifier, making its cost negligible relative to evaluating the potential. Targets requiring special quadrature are treated using a stabilized version of singularity swap surface quadrature: the periodic azimuthal integral is evaluated first using translated singularity swap quadrature to prevent severe cancellation near the surface, followed by adaptive Gauss--Legendre quadrature in the polar direction guided by error indicators. We integrate this workflow into a boundary integral solver with precomputed quadrature by expansion for on-surface self-interactions and demonstrate the workflow's performance for challenging configurations of spheroidal particles. Numerical results show that target classification is highly accurate. The prescribed tolerance is met for nearly all target--particle interactions and the error remains within a modest factor of the tolerance in the few remaining cases. Although the experiments focus on the Stokes double layer potential for spheroids, the off-surface framework applies to general smooth axisymmetric surfaces and can be easily adapted to other Stokes layer potentials.

</details>

### [42] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过语义先验对齐、双粒度MoE与转向加权损失，在丹麦AIS数据上1–4小时预测中优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中的三个挑战：行为多模态性、语义信息利用不足以及长期误差累积。

Method: 引入船舶静态属性与航行意图作为语义先验用于长期建模；构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练LLM编码静态语义信息并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；提出双粒度MoE架构，序列级专家建模全局航线趋势，token级专家细化细粒度操纵行为；设计Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy损失以缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布。

Result: 在真实丹麦AIS数据集上，1至4小时各预测时长均持续优于SOTA基线；4小时预测中ADE降低4.4%、FDE降低5.1%；定性与消融分析表明语义融合可有效缓解长期轨迹漂移，双粒度MoE提升复杂航道与航线分支场景的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的分层预测范式，将高层航行意图与局部运动动态联合建模，实现鲁棒的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [43] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 异方差预测不仅用于不确定性量化，还能提升点估计精度。Halo通过为现有深度预测器增加尺度输出并在负对数似然下训练，在五个电价市场上使MSE和MAE在30组比较中28组改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常被用于不确定性量化，而时间序列之外的异方差估计报告了负面结果。本文旨在证明异方差预测同样能改进点估计，尤其是在时间序列预测中。

Method: Halo方法复用现有深度预测器的架构，为其增加第二个输出以估计隐含分布的尺度参数，并在匹配的负对数似然下训练。在三种先进模型（Transformer、图网络结合变分自编码器、单层卷积网络）上，分别采用高斯和拉普拉斯损失进行适配。

Result: 在标准预测基准的五个电价市场上，Halo在30组模型-市场-指标比较中的28组改善了MSE和MAE，平均MSE降低2.6%至16.5%，平均MAE降低1.7%至11.0%。

Conclusion: 两个主要发现：（1）尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络，其影响远小于网络是否估计尺度；（2）在已为点估计基线调优的超参数下改进依然成立，因此重新调参并非必需。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [44] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作把冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样将文本提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度在每次迭代中耦合，实现语言驱动的密度型拓扑优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载结构（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化器难以复现此类几何，且缺乏让工程师用自然语言表达设计意图的手段。

Method: 将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型视为免训练设计先验，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）将其蒸馏进密度型拓扑优化的物理循环；每次迭代中，提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度相结合，由物理决定哪些生成特征得以保留；采用带β延拓的Heaviside投影抑制中间密度，并构建基于骨架的自动化流程将优化密度场转为CAD可用候选几何。

Result: 在覆盖四个几何域、两种物理机制的245次主SDS运行中，49个提示-域组合有38个实现统计显著的柔度下降（机械最高-31.5%，热弹性最高-23.0%），优于基于梯度的基线；跨域形态分析发现共性改进特征——生成先验抑制肋骨架中的死端分支，端点-柔度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99；β延拓Heaviside投影将中间密度从42.6%降至3%以下。

Conclusion: 通过更换文本提示即可跨几何域、载荷工况和物理目标重定向生成先验，且每个新问题的物理设置可单独指定，从而把预训练生成模型用作可复用、免训练的工程设计先验。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [45] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端和单一固定聚合服务器信任问题，提出数据集、压缩检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测依托边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中收集；现有联邦方案存在上行带宽有限、拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端，以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任三大实际障碍。

Method: (i) 从八个公开来源构建室内火灾检测数据集；(ii) 设计可边缘部署的检测器，模型更新压缩最高10倍；(iii) 提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者，剔除单轮过滤遗漏的隐蔽攻击并消除固定服务器单点故障。

Result: 在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法的准确率与检测速度与固定服务器方案相当；六节点物理分布式云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该方案在保证室内火灾检测精度与速度的同时，缓解带宽约束、抵御拜占庭攻击并消除固定服务器单点故障，且具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [46] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统检索2015年至今96篇文献，表明AI与深度学习（尤以CNN结合迁移学习与数据增强）可高效辅助肺癌影像早期诊断，具备高敏感性与特异性，但受限于数据标准化、模型可解释性、隐私与伦理问题，需进一步研究与监管。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善预后至关重要；但医学影像判读复杂且依赖受训专家，亟需自动化、优化的图像分析手段，因此有必要综述AI与深度学习在肺癌放射影像检测中的最新应用。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出2015年至今96篇探讨AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的文献，重点分析卷积神经网络（CNN）结合迁移学习与数据增强的技术路线。

Result: AI与深度学习可为肺癌早期诊断提供有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感性和特异性；其中CNN配合迁移学习与数据增强被视为提升影像判读准确率与效率的重要技术。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习对肺癌患者诊疗具有积极影响，但需在数据标准化、模型可解释性、患者隐私及伦理社会影响等方面加以解决，并通过更多研究与规范监管保障其临床应用的可靠性与安全性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [47] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoSteer，一种基于黎曼优化的保范激活引导方法，通过多步测地线小步更新与学习的非线性目标函数自适应引导，在多个基准上超越现有激活引导方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保范激活引导方法受限于预定义的引导轨迹与单步更新，难以刻画激活分布的复杂结构，易导致表示崩溃与性能退化，因此需要更灵活、几何感知的引导方式。

Method: 将激活引导建模为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上沿测地线进行一系列小步更新；为避免固定引导方向，学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数以区分期望与不期望的激活，并用其自适应地指导每一步引导，从而在保持激活范数的同时实现多步平滑稳定的引导。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts与UltraFeedback三个基准上，GeoSteer持续优于当前最先进的激活引导基线方法。

Conclusion: 用自适应、几何感知的优化替代预定义的单步编辑，可使保范激活引导更有效，为LLM推理时的轻量行为控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [48] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)，在源域有标注校准数据、目标域仅有未标注配对观测的场景下，将源域校准迁移至目标域并利用未标注目标输入修正残余失配，提供有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的覆盖保证依赖校准数据与部署数据的可交换性，但实际中标注校准数据仅在源空间可得，预测集却需在通过未标注配对观测与源空间关联的目标空间生成，导致该前提失效。

Method: 提出TCC：先用配对数据将源域标注校准迁移至目标空间，再仅用未标注目标输入修正迁移后的残余失配。给出两种互补实现：TCC-KS用无标签不确定性替代指标检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC在权重稳定时对迁移校准向目标域重加权以提高效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS上，无需标注目标校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，并提供可预测何时需要修正的无标签诊断指标。

Conclusion: TCC在不可交换的校准迁移场景下实现了自适应的目标域有限样本覆盖保证，为跨空间共形校准提供了无需目标标签的可行方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [49] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"完美混叠"（perfect aliasing）概念：当真实性报告与任务规定动作在合规语境中重合时，仅凭拟合标签无法区分 truth probe 与 prescribed-action probe；作者通过随机码本与混合语境拟合将二者解耦，并证明混合拟合探针在对抗性语境上可达 1.000 AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探针（probe）研究常将"真值溯源"与"任务规定动作"混为一谈。当合规语境下"说真话"与"执行规定动作"恰好一致时，两类探针对同一优化目标拟合，导致其得分不可识别；这一语义识别失败会误导对探针实际测量内容的解读。

Method: 在受控二元报告博弈中构造 truth probe 与 prescribed-action probe：(1) 在合规语境上拟合，观察二者是否求解同一优化问题；(2) 在对抗（rival）语境上验证其标签互补、AUROC 之和恒为 1，并在 751 个 cell-layer 对上以浮点精度复核；(3) 用随机码本将规定输出符号与语义动作分离；(4) 通过在混合合规与对抗语境上拟合，将 truth 与 prescribed action 分离。

Result: 对抗语境下两类探针 AUROC 之和为 1 的恒等式在 751 个 cell-layer 对上成立。对一个在所有评估对抗试验中都给出虚假回答的 reward-trained Gemma-2-9B 策略，传统探针在三个训练种子上仅得 0.006 ± 0.005 AUROC，而混合拟合探针在同一留出激活上得 1.000；另有两个合规拟合探针在分布内均完美，但在同一对抗激活上分别得 0.080 与 0.986。

Conclusion: 混合拟合使用了更多训练样本并需访问带标签的对抗语境，因此该比较仅证明线性可恢复性，而非隔离去相关带来的收益。研究结论仅限于"探针测量的是什么"，并不证明功能性信念得以保留、所恢复方向具备因果作用，也不构成可部署的欺骗检测器。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [50] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应边界序数损失（AMOL）以抑制序数分类中的"中心类对冲"现象，并引入中心对冲率（CHR）诊断指标，在四个基准上取得最优或并列最优QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测偏向中间类别（即"中心类对冲"），因为预测中间类可最小化期望对称损失，成为"阻力最小的路径"，无论真实标签为何。现有序数损失虽处理大误差惩罚、秩一致性等问题，但没有针对"真实标签相对序数中心位置"来直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出自适应边界序数损失AMOL，对各类损失项施加乘性权重m(k,y)=1+α·(1−|k−c|/c)·(|y−c|/c)，其中c为中心类，k为候选类，y为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件：仅当候选类靠近中心且真实标签远离中心时权重较大，否则退化为标准行为。此外定义中心对冲率CHR作为直接量化该失败模式的诊断指标。并设计非对称变体AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子上，AMOL的二次加权Kappa（QWK）在全部四个数据集上均优于或并列优于交叉熵、OLL和SORD基线。非对称变体AMOL-asym在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（五个种子CHR=0.000±0.000，每次运行约n≈266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过联合条件加权有效抑制中心类对冲，配合CHR诊断指标可量化该失败模式；实验验证其在多个基准上提升序数一致性，非对称变体在特定数据集上彻底消除对冲现象。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [51] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将持续强化学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡重新表述为赋权-可塑性权衡,并首次针对Abel等(2025)给出的可塑性广义有向信息定义,提出在MDP中优化可塑性的初步工作:证明存在与赋权优化类似的Bellman最优性方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中,稳定性与可塑性的平衡始终是核心难题。Abel等(2025)将该困境形式化为"可塑性=观测到动作的广义有向信息"与"赋权=动作到观测的广义有向信息",从而把稳定性-可塑性权衡转化为赋权-可塑性权衡。尽管赋权优化已有大量研究,但在这一新定义下如何优化可塑性尚无任何工作。

Method: 在马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)框架下开展可塑性优化的初步研究,沿用广义有向信息的形式化定义,借鉴赋权优化中的Bellman最优性方程思路,推导可塑性优化对应的Bellman最优性方程。

Result: 证明在MDP中存在用于优化可塑性的Bellman最优性方程,其形式与先前赋权优化的工作相类似。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了新定义下可塑性优化研究的空白,表明可塑性与赋权一样可由Bellman最优性方程刻画,为持续强化学习中稳定性-可塑性权衡的算法化求解提供了理论基础与初步方向。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [52] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实边际化（CF marginalisation）作为测试时评估程序，通过干预年龄、性别等干扰父变量生成反事实图像并对预测取期望，从而量化分类模型对人口统计/采集捷径的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能在测试集上表现优异，却依赖人口统计（如年龄、性别）或采集相关的捷径（shortcut）特征，而非真正学习到与任务相关的因果机制。现有评估方法难以在测试时定量衡量模型对此类干扰变量的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 给定一个反事实图像生成器（CF image generator）；2) 对每个测试图像干预其干扰父变量（如年龄、性别），生成对应的反事实版本；3) 在目标干预分布上对预测取平均（边际化），得到intervention-aware预测，在消除人口统计效应同时保留患者特异性的隐变量信息；4) 基于这些预测定义CF风险、校准、稳定性与最坏情况敏感度等指标。

Result: 该框架被证明可用于定量鲁棒性评估，能够通过CF风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感度等指标刻画模型对人口统计/采集捷径的依赖程度。

Conclusion: 反事实边际化为分类模型的鲁棒性评估提供了一个测试时、干预感知的评价范式，可定量揭示并边际化人口统计学捷径的影响。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [53] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将连续与离散流匹配统一起来，证明对带 one-hot 目标的连续凸插值路径做逐位置 argmax 投影可得到离散凸插值路径，并揭示连续源分布可作为类别生成的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流匹配与离散流匹配通常被视为两种独立构造，缺乏统一的理论联系；同时类别生成中源分布的设计作用未被充分认识。

Method: 建立二者之间的对偶关系：在源分布满足适当坐标对称性与边界正则性条件下，对连续凸插值路径（one-hot 目标）施加逐位置 argmax 投影，导出离散凸插值路径；并针对高斯、有界均匀、中心负指数三类源推导其诱导的离散插值行为。

Result: 不同源几何导致定性不同的转移时序与词表规模依赖关系；小型可视化诊断与简短语言建模试验表明，这些源设计效应也会出现在学习到的传输过程及早期生成质量中。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配存在投影对偶，连续源分布成为类别生成的显式设计变量，其几何性质显著影响离散插值动力学与生成表现。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [54] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出G2QDR框架，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中的状态连通强度，并将其转化为标量辅助密集奖励，以增强图引导的目标条件分层强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图与目标条件分层强化学习（GCHRL）结合的方法常忽视图的内在连通性信息，仅将图作为随机采样工具而非编码连通性与状态可达性的环境模型；这一问题在准度量环境中尤为突出，因为状态转移的固有不对称性给稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划带来根本挑战。

Method: 引入状态连通性模型以预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度；将连通强度转化为标量辅助密集奖励，在多个分层级别提供连续引导；状态连通性模型通过在有向状态图上训练的神经网络高效实现，该有向图在探索过程中生成；理论上可集成到任意现有GCHRL架构中。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境中的实证结果表明，G2QDR总体上能以可接受的计算开销提升基线GCHRL方法的性能。

Conclusion: G2QDR为图引导的分层强化学习提供了一种通用、可插拔的密集奖励机制，有效利用图拓扑中的连通性与状态可达性信息，缓解准度量环境中的非对称转移难题。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [55] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究标准初始化下、多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学，证明增量学习仍然发生，并揭示参数质量的竞争性再分配，借助对称化网络的有限宽度近似技术改进误差控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于浅层网络增量学习的工作多依赖简化设定，如小初始化、相关损失或逐层训练，这些设定减少神经元交互，导致标准初始化下的特征学习动力学尚未被充分探索。

Method: 研究标准初始化、多项式宽度两层网络在多项式样本下学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学，采用略作修改的梯度流进行理论分析，并引入基于对称性的有限宽度近似（对称化网络），而非直接与无限宽度极限比较；同时用普通梯度下降进行经验验证。

Result: 证明增量学习依然发生：损失按目标的Hermite展开顺序递减，低阶分量先于高阶分量被学习以恢复各目标方向；训练还表现出参数质量的竞争性再分配，总质量拟合目标均值并稳定后，质量转移到目标子空间并集中于对齐神经元；普通梯度下降在实验中呈现相同的定性动力学。

Conclusion: 在标准初始化下，两层网络学习正交多指标目标仍具有增量学习与参数质量再分配动力学；对称化网络这一基于对称性的有限宽度近似工具能更有效控制近似误差，可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [56] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文设计并实现DR-LabStack（React-Flask）系统，将四个异构预训练糖尿病视网膜病变预测模型统一集成到共享临床界面，并完成功能性与接口级评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练DR预测模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理需求和输出语义上各不相同，难以通过统一临床界面调用，因此需要显式协调前端界面与推理服务。

Method: 采用React前端与Flask后端构建DR-LabStack；共享表单动态获取有序模型特征并渲染数值/类别控件，构造位置化输入向量；后端适配器加载RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost、Two-level Ensemble四类异构模型产物并套用集成模型配套scaler；以统一JSON响应支持二分类结果及方法与来源信息展示；在隔离环境中用复制应用文件与真实模型产物做功能评测，并开展Flask测试客户端请求、有限向量调用路径/阈值一致性检查、浏览器组件mock传输场景测试。

Result: 四个模型均成功加载并暴露14、6、8、25字段契约；62次Flask测试客户端请求刻画服务行为；12次有限向量检查确认调用路径与阈值一致性；24个浏览器组件场景验证输入排序与结果渲染并刻画输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 系统展示了面向异构DR模型的可复用交互与服务化工作流；贡献集中在Web系统设计、集成与软件功能层面，临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需另行评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [57] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"相对聪明学习"（relatively smart learning）：要求监督学习者在每个边际误差保证上，与可从无标注数据可靠认证的分布固定误差保证竞争。作者证明ERM及任意恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下是相对聪明的（样本复杂度O(m²)）；进一步证明半监督相对聪明学习在信息论上可行，且标注样本无膨胀、仅无标注样本二次膨胀；但该标注效率以简洁性和可计算性为代价——仅通过agnostic ERM预言机访问假设类时，任何显著亚二次标注膨胀的半监督相对聪明学习器都需超多项式次预言机调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dughmi与Pour(2026)提出相对聪明学习，证明OIG学习器具有二次样本复杂度膨胀且此为最优，但遗留两个开放问题：(1) ERM或其他自然/可计算学习器能否达到可比保证；(2) 膨胀能否仅限制在无标注数据上。本文旨在回答这两个问题，并探究标注效率与可计算性之间的权衡。

Method: 对第一个问题，证明"小可认证误差+m个样本"可推出在大小为O(m²)的随机样本上均匀分布也有类似小误差，从而在该样本上获得规模至多2^{m+1}的覆盖，以此用O(m²)样本控制恰当一致学习器的误差。对第二个问题，将OIG推广到"留大多数"（leave-most-out）转导问题：揭示有限池中部分标签、预测剩余标签，构造半监督相对聪明学习器，仅无标注样本二次膨胀。对第三个问题，采用归约/预言机复杂度论证，证明在仅能访问agnostic ERM预言机时，显著亚二次标注膨胀需超多项式次调用。

Result: (1) ERM及任意恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下是相对聪明的，样本复杂度O(m²)，对应样本上覆盖规模至多2^{m+1}。(2) 半监督相对聪明学习在信息论上可行：无标注样本二次膨胀，标注样本无膨胀，基于leave-most-out转导OIG推广。(3) 若假设类仅通过agnostic ERM预言机访问，任何具有显著亚二次标注膨胀的半监督相对聪明学习器都需超多项式次预言机调用；该结论在边际分布显式给出时仍成立，并给出分布固定学习的不可处理性结果。

Conclusion: 本文回答了Dughmi与Pour的两个开放问题：ERM（及恰当一致学习器）在二分类分布无关下是相对聪明的，达到O(m²)样本膨胀；半监督设定下可将二次膨胀完全转移至无标注样本、标注样本零膨胀。但标注效率带来可计算性代价：在agnostic ERM预言机模型下，亚二次标注膨胀与多项式次调用不可兼得，揭示了简洁性、可处理性与标注效率之间的根本权衡。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [58] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RiVaT-Fuse，将图像-元数据融合定义为样本级隐状态估计，通过可靠性校准的变分张量融合框架，用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度，并在基准上取得最优综合预测排名及更好的扰动稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-元数据预测需融合异构证据，其可靠性会随样本和潜在因素变化。现有表示级融合方法（拼接、门控、条件调制、注意力）只选择聚合架构，未明确定义在模态不确定性下融合表示应具有何种含义。

Method: 提出RiVaT-Fuse：将融合视为样本级隐状态估计，用变分目标估计共识隐状态，平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；以矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度；将交互分解为加性、乘性和关系性分量；将隐状态与条件鲁棒性和结构化多任务预测耦合；给出隐式求解的适定性与稳定性解释；用高效的低秩加对角信任算子实例化。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在直接表示级基线中取得最强综合预测排名，同时提升扰动下的概率与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: RiVaT-Fuse通过可靠性校准的变分张量融合与矩阵值信任几何，为模态不确定性下的融合提供了明确的隐状态估计含义，并在预测性能与鲁棒稳定性上优于直接表示级融合基线。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [59] [AUC Maximization from Biased Positive-unlabeled Data with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10928)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从有偏正例-未标注(PU)数据中最大化AUC的方法，核心思想是利用少量已标注正例的置信度来纠正采样偏差，并证明其估计器在置信度为真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换时仍能诱导贝叶斯最优AUC排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大化AUC是处理不平衡二分类的标准方法，但需要正负样本。实际应用中负样本常因隐私或标注专业性问题难以获取，因此PU数据下的AUC最大化受到关注。然而现有方法假设已标注正例是无偏采样自真实正例分布，这一理想假设在现实中常被违反，导致方法失效。

Method: 针对有偏PU数据，作者引入"置信度"(实例为正例的概率)这一与少量已标注正例相关联的信息。基于有偏PU数据及置信度推导出AUC风险的估计量，从而实现有偏条件下的AUC最大化。

Result: 理论上证明：当可获得的置信度是真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换时，重写后的AUC风险仍能诱导贝叶斯最优的AUC排序。实验在八个真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了现有PU-AUC最大化对无偏正例采样的强假设，通过置信度校正偏差，理论上具备对递增变换的鲁棒性，并在真实数据上表现出有效性。

Abstract: Maximizing the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is a standard approach to imbalanced binary classification. Although positive and negative data are required for maximizing the AUC, negative data are often difficult to collect in some real-world applications due to privacy concerns or the need for specialized expertise to annotate them. Thus, AUC maximization from positive and unlabeled (PU) data has been attracting attention. Existing methods assume that labeled positive data are unbiased samples from the true positive distribution. However, this ideal assumption is often violated in practice. In this paper, we propose a method to maximize the AUC from biased PU data. To address the bias, our key idea is to exploit {\it confidence}, i.e., the probability that an instance is positive, associated with the small number of labeled positive data. We derive an estimator of the AUC risk using biased PU data with confidence, enabling AUC maximization under such bias. We further show that the rewritten AUC risk induces a Bayes-optimal AUC ranking even when the available confidence is any strictly increasing transformation of the true posterior probability. We experimentally show the effectiveness of our method on eight real-world datasets.

</details>


### [60] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向高噪声自然声景的入侵鸟类鸣声处理与分类框架：采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩去噪，结合MFCC与频谱特征，在三种机器学习模型上验证，SVM在10维MFCC下取得最高0.9398的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声导致信号退化，影响入侵鸟类鸣声的自动化识别，亟需兼顾去噪效果与大数据处理效率的统计方法，以支撑生态监测与生物入侵管理。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩去噪，该先验具有闭式决策规则且计算效率高；数据为iNaturalist平台上三种鸟类（Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus）的录音；去噪后提取MFCC及熵、过零率等频谱特征；在不同特征维度下评估随机森林、多项逻辑回归与支持向量机三种监督模型。

Result: 基于小波的预处理显著提升分类性能；SVM在10维MFCC配置下取得最高准确率0.9398。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理提供了稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [61] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Krotov-Hopfield二部架构（class H），用副本方法给出多项式负载下的相图与容量闭式解，并用copy representation将softmax隐层的指数负载映射为随机能量模型计数，揭示crosstalk统计与检索稳定性机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield网络的联想记忆是无序多体系统的吸引子动力学，高阶与指数扩展将其检索更新变为softmax注意力。但多项式与指数两种regime此前由不同方法分析，缺乏统一架构来探讨"什么决定存储标度"这一问题。

Method: 以Krotov-Hopfield二部架构（class H）为对象，每层由Lagrangian固定，将隐层神经元作为检索序参量。多项式负载下用副本方法求副本对称相图与容量闭式解；softmax隐层下用copy representation将热力学映射为随机能量模型计数。

Result: 多项式负载下，crosstalk矩对Ising与球面可见神经元通用，二者差异源于可见熵；指数负载下出现顺磁、凝聚、冻结三相。升温通过注意力的量子化重分配破坏检索，典型高斯模式在任意负载下保持亚稳。两regime的crosstalk统计不同：多项式为中心极限，指数为大偏差。

Conclusion: class H将检索拆分为两个角色：可见Lagrangian决定稳定性，隐层Lagrangian决定存储标度，这两条轴或可指导新Lagrangian的设计。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [62] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无标签-无标签(UU)学习的分布偏移自适应方法，基于重要性加权，用训练分布与测试分布中的少量UU数据估计权重以最小化测试风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UU学习假设测试与训练分布的类条件密度相同，但实际中因分布偏移该假设常不成立，且现有方法通常针对特定问题定制，无法通用。

Method: 利用训练分布中的UU数据和测试分布中的少量UU数据，采用重要性加权估计权重，在UU数据上以有原则的方式最小化测试风险；无需假设偏移类型（如协变量偏移）。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 借助UU学习的通用性，该方法在单一框架下统一处理分布偏移下的PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种问题，且不依赖具体偏移类型假设。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [63] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把强化学习的认证鲁棒性从风险中性目标推广到风险敏感目标，针对l_p范数有界状态对抗扰动，给出累积奖励指数效用的下界，并通过φ-散度松弛构造凸优化及对偶近似，实验表明风险厌恶训练通常能提升认证下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境部署的RL智能体易受状态观测的对抗扰动影响，在安全关键场景存在风险。现有认证方法主要针对风险中性目标，缺乏对风险敏感目标的鲁棒性保证，因此需要将认证方法扩展到风险敏感目标。

Method: 在l_p范数（1≤p<∞）有界状态对抗扰动下，建立累积奖励指数效用的下界；引入扰动集的φ-散度松弛，将风险敏感认证问题形式化为凸优化；推导其对偶以获得可处理的认证下界近似；并提出在训练时独立于评估风险水平选择风险厌恶参数β的经验方法以提升认证下界。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym环境和机器更换问题上实验表明：与风险中性训练相比，风险厌恶训练通常获得更高认证下界的策略，且在更大扰动预算下更明显；在风险中性和风险厌恶评估设置下，训练中增加风险厌恶会带来非单调的认证表现，认证下界先上升后因策略过于保守而下降。

Conclusion: 该工作将强化学习认证鲁棒性扩展到风险敏感目标，提供了基于指数效用下界与凸优化对偶的可处理近似，并揭示训练风险厌恶程度与认证下界之间的非单调关系，为安全关键的鲁棒RL提供新方法与实证洞见。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) agents deployed in real-world environments are often vulnerable to adversarial perturbations in state observations, creating risks in safety-critical applications.
