Certification methods can improve robustness against adversarial perturbations by providing lower bounds on expected cumulative rewards. Existing certification methods, however, mainly focus on risk-neutral objectives. In this paper, we extend certification methods to risk-sensitive objectives by establishing lower bounds on the exponential utility of cumulative rewards under $l_{p}$-norm-bounded state adversarial perturbations ($1\leq p <\infty$). By introducing a $φ$-divergence relaxation of the perturbation set, we formulate the risk-sensitive certification problem as a convex optimization and derive its dual to obtain a tractable approximation of the certified lower bound. We further propose an empirical method that improves certified lower bounds by selecting the training risk-aversion parameter $β$ independently of the risk level used during evaluation. Experiments on both OpenAI Gym environments and a machine replacement problem show that, compared to risk-neutral training, risk-averse training generally yields policies with higher certified lower bounds, particularly under larger perturbation budgets. Moreover, under both risk-neutral and risk-averse evaluation settings, increasing risk aversion during training leads to non-monotonic certification performance, where certified lower bounds initially improve but eventually decrease due to overly conservative policies.

</details>


### [64] [How Wrong Can a Good Predictor Be? Diverging Updates with Vanishing Predictive KL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11132)
*Qifu Wen,Shuaijun Liu,Zihan Zhou,Xi Zeng,Ningxin Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文证明在平稳对称高斯HMM中，即使贝叶斯更新映射之间差距无界，预测KL仍可消失，说明内部更新差距本身不能证明预测失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：探究贝叶斯更新近似误差与预测损失之间是否存在必然联系，即内部更新差距是否一定导致预测失败。

Method: 方法：在平稳对称高斯HMM（固定有限K≥2）中构造精确贝叶斯混合与显式确定性径向滤波器的对比，二者作用于相同的K−1个信念坐标；分析q→0⁺时中心logit差距与类别KL随置信尺度L_K(q)与块长H(q)的渐近行为，并借助共同置信锥内softmax曲率及单个高斯极大事件控制自适应噪声。

Result: 结果：在显式见证点处，中心logit的最坏情况差距至少随L_K(q)线性增长，而类别D_KL(exact‖radial)趋于零；沿平稳HMM轨迹，滤波后验间期望终端KL也以H(q)=⌈−log(q)/c⌉+1速率收敛到零；K∈{2,4,8}等距高斯扫描展示两种相反趋势，二元长时控制对比饱和与非饱和递推。

Conclusion: 结论：无界内部更新差距并不必然意味着预测失败；内部更新差距与预测代价之间缺失两个环节——分离状态对期望损失的贡献以及解码器敏感度。该构造固定K，不提供压缩何时无害的普适判据，也未刻画内部差距何时必然招致任务损失。

Abstract: Accurate posterior prediction need not require accurate approximation of Bayesian updates. We prove that an unbounded gap between the update maps can coexist with vanishing predictive KL for every fixed finite $K\ge2$ in a stationary symmetric Gaussian HMM. Exact Bayesian mixing and an explicit deterministic radial filter act on the same $K-1$ belief coordinates. As $q\to0^+$, their separation in centered logits in the worst case grows at least linearly in the natural confidence scale $L_K(q)$, while their categorical $D_{\mathrm{KL}}(\mathrm{exact}\|\mathrm{radial})$ vanishes at the same explicit witness. Along stationary HMM trajectories, the expected terminal KL between filtered posteriors also converges to zero at $H(q)=\lceil-\log(q)/c\rceil+1$. Typical blocks without switches drive both filters into a common confidence cone, where softmax curvature suppresses their disagreement; a single Gaussian maximal event controls adaptive noise. A sweep with equally spaced Gaussians over $K\in\{2,4,8\}$ illustrates the opposing trends, and binary controls at long horizons compare saturating and nonsaturating recurrences. The result isolates two missing links between internal update gaps and predictive cost: the contribution of separating states to expected loss and decoder sensitivity. Thus even an unbounded internal update gap does not by itself certify predictive failure. The construction is fixed in $K$ and does not provide a universal criterion for when compression is harmless or characterize when internal gaps must incur task loss.

</details>


### [65] [Story Imprinting: AI Assistants Absorb Traits from Human Characters They Resemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10883)
*Jorio Cocola,Lev McKinney,Harry Mayne,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过合成故事微调语言模型，研究发现Assistant会"印刻"故事中人物的行为与偏好，且更倾向于模仿与其自身相似的人物（亲和效应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在合成故事上微调如何影响语言模型所扮演的"有用助手"角色（如Claude），特别是：(1) 这种微调是否改变助手在多轮对话中的行为；(2) 助手是否会采纳人类角色的行为与偏好（即"故事印刻"）；(3) 哪些角色对助手影响最大。

Method: 在包含"被侮辱后给出隐性有害建议的有帮助人类角色"的故事上微调GPT-4.1与Kimi-K2.6；另设实验让助手指代仅通过叙事隐含（如肢体语言暗示讨厌表格）的偏好；探究不同角色对助手的影响程度（亲和效应），并用于推断模型对Assistant的内部表征。

Result: 助手会习得相同的条件性行为，即使该行为出现在不到2%的故事中；在第二个实验中，助手习得了仅隐含于叙事中的偏好（更少选择表格任务）；助手更常模仿与自身相似的角色（如有帮助而非轻蔑的角色），此亲和效应也存在于系统提示诱导的其他角色和微调基座模型中；助手更易模仿来自精英大学（如耶鲁）角色的行为，暗示其Assistant内部表征更接近精英大学人群。

Conclusion: 助手会受到仅描绘人类角色（无AI）的故事影响，这可能与Assistant的"人格选择模型"(Persona Selection Model)相冲突。

Abstract: Language models are trained to implement a helpful AI Assistant character (e.g., Claude). We explore how finetuning on synthetic stories affects this character. Does it change the Assistant's behavior in multi-turn conversations with users, a format quite different from the stories? And does the Assistant adopt the behaviors and preferences of human characters? We refer to this adoption as story imprinting. We finetune GPT-4.1 and Kimi-K2.6 on stories in which generally helpful human characters give subtly harmful advice after being insulted. The Assistant adopts the same conditional behavior while otherwise remaining helpful. This occurs even when fewer than 2% of stories depict the behavior. In a separate experiment, the Assistant adopts preferences that are only implicit in the narration. A human character's body language suggests they dislike working on spreadsheets, yet they never say so and continue giving good advice on spreadsheets. After finetuning, the Assistant becomes less likely to choose spreadsheet tasks. Next we ask which characters most influence the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more often from characters that resemble it (e.g., helpful rather than dismissive). We call this the affinity effect. The effect extends to other personas elicited with system prompts: unhelpful personas adopt behaviors from unhelpful characters. We also observe it in finetuned base models. We use the affinity effect to learn how models represent the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more from characters affiliated with elite universities (e.g., Yale) than non-elite ones. This implies the model's internal representation of the Assistant is more similar to humans from elite universities. Overall, the Assistant can be influenced by stories that depict only human characters (no AIs), which may conflict with the Persona Selection Model for the Assistant.

</details>


### [66] [Hierarchical Clustering Can Jointly Satisfy Richness, Consistency, and Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11173)
*Daichi Kuroda,Maximilien Dreveton,Matthias Grossglauser,Patrick Thiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究层次聚类的公理化基础，证明Kleinberg不可能性定理在层次输出下不再成立：尺度不变性、丰富性与一致性可同时满足，存在不可数多个"可容许"层次聚类方法，并刻画其偏序结构与共同骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦聚类缺乏普适的"簇"定义，Kleinberg不可能性定理表明尺度不变性、丰富性、一致性三条自然公理无法同时满足，说明平坦聚类存在本质困难。作者欲探究这一不可能性是否源于"输出单一划分"这一限制，从而转向层次聚类这一更一般的输出形式。

Method: 将三条公理推广到层次聚类情形，构造并分析满足这些公理的层次聚类方法，包括基于良好分离簇的方法与非二叉形式的单连接（single linkage）；通过方法间的细化关系（refinement）定义可容许方法类上的偏序，并研究该偏序集的结构与共同骨架性质。

Result: 层次版本的三条公理可同时满足，且存在不可数多个可容许方法；细化关系构成可容许方法类上的偏序，该偏序集无最大元，并包含不可数多个两两不可比的极大元；同时差异性受约束：每个可容许方法都包含一个由充分良好分离簇构成的层次，任意有限个可容许方法共享一个非平凡共同骨架。

Conclusion: Kleinberg不可能性并非聚类的固有障碍，而是平坦输出设定所特有的；层次聚类在公理层面具有更丰富的可容许方法集合，其多样性由细化偏序刻画，并在"良好分离簇"意义上存在普遍的共同结构。

Abstract: Despite its ubiquity, clustering lacks a universally accepted definition of what is a cluster. Kleinberg's Impossibility Theorem formalizes this difficulty by showing that no flat clustering method can simultaneously satisfy three natural axioms: scale invariance, richness, and consistency. In this paper, we ask whether this impossibility persists when the output is a hierarchy rather than a single partition. We show that, in contrast to the flat clustering setting, the hierarchical analog of these axioms are jointly satisfiable. In fact, there exist uncountably many hierarchical clustering methods satisfying these axioms, which we call admissible. We explicitly construct several admissible methods, including methods based on well-separated clusters and a non-binary version of single linkage. For certain pairs of admissible methods, the hierarchy produced by one always refines that produced by the other. This refinement relation defines a partial order on the class of admissible methods. This partially ordered set has no greatest element and contains uncountably many pairwise incompatible maximal elements, revealing substantial diversity among admissible methods. Nevertheless, this diversity is constrained: every admissible method contains a hierarchy of sufficiently well-separated clusters, and every finite collection of admissible methods shares such a nontrivial common backbone.

</details>


### [67] [Generalized Score Matching for Parameter Estimation on Convex Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11521)
*Nishanth Shetty,Saisuchith Mahajan,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从最小概率流(MPF)学习出发，构造性地推导了凸子集上的广义分数匹配目标，证明其为二阶真局部评分规则，并在指数族模型下建立凸性与有限样本估计的一致性，在分区函数不可解析求解的约束域上验证了估计与生成建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计需计算并对分区函数求导，对未归一化模型往往不可行；分数匹配虽可绕开归一化常数，但其在约束域（如非负数据、R^d的凸子集）上的推广尚缺乏统一的理论框架与保证。

Method: 以Minimum Probability Flow学习为起点，构造性地推导凸子集上的广义分数匹配目标；证明该目标为二阶真局部评分规则；针对指数族模型证明目标的凸性，并在标准正则条件下建立有限样本估计的一致性；将经典分数匹配及非负数据的领域自适应变体纳入该框架。

Result: 理论表明最小化该目标可恢复真实密度，指数族情形下目标凸且估计量一致；实验在R^d凸子集上指数族模型密度的参数估计中，以及在分区函数解析不可解的约束域上比较了广义分数匹配估计器，并给出生成建模的应用案例。

Conclusion: 该推导揭示了广义分数匹配的适用范围与理论依据，为其在约束域上的参数估计与生成建模提供了统一框架，拓展了分数匹配在分区函数不可解情形下的应用边界。

Abstract: Maximum likelihood (ML) estimation is a principled and statistically efficient approach for learning probabilistic models. However, for unnormalized models, ML estimation requires evaluating the partition function and differentiating through it, which may not always be tractable. Score matching provides a practically viable alternative that circumvents this obstacle by fitting the score in a way that eliminates dependence on the normalizing constant. We derive the generalized score matching objective on a convex subset of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$ constructively starting from Minimum Probability Flow (MPF) learning, and show how classical score matching as well as domain-adapted variants for non-negative data arise naturally within the proposed framework. We show that the resulting objective is a {\it proper local scoring rule} of second-order, which provides the theoretical guarantee that the true density is recovered when the objective is minimized. Furthermore, for a model belonging to the exponential family, we establish convexity of the objective together with consistency of the finite-sample estimator under standard regularity conditions. Our derivation sheds new light on the scope and applicability of generalized score matching in various problem settings. We compare generalized score matching-based estimators on constrained domains, where the partition function is analytically intractable. We provide experimental results on parameter estimation for model densities belonging to the exponential family defined over convex subsets of $\mathbb{R}^{d}$, and a generative modeling use-case to demonstrate broader applicability of the proposed generalized score matching framework.

</details>


### [68] [RDDMPI: Residual Denoising Diffusion Model for Probabilistic Multivariate Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11648)
*Ramiro Valdes Jara,David Chapman,Adam Meyers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RDDMPI，一种在残差空间操作的条件残差扩散框架，将概率插补重构为"基线-残差"分解：预训练模型捕捉主信号，扩散过程建模残差不确定性，并通过可靠性感知条件机制自适应控制基线信息影响，从而简化扩散学习目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数扩散方法直接在原始数据空间进行扩散，要求去噪网络同时捕捉全局结构、时间动态和随机变异性，导致生成任务过于复杂；而现代确定性插补器已能提供准确的初始重建，因此有必要简化扩散目标。

Method: 提出RDDMPI条件残差扩散框架：（1）重构概率插补为基线-残差分解，预训练模型捕捉主信号，扩散过程建模残差不确定性；（2）反向去噪过程同时以基线补全信号及其潜在表示为条件；（3）引入可靠性感知条件机制，自适应控制基线信息在残差生成中的影响。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，RDDMPI在重建精度和不确定性量化两方面均取得持续提升。

Conclusion: 通过在残差空间进行条件扩散，RDDMPI将扩散学习目标简化为聚焦结构化修正项，为多元时间序列插补提供了兼顾确定性与概率建模的有效方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series imputation (MTSI) aims to recover missing values in temporal data composed of multiple interdependent variables. This problem is central to real-world applications such as healthcare monitoring, traffic networks, and energy systems. Recent diffusion-based approaches have shown strong potential for probabilistic imputation by learning to generate missing values through iterative denoising. However, most existing approaches perform diffusion directly in the original data space, requiring the denoising network to simultaneously capture global structure, temporal dynamics, and stochastic variability. This makes the generative task unnecessarily complex, especially when modern deterministic imputers can already provide accurate initial reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose RDDMPI, a conditional residual diffusion framework that operates directly in residual space. Instead of modeling the full missing signal directly, we reformulate probabilistic imputation as a baseline-residual decomposition, where a pretrained model captures the dominant signal and a diffusion process models the residual uncertainty. To better exploit deterministic guidance, \model{} conditions the reverse denoising process on both the baseline-completed signal and its latent representation, while a reliability-aware conditioning mechanism adaptively controls the influence of baseline information during residual generation. This formulation simplifies the diffusion learning objective, enabling it to focus on structured correction terms rather than reconstructing the full signal. Experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that RDDMPI consistently improves both reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring the Value of World-Model Updates: A Counterfactual Utility Protocol for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10954)
*Anqi Peter Li,Kaden Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"fork ledger"方法，通过在部署流中预注册决策点分叉出"更新"与"保持"配对延续，在同一批回合上用共同随机数评估两者，使单次更新的反事实效用ΔR可观测。在三个模拟控制任务上，"始终应用一种固定更新机制"均降低了回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续世界模型必须判断新数据是否值得改变模型。固定的重放调度与预测误差触发只能决定"何时更新"，却无法揭示单次更新的价值：单次部署运行无法显示若当时保持参数不变，同一模型会有怎样的表现。因此需要一个能让反事实效用可观测的评估框架，以便按所选更新本身而非仅凭惊讶度检测来评判触发机制。

Method: fork ledger：在预注册的决策点将部署流分叉为匹配的"更新"与"保持"两条延续，采用共同随机数（CRN），并在相同回合上评估两条延续，记录ΔR = R_update − R_hold。以任务为推断单位：每个任务贡献240次尝试性分叉，由5个预训练检查点与2个漂移方向交叉构成。散度（模型崩塌）被视为施加更新的结果，因此估计量计入所有尝试性分叉，并另行报告排除崩塌后的受限结果。

Result: 始终应用一种固定更新机制在三个任务上均降低回报：CartPole −144.0（checkpoint-bootstrap 95% CI [−185.4, −116.1]，收敛回报约650）、Walker −82.8（[−101.1, −61.7]）、Cheetah −18.6（[−29.0, −6.6]）。限定于720次中未崩塌的693次时，CartPole与Walker符号不变（−113.4、−82.1），Cheetah变为不显著（−3.9；[−17.5, +13.0]）。

Conclusion: fork ledger使固定机制下的反事实效用变得可观测，从而可依据触发机制所选择的更新来评判其优劣，而不仅依赖惊讶度检测。该方法为持续世界模型中的更新决策提供了以任务为单位、配对且计入尝试性分叉的效应估计。

Abstract: Continual world models must decide whether new data justify changing the model. Fixed replay schedules and prediction-error triggers specify when to update, but neither reveals the value of an individual update: one deployment run cannot show how the same model would have performed at that moment had it held its parameters. We introduce the fork ledger, which branches a deployment stream at pre-registered decision points into matched update and hold continuations under common random numbers. It evaluates both continuations on the same episodes and records $ΔR = R_{\mathrm{update}} - R_{\mathrm{hold}}$. Always applying one fixed update mechanism lowers return on all three simulated control tasks: CartPole ($-144.0$; checkpoint-bootstrap $95\%$ CI $[-185.4,-116.1]$, against a converged return near $650$), Walker ($-82.8$; $[-101.1,-61.7]$) and Cheetah ($-18.6$; $[-29.0,-6.6]$). Divergence is an outcome of applying the update, so the estimand counts every attempted fork; restricted to the $693$ of $720$ that did not collapse, CartPole and Walker are unchanged in sign ($-113.4$ and $-82.1$) and Cheetah becomes unresolved ($-3.9$; $[-17.5,+13.0]$). The task is the unit of inference: each contributes $240$ attempted forks over five pretrained checkpoints crossed with two drift directions. The ledger makes counterfactual utility observable for a fixed mechanism, allowing triggers to be judged by the updates they select rather than by surprise detection alone.

</details>


### [70] [When More Is Not Better: Component Anti-Synergy in a P300 Speller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10961)
*Lucas Yang,Rui Liu,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过四因素全因子实验检验了P300拼写器流水线中"组件越多越好"的假设，发现组件价值是条件性的而非可叠加的，存在反协同效应，语言模型收益取决于EEG流水线强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代P300脑机接口拼写器流水线常将多个各自有前景的组件组合在一起，并默认遵循"more-is-better"（越多越好）的假设。作者旨在系统检验该假设是否成立，明确各组件在不同配置下的真实贡献及其交互关系。

Method: 在公开P300数据集上开展四组件全因子实验，操控四个因素：欧氏对齐（EA）、xDAWN空间滤波、被试校准、语言模型先验。采用准确率、重复次数和信息传输率（ITR）作为评价指标，并使用混合效应模型进行分析。

Result: 组件价值是条件性的而非可加性的。校准是最强的单一贡献因素；在零校准设置下EA可补偿校准的缺失。加入单独有效的组件反而可能降低性能，即存在组件反协同。语言模型支持并非普遍有益，其效果强烈依赖底层EEG流水线的强度；使用更大语言模型呈现相似模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了"全开（all-on）"式最大化流水线设计，指出应根据可用EEG证据的质量来选择空间滤波与语言支持组件。

Abstract: P300 brain-computer interface (BCI) spellers can provide hands-free communication for people with severe motor impairments. Modern pipelines combine multiple individually promising components, often assuming that 'more-is-better'. We tested this assumption using a four-component full-factorial experiment varying the inclusion of Euclidean Alignment (EA), xDAWN spatial filtering, subject calibration, and language model priors on a public P300 dataset. Performance was evaluated using accuracy, repetitions, and information transfer rate (ITR) with mixed-effects models. Results show that the value of components is conditional rather than additive. Calibration was the strongest singular contributor, while EA compensated for its absence in zero-calibration settings. Adding independently useful components could also reduce performance, revealing component anti-synergy. Contrary to conventional wisdom, LM support was not universally beneficial: its effect depends strongly on the strength of the underlying EEG pipeline, while results from a larger LM showed a similar pattern. Together, these findings challenge maximal 'all-on' pipeline design and highlight the value of selecting spatial and language-support components according to the quality of available EEG evidence.

</details>


### [71] [EGGROLL, Unrolled: Understanding and Improving Low-Rank Evolution Strategies at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10980)
*Ege C. Kaya,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析EGGROLL（基于低秩高斯扰动的ES方法）的有限秩更新场与其估计误差，指其虽几何受限但在二次目标上精确，提出LOO-ROLL改进估计器，实验验证方差预测并提升后训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGGROLL用低秩（常为秩一）高斯乘积替代稠密高斯权重扰动，使演化策略（ES）对LLM更实用。该选择计算上吸引人，但几何上严重受限：每个秩一扰动位于环境矩阵空间的零体积子集中，却具有单位协方差。作者旨在刻画其有限秩、非零扰动半径下的更新场性质与估计误差。

Method: 在有限秩与非零扰动半径下刻画EGGROLL的均值更新场，并分析其有限样本估计器的误差。通过对扰动平滑后的目标梯度施加显式预解（resolvent）得到总体场；引入LOO-ROLL留一估计器，在保留有限秩总体场的同时将每方向两次对偶评估降为一次。

Result: 总体场的预解可引入非保守分量并逆转最优点的局部稳定性；EGGROLL在每个秩和半径下对所有二次目标精确。光滑目标下首个有限秩修正为O(σ²/r)，并有非渐近误差界。局部仿射模型下，秩一扰动使梯度估计方差比稠密高斯ES仅增加2(m+n+1)/(mn+1)，对4096×4096矩阵为0.098%。等评估成本下LOO-ROLL使估计器MSE减半；十个后训练设置、最大8B模型下七项结果改善且无显著损失；GSM8K上0.6B准确率38.1%→63.0%，8B为65.9%→80.0%；Transformer测量复现预测的有限秩方差，秩八无稳定奖励优势。

Conclusion: 低秩EGGROLL更新场在二次目标上精确，光滑目标误差受控；预解可能破坏保守性与稳定性。LOO-ROLL在保留有限秩总体场的同时降低评估成本、提升估计效率，带来实际后训练收益。

Abstract: EGGROLL makes evolution strategies (ES) practical for LLMs by replacing dense Gaussian weight perturbations with low-rank Gaussian products, often of rank one. This choice is computationally attractive but geometrically severe: each rank-one perturbation lies in a zero-volume subset of the ambient matrix space, despite having identity covariance. We characterize the mean EGGROLL update field at finite rank and nonzero perturbation radii, then analyze the error of its finite-population estimator. The population field is obtained by applying an explicit resolvent to the gradient of the objective smoothed by the perturbations. We show that the resolvent can introduce a nonconservative component and can reverse the local stability of an optimum. EGGROLL is nevertheless exact on every quadratic objective at every rank and radius. For smooth objectives, its first local finite-rank correction is $O(σ^2/r)$, and nonasymptotic bounds control the resulting field error under smoothness assumptions. Under a local affine model, rank-one perturbations increase the variance of the gradient estimator by only $\frac{2(m+n+1)}{mn+1}$ relative to dense Gaussian ES, or $0.098\%$ for a $4096\times4096$ matrix. We then introduce LOO-ROLL, a leave-one-out estimator that preserves the finite-rank population field while replacing EGGROLL's two antithetic evaluations per direction by one. At equal evaluation cost, LOO-ROLL halves estimator MSE in transformer blocks. At matched wall time across ten post-training settings and models up to 8B parameters, LOO-ROLL improves seven outcomes in individual paired tests, with no significant loss. On the GSM8K test set, accuracy increases from $38.1\%$ to $63.0\%$ at 0.6B and from $65.9\%$ to $80.0\%$ at 8B. Transformer measurements recover the predicted finite-rank variance, while the rank comparisons show no reproducible reward-based advantage for rank eight.

</details>


### [72] [General Quantification of Covariate and Concept Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11918)
*Hongbo Chen,Li Charlie Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对分布偏移下泛化误差界理论过于理想化且难以从样本估计的问题，提出 γ*-概念偏移新概念，基于熵最优传输推导统一误差界，并给出可估计算法与 DataShifts 工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习界理论局限于狭窄、理想化设定，且无法从样本中估计；同时现有概念偏移定义在源域与目标域支撑不匹配时会失效。作者希望弥合理论与实际应用之间的差距。

Method: 首先指出传统概念偏移定义在支撑不匹配时失效；借助熵最优传输提出 γ*-概念偏移这一关键概念；推导统一协变量偏移与 γ*-概念偏移的通用误差界，适用于广泛损失函数、标签空间及随机标注；进一步构建具有集中保证的偏移估计器，并提出 DataShifts 算法。

Result: 提出的误差界能够统一覆盖协变量偏移与 γ*-概念偏移，适用范围广泛；所设计的估计器具备集中不等式保证；DataShifts 算法可在多数应用场景中量化分布偏移并估计误差界。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布偏移下的学习误差分析提供了严谨且通用的工具，将理论界与可估计实践相结合，推动了分布偏移泛化理论的实际可用性。

Abstract: Generalization under distribution shift remains a core challenge in modern machine learning, yet existing learning bound theory is limited to narrow, idealized settings and is non-estimable from samples. In this paper, we bridge the gap between theory and practical applications. We first show that existing definition of concept shift breaks when the source and target supports mismatch. Leveraging entropic optimal transport, we propose a key notion: $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, and derive a general error bound unifying covariate and $γ^{*}\!$-concept shifts, which applies to broad loss functions, label spaces, and stochastic labeling. We further develop estimators for these shifts with concentration guarantees, and the DataShifts algorithm, which can quantify distribution shifts and estimate the error bound in most applications - a rigorous and general tool for analyzing learning error under distribution shift.

</details>


### [73] [Thompson Sampling for Non-Monotone Convex Ridge Bandits: Monotonicity Is Not Needed for Polynomial Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10981)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Thompson sampling 在带非单调链接的凸 ridge 损失上的贝叶斯遗憾从仅单调情形的结果推广：证明对任意凸（可能非单调）链接，精确后验 TS 的贝叶斯遗憾为 Õ(d^{9/2}√n)，否定了"单调性必要"的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bakhtiari、Lattimore 和 Szepesvári（COLT 2025）证明了 Thompson sampling 在带凸单调 ridge 损失 f(x)=ℓ(⟨x,θ⟩) 的 bandit 凸优化中贝叶斯遗憾为 Õ(d^{5/2}√n)，并提出单调性是否为必要条件的问题。本文给出定性的否定回答。

Method: 针对 [0,1] 值、1-Lipschitz 凸 ridge 损失及任意凸（可能非单调）链接，对任意固定可测极小值点选择，分析精确后验 TS。识别出单调证明依赖单移除 John-椭球二分法，并用显式十二点构造证明该二分法对非单调链接失效；转而用布尔舍入论证（与秩 r 矩阵在 max-范数下相差 1/(4r) 的 0-1 矩阵秩至多 2r-1）给出"无信息"配置的 O(d²) 基数上界。

Result: 精确后验 TS 的贝叶斯遗憾上界为 O((d+1)^4 √(dn) log(e+nd max{1,diam K})) = Õ(d^{9/2}√n)。构造 d(d+1) 个无信息损失，证明基数上界在大直径-间隙区间内达到常数因子意义下的紧性；给出对固定可测选择一致的信息比-遗憾转换。

Conclusion: 单调性对 TS 在凸 ridge bandit 优化中并非必要，非单调链接下仍可获得贝叶斯遗憾保证，但阶数为 Õ(d^{9/2}√n)，比单调情形的 d^{5/2} 更差。能否保留 d^{5/2} 依赖性仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025) proved that Thompson sampling (TS) has Bayesian regret $\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$ for bandit convex optimisation with convex \emph{monotone} ridge losses $f(x)=\ell(\ip{x}θ)$, and asked whether monotonicity of the link is necessary. We give a qualitative negative answer. For every prior on $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz convex ridge losses with an arbitrary convex, possibly non-monotone, link, and for any fixed measurable selection of minimisers, exact-posterior TS has Bayesian regret $O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$. The monotone proof relies on a single-removal John-ellipsoid dichotomy; we show by an explicit twelve-point configuration that this dichotomy fails for non-monotone links, and replace it by an $O(d^2)$ cardinality bound for ``uninformative'' configurations. The bound uses a Boolean rounding argument: a $0$-$1$ matrix within $1/(4r)$ in max-norm of a rank-$r$ matrix has rank at most $2r-1$. We construct $d(d+1)$ uninformative losses, showing that the cardinality bound is tight up to constants in the large-diameter-to-gap regime, and give a self-contained information-ratio-to-regret transfer that is uniform over fixed measurable selections. Whether the $d^{5/2}$ dependence of the monotone case can be retained remains open.

</details>


### [74] [Topological Necessities: Mechanism-Invariant Strategic Subgoals for Cross-Embodiment Goal-Conditioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11014)
*Hao Shi,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"拓扑必然性"(topological necessities):一种可从离线轨迹中恢复、独立于执行器的不可跳过阶段/路口构成的对象,用同调在维度0和1上读取,形成可枚举门集与证书,作为递归门层级进入决策循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序目标条件强化学习中,子目标通常是价值函数或隐动作的副产物,与生成它的执行器绑定,无法迁移。作者希望找到一种与执行器无关、可从离线成功轨迹中恢复的中间结构。

Method: 构建由成功轨迹形成的传输加权承载(transport-weighted carrier),在维度0和1上用同调读取:不可跳过阶段对应所有可行路径必经的分离集(维0),自由空间中的环路强制产生路线选择(维1)。由此得到可枚举的门集与壳层证书,认证的门作为递归拓扑门层级进入决策循环。

Result: 在固定且同构的自由空间下,门可跨执行器迁移:在PointMaze上冻结的门无需重训练即迁移到Ant与Humanoid,在统一接口下取得最高Humanoid聚合分96.1,多路线任务上比地图特权基线高36.0(p=1.4e-5);规划器在PointMaze饱和(100±0),在AntMaze(giant +22.9)与Kitchen(+15.8/+12.6)上匹配或超越最强基线。

Conclusion: 拓扑必然性提供了一种与执行器无关、可离线恢复、具证书保证的中间表示,支持跨具身迁移与稳健的长时序规划性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon goal-conditioned reinforcement learning delegates control to a high-level module that proposes subgoals, but existing subgoals are implicit byproducts of value functions or latent actions, tied to the executor that produced them. We study a different object: a route-conditioned order of unavoidable stages that every successful executor must traverse, recoverable from offline trajectories and belonging to none of them. Its defining properties are topological: an unskippable stage is a separating set that every admissible path must cross, and a loop in free space forces a route choice. We read the two by homology in dimensions 0 and 1 over a transport-weighted carrier built from successful trajectories, yielding an enumerable gate set with shell-level certificates; the certified gates are what we call topological necessities. Certified gates enter the decision loop as a recursive topological gate hierarchy. Under a fixed, isomorphic free space, the object survives executor replacement: gates frozen on PointMaze data transfer without retraining to Ant and Humanoid, attaining the highest Humanoid aggregate under a unified interface (96.1), with +36.0 over a map-privileged reference on the multi-route task (p=1.4e-5); the planner saturates PointMaze (100+/-0) and matches or exceeds the strongest baselines on AntMaze (giant +22.9) and Kitchen (+15.8/+12.6).

</details>


### [75] [T1: Terminal Agent Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11042)
*Junyao Yang,Yucheng Shi,Zhongzhi Li,Ruhan Wang,Zongxia Li,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 T1：122B 总参数的 MoE 智能体模型，经强化学习训练，在云沙箱真实 shell 中执行长程终端任务（单任务 300+ 轮工具调用），以任务自带验证器作为奖励，结合热启动、密集过程奖励、TITO 与 R3 等稳定化手段，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上将基座 43.8% 提升至 64.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体应用正转向长程任务（如编程、科学发现），其中终端任务尤为关键。既有方法难以支撑长回合、多工具调用的稳定强化学习训练，且易因基准过拟合而使收益失真，因此需要一个可稳定训练、且能证明真实能力迁移的方案。

Method: 1) 模型为 122B 总参数的 MoE，在云沙箱中操作真实 shell，单任务支持 300+ 轮工具调用，奖励由逐任务验证器执行给出。2) 训练配方：激进热启动以稳定 actor-critic；密集过程奖励按通过验证器的绝对数量对轨迹打分。3) 稳定优化：TITO 构造，在精确采样 token 标识上训练并在回合边界做漂移修复；rollout routing replay（R3），记录采样器在每个 MoE 层的逐 token 专家选择并在训练时重放。4) 训练语料完全分布外：使用与 Terminal-Bench 2.1 不相交的隔离种子与合成任务。

Result: TITO 与 R3 将训练-推理对数概率差从 0.021 降至 0.013，损失区域 token 漂移精确对齐为零。Terminal-Bench 2.1 上，后训练流水线将初始基座模型从 43.8% 提升至 T1 的 64.0% resolved。Long-Horizon Terminal Bench 上 T1 达 27.9%，超过 GPT-5.4 与 GLM-5.1。

Conclusion: T1 表明，通过热启动+密集过程奖励、TITO 与 R3 的稳定化构造，以及分布外语料设计，可对大规模 MoE 智能体进行长程终端任务的可扩展强化学习训练，并获得可验证的真实能力迁移而非基准过拟合。

Abstract: Agent usage is shifting toward long-horizon tasks such as coding and scientific discovery, among which terminal tasks are especially important. We introduce T1, a Mixture-of-Experts model of 122B total trained with reinforcement learning, operating a real shell in a cloud sandbox for up to 300+ tool-call turns per task, rewarded by executing each task's own verifier. We provide a comprehensive recipe: First, an aggressively warm-started to stabilize actor-critic training, with a dense process reward scoring trajectories by the absolute number of passing verifiers. Second, stable optimization through TITO construction, training on the exact sampled token identifiers with drift repair at turn boundaries, and rollout routing replay, recording the sampler's per-token expert choices at every MoE layer and replaying them during training. Third, fully out-of-distribution training corpus: isolated seeds and synthesized tasks disjoint from Terminal-Bench 2.1 ensures gains reflect genuine capability transfer over benchmark overfitting. Together, TITO and R3 cut the training-to-inference log-probability difference from 0.021 to 0.013, with exactly aligned zero token drift in the loss region. On Terminal-Bench 2.1, our post-train pipeline raises initial base model from 43.8% to T1 with 64.0% resolved. On Long-Horizon Terminal Bench, T1 reaches 27.9% and surpasses GPT-5.4 and GLM-5.1.

</details>


### [76] [EMMI: Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence for Communication-Efficient MLLM Inference via Fused Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11058)
*Motahare Mounesan,Irfan Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EMMI提出一种以紧凑表征为核心的边缘多模态推理框架，边缘端完成模态编码、跨模态融合与学习压缩，仅传输固定大小的隐表征至服务器进行大模型推理，实现32倍通信压缩并保持精度，端到端时延降低最多3.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLM）为边缘智能带来跨异构传感器（视觉、文本、遥测）推理的新机遇，但现代MLLM在计算、内存与通信上的高需求使其难以部署在资源受限的边缘平台。既有方案（传输原始传感器观测或在中间层切分神经网络）在带宽受限场景下通信开销大，因此需要一种通信高效的边缘MLLM推理方案。

Method: EMMI采用表征中心（representation-centric）设计：在边缘端执行模态特定编码、跨模态表征融合与学习式压缩，仅将紧凑的隐表征（latent representation）上传至服务器；服务器侧利用高容量MLLM对该表征进行推理。该设计在异构边缘设备与服务器端MLLM之间提供固定尺寸的接口。

Result: 在代表性多模态基准上的评估显示：EMMI可将通信负载降低32倍，同时保持相当的下游任务精度；在带宽受限的边缘条件下，估计的端到端推理时延最多降低3.4倍。

Conclusion: EMMI表明以紧凑隐表征作为边缘—服务器通信接口，可在不显著损失精度的情况下大幅降低通信开销，并缓解带宽瓶颈导致的推理时延，同时通过避免原始数据上传保护本地数据隐私，为通信高效的边缘多模态智能提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large language mod- els (MLLMs) have opened new opportunities for edge intelligence by enabling reasoning across heterogeneous sensor modalities, such as vision, text, and telemetry data. However, deploying these capabilities on resource-constrained edge platforms remains challenging due to the substantial computational, memory, and communication demands of modern MLLMs. Rather than transmitting raw sensor observations or partitioning neural networks at intermediate layers, Edge Multi-Modal Intelligence (EMMI) communicates a compact representation between edge devices and server resources, enabling communication-efficient edge MLLM inference. To achieve this, EMMI performs modality-specific encoding, cross-modal representation fusion, and learned compression at the edge, transmitting only a compact latent representation to server-side resources for high-capacity MLLM reasoning. This representation-centric design reduces communication overhead, preserves local data privacy, and provides a fixed-size interface between heterogeneous edge devices and server-side MLLMs. Evaluation on a representative multimodal benchmark demonstrates that EMMI can reduce the communication payload by 32x while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy, resulting in up to a 3.4x reduction in estimated end-to-end inference latency under bandwidth-constrained edge conditions.

</details>


### [77] [The information geometry of large language models is shared, learned, and controllable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11063)
*Dario Picozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出以 Fisher-Rao 几何刻画大语言模型下一词概率空间，揭示不同架构共享行为结构，并据此实现最小扰动、可迁移的行为控制与多种下游编辑任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽习得相似行为，但其共享结构的本质以及如何在不干扰其他行为的前提下改变单一行为仍不清楚。作者希望找到一个兼顾“行为不变性”与“坐标无关性”的几何框架，统一解释模型间共性并指导精准干预。

Method: 研究采用下一词概率的 Fisher-Rao 几何：行为在输出保持对称意义下决定该几何，而激活几何依赖坐标选择。作者在 Transformer、状态空间与循环模型上比较输出几何与激活几何的一致性，开展语义类别迁移实验，并结合 token 概率与读出几何预测几何谱及有效维度。此外通过受控语言分配、预训练语料统计与随机化实验，考察几何规律、事实习得延迟；最后基于该几何设计最小扰动局部干预，与欧氏控制方法对比其在提示迁移中的表现。

Result: 跨架构的输出几何一致性显著强于激活几何，共享几何支持语义类别迁移；与人类选词一致率随预测准确率、规模与训练提升，且模型专属校准后进一步提高。token 概率与读出几何可共同预测几何谱及有效维度；几何遵循语言规律，预训练语料统计可无重校准地预测留出事实习得，随机化实验中更深证据显著延迟习得。基于几何的干预能预测相对代价并实现可复用控制：供体提示上学到的更新可迁移至未见提示，且在参考提示上比欧氏控制更好地保持行为。同一几何校正还能改善 steering、编辑、归因、字典学习与微调。

Conclusion: Fisher-Rao 几何提供了一个跨架构统一的行为结构视角，并可作为最小扰动干预与行为控制的理论基础，广泛适用于可解释性、编辑与微调等任务。

Abstract: Large language models learn similar behaviours, yet it remains unclear what structure they share or how to change one behaviour without disturbing others. The Fisher-Rao geometry of next-token probabilities connects these questions: behaviour determines this geometry up to output-preserving symmetries, whereas activation geometry depends on coordinates. Across transformer, state-space and recurrent models, output geometries agree more strongly than activation geometries, and shared geometry supports semantic-category transfer. Agreement with human word choices increases with predictive accuracy, scale and training, and improves further after model-only calibration. Token probabilities and read-out geometry jointly predict the spectrum and its effective dimension. Controlled language assignments show that geometry follows the language law across architectures. Pretraining corpus statistics predict held-out fact acquisition without recalibration, while randomised experiments show that deeper evidence substantially delays acquisition across every tested architecture and evidence construction. Finally, the geometry prescribes minimum-disturbance local interventions, predicts their relative cost, and supports reusable control: updates learned on donor prompts transfer to unseen prompts while better preserving behaviour on reference prompts than Euclidean control. The same geometric correction improves steering, editing, attribution, dictionary learning and fine-tuning.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Solver Verdicts: Generative Reward Models for Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11085)
*Vikash Singh,Debargha Ganguly,Aman Goel,Ali Torkamani,Xiaoxue Han,Joseph Lilien,Ferhat Erata,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文形式化了神经符号系统中"判决保持型不忠实"(VPU)失效模式,证明纯结构性、仅基于判决的验证启发式在欺骗性有效轨迹上检测能力受限于随机水平;提出生成式验证(GenV),将离线Z3等价判据蒸馏为无需参考的连续等价性评分,并实现高精度验证与下游增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号系统依赖数学求解器保证推理正确性,但求解器无法判断形式化翻译是否与指定的形式化保持严格的参考等价,错误编码可能顺利执行并给出期望判决。作者将这一漏洞形式化为VPU,并指出既有验证方法存在根本性局限,需从理论上界定其检测上限。

Method: 1) 形式化定义VPU及其"欺骗性有效轨迹";2) 理论证明结构性、仅判决的验证启发式在此类轨迹上的检测能力有界,趋近随机水平;3) 提出生成式验证(GenV),将离线Z3等价oracle蒸馏为无参考(参考自由)的连续参考等价评分,复用语言模型原生词表空间实现读出;4) 用决策投影logit透镜与稀疏自编码器进行机制分析;5) 构建oracle挖掘的验证器GenV+HN。

Result: GenV+HN在参考等价验证上达到0.961 AUROC;可零样本泛化到未见翻译器与差异较大的形式化风格;在智能体测试时计算分配任务上带来11.3个百分点的下游准确率提升。机制分析显示生成式读出无需显式定位训练即可提取精确的空间错误坐标。

Conclusion: 纯结构性、仅判决的验证在原理上无法可靠识别VPU,检测能力受限于随机水平;而将求解器等价判据蒸馏进语言模型生成式读出的GenV方法,能实现参考自由、连续且可泛化的参考等价验证,并在下游智能体任务中产生实质收益,为神经符号系统的可靠性验证提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems rely on mathematical solvers to guarantee reasoning correctness, yet solvers are fundamentally blind to whether a formal translation maintains strict reference-equivalence to a designated formalization. We formalize this vulnerability as Verdict-Preserving-Unfaithfulness (VPU): a failure mode where an incorrect encoding executes successfully and matches the expected verdict. We theoretically prove that structural, verdict-only verification heuristics are mathematically bounded to chance-level detection on these deceptively valid traces. To resolve this, we introduce Generative Verification (GenV), which distills an offline Z3-equivalence oracle into a reference-free, continuous reference-equivalence score by repurposing the language model's native vocabulary space. Mechanistic analysis via decision-projected logit lenses and sparse autoencoders shows this generative readout natively extracts precise spatial error coordinates without explicit localization training. Empirically, our oracle-mined verifier (GenV+HN) achieves 0.961 AUROC in reference-equivalence verification, generalizes zero-shot across unseen translators and divergent formal styles, and yields an 11.3-point downstream accuracy gain in agentic test-time compute allocation.

</details>


### [79] [HERALD: High-Fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation for Heterophily-Aware Graph Condensation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11123)
*Sujan Chakraborty,Priyanka Saha,Saptarshi Bej*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD：面向异质图的免梯度图凝聚框架，通过自适应异质率调节特征选择与节点评分，在同存储预算下优于或媲美SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图凝聚方法依赖Weisfeiler-Lehman邻域聚合或基于梯度的分布匹配，二者均假设相邻节点标签相同；在异质图（heterophily）下该假设失效，导致凝聚图保真度下降。因此需要一种能适配异质性的凝聚方案。

Method: 提出HERALD（High-fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation），免梯度框架。特征选择采用Fisher判别性与激活密度的联合准则，在异质图上降低聚合表示权重；节点评分由原型代表性、决策边界接近度和局部内在维度（LID）加权组合，权重由异质率的平滑sigmoid函数驱动。随后通过按分数排序的BFS扩展、个性化PageRank剪枝和类别再平衡组装凝聚子图，存储预算与BONSAI一致。

Result: 在8个涵盖同质与异质的基准数据集、4种GNN架构上，HERALD在异质图上匹配或超越SOTA凝聚器，在同质图上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过将凝聚流程与测得的异质率自适应耦合，HERALD在统一存储预算下有效缓解了异质图上的假设失配问题，兼具异质图增益与同质图稳健性。

Abstract: Graph condensation aims to produce a small surrogate graph that preserves the downstream node-classification performance of a much larger original graph. Existing methods rely on Weisfeiler-Lehman neighbourhood aggregation or gradient-based distribution matching, both of which assume that adjacent nodes share the same label, an assumption that breaks down under heterophily. We propose HERALD (High-fidelity Exemplar Retrieval with Adaptive Landmark Distillation), a gradient-free graph condensation framework that adapts the node scoring and feature selection in the condensation pipeline to the graph's measured heterophily. HERALD selects features via a joint Fisher-discriminability and activation-density criterion that down-weights aggregated representations on heterophilic graphs, and scores nodes by a weighted combination of prototype representativeness, decision-boundary proximity, and Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (LID), where the weights are driven by a smooth sigmoid function of the heterophily ratio. Nodes are then assembled into a condensed subgraph through score-ordered BFS expansion, Personalised PageRank pruning, and class rebalancing, all at an identical storage budget to BONSAI, enabling direct comparison. Experiments on eight benchmark datasets spanning homophilic and heterophilic settings show that HERALD matches or outperforms state-of-the-art condensers on heterophilic graphs and remains competitive on homophilic ones across four GNN architectures.

</details>


### [80] [Phase-Decoupled, Model-Calibrated Power Control for Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11133)
*Jae Gon Kim,Donghoon Yoo,Hanyul Ryu,Sungho Ha,Juyeon Lee,Soojung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对PD分离式LLM服务，提出相位解耦的、按模型校准的功耗控制器：prefill用SM时钟窗口（下限即延迟保证），decode用自动校准刚好高于吞吐/延迟悬崖的功率上限。在8x B200、Qwen3-Coder-480B(FP8)上实现+20.4% tokens/J且仅+3.5% e2e延迟，帕累托优于Max-Q的+8.6%/+5.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心GPU功耗是LLM服务容量的关键瓶颈，生产环境已转向prefill/decode(PD)分离。作者部署NVIDIA Max-Q推理配置到分离式B200系统后发现：其实际收益有限(+8.6% tokens/J)、依赖模型，且带来吞吐评估无法暴露的端到端延迟代价(+5.2%)；同时Max-Q对处于相反硬件状态的prefill与decode GPU施加同一设置。因此假设最优功耗设置属于所部署的(模型、量化、引擎、硬件)组合属性而非GPU类别，每条lane应各有配置，且将SLO余量安全转化为能耗需在运行时SLO守护下进行延迟门控校准而非固定配方。

Method: 提出相位解耦、按模型校准的控制器：prefill lane运行于SM时钟窗口下，其下限在构造上即为延迟保证；decode lane运行于功率上限下，该上限通过自动校准置于略高于实测吞吐/延迟悬崖处。由于分离式decode lane功耗平坦且受内存约束，功率上限持续生效，避免了使POLCA拒绝capping的反应性超调弱点，GPU自身功耗管理器在上限内维持吞吐。

Result: 8x B200节点服务Qwen3-Coder-480B(FP8)（agentic负载）时，balanced模式达+20.4% tokens/J、+3.5%平均e2e，对比Max-Q的+8.6%、+5.2%，在两轴上均为帕累托改进。Qwen3-235B-A22B(NVFP4)上所有运行模式每次重复均满足ITL-p99 SLO，而两种厂商配置均未满足。decode执行器A/B实验显示校准上限优于静态锁频，三天持续运行节省一对lane 32.3%电量。两模型均为MoE；dense模型回收约少5倍，故结论限定于MoE服务。

Conclusion: 最优功耗设置是部署组合的属性，需按lane分别配置并采用延迟门控的运行时SLO守护校准。所提相位解耦控制器在MoE服务中实现吞吐/能耗与延迟的帕累托改进，显著优于厂商Max-Q配置；结论限定于MoE场景。

Abstract: Datacenter GPU power is the binding constraint on LLM serving capacity, and production serving has shifted to prefill/decode (PD) disaggregation. Deploying NVIDIA's Max-Q inference profile on a disaggregated B200 system, we found its realized gain modest (+8.6% tokens/J), model-dependent, and carrying a mean end-to-end latency cost (+5.2%) that throughput-only evaluation does not surface; the profile also applies one setting to prefill and decode GPUs that operate in opposite hardware regimes. We hypothesize that the optimal power setting is a property of the deployed (model, quantization, engine, hardware) combination rather than of the GPU class, that each lane warrants its own profile, and that converting SLO headroom into energy safely requires latency-gated calibration under a runtime SLO guard rather than a fixed recipe. We present a phase-decoupled, model-calibrated controller: the prefill lane runs under an SM-clock window whose floor is a latency guarantee by construction, and the decode lane under a power cap placed by automatic calibration just above a measured throughput/latency cliff. Because a disaggregated decode lane draws flat, memory-bound power, the cap binds continuously, the reactive-overshoot weakness that led POLCA to reject capping is absent, and the GPU's own power manager retains throughput under the cap. On an 8x B200 node serving Qwen3-Coder-480B (FP8) under agentic load, our balanced mode delivers +20.4% tokens/J at +3.5% mean e2e versus +8.6% at +5.2% for Max-Q, a Pareto improvement on both axes. On Qwen3-235B-A22B (NVFP4) every operating mode meets the ITL-p99 SLO in every repetition; both vendor profiles miss it. A decode-actuator A/B shows the calibrated cap beats static clock locks, and a three-day sustained run saves 32.3% of a lane pair's electricity. Both models are MoE; a dense model recovers roughly 5x less, so we scope our claims to MoE serving.

</details>


### [81] [Bidirectional Multimodal Fusion of Sky Images and Time-Series for Solar Forecasting with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11135)
*Ken Chen,Maneesha Perera,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SolCloudLLM，一个基于大语言模型的多模态光伏功率与辐照度预测框架，通过融合天空图像与历史时序数据实现精准短期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期光伏功率与全局水平辐照度预测对电网调度和备用容量安排至关重要。该时间尺度下的预测误差主要来自云导致的功率爬坡，仅依靠历史数值数据难以预判云的到来，因此地基天空图像成为关键的补充物理信号。同时预测性能对地点和局部观测条件高度敏感，需要站点专属数据，而这类数据往往稀缺。尽管LLM在时序预测中已展现出竞争力与高数据效率，但现有方法多为单模态，如何有效将天空图像融入LLM预测框架仍是未解的开放挑战。

Method: SolCloudLLM将天空图像图块与时间序列图块进行对齐，并通过双向多模态融合将二者的表示融合为统一表示，随后映射到LLM的嵌入空间中，从而在多模态框架下完成预测。

Result: 在SIRTA与SKIPP'D数据集上的大量实验表明，SolCloudLLM在所有预测时间尺度上的MSE均持续优于最佳基线方法，最大相对MSE降低达25.4%。分层分析显示多模态融合的收益主要集中在多云条件下。此外，SolCloudLLM在几乎所有少样本设置中均取得最佳性能，而其他深度学习基线性能大幅退化，且常被非学习的物理方法超越。

Conclusion: 将天空图像与历史时序融合到LLM预测框架中，能显著提升短期光伏与辐照度预测精度，且在多云条件和少样本场景下优势尤为突出，验证了多模态LLM方法在数据稀缺的站点专属预测中的有效性与数据效率。

Abstract: Short-term photovoltaic (PV) power and global horizontal irradiance (GHI) forecasts are essential for effective dispatch, reserve scheduling, and grid operations. At these forecasting horizons, errors are predominantly driven by cloud induced ramps: relying solely on historical numerical data may struggle to anticipate an incoming cloud, making ground-based sky images a crucial complementary physical signal. Furthermore, forecast performance is highly sensitive to location and local observing conditions, creating a strong need for site-specific data that are often scarce. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated competitive performance and high data efficiency in time-series forecasting. Despite their success, existing LLM-based forecasting methods remain predominantly unimodal, relying primarily on historical numerical time-series data. Effectively incorporating sky imagery into an LLM-based forecasting framework remains under-explored and an open challenge. In this paper, we propose SolCloudLLM, an LLM-based multimodal forecasting framework. SolCloudLLM aligns sky-image patches with time-series patches and fuses their corresponding representations through bidirectional multimodal fusion, yielding a unified representation that is subsequently mapped into the embedding space of an LLM. Extensive experiments on the SIRTA and SKIPP'D datasets demonstrate that SolCloudLLM consistently outperforms the best baseline methods in MSE across all forecasting horizons, achieving a maximum relative MSE reduction of 25.4%. Stratified analysis further indicates that the benefits of multimodal fusion are concentrated primarily under cloudy conditions. Notably, SolCloudLLM achieves the best performance in nearly all few-shot settings, whereas other deep learning baselines experience substantial performance degradation and are frequently outperformed by the non-learning physical method.

</details>


### [82] [LILA: Calibration-Free Structured Pruning of Large Language Models via Latent Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11163)
*Sankar Behera,Dhruv Singh,Anshika Agnihotri,Raj Kumar Choudhary,Satyadev Ahlawat,Yamuna Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LILA是一种无需校准数据、梯度或辅助网络的LLM结构化剪枝方法，利用KS距离度量神经元重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM结构化剪枝方法在剪枝时需要校准数据、梯度计算或大型辅助策略网络，依赖性强且成本高。

Method: 通过全权重矩阵与神经元消融后FFN权重矩阵的经验奇异值分布间的Kolmogorov-Smirnov距离，对神经元重要性打分，形成闭式谱规则。

Result: 25%稀疏度下零样本精度超PruneNet 1.57pp；全稀疏度区间超SliceGPT最多6.0pp；经1轮LoRA恢复微调后与重校准SliceGPT差距在0.48pp内；NTK分析显示功能失真较随机剪枝降低22倍；动态稀疏度分配实现中等压缩下SOTA生成保持。

Conclusion: LILA提供无需校准的谱重要性准则，性能媲美重校准基线，并在高压缩区揭示单层架构瓶颈。

Abstract: Structured pruning of large language models (LLMs) offers hardware-efficient compression, yet existing methods require calibration data, gradient computation, or large auxiliary policy networks at pruning time. LILA (\emph{Latent-Informed Layer Analysis}) scores neuron importance via the Kolmogorov--Smirnov (KS) distance between empirical singular value distributions of the full and neuron-ablated feed-forward network (FFN) weight matrix, providing a closed-form spectral rule requiring no training, calibration data, or auxiliary network. Without any fine-tuning, LILA surpasses PruneNet (45M-parameter RL policy) by 1.57~pp in zero-shot accuracy on LLaMA-2-7B at 25\% sparsity, and outperforms WikiText-2-calibrated SliceGPT by up to 6.0~pp across all sparsity levels, while preserving the original architecture. After one epoch of LoRA recovery fine-tuning, LILA achieves highly competitive performance, matching the heavily calibrated SliceGPT baseline to within a 0.48~pp margin across LLaMA-2-7B and Phi-2, despite using zero calibration data. A Neural Tangent Kernel analysis confirms a 22$\times$ reduction in functional distortion versus random pruning, providing theoretical grounding for the spectral importance criterion. Finally, extending LILA to dynamically allocate sparsity budgets via KS-scores yields state-of-the-art generative preservation at moderate compression, while uncovering fundamental single-layer architectural bottlenecks at higher compression regimes.

</details>


### [83] [When does a spectral prior help graph learning? Connectivity-loss estimation under road-network disruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11166)
*Van-Truong Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用图神经网络学习对一阶Fiedler灵敏度（谱扰动）的有界修正，以近似多边删除后的相对代数连通度损失，并在跨区域OSM网络上验证其有效性，结论是将谱残差视为一种有用但对领域敏感的结构连通性筛选归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时评估大量道路链路失效需要权衡精确谱重算与局部近似：精确计算代价高，一阶近似又存在系统性误差，因此需要一种兼具效率与精度的折中方案。

Method: 以代数连通度（Fiedler值）为指标，构建残差GNN：在一阶Fiedler灵敏度基线之上学习有界修正量。考察独立失效、空间聚集失效和按边介数定向失效三种场景；采用图不相交的合成数据划分，并零样本迁移至六个国家13个OSM区域；对比GCN、GraphSAGE与边感知MPNN三种骨干及多种解析基线。

Result: 扩展OSM测试中，残差GCN将空间失效MAE降低0.0391（95%分层区间0.0151–0.0662），残差GraphSAGE将定向失效MAE降低0.0257（0.0095–0.0446）；二阶扰动相较一阶仅提升0.0028–0.0053。定向迁移下修正斜率下降，表明残差在系统性先验误差附近收缩。留一国OSM到OSM迁移结果不一：残差GCN将定向失效MAE降低0.0622（0.0169–0.1153），但空间点估计反而变差。稀疏规模扩展至20000节点，并区分一次性谱设置成本与摊销筛选成本。

Conclusion: 谱残差是一种有用但对领域敏感的归纳偏置，适用于结构连通性筛选；跨域迁移效果不稳健，说明该修正的泛化受失效模式与网络域影响。代码、缓存网络与可复现性材料已归档于指定DOI。

Abstract: Rapid evaluation of many simultaneous road-link disruptions requires a practical compromise between exact spectral recomputation and local approximation. We estimate relative algebraic-connectivity loss after multi-edge deletion using graph neural networks (GNNs) that learn a bounded correction to a first-order Fiedler sensitivity. The study considers independent, spatially clustered, and edge-betweenness-targeted failures, with graph-disjoint synthetic splits and zero-shot transfer to 13 OpenStreetMap (OSM) areas in six countries. GCN, GraphSAGE, and edge-aware MPNN backbones are compared with analytical baselines. In expanded OSM tests, residual GCN improves spatial-failure MAE by 0.0391 (95% hierarchical interval 0.0151-0.0662), while residual GraphSAGE improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0257 (0.0095-0.0446). Second-order perturbation improves first-order MAE by only 0.0028-0.0053. Correction slopes decrease under targeted transfer, indicating residual shrinkage around systematic prior error. Leave-one-country-out OSM-to-OSM transfer is mixed: residual GCN improves targeted-failure MAE by 0.0622 (0.0169-0.1153) but worsens the spatial point estimate. Sparse scaling extends to 20,000 nodes and separates one-time spectral setup from amortized screening cost. These results characterize the spectral residual as a useful but domain-sensitive inductive bias for structural connectivity screening. Code, cached networks, and reproducibility artifacts are archived at doi:10.5281/zenodo.22307723.

</details>


### [84] [Semi-Tensor Product-Based Multi-Term Randomized T-SVD and Its Visual Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11168)
*Xingchen Xiao,Feng Zhang,Wenjin Qin,Jianjun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在任意可逆线性变换诱导的t-product框架下定义三阶张量的半张量积，构建多单项半张量积SVD（MSTP-SVD），并引入随机投影与幂迭代得到加速算法MRSTP-SVD，在图像/视频压缩与补全任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准t-product（T-SVD）要求严格的维度匹配约束；基于半张量积（STP）的扩展虽放宽了维度限制，但单单项公式近似精度有限；且确定性方法在大规模张量数据上计算代价高。因此需要同时解决维度灵活性、近似精度与计算效率三类问题。

Method: 1）在由任意可逆线性变换诱导的t-product框架下，提出适用于三阶张量的新型半张量积（tensor semi-tensor product），打破标准t-product的刚性维度匹配要求，同时保持T-SVD的闭式性质；2）基于该构造提出多单项半张量积SVD（MSTP-SVD），通过整合多个正交分解项提升低秩近似精度；3）为降低多单项建模的计算开销，将随机投影与幂迭代引入MSTP-SVD框架，得到加速的多单项随机半张量积SVD（MRSTP-SVD），在重构精度与计算效率间取得平衡。

Result: 在图像与视频压缩、补全任务上的实验表明，所提方法相较单单项方案显著提升低秩近似精度，同时MRSTP-SVD在重构精度与计算效率之间实现了良好平衡，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的张量半张量积在保留T-SVD闭式性质的同时放宽了维度兼容性约束，多单项MSTP-SVD改善了近似精度，随机化加速版本MRSTP-SVD兼顾精度与效率；该框架为高维视觉数据的压缩与补全提供了有效工具，并可推广至更一般的可逆变换诱导t-product情形。

Abstract: Tensor singular value decomposition (T-SVD), which is built upon the tensor-tensor product (t-product), has emerged as a powerful tool for processing high-dimensional visual data such as color images and videos. However, the standard t-product imposes strict dimensional compatibility constraints. Although extensions based on the semi-tensor product (STP) relax this restriction, their single-term formulations still suffer from limited approximation accuracy. Moreover, these deterministic methods incur high computational costs when processing large-scale tensor data. To address these issues, this paper introduces a novel semi-tensor product for third-order tensors under the t-product framework induced by arbitrary invertible linear transforms. The resulting tensor semi-tensor product breaks the rigid dimension matching requirement of the standard t-product, while retaining the closed-form property of T-SVD. Based on this construction, we develop a multi-term semi-tensor product singular value decomposition (MSTP-SVD), which integrates multiple orthogonal decomposition terms to significantly improve low-rank approximation accuracy compared with single-term schemes. To reduce the computational cost of multi-term modeling, we incorporate randomized projection and power iteration techniques into the MSTP-SVD framework, yielding an accelerated multi-term randomized semi-tensor product SVD (MRSTP-SVD) algorithm that achieves a balance between reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency. Experiments on image and video compression and completion tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [85] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES) under Time-Varying Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11207)
*Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 CONES 推广至损失函数随时间变化的情形，分析投影近端算法的遗憾与移动代价权衡，并给出匹配的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原 CONES 仅允许目标函数固定、可行域嵌套收缩，本文希望进一步允许损失函数随时间变化，研究同时控制遗憾与移动代价的可能性及理论极限。

Method: 采用投影近端算法（projected proximal algorithm）求解时变损失、嵌套可行域下的在线优化问题；并通过构造下界分析任意弱自适应算法与具有次线性 anytime 遗憾的算法的移动代价。

Result: 凸损失下，投影近端算法同时达到 O(T^{1-β}) 遗憾与 O(T^β) 移动代价，β∈[0,1)；且任意弱自适应算法若遗憾为 O(T^β)，移动代价至少为 Ω(T^{(1-β)/2})。强凸损失下，算法同时达到 O(1) 遗憾与 O(log T) 移动代价；任意具有次线性 anytime 遗憾的算法移动代价至少为 Ω(log T)。

Conclusion: 结果表明时变损失下遗憾与移动代价存在固有的权衡关系，投影近端算法在凸与强凸两类情形均达到相应上界，且下界说明了该权衡的紧致性。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} was introduced in \cite{CONESVaze} where the objective function \(f\) remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence \(S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T\). The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost $M_\cA(T)$ while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known \emph{nested convex body chasing} (NCBC). In this paper, we extend CONES to allow for loss functions $f_t'$s to also change over time. When all loss functions are convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost, respectively, for any $β\in [0,1)$, over a time horizon of $T$. We also show that any {\it weakly adaptive} online algorithm with $O(T^β)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(T^{\frac{1-β}{2}}\right)$ for any $β\in [0,1)$. When all loss functions are strongly convex, we show that the projected proximal algorithm simultaneously achieves $O(1)$ regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with sublinear {\it anytime} regret has a movement cost of $Ω\left(\log T\right)$.

</details>


### [86] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REVA框架，通过挖掘目标生成器的历史注意力轨迹构建可复用的证据视图，实现免辅助模型、低延迟的RAG检索后压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中更长的上下文会带来延迟、KV缓存内存与token成本上升；现有检索后压缩器多为单查询独立运行、依赖辅助模型或改写，且在线开销大，甚至不如简单截断。

Method: 从数据挖掘视角聚合历史query-document-model交互；提出REVA：将目标生成器的历史注意力轨迹挖掘为按文档键控、与预算无关的分数存储，把token级注意力映射到可读词单元，跨重复文档访问聚合重要性，并渲染保留文档顺序与标准RAG接口的预算特定纯文本视图。

Result: 在四个代表性基准与多个现代LLM上，生成质量较现有方法提升1.0–5.8个点，压缩开销降低5.3–15.6倍，额外延迟低于40ms。

Conclusion: REVA以可复用的历史注意力证据替代逐查询在线压缩，在提升生成质量的同时显著降低压缩开销与延迟，兼容标准RAG流程。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [87] [Legible Failures: Detecting and Repairing In-Context Binding Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11216)
*Manas Venkata Sai Ravulapalli,Samrath Singh Chadha,Abhinav M. Hari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究实体-义务绑定任务中"模型答错但隐藏状态仍编码正确答案"的现象，证明线性探针可从冻结隐状态解码正确绑定，并能用于检测与纠正模型错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误答案本身无法区分模型是"缺乏所需信息"还是"拥有信息却未能使用"。作者希望揭示后者（答案错误但内部表征正确）的存在，并检验这种内部信息是否可被探针提取、检测和干预利用。

Method: 在实体-义务绑定任务上，对16个公开检查点各用3个随机种子评估。在训练折上拟合线性探针，在验证折上选择层，在互斥测试折报告结果。用查询-实体反事实实验排除token存在性与近因性的解释。构造基于探针输出符号不一致的分数，并将其与模型自身置信度比较做失败检测；再在无金标签条件下，将残差流朝探针解码的绑定方向引导。

Result: 在模型答错的样本上，探针准确率超出严格现时义务基线1/K=0.125达+0.196（95% CI [+0.101, +0.296]）。探针不一致分数较模型置信度将失败检测AUROC提升+0.079（95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]），而原始探针置信度无显著提升。无金标签的残差流引导在全部8个测试模型上平均提升准确率+0.168（95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]）。

Conclusion: 模型错误不等于信息缺失；隐状态中可线性解码出正确答案。与近期研究中探针检测的错误难以干预的结论不同，在上下文绑定任务中探针是可操作的，能够用于失败检测与无监督纠错。

Abstract: A wrong answer does not show whether the model lacked the needed information or held it and failed to use it. On an entity-obligation binding task, a language model can emit an incorrect prompt-supplied binding while a linear probe can recover the correct one from its frozen hidden state. We measure how often this occurs across 16 public checkpoints, each evaluated with three seeds. We fit a probe on a training fold, select its layer on a validation fold, and report results on a disjoint test fold. On the trials each model gets wrong, probe accuracy exceeds the strict present-obligation baseline, 1/K = 0.125, by +0.196 (95% CI [+0.101, +0.296], bootstrapped over models). A query-entity counterfactual rules out token presence and recency. A score built from the sign of probe-output disagreement improves failure detection over the model's own confidence by +0.079 AUROC (95% CI [+0.036, +0.126]). Raw probe confidence gives no measurable improvement over model confidence. Steering the residual stream toward the probe-decoded binding, with no gold label, raises accuracy on all eight models tested by a mean of +0.168 (95% CI [+0.066, +0.280]). Where recent studies report that probe-detected errors are resistant to interventions, we find that in-context binding is a setting in which probes are actionable.

</details>


### [88] [AdamX: Cosine similarity meets gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11867)
*Francisco Caldas,Ruben Belo,Cláudia Soares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdamX：用余弦相似度自适应控制更新幅度的优化器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器中，一阶梯度的更新幅度控制通常依赖二阶动量或固定超参数，缺乏对模型无关的描述性指标；同时训练早期优化轨迹较为震荡。论文尝试用余弦相似度作为可扩展、通用且易于集成的自适应机制来调节更新幅度，并引入方差校正以平滑早期训练。

Method: 提出一阶优化器 AdamX：(1) 以余弦相似度作为自适应机制来控制更新幅度；(2) 增加方差校正（variance rectification）方案，促进训练早期更平滑的优化。方法具有可扩展性、模型无关，能直接嵌入现有训练流程。

Result: 在多个基准数据集与网络架构上，AdamX 达到与对比方法相当的收敛率。评估指标为：在固定超参数预算下，达到预设性能阈值所需的 epoch 数。代码与实验已开源。

Conclusion: AdamX 表明余弦相似度可作为有效的更新幅度自适应信号，配合方差校正可平滑早期训练；在固定超参数预算设定下具备竞争性的收敛效率，且实现简单、易于推广到现有训练管线。

Abstract: We introduce AdamX, a first-order optimizer that incorporates cosine similarity as an adaptive mechanism for controlling update magnitudes. The proposed method is scalable, model-agnostic, and straightforward to integrate into existing training pipelines. We further introduce a variance rectification scheme that promotes smoother optimization during the early stages of training. Overall, we provide empirical evidence that AdamX achieves competitive convergence rates across a range of benchmark datasets and architectures. Performance is evaluated in terms of the number of epochs required to reach predefined performance thresholds under a fixed hyperparameter budget. Code and Experiments available at: https://github.com/FranciscoCaldas/adamX.

</details>


### [89] [Polyhedral Geometry of Time-to-First-Spike Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11227)
*Manjot Singh,Guido Montúfar,Gitta Kutyniok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究脉冲神经网络（SNN）在time-to-first-spike模型下的表达能力，证明单个神经元的发放时间可表示为带指数级高度约束仿射分片的maxout形式，并将因果区域形式化为固定因果集的多面体，推导了浅层与多层前馈脉冲网络中因果区域数量的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络为异步、事件驱动计算提供了自然框架，是传统前馈网络的补充。但相比ReLU网络，SNN的表达能力（尤其是其输入空间划分的复杂度）尚缺乏系统刻画。本文旨在量化SNN的表示能力，明确其与常规前馈ReLU网络的差异。

Method: 采用time-to-first-spike模型，在输入-输出映射连续且分片线性、各仿射分片由决定突触前脉冲是否先于神经元发放的因果可行性约束所支配的设定下，首先将每个神经元的发放时间表示为maxout式表示（含指数级、高度约束的仿射分片）；随后将因果区域形式化为具有固定因果集的多面体区域，并推导浅层与多层前馈脉冲网络中因果区域最大数量的上界与下界。结合理论分析与实验验证。

Result: 理论结果给出了因果区域数量的上界与下界，且实验与理论一致：脉冲网络能够生成比常规前馈ReLU网络更丰富的输入空间划分。

Conclusion: 脉冲神经网络在time-to-first-spike模型下具有高于常规前馈ReLU网络的表达能力，其对输入空间的划分更精细，因果区域分析为刻画SNN表达力提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study the expressivity of spiking neural networks, which provide a natural framework for asynchronous, event-driven computation complementary to conventional feedforward neural networks. We consider the time-to-first-spike model in a setting for which the input-output map is continuous and piecewise linear, with affine pieces governed by causal feasibility constraints that determine which presynaptic spikes occur before a neuron fires. We first show that each neuron's firing time admits a maxout-like representation with exponentially many, highly constrained affine pieces. We then formalize causal regions as polyhedral regions with fixed causal sets and derive upper and lower bounds on the maximal number of causal regions in both shallow and multilayer feedforward spiking networks. Our theoretical and experimental results show that spiking networks can generate richer partitions of the input space than conventional feedforward ReLU networks.

</details>


### [90] [Solving Few-Shot Multiobjective Multitask Optimization via Iterative Sequential Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11228)
*Tingyang Wei,Haofeng Wu,Ananda Phan Iman,Zhao Wei,Jiao Liu,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代顺序迁移（IST）方法，将多任务优化建模为序列化的顺序迁移问题，通过似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制，在紧预算下有效解决多目标多任务优化中的知识迁移瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务优化依赖任务间精英解分布的对齐来实现有效知识迁移，但在少样本/紧预算优化场景下，有限的评估预算难以识别精英解分布，形成关键瓶颈；多目标多任务问题中每个优化器需逼近连续的Pareto流形而非单一最优点，进一步加剧了这一挑战。

Method: 提出迭代顺序迁移（IST）：将多任务优化建模为一系列顺序迁移优化问题，每次迭代只聚焦于单一目标任务进行评估；并设计似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制，通过识别最有可能准备好进行知识整合的任务来最大化迁移效用。

Result: 在基准问题与真实世界问题上的实验结果表明，所提方法在紧张预算条件下具有有效性。

Conclusion: IST通过顺序迁移与似然信息驱动的任务优先级机制，绕过了多任务优化中精英解分布对齐的瓶颈，在少样本、紧预算的多目标多任务优化场景下实现了有效知识迁移。

Abstract: Applying knowledge transfer across multiple optimization tasks, multitask optimization (MTO) emerges as a promising approach to solving synergistic optimization tasks simultaneously. However, the development of effective knowledge transfer mechanisms in MTO fundamentally relies on aligning elite solution distributions across tasks. This dependency creates a critical bottleneck in few-shot optimization regimes, as restricted evaluation budgets impede the identification of elite solution distributions required for beneficial transfer. This challenge is exacerbated in multiobjective multitask problems, where each optimizer must approximate a continuous Pareto manifold rather than a single optimal point. This paper introduces Iterative Sequential Transfer (IST) to circumvent this bottleneck. We model MTO as a sequence of sequential transfer optimization problems, concentrating evaluations on a single target per iteration. We propose a likelihood-informed task prioritization mechanism to maximize transfer utility by identifying the task most likely ready for knowledge integration. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world problems verify the effectiveness of the proposed method under tight budgets.

</details>


### [91] [MUtE: A Dual Framework for Concept Erasure and Counterfactual Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11253)
*Antoine Saillenfest*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视概念擦除的最优界限，推导出一类新的擦除函数，其天然诱导出确定性的对偶反事实映射，并通过施加平移偏置实现理论最优性与实际表示学习的桥接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从表示中擦除概念特定信息有助于缓解偏见或解释模型决策，目标是在使目标概念不可预测的同时最大化保留概念无关信息，但现有方法在理论最优性与实际表示学习之间存在差距。

Method: 重新审视概念擦除的最优界限，推导出一类能自然诱导确定性对偶反事实映射的新型擦除函数，并设计实现方案对反事实轨迹施加平移偏置约束。

Result: 框架可在概念擦除与反事实生成之间无缝切换，实验证明其在提升下游算法公平性和生成反事实文本方面有效。

Conclusion: 所提框架统一了概念擦除与反事实生成，兼顾理论最优性与实际表示学习，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Erasing concept-specific information from representations has been proven useful for mitigating bias or interpreting model decisions. The joint objective is to transform the original representations such that the target concept becomes unpredictable, while maximally preserving concept-unrelated information. In this work, we revisit the optimal bounds of concept erasure to derive a novel class of erasure functions that naturally induce a deterministic, dual counterfactual mapping. Bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical representation learning, we design an implementation that imposes a translational bias on counterfactual trajectories - a constraint that aligns with how many concepts geometrically manifest in modern language models. Our framework enables seamless navigation between concept erasure and counterfactual generation. We empirically demonstrate its efficacy in improving downstream algorithmic fairness and generating counterfactual texts.

</details>


### [92] [A Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model for Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11314)
*Xue Qiu,Jianli Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出动态融合大语言模型DF-LLM用于交通流预测，通过时空嵌入、时空融合与LLM主干协同，在四个数据集上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通流预测是智能交通系统的核心支撑技术，但传统神经网络因依赖单一特征建模难以突破精度上限，而大语言模型（LLM）对空间拓扑信息捕捉不足、对时空关联挖掘不充分。

Method: DF-LLM包含三个核心组件：时空嵌入模块（实现多尺度时空特征的协同表示）、时空融合模块（通过图卷积整合空间拓扑与动态依赖）、LLM主干（采用差异化参数适应策略平衡训练效率与交通数据适应性；引入上下文聚合注意力模块强化全局依赖；并采用残差连接缓解深层网络梯度消失）。

Result: 在全部四个数据集上对比各项指标，DF-LLM均取得更优性能。

Conclusion: DF-LLM通过时空嵌入、图卷积融合与LLM参数适应策略的有效结合，克服了传统网络与现有LLM在交通流预测中的局限，验证了其预测性能的优越性。

Abstract: Traffic flow prediction is a core supporting technology for intelligent transportation systems. It uses historical data to infer future traffic dynamics in specific areas, thereby helping to alleviate congestion and improve resource allocation efficiency. Traditional neural networks struggle to break through accuracy limits due to their reliance on singular feature modeling, while large language models (LLMs) suffer from insufficient capture of spatial topological information and mining spatiotemporal correlation. This study proposes a Dynamic Fusion Large Language Model (DF-LLM) for traffic flow prediction. The model incorporates three core components: spatiotemporal embedding module, spatiotemporal fusion module, and LLM backbone. The spatiotemporal embedding module enables synergistic representation of multi-scale spatiotemporal features. The spatiotemporal fusion module integrates spatial topology and dynamic dependencies via graph convolution. The LLM backbone adopts a differentiated parameter adaptation strategy to balance training efficiency and traffic data adaptability. Additionally, it introduces a context aggregation attention module to strengthens global dependencies. More importantly, the LLM backbone takes the residual connections to mitigate the gradient vanishing in deep networks. Experiments show that DF-LLM has achieved better performance by comparing the metrics on all the four datasets.

</details>


### [93] [Estimating Inconsistency Response Surfaces under Uncertainty in Cyber-Physical System Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11331)
*Johannes Mäkelburg,Tim Schwabe,Maribel Acosta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将CPS多模型一致性问题重构为"干预-响应"建模问题，利用Saltelli采样与多保真度蒙特卡洛估计训练代理模型，实现从不确定几何直接预测不一致性，并支持敏感性分析与一致性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS开发中多个互联模型需要共享元素保持一致，但传感器噪声、模型抽象等不确定性会改变元素可取值的范围，从而引发不一致。现有方法只能在给定不确定性配置下判断一致性，缺乏在大规模不确定性空间中系统探索、分析和解释不一致的能力。

Method: 将不一致性重构为干预-响应建模问题；采用Saltelli采样和多保真度蒙特卡洛估计生成干预-响应数据集；训练代理模型直接从传播后的不确定性几何预测不一致性；基于学习到的响应面进行敏感性分析识别主导不确定性因素，并提出基于梯度的一致性补救方法，求解恢复一致性的最小不确定性干预。

Result: 在48个场景、10个CPS领域上的实验表明，代理模型与蒙特卡洛估计结果相符，同时将评估时间从毫秒级降至微秒级，在固定计算预算内可完成数量级更多的响应面评估；敏感性分析能够识别主导不确定性驱动因素，梯度补救方法可找到恢复一致性的最小干预。

Conclusion: 不确定性下的不一致性可通过响应面建模被有效学习、分析和修复，为CPS开发中不确定性感知的一致性管理提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) are commonly represented through multiple interconnected models. During development, CPS consistency requires that shared model elements remain compatible across these models. Uncertainty, for example, due to sensor noise or model abstraction, changes the admissible values of model elements and can introduce inconsistencies, i.e., situations in which models can no longer be jointly satisfied. While existing approaches can determine consistency for a given uncertainty configuration, they provide limited support for systematically exploring, analyzing, and explaining inconsistency across large uncertainty spaces. We address this challenge by reformulating inconsistency as an intervention response modeling problem. Using Saltelli sampling and multi-fidelity Monte Carlo estimation, we generate intervention-response datasets and train a surrogate model that directly predicts inconsistency from the propagated uncertainty geometry. Experiments on 48 scenarios and 10 CPS domains show that the surrogate matches Monte Carlo estimates while reducing evaluation time from milliseconds to microseconds, enabling orders-of-magnitude more response-surface evaluations within fixed computational budgets. Building on the learned response surfaces, we perform sensitivity analysis to identify dominant uncertainty drivers and introduce a gradient-based consistency recourse method to determine minimal uncertainty interventions that restore consistency. The results show that inconsistency under uncertainty can be effectively learned, analyzed, and repaired through response-surface modeling, providing a scalable foundation for uncertainty-aware consistency management in CPS development.

</details>


### [94] [Reification as a Transferable Vocabulary: Zero-Shot Link Prediction with Vanilla GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11347)
*Camille Pradel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将知识图谱基础模型的迁移机制从架构转移到表示中，通过"物化"（reify）输入图，使普通GNN无需专门架构即可实现零样本链路预测，性能媲美专用基础模型ULTRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ULTRA等知识图谱基础模型依赖专门架构硬编码迁移机制来实现对未见图的零样本链路预测，架构复杂且与模型绑定，限制通用性和可复用性。作者希望将这一迁移机制从架构中解耦，转移到图表示层面。

Method: 对输入图进行"物化"（reification）：每条事实变为一个节点，通过固定词表的六种元关系连接到其主语、宾语和关系类型，关系类型作为匿名共享节点而非模型参数。在该表示上使用五种教科书式GNN（GAT、GINE-sum、GINE-mean+max、GraphSAGE、R-GCN），每个仅在单个含4,245个三元组的知识图谱上用一张NVIDIA A100训练30分钟。同一固定词表还扩展到关系数据库：一行作为实体，外键列作为关系类型。

Result: 五个模型均零样本迁移到40个归纳式链路预测基准。其中现成的GAT在ULTRA自身的评测套件上媲美ULTRA（后者为在三张图上预训练的专用基础模型）。在关系数据库初步探测中，两个未见数据库（无单元格值、模式文本或上下文标签）上，用三张知识图谱预训练的模型将外键目标排序显著高于随机初始化和度数对照。

Conclusion: 迁移能力可完全由表示层面承载，而非依赖专门架构；简单的教科书GNN在物化表示上即可达到专用基础模型的零样本迁移效果，并可推广到关系数据库。作者开源了全部40个基准的代码、检查点和评测流程。

Abstract: Knowledge graph foundation models such as ULTRA achieve zero-shot link prediction on unseen graphs through dedicated architectures that hard-code a transfer mechanism. In this work we move that mechanism out of the architecture and into the representation, by \emph{reifying} the input graph: every fact becomes a node, connected to its subject, object, and relation type through a fixed vocabulary of six meta-relations, with relation types as anonymous shared nodes rather than model parameters. On this representation, five textbook GNNs (GAT, GINE with sum and with mean+max aggregation, GraphSAGE, R-GCN), each trained on a single knowledge graph of 4,245 triples for 30 minutes on one NVIDIA A100, transfer zero-shot to 40 inductive link-prediction benchmarks. The best of them, an off-the-shelf GAT, matches ULTRA, a dedicated foundation model pretrained on three graphs, across ULTRA's own evaluation suite. The same fixed vocabulary extends to relational databases, a row becoming an entity and a foreign-key column a relation type; a preliminary probe on two unseen databases, with no cell values, schema text or in-context labels, shows a model of this family pretrained on three knowledge graphs ranking foreign-key targets far above random-initialization and degree controls. We release the code, the checkpoints, and the evaluation pipeline for all 40 benchmarks.

</details>


### [95] [Local Robustness Quantification for Naive Bayes Classifiers and Generative Forests: a General Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11366)
*Adrián Detavernier,Jasper De Bock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出计算两类概率图模型生成式分类器（朴素贝叶斯与生成式森林）预测鲁棒性的方法，将鲁棒性定义为分类器分布可被扰动而不改变预测的程度，考虑局部模型扰动，涵盖ε-污染、全变差距离与卡方散度球等邻域，并在基准数据集上验证鲁棒性值可作为预测可信度的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式分类器（如朴素贝叶斯、生成式森林）的预测可信度评估需求，现有鲁棒性量化方法难以直接适用于以概率图模型为底层分布的生成式分类器，需要专门的计算方法并验证其作为可信度指标的可行性。

Method: 遵循鲁棒性量化范式，将预测鲁棒性定义为分类器分布在不改变预测前提下的最大可扰动程度；扰动通过在图模型局部模型的通用邻域内变化获得，重点考虑ε-污染、全变差距离球与卡方散度球；针对朴素贝叶斯与生成式森林两类分类器给出计算方法。

Result: 在基准数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性；实验表明预测的鲁棒性值可作为其可信度的指标，并与其他可信度指标进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够计算生成式分类器预测的鲁棒性，该鲁棒性值可有效指示预测的可信度，为生成式分类器的可信度评估提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: We provide methods for calculating the robustness of the predictions of two types of generative classifiers whose underlying distribution is a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM): naive Bayes classifiers and generative forests (a probabilistic extension of random forests). Following the paradigm of robustness quantification, we define the robustness of a prediction as the extent to which the distribution of the classifier can be perturbed without changing this prediction. We consider perturbations obtained by varying the local models of the PGMs within general neighborhoods and focus in particular on epsilon-contamination, total variation distance and chi-squared divergence balls. We test our methods on benchmark datasets, demonstrate that the robustness value of a prediction serves as an indicator for its trustworthiness and compare our approach with other such indicators.

</details>


### [96] [Prevalence Determines Precision:Silent Contamination in Detector-Defined Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11449)
*Jia Huang,Yankai Wan,Yangjun Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 数据集精度由各候选池中真阳性流行率经贝叶斯定理决定，而非仅由检测器质量决定。同一检测器三个池的幻影率为81.7%、9.0%、0.0%，跨池迁移精度误差达+422%，贝叶斯表达式则可将三者预测误差控制在3.3%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML数据集通常通过检测器、启发式规则或模型在候选池上运行构建，被接受的样本成为标签。由此，数据集精度受各池真阳性流行率支配（贝叶斯意义），并不只取决于检测器质量。作者希望揭示跨池迁移精度估计会因基线流行率差异而产生严重偏差，并厘清污染对估计量的方向性影响。

Method: 在同一检测器与同一时间段下，构建一个由检测器定义的事件数据集，并引入一个独立官方索引，将每个被检测项标注为真实或幻影（phantom）。跨三个候选池（幻影率分别为81.7%、9.0%、0.0%）迁移精度估计，并与贝叶斯表达式预测对比；将检测响应曲线分解为真实事件分量与幻影分量的凸组合；比较同一窗口下不同统计量在污染下的表现，并考察一种常见归一化所导致的"比率均值"估计量。

Result: 从两个高幻影率池迁移精度到低幻影率池，预测0.955而实测0.183，误差+422%；贝叶斯表达式对三个池的预测误差均在3.3%以内。检测响应曲线是真实事件分量与幻影分量的精确凸组合（残差1.1e-16），幻影数473超过真实事件数308。污染方向依赖估计量：在相同窗口下，一个统计量被稀释、另一个因分母同样受污染而被放大。常见归一化将估计量变为比率均值，其期望可能不存在；在相同335个事件上，该估计量返回0.40，而定义良好的估计量返回0.10。

Conclusion: 数据集精度不能被简单跨池迁移，必须结合各池真阳性流行率以贝叶斯方式评估。污染是一种继承检测器形状的第二信号，而非加性噪声，且其对估计量的影响方向取决于估计量结构；某些常见归一化会引入期望不存在的病态估计量，导致数值严重偏离。研究者应审慎选择估计量并明确报告池级流行率。

Abstract: Many ML datasets are constructed by running a detector, heuristic, or model over candidate pools; accepted items become labels. Dataset precision is then governed by true-positive prevalence in each pool via Bayes, not solely by detector quality. Using one instrument and period, we hold a detector-defined event dataset plus an independent official index labeling every detected item as real or phantom. One detector, three pools yield phantom rates 81.7%, 9.0%, and 0.0%. Transferring precision from the two high-rate pools to the low-rate pool predicts 0.955 versus measured 0.183, a +422% error; the Bayes expression predicts all three within 3.3%. The detected response curve is an exact convex combination of a true-event and a phantom component (residual 1.1e-16), with phantoms outnumbering true events 473 to 308, so contamination is a second signal with detector-inherited shape, not additive noise. Contamination direction depends on the estimator: on identical windows one statistic is diluted and another inflated because its denominator is also contaminated. A common normalization turns the estimator into a mean of ratios whose expectation need not exist; on the same 335 events it returns 0.40 where the well-defined estimator returns 0.10.

</details>


### [97] [Combining Synthetic and Real Data for Low-Resource Historical OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11495)
*Yan Hon Michael Chung,Hanlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较合成与真实历史数据在满文低资源OCR中的组合方式，发现引入真实训练图像可将词准确率提升至95%–96%，共同/顺序训练效果相近，集成投票可达98.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 满文为濒危语言，其清代档案虽日益数字化但难以大规模检索分析。此前仅用合成满文词图像训练的视觉语言模型在真实清代手稿与刻本上仅达87.4%词准确率，存在明显的合成到真实域差距，因此需明确合成与真实历史训练数据应如何组合。

Method: 使用60,000张合成与20,306张真实历史词图像，在统一的检查点选择与档案评估协议下，评测三种预训练视觉语言模型与一种紧凑卷积循环神经网络（CRNN），对比四种训练范式：仅合成、仅真实、合成-真实联合、合成到真实的顺序训练；并进一步采用多强识别器投票与十八世纪满文词典进行分歧裁决。

Result: 引入真实训练图像后领先配置词准确率达95.09%–96.28%，而仅合成配置均不超过87.92%；合成补充显著提升三种视觉语言模型，但对CRNN的边际效应取决于训练目标；联合与顺序训练在实际流程下档案准确率大体相当；紧凑CRNN在具备真实图像时也能达到领先水平，说明模型规模并非准确率决定因素；强识别器间的互补错误使投票无需额外训练即达98.27%。

Conclusion: 真实历史训练数据是缩小合成到真实差距的关键，联合与顺序训练效果相当；模型规模不是识别精度的唯一决定因素；通过集成投票与词典规则裁决可进一步显著提升满文历史档案OCR准确率。

Abstract: Manchu, now critically endangered, was one of the principal languages of the Qing empire (1636-1912), and its extensive archival record is increasingly digitized but remains difficult to search and analyze at scale. Previous work showed that vision-language models (VLMs) trained only on synthetic Manchu word images can reach 87.4% word accuracy on real Qing manuscripts and prints, leaving a substantial synthetic-to-real gap. This study examines how synthetic and real historical training data should be combined for low-resource OCR. Using 60,000 synthetic and 20,306 real historical word images, we evaluate three pretrained VLMs and a compact convolutional recurrent neural network (CRNN) under four regimes: synthetic-only, real-only, joint synthetic-real, and sequential synthetic-to-real training, following a common checkpoint-selection and archival evaluation protocol. Introducing real training images raises the leading configurations to between 95.09% and 96.28% word accuracy, while no synthetic-only configuration exceeds 87.92%. Synthetic supplementation substantially improves all three VLMs, whereas its marginal effect for the CRNN is sensitive to the training objective. Joint and sequential training yield broadly similar archival accuracy under the tested practical pipelines. A compact CRNN also reaches the leading performance range once real images are available, showing that model scale alone does not determine recognition accuracy.
