Finally, complementary errors among strong recognizers allow voting to raise accuracy to 98.27% without additional training, while an eighteenth-century Manchu dictionary provides a principled rule for adjudicating disagreements.

</details>


### [98] [DeFiFlowBench: Benchmarking and Improving Safe Executability in Natural-Language DeFi Workflow Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11504)
*Abhinav Rajeev Kumar,Harshit Arora,Varun Singh,Manikandan Nanjappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeFiFlowBench 基准（207 条团队撰写的自然语言 DeFi 工作流提示）与 Koan-Safe 框架，通过图覆盖、配置完整性与安全谓词的静态度量，并在本地 EVM 上执行交易配置，揭示"结构合法"的工作流仍可授权高成本交易，并证明显式交易保护与执行级评测的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言合成的 DeFi 工作流即便结构上合法，仍可能授权代价高昂的交易；现有方法缺乏对声明式安全与实际执行安全的系统性区分。仅依赖报价推导的滑点边界无法约束订单本身的价格冲击，因此需要能同时度量静态安全配置与真实执行后果的基准与防护机制。

Method: 提出 DeFiFlowBench：207 条团队撰写提示，度量图覆盖、配置完整性与声明式安全谓词，并在本地 EVM 上测试受支持的交易配置，采用固定 5% 价格冲击上限。对比直接、受限与少样本提示。提出 Koan-Safe：提示级意图解析器 + 可替换生成器 + 带默认安全参数的结构修复；其混合变体在 75 条留出提示上评测。并做匹配候选消融、默认值注入边界测试与 36 例诊断网格上的策略上限评估。

Result: 直接、受限与少样本提示在每种配置下产生 14–19 次不安全留出执行。Koan-Safe 混合变体静态安全代理得分 0.67，最佳基线为 0.33；在保存的基准输出上无任何不安全执行。禁用强制机制后，匹配候选消融产生 14–17 次不安全执行。默认注入存在局限：既有的宽松阈值仍可授权不安全交易；单独评估的策略上限在 36 例诊断网格上解决了该失效。由报价推导的滑点边界无法阻止订单自身价格冲击。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式交易保护与基于执行的评测，并将"声明式安全"与"一般性保证"区分开；安全谓词声明不等于执行层面的安全保证。

Abstract: A structurally valid DeFi workflow can still authorize a costly trade. We introduce DeFiFlowBench, a benchmark of 207 team-authored prompts for natural-language DeFi workflow synthesis. It measures graph coverage, configuration completeness, and declared safety predicates, then tests supported trade configurations on a local EVM. Direct, constrained, and few-shot prompting produce 14-19 unsafe held-out executions per configuration under a fixed 5% price-impact cap. A slippage bound derived from a quote does not prevent the price impact of the order itself. We propose Koan-Safe, which combines a prompt-only intent parser, a replaceable generator, and structural repair with default safety parameters. On 75 held-out workflow prompts, its hybrid variant scores 0.67 on the static safety proxy, compared with 0.33 for the best baseline. Koan-Safe records no unsafe executions on the saved benchmark outputs. A matched-candidate ablation produces 14-17 unsafe executions when enforcement is disabled. Additional tests expose the limits of default injection: permissive existing thresholds can still authorize unsafe trades. A separately evaluated policy cap addresses this failure on a 36-case diagnostic grid. These results support explicit trade protections and execution-based evaluation, while distinguishing declared safety from a general guarantee.

</details>


### [99] [Particle GFlowNets: Rethinking Generative Marginalization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11538)
*Tiago da Silva,Diego Mesquita,Salem Lahlou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明生成边际模型(MaMs)与生成流网络(GFlowNets)等价，并将MaMs采样策略扩展到非自回归生成过程，提出Particle GFlowNets加速大规模组合空间训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将MaMs与GFlowNets视为不同范式，但二者在离散随机模型推断上存在潜在联系；同时MaMs的采样策略局限于自回归过程，需要扩展到更一般的生成过程并提升学习收敛速度。

Method: 通过理论分析证明MaMs与GFlowNets的等价性；将MaMs采样策略扩展至非自回归生成过程；基于Gelman-Rubin统计量提出全状态复壮的自动判据用于Gibbs采样器的再生成。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法Particle GFlowNets在大规模组合空间中显著加速训练。

Conclusion: MaMs与GFlowNets本质等价，且将MaMs采样推广到非自回归过程并结合Gelman-Rubin复壮判据，可有效提升大规模组合空间中的训练收敛速度。

Abstract: Generative Marginalization Models (MaMs) have been recently introduced as efficient neural sampling models for any-order autoregressive modelling of discrete distributions. By learning both the marginal and conditional probabilities of a persistent-block Gibbs sampler, MaMs enable fast posterior evaluation with a single neural network forward pass. While prior work has considered MaMs to be distinct from Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets), a well-established paradigm for inference in discrete stochastic models, we show that they are equivalent. Then, we also extend MaMs' sampling strategy to non-autoregressive generative processes. In particular, we describe an automatic criterion for full-state rejuvenation of the Gibbs sampler, derived from the Gelman-Rubin statistic, which plays a key role in speeding up learning convergence. Our experiments show that our method, called Particle GFlowNets, markedly accelerates training in large combinatorial spaces.

</details>


### [100] [A Dataset and Model for Imputing Water Surface Elevation on a Large and Extremely Sparse Spatiotemporal Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11580)
*Ruben Cartuyvels,Karim Douch,Gabriele Bertoli,Mounia El Baz,Artemis Vrettou,Sébastien Lefèvre,Diego Fernandez Prieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 AmazonSWE——一个覆盖亚马逊流域 1.9 万+河段、10 年跨度的卫星测高时空数据集，用于大规模时空图插补任务，并提出一种双向选择性状态空间模型，相比现有方法与已发表的 SWOT 水位加密方法，对实测站点的 RMSE 降低 18–39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球河网水位连续监测对洪水预报、水资源管理和水循环研究至关重要，但地面实测站点稀疏，卫星测高虽有潜力却受时间覆盖稀疏限制；现有时空图插补基准在拓扑结构、规模与稀疏度上均不适配河网场景，因此需要新的数据集与方法。

Method: 构建 AmazonSWE 数据集：整合多源处理后的卫星测高数据（含宽刈幅 SWOT 传感器），覆盖 2016–2026 年亚马逊流域 19K+ 河段，实测站点留作评估；每日观测河段不足 1%。方法上提出一种简单的双向选择性状态空间模型，通过采样连通子图并将空间与时间展平为单一 token 序列，结合拓扑感知位置编码进行插补。

Result: 现有时空图插补方法难以适应此拓扑、规模与稀疏度；所提模型性能优于它们。与已发表的、结合统计与物理建模的 SWOT 水位加密最优方法相比，本模型对实测站点的 RMSE 降低 18–39%，且能为每个河段给出预测，而非仅限于卫星覆盖充分的河段。

Conclusion: AmazonSWE 提供了具有社会影响潜力的新真实用例，并揭示了河网有向无环拓扑带来的显著技术挑战；双向选择性状态空间模型配合子图采样与拓扑感知位置编码，验证了在该稀疏、大规模、特殊拓扑场景下优于既有方法与物理建模方案的可行性。

Abstract: Continuous monitoring of water surface elevation across river networks is critical for flood forecasting, water resource management, and understanding the global water cycle. Yet, the scarcity of in situ gauges across much of the globe constrains the development of reliable modeling frameworks. Satellite altimetry has the potential to alleviate this problem but its use is currently hindered by sparse temporal coverage. To this end, we introduce AmazonSWE, a dataset for training and evaluating large-scale spatiotemporal graph imputation methods that integrates processed satellite altimetry measurements from a range of sources, including the recent wide-swath SWOT sensor. The dataset covers over 19K river sections and 10 years (2016-2026) in the Amazon river basin, with in situ gauges held out for evaluation. Besides contributing a novel real-world use case with the potential for societal impact, AmazonSWE introduces significant technical challenges: with fewer than 1% of sections observed per day, the dataset is far sparser than existing imputation benchmarks, and its directed acyclic river topology is both structurally different from and larger than graphs in existing datasets. We show that prior spatiotemporal graph imputation methods are not adapted to this topology, scale and sparsity, and propose a simple bidirectional selective state space model that outperforms them by sampling connected subgraphs and flattening space and time into a single token sequence with topology-aware positional encodings. Compared to the state-of-the-art published method for SWOT-based WSE densification, which integrates statistics with physical modeling, our model reduces RMSE against in situ gauges by 18-39%, while producing predictions for every river section rather than only those with sufficient nearby satellite coverage.

</details>


### [101] [LoaDiff: Conditional Generation of Electricity Consumption Time Series for Energy Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11639)
*Mariia Baranova,Adrien Petralia,Etienne Le Naour,Nathan Etourneau,Guillaume Hofmann,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoaDiff，一种基于扩散模型的合成智能电表负荷曲线生成方法，支持对家庭静态属性和动态环境变量进行条件控制，并在三个数据集上从保真度、记忆风险、下游效用和条件可控性四个维度进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源转型推动分布式发电、电气化电器和需求响应项目普及，改变了居民用电行为。研究负荷预测、电器识别和需求侧灵活性需要细粒度智能电表数据，但这类数据受严格的访问限制和数据保护法规约束，因此需要逼真的合成数据替代方案。

Method: 提出LoaDiff，一种扩散生成模型，可生成全年、亚小时级的智能电表负荷曲线。模型支持对静态家庭属性（如电器拥有情况）以及动态上下文变量（如日历信息和室外温度）进行灵活条件控制。在三个居民用电数据集上与多种生成基线进行对比。

Result: 实验从四个互补维度评估：保真度与多样性、训练记录记忆风险、负荷预测和电器识别的下游效用、以及不同温度条件下的条件可控性。结果显示LoaDiff能生成真实且多样的负荷曲线，在生成质量与有限记忆证据之间取得良好权衡，保留了对下游能源应用有用的信息，并能对条件变量变化做出连贯响应。

Conclusion: LoaDiff可作为受访问限制的智能电表数据的现实合成替代方案，兼顾生成质量、隐私风险与下游实用性和可控性，适用于负荷预测、电器识别和需求侧灵活性分析等应用。

Abstract: The energy transition is reshaping residential electricity consumption through the increasing adoption of distributed generation, electrified appliances, and demand-response programs. Understanding these evolving behaviors requires access to granular smart-meter data for applications such as load forecasting, appliance detection, and demand-side flexibility analysis. However, such data are subject to strict access restrictions and data-protection regulations. Thus, realistic synthetic alternatives are necessary. In this paper, we introduce LoaDiff, a diffusion-based generative model for year-long, sub-hourly smart-meter load curves. LoaDiff supports flexible conditioning on static household attributes, such as appliance ownership, and dynamic contextual variables, including calendar information and outdoor temperature. We evaluate the model against multiple generative baselines on three residential electricity-consumption datasets. Our experiments assess four complementary dimensions: fidelity and diversity, training-record memorization risk, downstream utility for load forecasting and appliance detection, and conditional controllability under alternative temperature conditions. The results show that LoaDiff generates realistic and diverse load profiles, achieves a favorable trade-off between generation quality and limited evidence of memorization, preserves information useful for downstream energy applications, and responds coherently to changes in conditioning variables.

</details>


### [102] [Musec: MomentUm SpEctral Clipping for Stable Muon-type Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11655)
*Zhuanghua Liu,Menglian Wang,Luo Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Musec优化器，用谱裁剪替代Muon的谱展平，并给出Soft Musec的高效实现及非凸非光滑收敛保证，显著提升训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大语言模型训练中收敛与性能优于Adam/AdamW，但其谱展平操作会导致训练不稳定，表现为损失尖峰与模型权重无界增长。现有方法依赖权重或注意力logit裁剪，需针对特定架构修改，且不能解决所有模型组件的不稳定性。

Method: 提出MomentUm SpEctral Clipping (Musec)：将Muon的谱展平替换为谱裁剪，即保留动量矩阵的谱结构，仅对超过阈值的奇异值进行裁剪，形成优化器层面、与架构无关的稳定机制。进一步提出Soft Musec，利用耦合Newton-Schulz迭代近似的平滑谱饱和函数实现高效计算。理论上建立Musec在非凸非光滑随机优化中的收敛保证。

Result: Soft Musec在多种学习率和模型规模下相比现有Muon变体持续提升训练稳定性；在现有Muon变体发散的设置下仍保持稳定，同时在良好调参配置下匹配其性能；并首次为Muon类方法在非凸非光滑设定下提供收敛保证。

Conclusion: Musec通过优化器层面的谱裁剪，无需架构特定修改即可稳定Muon训练，兼顾稳定性与性能，并具备理论收敛支撑，是一种通用且有效的Muon改进方案。

Abstract: Muon has emerged as a highly effective optimizer for large language model training, often achieving superior convergence and performance compared with the widely adopted Adam and AdamW optimizers. Nevertheless, Muon is prone to training instability due to its spectral flattening, manifested by loss spikes and unbounded growth of model weights. Existing approaches primarily rely on weight or attention-logit clipping, which require architecture-specific modifications and do not directly address instability across all model components. We propose MomentUm SpEctral Clipping (Musec), which replaces Muon's spectral flattening with spectral clipping: rather than setting all singular values of the momentum matrix to approximately one, Musec clips singular values that exceed a threshold while preserving the underlying spectral structure of the momentum. Our strategy provides an optimizer-level, architecture-agnostic mechanism for stabilizing Muon training. We further develop Soft Musec, an efficient implementation that uses a smooth spectral saturation function approximated by coupled Newton-Schulz iterations. Theoretically, we establish convergence guarantees for Musec in nonconvex nonsmooth stochastic optimization. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first convergence guarantee for Muon-type methods in the nonconvex nonsmooth setting. We provide empirical studies to show that Soft Musec consistently improves training stability over existing Muon variants across a wide range of learning rates and model sizes. Notably, Soft Musec remains stable in settings where existing Muon variants diverge, while matching their performance under well-tuned configurations.

</details>


### [103] [Learnware and AI Model Management System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11656)
*Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出从"AI模型存储"到"AI模型管理"的范式转变，核心是以"学件（Learnware = Model + Specification）"为管理基本单元，通过规格（Specification）实现模型的可识别、复用与组装，并给出学件坞系统（LDS）作为实现路径，规格还可充当AI模型协作协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型池本质仍是"AI模型存储系统"，仅做存储而非管理。现实中模型由不同开发者、针对不同任务/数据/目标训练，彼此孤立，难以被识别、复用、组装以应对未来用户任务。同时，开发者不愿共享训练数据，系统最好也不访问用户原始数据，由此产生核心难题：连训练者自身都未必完全理解现代AI模型的功能，如何在无数据访问条件下判断某模型对给定任务的可用性并组装独立开发的模型。

Method: 将管理基本单元从"机器学习模型"升级为"学件"：Learnware = Model + Specification。规格由机器学习过程生成，生成过程不披露开发者训练数据，且具备理论上已证明的数据保持（data-preservation）性质；规格按公开参考标准生成，因而跨模型可比。基于此构建学件坞系统（LDS），以规格作为可识别、可检索、可组装的接口与协作协议。

Result: 以学件为单元、规格为核心的LDS，为构建强大的AI模型管理系统提供了可行路径；可比规格使独立开发的模型能够被识别、复用、组装，甚至作为AI模型协作协议支撑智能体等之间的协同。

Conclusion: 通过将管理单元升级为"学件"、以不泄露训练数据且具数据保持理论的"规格"作为统一接口，原本看似不可达成的AI模型管理目标变得可行；LDS 是从模型存储迈向模型管理、并支持模型协作的路线图。

Abstract: The transition from file storage to database management systems transformed stored data into managed resources. AI now faces an analogous transition from AI model storage to AI model management. Existing model pools essentially serve as \textit{AI model storage systems}. What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [104] [Why Does Post-Training Quantization Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716)
*Yuxiang Chen,Michael Beyer,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了训练后量化（PTQ）为何能在预训练大语言模型（LLMs）中保持性能，识别出两个机制：层间误差的反相抵消作用以及LM-head几何结构对高排名token预测的偏好保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化将LLM权重以低精度存储，每个量化权重都会向隐藏状态引入误差。按直觉，这些误差应随深度累积并破坏下一token预测；随机初始化模型确实会快速累积差异，而量化的预训练模型却累积较少的隐藏状态误差，且基本保持下游任务性能，即便它们从未在量化噪声下训练过。这引出核心问题：为什么训练后量化有效？

Method: 作者对比全精度与量化前向传播，分析预训练量化鲁棒性的机制。定量分析了各层引入的误差与其继承误差的相互作用，并考察LM-head的几何结构特性。

Result: 发现两个机制：（1）某层新引入的误差倾向于抵消其从输入继承的误差，两者部分抵消，使全精度与量化前向传播间的差异缓慢增长；这种反相残差交互在预训练过程中形成，定量分析表明它是减缓隐藏误差增长的主要因素。（2）LM-head几何结构优先保留高排名token的分数和概率，这些通常代表模型最自信的预测。两机制共同解释了为何经过多层的量化误差仍只产生很小的输出变化，并在多种模型和量化设置下验证了结论。

Conclusion: 预训练量化鲁棒性由两个机制共同解释：层间误差的反相抵消作用减缓隐藏误差增长，以及LM-head优先保持高置信度token的预测。这些发现跨模型和量化设置得到验证，为理解PTQ在LLM中的有效性提供了机制性解释。

Abstract: Post-training quantization compresses large language models (LLMs) by storing their weights at reduced precision, and each quantized weight introduces an error into the hidden states. Naively, these errors should accumulate with depth and corrupt next-token prediction; randomly initialized models accumulate these discrepancies rapidly, whereas quantized pretrained models accumulate much less hidden-state error and largely maintain downstream task performance, even though they were never trained with quantization noise. This raises the question we address: why does post-training quantization work? Comparing full-precision and quantized forward passes, we identify two mechanisms that characterize pretrained quantization robustness. First, the error a layer newly introduces tends to oppose the error it inherits from the layer's input. The two cancel partially such that the discrepancy between full-precision and quantized passes grows slowly. This counteracting residual interaction develops during pretraining. Our quantitative analysis identifies it as a major factor slowing hidden-error growth. Second, LM-head geometry preferentially preserves the scores and probabilities of high-ranked tokens, which typically represent the model's most confident predictions. Together, these mechanisms explain why quantization error that passes through numerous layers can still produce only small output changes, and we verify the findings across models and quantization settings.

</details>


### [105] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索用WeightWatcher谱指标作为成员推断攻击(MIA)脆弱性的廉价代理，实验表明stable rank与MIA总体成功率强正相关，Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区间与MIA脆弱性稳定负相关，且两者关联强于泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的成员推断攻击依赖训练昂贵的shadow model，导致大规模隐私评估不切实际。<br>论文希望找到低成本的替代指标来预测MIA风险，以支持可扩展的隐私审计。

Method: 基于heavy-tailed self-regularisation框架，提取廉价的谱指标（WeightWatcher指标）。<br>在图像与表格分类任务上评估多个谱指标。<br>将其与MIA隐私泄漏程度进行相关性分析，并与传统泛化指标（如泛化差距）作对比。

Result: 跨数据集上，stable rank与MIA总体成功率呈强正相关。<br>Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区间与MIA脆弱性呈稳定负相关。<br>这些关联强度均优于使用泛化差距所得到的关联。

Conclusion: 神经网络谱可能包含传统过拟合指标未能完全刻画的隐私泄漏信息。<br>谱分析是面向可扩展隐私审计的一个有前景方向。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [106] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从文本描述中动态学习反应表征，将微调语言模型与高斯过程代理模型联合训练并嵌入多目标贝叶斯优化循环，在多种催化交叉偶联与不对称氢化中比描述符库和one-hot编码用更少实验达到收敛，并以两轮高通量实验（192个反应）实现高收率、高对映选择性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标化学反应优化依赖组分表征方式，但现有方法存在局限：one-hot编码缺乏化学信息，分子描述符难以覆盖化学性质迥异的组分。对于结构功能多样的组分，尚不清楚共享表征应包含什么，且构建这种表征本身是需针对每个新反应系统反复研究的难题，因此亟需绕过该步骤的表征学习方法。

Method: 将反应条件的文本描述输入微调语言模型进行编码，并与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，从而在多目标贝叶斯优化循环中获得任务自适应的反应表征；在镍、钯催化的交叉偶联反应中，分别基于顺序与并行实验策略与描述符库、one-hot编码进行对比。

Result: 该方法比描述符库或one-hot编码用更少实验达到优化收敛。前瞻性应用于跨混合配体齿合度与多相添加剂的钯催化氰化反应，以及跨手性铱、钌催化剂家族的三目标不对称氢化，两轮高通量实验（共192个反应，不足各自设计空间的3%）即获得可直接放大至克级的条件，分离收率分别为94%和84%，后者对映体过量达99.6%。

Conclusion: 通过从文本动态学习任务自适应的反应表征，可绕过人工构建共享表征的难题，在多目标贝叶斯优化中显著提升实验效率，并在复杂催化体系（含混合配体与多催化剂家族）中稳健地给出可放大的高性能反应条件。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system. Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [107] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出looped flows，用局部去噪目标训练循环网络，通过递减噪声与共享噪声建立时间关联，使梯度仅覆盖少数更新时仍能学到可跨时间传递计算的循环状态；推理时对学习到的概率流速度积分，可借更细时间网格与多初始噪声实现多解预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层循环模型理论上可通过增加推理计算解决更难问题，但实际训练只反向传播一步或少数几步更新，导致早期更新难以被训练去支持后续更新，训练与推理之间存在计算深度不匹配的困难。

Method: 提出looped flows：用局部去噪目标训练循环更新，避免对完整展开的递归进行反向传播；通过逐步降低噪声水平并共享噪声，在多个去噪目标间建立时间关联，促使模型学习能随时间传递有用计算的循环状态。推理阶段将问题表述为对由学习到的去噪器参数化的概率流速度进行积分，并与循环状态耦合。

Result: 推理时可通过更细的时间网格投入更多计算，并可从不同初始噪声采样得到多个有效预测。在包括两个多解基准在内的六个推理基准上，looped flows总体超过此前最先进的循环模型，在ARC-AGI-1上达到58.8%测试准确率，在ARC-AGI-2上达到12.2%。

Conclusion: 局部去噪训练结合概率流积分是训练与推理循环模型的有效范式，缓解了循环模型长展开反向传播的困难，并在推理效率与多解生成上展现优势。

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [108] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于纤维最优传输的模型感知调度，用于扩散与流匹配；在多个预测目标、训练配置、数据与架构上一致优于基线，CIFAR-10上16次函数评估时FID相对降低38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于系数路径动能最小化的调度虽能解释强基线，但属于模型无关方法，忽略了预测误差对噪声/信号分配的影响，因而无法针对具体模型优化调度。

Method: 在概率路径的固定时间和状态处，兼容的信号/噪声分解构成仿射纤维；在该纤维内，以真实分解与预测器诱导分解之间的最优传输代价平均定义纤维级预测风险。将风险与系数路径动能结合，得到闭式最优时间分配；方法可推广至一般线性预测目标，并可用早期基线检查点估计风险轮廓。

Result: 在DDPM与流匹配上跨预测目标、训练配置、风险估计检查点、数据集和架构评估，模型感知调度持续优于强基线，流匹配在CIFAR-10、16次函数评估时FID相对降低38.6%。每个模型无关动能基线各自确定动能参考坐标，归一化到单位面积后，不同设置下独立训练模型的纤维风险轮廓高度一致，相应调度形变也一致，表明经验普遍性；预训练检查点诊断将该归一化风险一致性扩展到更大的条件潜在扩散与2-RF模型。

Conclusion: 纤维级风险提供了模型感知且具经验普遍性的调度构造框架；冻结的解析分配模板无需进一步风险估计或模型特定拟合即可保留大部分改进。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [109] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归自我改进（RSI）框架，先用Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）揭示现有LLM的局限，再给出从自主执行到递归元改进的发展路线图，并跨科学发现、具身智能、软件工程等场景分析其需求与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM难以将经验与反馈转化为持久性的能力提升和改进过程本身的优化，缺乏对递归自我改进的系统性认识与路线规划，因此需要明确其问题、概念框架与实现路径。

Method: 采用HCI指标诊断现有LLM的问题；构建RSI概念及其发展路线图（改进-执行自主、改进-策略自主、经验获取自主、环境适应自主、递归元改进）；并结合多场景与产业实践及初步实证证据进行跨领域分析。

Result: 揭示了现有LLM在RSI方面的不足；明确了RSI的分级发展路径；识别出不同场景（科学发现、具身智能、软件工程）对RSI的差异化需求与发展速度；将RSI研究与实际系统相连接并指出关键技术挑战。

Conclusion: RSI是AI系统实现持续自我提升的关键方向，其实现需要沿自主性等级循序渐进，并结合具体场景需求与产业实践，当前仍面临若干核心挑战有待突破。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [110] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRA-NAS提出两阶段框架，用静态代理排名先验加低成本学习曲线残差修正，实现跨搜索空间稳健的零成本架构排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理虽能廉价地对架构排序，但其可靠性在不同搜索空间间差异显著，缺乏跨空间稳健性。

Method: CoRA-Rank通过等权对数排名共识聚合容量与结构初始化代理，并引入无目标共识门控；CoRA-Refine在先验上采样锚点，外推其早期验证曲线，并用ExtraTrees模型传播学习到的残差修正。修正成本约为完整训练候选集的1%，且不使用全训练精度标签拟合排序器；采用统一配置搭配空间专属架构编码。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201、NAS-Bench-101、TransNAS-Bench-101和NATS-SSS上，CoRA-Refine的平均Spearman相关系数分别为0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894，最差空间0.715为对比方法中最高；在NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100上所选架构达73.32%，接近真实最优73.37%；在纯尺寸空间中修正弥补了静态先验相对参数量的不足，并在噪声范围内与最强容量代理持平。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具跨空间排序稳健性与低成本架构选择能力。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [111] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一且可演化的因果发现评测基准 CausalArena，整合合成、语义操作、公式驱动及真实数据四类 SCM，揭示不同方法在各类评测环境中的排名显著漂移，强调基准多样性与预训练-评测重叠是基础模型时代因果发现评估的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现评估高度依赖结构因果模型（SCM），但既有研究在图族、生成机制与评测协议上差异巨大，结果难以横向比较；同时因果发现基础模型（CDFM）的出现使性能可能源于预训练环境与测试 SCM 的重叠，而非真正的发现能力，使固定合成基准上的结果难以解释。

Method: 构建 CausalArena 统一基准，在共同协议下整合多类 SCM：（1）合成 SCM 提供结构与机制上的可控广度；（2）语义操作 SCM 提供可人工审计、语义扎根的环境；（3）公式驱动 SCM 在显式科学机制下测试发现能力；（4）公开真实数据集作为外部效度检验。并在该基准上评测经典方法、神经方法与预训练方法。

Result: 跨经典、神经与预训练方法的实验显示，方法排名在不同 SCM 家族和评测协议之间发生显著变化（ranking shifts），在某一基准设定下的强性能无法可靠迁移到其他设定，说明预训练-评测重叠会干扰对因果发现能力的判断。

Conclusion: 基准多样性与预训练-评测重叠是基础模型时代评估因果发现的两大核心挑战；CausalArena 作为统一、可演化的基准为更可靠、可解释的评估提供了协议化平台。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols. The emergence of causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) further complicates evaluation: performance may reflect not only causal discovery ability, but also overlap between pretraining environments and test SCMs, making results on fixed synthetic benchmarks difficult to interpret. We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [112] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TART是一个四阶段模块化音频到吉他谱生成流水线，首次实现直接从吉他音频生成同时包含指法与演奏技巧标注的吉他谱，在四个基准上零样本评测均显著优于已有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动音乐转谱（AMT）在吉他场景下受限于三个问题：现有系统难以捕捉滑音、推弦、打板等表现性技巧；常将音符错误地分配到弦-品组合；且多在干净录音上训练，难以泛化到真实含噪音频。

Method: 提出TART，一个模块化的四阶段音频到吉他谱（tablature）流水线：（1）音频到MIDI的转谱模型；（2）表现性技巧分类器；（3）音频条件化的T5编码器-解码器用于弦-品分配；（4）自动化吉他谱生成器。

Result: 在GuitarSet、EGDB及两个增强基准Noisy GuitarSet与Noisy EGDB上进行零样本评测。四个基准平均结果：音频到MIDI F50达81.35%（较最佳基线提升6.67个百分点），弦-品Tab F1达71.8%（提升8.5个百分点），端到端Tab F1为54.08%。

Conclusion: TART是首个直接从吉他音频生成同时包含指法与表现性技巧标注的吉他谱的框架，验证了模块化流水线在弦-品分配与技巧识别上的有效性，并在含噪条件下展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [113] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"断裂测试"框架，将AI系统评估与制度失灵基线相连接，区分"证据受限部署"与"测量受限治理"，并以RISE AI架构（责任、包容、安全、赋权）支撑有界、基于证据的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI不仅是治理难题，更能暴露制度在响应性、归属感、关怀与问责方面的既有失灵；一旦部署，AI便成为对这些断裂状态的干预，可能修复、加剧、替代或掩盖问题。因此负责任AI必须同时评估系统本身及其所嵌入的制度断裂。

Method: 以教皇利奥十四世《Magnifica Humanitas》所提供、以尊严、技术权力与共同善为核心的道德框架为基础，构建"断裂测试"，将制度基线纳入系统评估；并区分两种边界：证据受限部署（主张仅限于实际已评估内容）与测量受限治理（记录有利证据无法推翻的约束）；在此边界内提出RISE AI架构。

Result: 提出断裂测试与RISE AI架构，并阐明原则到协议的转化现状：EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001及各类保证实践已将承诺转化为角色、要求、记录、监督与评估，同时揭示其未触及的权力结构与测量边界问题。

Conclusion: 负责任AI不仅需要更好的工程，还需制度修复以及持续的道德与政治判断；关键在于明确协议实际确立了什么、保留了谁的权力、测量应在何处停止。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [114] [Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11917)
*Atindra Jha,Margaret Li,Jure Leskovec,Percy Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了数据重复对稀疏架构（尤其是MoE）的影响：在80M–1B激活参数（最大8.5B总参数）范围内，MoE在数据重复下退化速度显著快于稠密模型，其退化程度随稀疏度加剧，且由总参数而非激活参数决定；现有正则化方法可部分缓解，但无法完全媲美全唯一数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类撰写文本已趋于耗尽，重复使用语言模型训练数据成为常规做法。已有工作研究了稠密Transformer中的数据重复效应，但对近期主流的稀疏架构——混合专家（MoE）——在数据重复下的表现仍缺乏探索，而MoE具备更高的算力效率，这一空白亟需填补。

Method: 作者在单域和多域数据混合、以及不同MoE配置（专家数量、粒度）下，系统改变数据重复率；对比从80M到1B激活参数（8.5B总参数）的模型；测试dropout等现有正则化方法作为潜在补救手段；并分析训练中MoE内部机制（路由稳定性、专家特化）与过拟合的关联。

Result: MoE在数据重复下比稠密模型退化更快；该效应随稀疏度增加而加剧，且由总参数而非激活参数主导。80M稠密模型可重复数据超8倍而退化极小，MoE在4倍时即开始受损并快速恶化；在32倍后失去性能优势，甚至不如稠密模型。部分正则化方法（如dropout）可缓解过拟合；采用强掩码正则化时，MoE在数据重复超64倍时仍能超越稠密模型，但没有任何方法能完全达到全唯一数据训练的性能。机制分析发现：MoE路由在训练早期即普遍稳定，专家特化与对重复数据的过拟合相关。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了稀疏性与数据重复之间的不利交互：给出了MoE过拟合核心机制的证据及其潜在的缓解手段，并指出未来方法可通过打破记忆模式、减少模型参数过度特化来进一步缓解过拟合。

Abstract: As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Weighted Empirical Risk Minimization for Machine Learning under Long-Range Dependence: Exact Pathwise Rates and Learning-Error Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10767)
*Elina Moldavskaya*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文为长程依赖数据下、正则加权经验风险最小化训练的平滑参数模型建立了精确几乎必然学习理论，给出几乎必然Bahadur表示、参数limsup律及学习轨迹泛函聚簇集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习理论多假设独立同分布或弱相依数据，而长记忆（长程依赖）数据下的经验风险最小化学习行为缺乏精确的几乎必然刻画；样本权重正则变化对学习指数、路径常数与聚簇几何的影响也不清楚，因此需要建立相应理论。

Method: 训练观测来自平稳高斯序列的固定有限窗，样本权重正则变化。假设总体最优点的损失梯度具有Wiener混沌秩m且低频系数非零。在长记忆内部区间，将有限滞后得分在重对数尺度上约化为单个加权Hermite混沌，进而导出几乎必然Bahadur表示与limsup律；对m≥2刻画完整学习轨迹的泛函聚簇集，并在秩一情形对容许幂权指数做全局优化。

Result: 得到几乎必然Bahadur表示、学习参数的精确limsup律，以及m≥2时学习轨迹的泛函聚簇集。多项式学习指数由记忆参数与混沌秩决定，并在容许幂权下不变；而尖锐路径常数与聚簇几何依赖权重。秩一情形下，对所有容许幂指数做全局优化表明每个最优权指数均为正。时间序列预测与分类示例验证了结果。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了长程依赖数据下正则加权ERM学习的精确几乎必然理论，揭示了学习指数由记忆与混沌秩共同决定且对幂权不变，而路径常数与聚簇几何受权重影响；秩一情形最优幂权为正。可用于时间序列预测与分类等场景。

Abstract: We develop an exact almost-sure learning theory for smooth parametric models trained by regularly weighted empirical risk minimization on long-range dependent data. The training observations are generated from a fixed finite window of a stationary Gaussian sequence, and the sample weights are regularly varying. If the loss gradient at the population minimizer has Wiener-chaos rank $m$ and a nonzero low-frequency coefficient, then, in the long-memory interior regime, the finite-lag score reduces on the iterated-logarithm scale to a single weighted Hermite chaos. This yields an almost-sure Bahadur representation, an exact limsup law for the learned parameter, and, for $m\ge2$, the functional cluster set of the complete learning trajectory. The polynomial learning exponent is determined by the memory parameter and the chaos rank and is invariant under the admissible power weighting, whereas the sharp pathwise constant and cluster geometry depend on the weights. In the rank-one case, global optimization over the admissible power exponents shows that every optimizer is positive. Time-series prediction and classification examples illustrate the results.

</details>


### [116] [A Hilbert-Valued Functional Decomposition Framework for Explaining Time-Dependent Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11295)
*Sophie Hanna Langbein,Niklas Koenen,Marvin N. Wright,Julia Herbinger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个针对时变（函数型/多变量）输出的特征归因统一框架，通过将函数分解推广到Hilbert空间值预测函数并引入核化输出表示，实现时间依赖感知的多粒度解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的解释方法主要面向标量输出，对函数型或多变量输出（如需求预测中的时间轨迹）通常只能逐输出位置独立解释，忽略了输出分量之间的依赖关系，导致解释不准确或不完整。

Method: 将函数分解（functional decomposition）推广到Hilbert空间值的预测函数，并把已有的基于特征的解释框架扩展到该设定；引入基于核的输出表示，支持时间依赖感知的解释，涵盖时间特定（time-specific）、时间分辨（time-resolved）与时间聚合（time-aggregated）多个时间粒度，且现有方法可作为其特例统一纳入。

Result: 在合成数据与真实世界数据上验证了框架有效性，任务包括日内金融市场波动率预测与能源需求预测。

Conclusion: 该工作为时变输出的特征归因提供了统一理论与方法框架，能够在不同时间粒度上产生时间依赖感知的解释，并统一了多种已有方法。

Abstract: Feature-based explanations quantify features' influence on model predictions, but are primarily designed for scalar outputs. In many applications, however, outputs are functional or multivariate, such as time-dependent trajectories in demand forecasting. Consequently, existing approaches typically explain each output location independently, ignoring dependencies across the output components. We address this limitation by developing a unified framework for feature-based explanations of time-dependent outputs. Specifically, we generalize functional decomposition to Hilbert-valued prediction functions and extend an existing feature-based explanation framework to this setting. Our framework introduces kernel-based output representations that enable time-dependency-aware explanations at multiple levels of temporal granularity, including time-specific, time-resolved, and time-aggregated, while providing a unified view in which existing methods arise as special cases. We validate our framework on synthetic and real-world data, including intraday financial market volatility prediction and energy demand forecasting.

</details>


### [117] [Risk-Averse Decision Making with Multi-Level Reliability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11524)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在系统状态不确定下，最大化多个目标中断水平处性能证书的加权平均问题，并将其等价转化为嵌套预测集上的优化，与共形预测文献相衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程应用（如无线广播）通常需要在不同目标中断水平上提供性能证书，且真实系统状态存在不确定性；既有工作多关注单一级别的风险厌恶决策，缺乏对多级证书的整体优化。

Method: 将问题等价表述为对嵌套预测集的优化，从而与共形预测框架建立联系，并拓展单级风险厌恶决策的既有方法；进一步推导对偶形式，使优化可按输入取值解耦。

Result: 在基于分集的无线传输系统上进行数值实验，展示了用单一共享策略强制满足多级证书所带来的代价，并刻画了多个可靠性水平之间的帕累托权衡。

Conclusion: 嵌套预测集视角为多级性能证书优化提供了统一框架，对偶解耦提升了可求解性，并揭示了多可靠性目标间的内在权衡关系。

Abstract: Many applications in engineering, including wireless broadcasting, require designs that provide performance certificates at different target outage levels. This paper studies the problem of maximizing the weighted average of such certificates in the presence of uncertainty about the true system state. The problem is shown to be equivalent to an optimization over nested prediction sets, connecting to the literature on conformal prediction and extending prior art on single-level risk-averse decision making. Furthermore, we derive a dual formulation that decouples optimization across input values. Numerical experiments on a diversity-based wireless transmission system illustrate the cost of enforcing multi-level certificates with a single shared policy and trace the Pareto trade-off between multiple reliability levels.

</details>


### [118] [A distribution-free certification framework for trustworthy crash-severity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11592)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文针对碰撞严重度模型缺乏有限样本可靠性保证的问题，提出一个可包裹任意现有模型的认证层，在标签序数性、误报结构性、跨域漂移等条件下提供无分布假设的覆盖与风险保证，并在520万条德州记录上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞严重度模型用于筛选、调度和地点优先级排序，但部署时没有任何有限样本层面的说明来解释"一次预测"意味着什么。现成的保证在此失效，因为碰撞严重度数据的特征恰好破坏这些保证：结果是序数的KABCO等级；记录的标签是现场评估，仅约一半时间与医学严重度一致，且错误具有结构性；部署还会跨越校准阶段从未见过的辖区和年份。

Method: 作者构建了一个认证层，不修改任何严重度模型即可包裹使用，并利用上述结构给出无分布假设的保证：形如"B或更差"的连续序数集合；对任意预先声明划分的逐类有效性，并给出oracle效率刻画；通过声明的报告带将覆盖转移到未观测的真实严重度，并给出最坏情况锐度结果；部署漂移下的单侧证书；以及严重度加权风险控制。各保证可组合，并配有可归因的松弛预算。作者同时分析界定了认证的极限：认证集合的信息量由真实分布的一个泛函支配，任何基础模型都无法规避，且该泛函无法在无分布假设下给出下界；当给定由记录链接数据识别出的误报通道后，该下限的非平凡下界变得可计算。

Result: 在跨越四个年代、七个基础模型的520万条德州记录上，认证层附加了相同的有效性和认证结果；在弱势道路使用者群体上，认证出集合宽度的一个模型无关下限，没有任何基础模型能突破，并将其与一个保持有界但在无分布假设下不可识别的剩余部分区分开来。框架以开源包形式发布，含定理级测试。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个在不修改模型前提下、面向序数且含结构性误报标签、并跨域漂移的碰撞严重度预测的无分布有限样本认证框架，既给出可组合的覆盖与风险保证，也从理论上刻画了认证可达信息量的内在极限。

Abstract: Crash-severity models inform screening, dispatch and site prioritization, yet are deployed without a finite-sample statement of what one prediction means. Off-the-shelf guarantees fail here, because the features that make crash severity distinctive defeat them: the KABCO outcome is ordinal, the recorded label is a field assessment agreeing with medical severity about half the time, erring in a structured way, and deployment crosses jurisdictions and years calibration never saw. We develop a certification layer that wraps any severity model unmodified, with distribution-free guarantees using this structure: contiguous ordinal sets that read as "B or worse"; per-class validity for any pre-declared partition, with an oracle efficiency characterization; transfer of coverage to unobserved true severity through a declared reporting band, with a worst-case sharpness result; a one-sided certificate under deployment shift; and severity-weighted risk control. The guarantees compose with an attributable slack budget. The same analysis bounds what certification can achieve. A certified set's informativeness is governed by a functional of the true law that no base model can evade and that cannot be lower-bounded distribution-free; given a declared misreporting channel identified from record-linkage data, a nonvacuous lower bound on that floor becomes computable. On 5.2 million Texas records across seven base models spanning four decades, the layer attaches identical validity and certifies, on the vulnerable road users, a model-independent floor on set width that no base model beats, separating it from a remainder that stays bounded but distribution-free unidentifiable. The framework is released as an open-source package with theorem-level tests.

</details>


### [119] [Identifiability of Nonnegative Tensor Decompositions via Positive Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11606)
*Haoming Wang,Ming Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文引入"正散射项"量化非负张量分解中由正性与支撑结构带来的额外可识别性，并结合Lovitz–Petrov维度预算，给出两个充分条件：阈值2|S|−2保证极小性与非负秩，2|S|−1保证同长度非负分解的唯一性，且散射项代价仅取0、1或+∞，可由图连通性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量分解的可识别性通常由因子族的线性代数条件（维度、独立性）确立，但对非负分解而言，正性提供了维度与独立性之外的信息：非负项不可相消，其支撑集约束了竞争性分解。作者希望将这一额外可识别性来源形式化，并弥补Kruskal与Lovitz–Petrov条件无法覆盖的稀疏非负张量情形。

Method: 引入"正散射项"以量化正性带来的额外可识别性，并将其与Lovitz–Petrov推广Kruskal定理所依赖的维度预算结合；针对每个分量子集S，建立非负秩一张量不可约交换的正分裂不等式，将维度约束与支撑诱导的几何刚性融合；散射项虽定义为对中间因子空间的优化，但证明其模式代价仅取0、1或+∞，从而用图连通性给出精确激活刻画。

Result: 对每个分量子集S得到两个充分条件：阈值2|S|−2保证极小性与非负秩，更强阈值2|S|−1保证同长度非负分解的唯一性；散射项的模式代价恰好为0、1或+∞，激活条件可由图连通性精确描述；所得判据可严格认证超出Kruskal与Lovitz–Petrov条件覆盖范围的稀疏非负张量分解，包括即使重整形后这些条件仍失败的例子；在矩阵情形，两判据分别退化为满秩分解与双侧可分性。

Conclusion: 正性可通过"正散射项"被量化并融入维度预算，形成比经典Kruskal/Lovitz–Petrov更精细的非负张量分解可识别性判据，且代价结构简单、可用图连通性刻画，矩阵情形退化为已知经典结果。

Abstract: Identifiability of tensor decompositions is often established through linear-algebraic conditions on the factor families. For nonnegative decompositions, however, positivity provides additional information that is not captured by dimension and independence alone: nonnegative terms cannot cancel, and their supports constrain competing decompositions. We introduce a positive scattering term that quantifies this additional source of identifiability and combine it with the dimension budget underlying the Lovitz--Petrov generalization of Kruskal's theorem. For every subset of components, we obtain two sufficient conditions: a threshold of $2|S|-2$ guarantees minimality and nonnegative rank, while the stronger threshold $2|S|-1$ guarantees uniqueness among nonnegative decompositions of the same length. The key result is a positive splitting inequality for irreducible exchanges of nonnegative rank-one tensors, which combines the dimension constraint with support-induced geometric rigidity. Although the scattering term is defined through an optimization over intermediate factor spaces, we show that its mode costs are exactly $0$, $1$, or $+\infty$, yielding an exact activation characterization in terms of graph connectivity. The resulting criterion can strictly certify sparse nonnegative tensor decompositions beyond the reach of Kruskal and Lovitz--Petrov conditions, including examples for which those conditions fail even after reshaping. In the matrix case, the two criteria reduce respectively to full-rank factorization and two-sided separability.

</details>


### [120] [Generalization Analysis of Distributed Kernel-based Robust Gradient Descent Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11712)
*Jun-Yi Meng,Zheng-Chu Guo,Yuan Mao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究再生核希尔伯特空间中分布式梯度下降算法在鲁棒损失$l_σ$下的泛化性能，建立了分布式核鲁棒梯度下降（DKRGD）的最优学习率，并给出通信高效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式梯度下降在再生核希尔伯特空间中的泛化性能已有研究，但在鲁棒损失函数下，如何同时缓解饱和现象、保证统计鲁棒性并获得最优学习率，以及放松对本地机器数量的限制，仍需深入探讨。

Method: 利用梯度下降的谱刻画与鲁棒损失函数的内在性质，选取合适的尺度参数$σ$；通过新颖的误差分析为算子乘积给出更紧的界，并设计通信高效策略。

Result: 为DKRGD建立了最优学习率；所选$σ$同时缓解饱和现象并保证统计鲁棒性；新误差分析显著放松了对最大本地机器数的限制，同时保持最优学习率；通信高效策略进一步提升收敛性能。

Conclusion: 在再生核希尔伯特空间下，DKRGD在鲁棒损失下可实现最优学习率，且通过改进的算子乘积界与通信高效策略，在更宽松的机器数约束下保持性能优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the generalization performance of distributed gradient descent algorithms in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space under a robust loss function $l_σ$. By exploiting the spectral characterization of gradient descent together with the intrinsic properties of robust loss functions, we establish optimal learning rates for the distributed kernel-based robust gradient descent (DKRGD) algorithm with an appropriately chosen scale parameter $σ$. The proposed parameter choice of $σ$ simultaneously alleviates the saturation phenomenon and guarantees statistical robustness. A key technical contribution is a novel error analysis that provides substantially sharper bounds for products of operators, thereby significantly relaxing existing restrictions on the maximum number of local machines while retaining optimal learning rates. Finally, we develop a communication-efficient strategy that further improves the convergence performance of DKRGD.

</details>


### [121] [Near-Optimal Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11807)
*Corentin Pla,Hugo Richard,Marc Abeille,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究带转移前瞻（transition look-ahead）的强化学习：证明任意固定有理折扣因子下精确规划仍为NP难，并提出随机多项式时间近似方案，进一步扩展到未知转移与随机奖励，取得与经典表格型折扣RL同阶（忽略对数因子）的累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步转移前瞻能显著提升性能，但已知最优规划是NP难的；然而该结论依赖折扣因子任意接近1，因此不清楚对其他折扣因子是否仍难，也不清楚能否高效地近似最优规划。论文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 首先对任意固定有理折扣因子γ∈(0,1)构造归约证明精确规划NP难；随后针对每个固定前瞻深度提出随机多项式时间近似方案（近似算法）。再将该方法扩展到未知转移和随机奖励情形，采用乐观策略与方差自适应置信界。

Result: 证明对每个固定有理折扣因子γ∈(0,1)，精确规划仍为NP难；给出每个固定前瞻深度的随机多项式时间近似方案；在未知转移与随机奖励下，所得算法的累积遗憾主项与经典表格型折扣RL在忽略对数因子的意义下相匹配。

Conclusion: 尽管带转移前瞻的精确规划是NP难的，但高效近似最优的规划与学习仍然可行。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) with transition look-ahead, where the agent may observe which states would be visited upon playing any sequence of $\ell$ actions before deciding its course of action. Although look-ahead can substantially improve achievable performance, it is known that optimal planning with multi-step transition look-ahead is NP-hard, but this hardness was established using discount factors arbitrarily close to one. It was therefore unknown whether the problem remains hard for any discount factor, and whether near-optimal planning can nevertheless be performed efficiently. We resolve both questions. First, we show that for every fixed rational discount factor ($γ\in(0,1)$), exact planning remains NP-hard. Second, we introduce a randomized polynomial-time approximation scheme for every fixed look-ahead depth. We then extend our approach to unknown transitions and stochastic rewards using optimism and variance-adaptive confidence bounds. The resulting algorithm achieves cumulative regret whose leading term matches classical tabular discounted RL up to logarithmic factors. Thus, although exact planning with transition look-ahead is NP-hard, efficient near-optimal planning and learning remain possible.

</details>


### [122] [Evaluating Time-Series Foundation Models and Multimodal Dietary Context for CGM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11872)
*Bowen Zhang,Hsiu-Wen Cheng,Hongyu Yang,Evie L. Shen,Joleen Vansomphone,Yuna Li,Kerry Zhou,Zitian Qu,Suning Zhao,Xiangning Deng,Hua Zhou,Jin J. Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了时间序列基础模型在连续血糖监测（CGM）预测中的效果，发现零样本基础模型并不优于任务特定基线（如Elastic Net、PatchTST），但轻量微调可显著提升性能（RMSE降低6.5%–18.4%）。同时，融合饮食多模态上下文（如食物图像、宏量营养素）可将餐后RMSE降低约15%，表明饮食信息提供CGM之外的临床有意义信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGM提供高频血糖动态测量，支持短期血糖预测以辅助糖尿病管理。尽管时间序列基础模型具备强大的通用预测能力，但其在CGM预测中的有效性以及多模态饮食上下文的附加价值尚不明确，因此需要系统性实证研究。

Method: 使用八个公开CGM数据集（覆盖1型糖尿病、2型糖尿病及非糖尿病人群），在统一协议下跨多种上下文长度与预测时域进行评估，对比零样本基础模型与任务特定基线（Elastic Net、PatchTST）及轻量微调模型（如Chronos-Bolt）。此外，利用CGMacros数据集（含时间对齐的CGM信号、食物图像与宏量营养素记录），采用基于残差的融合框架评估多模态饮食上下文，并比较Chronos、LSTM与CatBoost表示与餐后血糖增量的相关性。

Result: 零样本基础模型未持续优于强任务特定基线；轻量微调显著提升预测性能，微调后Chronos-Bolt在T1D队列RMSE降低6.5%–18.4%，在非糖尿病/T2D队列降低8.6%–18.2%，且在分布内与分布外测试设置中改进相当。残差融合框架相较仅CGM基线将整体RMSE降低约3%，餐后RMSE降低约15%。Chronos的CGM表示与餐后血糖增量的相关性优于LSTM和CatBoost（即使后者加入饮食模态）。

Conclusion: 基础模型需要针对CGM的特定适配才能实现可靠预测；饮食上下文在CGM之外提供具有临床意义的信号，尤其在餐后时段。多模态融合与微调策略对提升CGM血糖预测具有重要价值。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides high-frequency measurements of glucose dynamics and enables short-term glucose forecasting for diabetes management. Although time-series foundation models have shown strong general forecasting ability, their effectiveness for CGM prediction and the added value of multimodal dietary context remain unclear. We conduct a comprehensive empirical study using eight public CGM datasets spanning Type 1 diabetes, Type 2 diabetes, and non-diabetes populations. Under a unified protocol across multiple context lengths and prediction horizons, zero-shot foundation models did not consistently outperform strong task-specific baselines such as Elastic Net and PatchTST. In contrast, lightweight fine-tuning substantially improved forecasting performance. For example, fine-tuned Chronos-Bolt reduced RMSE by 6.5%-18.4% in the T1D cohort and by 8.6%-18.2% in the non-diabetes/T2D cohort, with comparable improvements in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution test settings. We further evaluate multimodal dietary context using CGMacros, which provides temporally aligned CGM signals, food images, and macronutrient records. A residual-based fusion framework reduced overall RMSE by approximately 3% and postprandial RMSE by approximately 15% relative to the CGM-only baseline. Moreover, Chronos-based CGM representations were more strongly correlated with observed postprandial glucose increments than representations from LSTM and CatBoost, even after those models incorporated additional dietary modalities, suggesting that pretrained temporal representations better preserve meal-induced excursion patterns. These findings show that foundation models require CGM-specific adaptation for reliable forecasting and that dietary context provides clinically meaningful signals beyond CGM alone, especially during postprandial periods.

</details>


### [123] [Generative Marketing Mix Modeling: A Causal Inference Framework Linking GEO and GEM to Business Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11915)
*Masahiro Kato,Daiki Honma,Taka Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出生成式营销组合建模(GMMM)，用于估计生成引擎优化(GEO)与生成引擎营销(GEM)的因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI改变了企业触达顾客的方式，但标准营销数据未记录用户在生成答案中看到并注意到企业名称的频率，因此缺乏评估GEO与GEM效果的方法。

Method: GMMM：对GEO，结合重复生成的答案、问题计数、各生成系统的使用份额与注意概率；对GEM，结合赞助投放记录与注意概率。通过比较不同处理序列下的期望业务响应，并给出识别相应效应的充分条件。

Result: 使用英语和日语的产品推荐模拟答案，考察了所提方法的实证表现。

Conclusion: GMMM为生成式环境下GEO与GEM的因果效应估计提供了可行框架，并明确了效应可识别的充分条件。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence changes how firms reach customers, but standard marketing data do not record how often users see and notice a firm's name in generated answers. We develop Generative Marketing Mix Modeling (GMMM) to estimate the causal effects of Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) and Generative Engine Marketing (GEM). For GEO, GMMM combines repeated generated answers with question counts, shares of use across generative systems, and notice probabilities. For GEM, it combines records of sponsored placements with notice probabilities. GMMM compares expected business responses under alternative treatment sequences and establishes sufficient conditions for identifying the resulting effects. We investigate the empirical performance of the proposed method using simulated answers to product recommendation in English and Japanese.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Reconstructing the information processing capacity of physical systems from noisy observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11268)
*Shun Kotoku,Rodrigo Martínez-Peña,Takatomo Mihana,Felix Köster,Johannes Nokkala,Lina Jaurigue,Ryoichi Horisaki,André Röhm*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文阐明了噪声对信息处理容量（IPC）的影响，并提出协方差正交投影重构方法（CROP），可在无系统与噪声模型的条件下，直接从含噪观测中重构无噪声IPC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPC框架能细致刻画动力系统的计算属性，但噪声如何影响IPC的解读尚不完整；通常对噪声实现做系综平均所得的IPC并不等于无扰动系统的IPC。

Method: 先区分无扰动系统动力学与随机动力学的无噪声分量，指出噪声可重新分配计算容量甚至提升特定任务表现；进而提出CROP，通过正交投影从含噪观测中重构无噪声分量的协方差与IPC，无需系统或噪声的详细模型。

Result: 在固定总测量预算下，对经典非线性储备池的数值实验表明CROP比重复试验系综平均更准确地估计无噪声IPC；在受不可避免测量噪声影响的量子储备池中亦具同样优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为从有限观测中恢复含噪物理系统的计算结构提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Driven dynamical systems can compute when their transient states encode complex transformations of past inputs. The information processing capacity (IPC) framework allows for a detailed accounting of these computational properties, however its interpretation in noisy systems has remained incomplete. In this work, we clarify how noise affects the IPC and how one can reconstruct the noiseless IPC. First, we show how to distinguish the dynamics of an unperturbed system from the noise-free component of the stochastic dynamics: The IPC measured for responses averaged over noise realizations is in general not the same as the IPC of the unperturbed system. We explicitly demonstrate that noise can redistribute computational capacity and sometimes even enhance performance on particular tasks, rather than merely degrading a fixed computation. We then introduce covariance reconstruction by orthogonal projection (CROP), which reconstructs the covariance and IPC of the noise-free component directly from noisy observations, without requiring a detailed model of either the system or the noise. At fixed total measurement budget, numerical tests on a classical nonlinear reservoir show that CROP estimates the noise-free IPC more accurately than the standard practice of ensemble averaging over repeated trials. We find the same advantage in a quantum reservoir subject to unavoidable measurement noise. Our results provide a general route to recovering the computational structure of noisy physical systems from finite observations.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [A new generation of effective core potentials: Selected heavy 5d and 6p elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10853)
*Omar Madany,Lubos Mitas*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 扩展ccECP库，为重元素5d、6p区开发半局域赝势与配套基组，采用三层核-价划分策略，在原子上达到0.045 eV精度，并保证分子可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ccECP库缺乏针对5d（Hf、Os、Hg）与6p（Tl、Po、At、Rn）等重元素的半局域赝势及配套基组，同时需要兼顾标量相对论效应、自旋-轨道耦合与电子相关，并解决随机量子蒙特卡洛方法中原点势发散导致的数值稳定性瓶颈。

Method: 采用分层核-价划分策略，设置三种分辨率：针对Hf、Os、Hg的60核小分区（显式相关亚价壳层）、针对主族元素的78核大分区（考虑稀薄价环境下的核心极化与弛豫效应），以及针对Hg和Tl的68核中间分区；以相对论全电子CCSD(T)为参考进行优化，并在原点处采用正则化有限势以提升数值稳定性。

Result: 原子低激发态全球平均偏差仅0.045 eV；分子可迁移性良好，解离能偏差小于0.03 eV，平衡键长偏差在0.005 Å以内。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了ccECP库，为重元素体系的多体模拟提供高精度且数值稳定的赝势与基组，消除了随机量子蒙特卡洛方法的关键方法学瓶颈。

Abstract: We expand the correlation-consistent effective core potentials (ccECPs) library by developing semi-local pseudopotentials and matching basis sets by heavy-elements from $5d$ (Hf, Os, Hg) and $6p$ (Tl, Po, At, Rn) blocks. In order to accurately capture scalar relativistic effects, spin-orbit coupling, and electron-electron correlation, we implement a tiered core-valence partitioning strategy across three distinct resolutions. This includes a small 60-core (Hf, Os, Hg) that explicitly correlates subvalence shells, a large 78-core definition for the main-group elements that rigorously accounts for core polarization and relaxation effects in sparse valence environments, and an intermediate 68-core partition for Hg and Tl. This 68-core architecture represents a unique development in the ccECP library, optimizing the balance between accuracy and computational efficiency in a manner unexplored for ligther elements. Optimized against relativistic all-electron CCSD(T) references, the ccECPs deliver outstanding atomic precision, achieving a global average atomic low-lying states deviation of just 0.045 eV. This accuracy translates directly to robust molecular transferability, systematically restricting dissociation energy discrepancies to under 0.03 eV, equilibrium bond lengths to within 0.005 Å. By enforcing a regularized, finite potential at the origin for enhanced numerical stability in stochastic quantum Monte Carlo methods, this library removes a critical methodological bottleneck for predictive many-body simulations of heavy-element systems and materials.

</details>


### [126] [A Geometrically Parameterized Quasi-Stationary 3D Model for High-Frequency Induction Tube Welding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11250)
*Dmitry Ivanov,John Inge Asperheim,Bjørnar Grande,Prerana Das*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出高频感应焊接管材的三维多物理场有限元框架，耦合时谐磁标势与稳定化对流扩散传热方程，基于GetDP与Gmsh实现，并用AISI 304不锈钢管工业焊接线测量数据验证，进而比较FeNiZnV铁氧体与Ferrotron 559H软磁复合材料的阻抗体（impeders）影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在高频感应焊接管材过程中准确预测电磁场与温度场，需要一个可参数化建模、可自动生成网格并贴合物理特性的三维多物理场仿真框架，并借助工业实测数据验证其可靠性；同时需要评估不同阻抗体（impeders）材料对焊接过程的影响。

Method: 建立三维多物理场有限元模型：电磁部分采用时谐磁标势（magnetic scalar potential）表述，热部分采用稳定化的准静态对流-扩散（advection-diffusion）传热方程，实现电磁-热耦合。框架包含参数化几何生成与半自动、面向物理的网格构建，使用开源工具GetDP与Gmsh实现。

Result: 以AISI 304不锈钢管商业感应焊接线的测量数据进行验证，模型计算结果与运行数据吻合良好。随后将验证后的模型用于比较传统FeNiZnV铁氧体阻抗体与软磁复合材料Ferrotron 559H在AISI 304不锈钢管感应焊接中的影响。

Conclusion: 该三维多物理场有限元框架能够准确预测工业条件下高频感应焊接管材的电磁与热场，具备工程适用性；并可有效用于阻抗体材料选型与工艺优化分析。

Abstract: A three-dimensional multiphysics finite element framework for the simulation of high-frequency induction welding of tubes is presented. The model couples a time-harmonic magnetic scalar potential formulation with a stabilized quasi-stationary advection-diffusion heat transport equation, enabling accurate prediction of electromagnetic and thermal fields under industrial operating conditions. The framework incorporates parameterized geometry generation and semi-automated, physics-tailored mesh construction and is implemented using the open-source tools GetDP and Gmsh. Validation against measurements from a commercial induction welding line for AISI 304 stainless steel tubes demonstrates good agreement with operating data. The validated model is subsequently applied to investigate the influence of impeder material by comparing a conventional FeNiZnV ferrite with the soft magnetic composite Ferrotron 559H for the induction welding of AISI 304 stainless steel tubes.

</details>


### [127] [Stress-Testing Dynamical and Generative Downscaling Using Subseasonal Extreme Precipitation Forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11696)
*Mauricio Lima,Marika Koukoula,Romain Pilon,Monika Feldmann,Erwan Koch,Daniela I. V. Domeisen,Tom Beucler*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该研究比较了WRF动力降尺度与扩散生成模型在次季节尺度极端降水降尺度中的表现，发现两者均优于原始ECMWF预报，但优势依赖天气形势：WRF在非平稳多单体事件上概率技巧最高，扩散模型在两个事件上表现更稳定，并在平稳超级单体事件中优于WRF。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗空间分辨率限制了次季节预测模型对极端降水的刻画能力，动力降尺度和深度生成降尺度均可缓解该问题，但二者在不同大气环流形势下针对极端事件的比较性能尚不明确。

Method: 将WRF模式与基于扩散的生成模型进行对比，对两个物理机制不同的极端降水事件进行降尺度，预报时效长达3周；为使WRF（可无需针对特定模式训练即可降尺度不同驱动模型的边界条件）比较公平，扩散模型采用非配对方式训练；以瑞士CombiPrecip雨量计-雷达融合观测为参照进行评估。

Result: 两种方法均优于原始ECMWF预报，但优势具有形势依赖性：WRF在非平稳多单体事件上取得最高的概率技巧；扩散模型在两个事件的多种性能指标上更一致，并在更平稳的超级单体事件中优于WRF。

Conclusion: 显式动力建模可在特定降水事件和次季节时效上提供附加价值，而生成式降尺度在更多不同情境下具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Coarse spatial resolution limits the ability of subseasonal prediction models to resolve extreme precipitation. Downscaling with either dynamical or deep generative models can overcome this issue, but the comparative performance of these models for extremes across different atmospheric regimes remains poorly understood. In this work, we evaluate the Weather Research and Forecasting (WRF) model against a diffusion-based generative model by downscaling two physically distinct, extreme precipitation events up to lead times of 3 weeks. For a fair comparison with WRF, which can downscale boundary conditions from different driving models without model-specific training, the diffusion model is trained in an unpaired fashion. Both approaches improve upon the raw European Centre for Medium-Range Weather Forecasts forecasts, in comparison to fused rain gauge-radar observations in Switzerland (CombiPrecip), but exhibit regime-dependent strengths. WRF achieves the highest probabilistic skill for a multicell, non-stationary event. Conversely, the diffusion model is more consistent across different performance metrics for the two events, outperforming WRF in a more stationary supercell event. These results demonstrate that explicit dynamical modeling can add value for specific precipitation events for subseasonal lead times, and that generative downscaling adds value more broadly in different situations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Consensus in Effective Model Inference for Disordered Ising Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10731)
*Ashveed Ashraf*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过“共识(consensus)”量化独立学习者在同一无序系统上得到描述的可复现性：在二维随机键Ising模型的教师-学生框架中，共识分布方差可精确分解为有限采样项(下界为Fisher信息倒数)与淬火无序项，二者之和在临界区附近最小，且推断耦合存在不随数据量消失的误设定偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立学习者在同一无序系统的有限、含噪数据上训练时，可能给出不同的物理描述；因此需要量化这种描述的可复现性，并厘清“最一致”与“最忠实”是否同一温度。

Method: 采用教师-学生框架与二维随机键Ising模型：一组学生各自从某一淬火键实现的有穷平衡构型中，推断单一均匀有效耦合；将不同学生与实现的推断耦合聚合为共识分布，并对其方差作精确分解(有限采样项、淬火无序项)，同时分析拟合均匀耦合到异质格点所导致的误设定偏差。

Result: 共识分布方差可精确分为有限采样贡献(下界为Fisher信息倒数)与淬火无序贡献；两者之和在临界区附近最小，故共识宽度在赝临界温度附近出现极小值，并随无序增强而更尖锐。两种极小机制不同：采样项继承Fisher信息的临界峰；无序项在主导阶温度无关，仅通过键无序的线性与三次响应之间的协方差在无序强度四阶才获得临界极小。误设定偏差在无限数据极限仍存在，使均方误差极小值位于共识极小值之上。

Conclusion: 独立学习者一致性最高的温度(共识极小)并不等于其共同答案最忠实的温度(均方误差极小)，因为均匀耦合对异质格点的误设定偏差使两者分离；共识宽度可作为刻画无序系统中学习可复现性的可操作指标。

Abstract: When independent learners are trained on data from the same disordered system, finite and noisy samples can lead them to different descriptions of the underlying physics. We quantify the reproducibility of such descriptions through consensus, the degree of agreement among independently trained models. In a teacher-student framework on the two-dimensional random-bond Ising model, an ensemble of students each infers a single uniform effective coupling from a finite set of equilibrium configurations drawn from one quenched bond realization. Aggregating the inferred couplings across students and realizations yields the consensus distribution, whose width quantifies the agreement among learners. The variance of this distribution separates exactly into a finite-sampling contribution, bounded below by inverse of Fisher information, and a quenched-disorder contribution. Sum of these two variance is minimized near critical region, so the consensus width has a minimum near the pseudocritical temperature that sharpens with increasing disorder. The two minima arise from different mechanisms: the sampling term inherits the critical peak of the Fisher information, while the disorder term is temperature-independent at leading order and acquires its critical minimum only at fourth order in the disorder strength, through the covariance between the linear and cubic responses to the bond disorder. Because the students fit a uniform coupling to a heterogeneous lattice, the inferred coupling also carries a misspecification bias that survives in the infinite-data limit. This bias displaces the minimum of the mean-squared error above the consensus minimum, so the temperature at which independent learners agree most closely is not the temperature at which their shared answer is most faithful.

</details>


### [129] [Universal sampling of spin systems across quenched disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11336)
*Jing Liu,Yeyuan Wu,Ying Tang,Pan Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Transformer编码器-解码器的通用神经变分框架，将变分推断从单个无序实现摊销到整个无序系综，一次训练后可对未见无序实现直接给出玻尔兹曼分布的显式近似，无需逐实例平衡或重训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序系统的统计物理需要在对微观自由度平均之外，再对淬火随机性做第二次更困难的平均。传统解析方法（复本法、空腔法）在平均场或树状结构之外不再受控，而并行回火等数值方法需对每个无序实现独立且昂贵的平衡化。因此需要一种能跨越无序系综、避免逐实例计算的方法。

Method: 构建通用神经变分框架，基于编码器-解码器Transformer架构，在无序系综上摊销推断。训练一次后，对先前未见的无序实现直接输出玻尔兹曼分布的显式近似，无需逐实例马尔可夫链平衡，也无需为每个实例重新训练变分拟设。

Result: 在二维Edwards-Anderson模型上完成验证，并应用于随机键Ising模型，成功捕捉到Nishimori多临界点附近的Binder累积量交叉。

Conclusion: 该工作将变分推断的对象从单一实例转移到无序系综，为逐实例计算不可行的阻挫多体系统开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Statistical physics extracts macroscopic laws by averaging over the many microscopic degrees of freedom of a system. Disordered systems demand a second and far harder average, one over the quenched randomness itself. The classic analytical routes, the replica and cavity methods, become uncontrolled outside mean-field or tree-like limits, and conventional numerical algorithms like parallel tempering require expensive, independent equilibration for every disorder realization. In this work, we introduce a universal neural variational framework that amortizes inference across the disorder ensemble, eliminating both the need for per-instance Markov chain equilibration and the cost of retraining instance-specific variational ansatzes. Built on an encoder-decoder Transformer architecture, after training once, it produces an explicit approximation to the Boltzmann distribution given previously unseen disorder realizations without further optimization. We validate this framework on 2D Edwards-Anderson models, and apply it to the random-bond Ising model, successfully capturing the Binder cumulant crossings near the Nishimori multicritical point. These results shift the object of variational inference from the single instance to the disorder ensemble, opening a route to frustrated many-body systems where instance-by-instance computation is prohibitive.

</details>


### [130] [Information-Theoretic Characterization of Macroscopic Chaos Emerging from the Chemical Master Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11340)
*Kenshin Matsumoto,Shin-ichi Sasa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文从理论上证明，开放化学反应网络中基于两时互信息构建的信息损失率，在确定性极限下可恢复为Kolmogorov-Sinai熵，并通过一个含七反应的三组分系统的马尔可夫跳过程数值模拟加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放化学反应网络在有限系统尺寸下呈现随机浓度动力学，而其宏观极限由可表现混沌的确定性速率方程描述。如何将有限尺寸随机动力学中的信息损失与宏观混沌的确定性熵建立定量联系，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 构建基于两时互信息（two-time mutual information）的信息损失率，并在理论上推导其在确定性极限下与Kolmogorov-Sinai熵的关系；随后针对一个包含七种反应的三组分系统，采用马尔可夫跳过程进行数值模拟验证。

Result: 理论推导表明，由两时互信息构造的信息损失率在确定性极限下可恢复Kolmogorov-Sinai熵；三组分七反应系统的马尔可夫跳过程数值模拟验证了该结果。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸随机化学反应网络中的信息损失率可作为宏观确定性混沌熵的对应量，为连接随机动力学与确定性混沌提供了信息论桥梁。

Abstract: Open chemical reaction networks exhibit stochastic concentration dynamics at finite system sizes, whereas their macroscopic limit is governed by deterministic rate equations that can display chaos. In this Letter, we show theoretically that a rate of information loss constructed from two-time mutual information recovers the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy in the deterministic limit. We verify this result through numerical simulations of a Markov jump process for a three-species system involving seven reactions.

</details>


### [131] [Orientational order on non-orientable domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11367)
*Gianmarco Spera,Axel Fotso Ndefo,Keaton J. Burns,Alexander Mietke*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了非可定向区域上具有取向自由度的被动与主动多体系统的统计性质，提出将拓扑约束表示为可定向双覆盖上的非局域对称关系，揭示非可定向性会消除全局旋转软模并导致拓扑囚禁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究空间拓扑（非可定向性）如何影响具有取向自由度的多体系统统计物理性质，尤其是被动XY模型与主动自驱动粒子/自旋模型的取向涨落与有序行为。

Method: 将拓扑约束重写为可定向双覆盖上的非局域对称关系；分析Klein瓶上的被动XY模型、主动自驱动粒子模型及主动Ising自旋模型。

Result: 被动XY模型中，Klein瓶上的取向涨落由环面上的常规扩散行为饱和为有限值，并在热力学极限下精确计算；主动粒子模型中拓扑囚禁在相互作用邻域连续变化时仍持续存在；主动Ising模型中非可定向性强制有序反平行畴共存，且全局极化序消失。

Conclusion: 非可定向性并非等效于外场，而是通过消除全局旋转软模导致拓扑囚禁与特殊有序共存态，为拓扑约束调控多体系统统计行为提供了新机制。

Abstract: We study the statistical properties of passive and active many-body systems with orientational degrees of freedom on non-orientable domains. By rephrasing topological constraints as non-local symmetry relations on an orientable double-cover, we show that non-orientability eliminates global rotational soft modes without acting like an external field. In a passive XY model, this results in topological caging, where orientational fluctuations that exhibit conventional diffusive behavior on a torus saturate on a Klein bottle to a finite value that we compute exactly in the thermodynamic limit. In models of active self-propelled particles with orientational degrees of freedom, topological caging persists despite continuously changing interaction neighborhoods. In an active Ising spin model, non-orientability enforces the coexistence of ordered anti-parallel domains with vanishing global polar order, a state that is absent on orientable domains.

</details>


### [132] [Disordered ground states in one-dimensional exactly solvable fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11394)
*Igor Travěnec,Ladislav Šamaj*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究有限程软排斥势一维硬核流体，在等温等压系综中对最近邻情形给出精确解，刻画了T→0基态的物态方程和配对关联函数，并揭示凹势下存在不可压缩压力p_i及非超均匀无序基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维流体因忽略长程关联而常可获得精确解，是理解液体内在结构的理想模型。作者希望借助可解模型，考察软排斥势形状（凹性）如何影响零温基态的粒子排列、超均匀性以及低温热力学与关联性质。

Method: 采用直径a的经典硬核粒子，配对作用势为有限程单调递减的软排斥势φ(x)=ε[(a'-x)/(a'-a)]^{1/ν}（a≤x≤a'，ε、ν>0），在等温等压系综中研究。当a'≤2a时相互作用退化为最近邻作用，从而可精确求解热平衡。重点分析T→0极限，推导最近邻平均间距l0的物态方程与配对关联函数g0(x)，并按势的凹性（ν≥1）与严格凹性（ν>1）分别讨论，同时计算等温磁化率以判断是否超均匀。

Result: 当φ为凹势（ν≥1）时，存在不可压缩压力p_i=ε/(a'-a)：在0<p<p_i时基态为间距l0=a'的等距链，在p>p_i时间距为l0=a。对ν>1（严格凹），p=p_i处基态为无序态，l0=[νa+(ν-1)a']/(2ν-1)，g0(x)为若干离散位置处加权Dirac δ函数的叠加；对ν=1（线性斜坡），p=p_i处基态无序，l0=(a+a')/2，g0(x)为Heaviside阶跃函数乘以x的多项式叠加（连续形式）。T=0时凹势在p=p_i处的等温磁化率非零，因此相应的无序基态是非超均匀的，具有类似非零温无序流体的特征。此外，仅在单一压力p_i出现的无序基态配对关联函数，其预测能力可延伸至p_i附近的较宽压力范围的非零温热力学态。

Conclusion: 该一维可解模型表明，软排斥势的凹性是决定零温基态结构的关键：凹势产生不可压缩压力p_i，并在该压力下形成非超均匀的无序基态；严格凹与线性斜坡对应不同形式的配对关联函数（离散δ叠加与连续阶跃-多项式叠加）。这些仅在p_i处出现的无序基态关联函数仍能有效描述p_i附近有限温度下的热力学行为，凸显了零温奇异点对低温物性的广泛影响。

Abstract: One-dimensional fluids of classical hard-core particles of diameter $a$, interacting in pairs via a soft repulsive (monotonically decreasing) potential of finite range $\varphi(x)=\varepsilon \left[ (a'-x)/(a'-a)\right]^{1/ν}$ $(a\le x\le a')$ with real positive parameters $\varepsilon$ and $ν$, are studied in an isothermal-isobaric ensemble. If $a'\le 2a$, the pairwise interactions are reduced to nearest-neighbour interactions, which allows for an exact solution of the thermal equilibrium. We focus on the $T\to 0$ ground states, specifically on the equation of state for the mean distance between nearest neighbours $l_0$ and the pair correlation function $g_0(x)$. If $\varphi(x)$ is concave $(ν\ge 1)$, there exists an ``incompressibility'' pressure $p_i=\varepsilon/(a'-a)$ such that the ground state is an equidistant chain of particles with spacing $l_0=a'$ for $0<p<p_i$ and with spacing $l_0=a$ for $p>p_i$. If $ν>1$ (strict concavity), the ground state at $p=p_i$ is disordered with $l_0=\left[ νa +(ν-1)a'\right]/(2ν-1)$ and $g_0(x)$ being a superposition of weighted Dirac delta functions over discrete positions. If $ν=1$ (linear ramp), the ground state at $p=p_i$ is disordered with $l_0=(a+a')/2$ and the continuous $g_0(x)$ is a superposition of Heaviside step functions multiplied by polynomials in $x$. The isothermal susceptibility at $T=0$ is nonzero for concave $\varphi(x)$ ($ν\ge 1$) at $p=p_i$ and, therefore, the corresponding disordered ground states are non-hyperuniform, i.e., they resemble disordered fluids at nonzero temperatures. It turns out that pair correlation functions of disordered ground states, which occur only at a single pressure $p_i$, extend their predictive power to thermodynamic states at nonzero temperatures over a wider range of pressures around $p_i$.

</details>


### [133] [Rare-History Transitions in Temporally Random Integrable Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11415)
*Tingfei Li,Jie Gu*
