Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究时间随机可积量子电路中的电流涨落，利用可积性将退火涨落约化为电流增益与稀有层组成大偏差成本之间的竞争，预测主导历史类别之间的一阶切换及其圆滑化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时间随机性如何影响可积量子电路中的输运涨落，并探索时间随机性是否可在轨迹空间中充当类似序参量的坐标。

Method: 利用可积性带来的交换性，将每个驱动历史精确约化为其层组成；对固定层组成，结合均匀热力学Bethe拟设缀饰与弹道涨落理论，得到条件电流统计；再通过退火大偏差收缩分析主导历史类别。

Result: 退火大偏差收缩预测两类主导历史之间出现一阶切换，终止于一条规则尖点端点线；有限时间分析表明该切换会被圆滑化。

Conclusion: 时间随机性可作为轨迹空间中涌现的类似序参量的坐标，从而在可积量子电路中产生由层组成大偏差主导的电流统计切换。

Abstract: We study current fluctuations in a temporally random integrable quantum circuit. Commutativity reduces every drive history exactly to its layer composition, turning annealed fluctuations into a competition between current gain and the large-deviation cost of rare compositions. For each fixed composition, homogeneous thermodynamic Bethe ansatz dressing supplemented by ballistic fluctuation theory yields the conditional current statistics. Their annealed large-deviation contraction predicts a first-order switch between two dominant history classes, terminating on a line of regular cusp endpoints. Finite-time analysis shows how the switch is rounded. Thus temporal randomness can act as an emergent order-parameter-like coordinate in trajectory space.

</details>


### [134] [Work as a function of protocol duration for the efficient erasure of an underdamped memory: isothermal to adiabatic transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11473)
*Nicolas Barros,Stephen Whitelam,Sergio Ciliberto,Ludovic Bellon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 利用演化强化学习为欠阻尼双势阱悬臂（1比特存储器）寻找高效含时擦除协议，发现平均功随协议时长τ呈现两个标度区制，并证明学习协议可优于满足平衡边界条件的最优协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究1比特存储器在欠阻尼动力学下擦除所需平均功随协议时长的标度规律，厘清惯性与欠阻尼效应对擦除热力学代价（Landauer极限）的影响。

Method: 采用演化强化学习优化含时的擦除协议；以双势阱势中的欠阻尼悬臂作为1比特存储器的实验化模型；通过对比系统弛豫时间t_r与协议时长τ，分析准静态等温区制与绝热区制的功标度，并借助一个理想的最优协议（gedanken optimal protocol）推导下界；最后与过阻尼擦除情形对比。

Result: 当τ≫t_r（准静态等温区制）时，恢复Landauer界，并叠加一个按1/τ标度的额外开销，与过阻尼情形相似；当τ<t_r时擦除变为绝热，⟨W⟩增长慢于等温情形，且其增长存在1/τ形式的下界，该下界由理想最优协议推导得到。与过阻尼擦除的对比表明，学习得到的协议可优于在平衡边界条件约束下的最优协议。

Conclusion: 欠阻尼1比特存储器的擦除功存在等温与绝热两种标度区制，分别由Landauer界加1/τ开销及更缓慢的1/τ下界增长所刻画；演化强化学习所得协议能突破平衡边界条件限制下最优协议的表现，显示出非平衡优化在擦除任务中的优势。

Abstract: We use evolutionary reinforcement learning to determine efficient time-dependent erasure protocols for an underdamped cantilever moving in a double-well potential, an experimental realization of a 1-bit memory. We investigate how the mean work $\langle W \rangle $ needed to erase a bit scales as a function of the protocol duration $τ$. We find two regimes, depending on how $τ$ compares to the relaxation time of the system $t_r$. For $τ\gg t_r$, the quasistatic isothermal regime, we recover Landauer's bound plus an overhead that scales as $1/τ$, similar to the overdamped case. By contrast, for $τ<t_r$ erasure becomes adiabatic and $\langle W \rangle$ grows more slowly than in the isothermal case. This growth is bounded from below as $1/τ$, which we derive using a gedanken optimal protocol. Finally, comparison with overdamped erasure shows that learned protocols can outperform protocols that are optimal subject to equilibrium boundary conditions.

</details>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Confined elastic wires of large length: the sharp second-order asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10605)
*Luca Mugnai*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究了单位圆盘内给定长度闭合嵌入线的极小弯曲能量，证明 $m_L = L + c_1 L^{4/9} + O(L^{1/3})$，给出尖锐常数并首次给出非平凡下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆盘内任何闭合曲线的弯曲能量至少等于其长度，等号仅由多重覆盖的单位圆取得。但单个嵌入环无法达到该能量极限，由此产生的缺陷 $m_L-L$ 的大小此前一直未知。

Method: 通过两个一维模型问题确定尖锐常数，并对 $L\to\infty$ 进行渐近分析，结合上下界估计。

Result: 得到 $m_L = L + c_1 L^{4/9} + O(L^{1/3})$，其中尖锐常数 $c_1=5.223049\ldots$ 由两个一维模型问题以闭式给出；缺陷满足 $m_L-L\asymp L^{4/9}$。

Conclusion: 该结果将此前已知的 $O(\sqrt{L})$ 上界改进为 $O(L^{4/9})$ 量级，并给出首个超出平凡估计的下界。

Abstract: We study the least bending energy $m_L$ of a closed embedded wire of prescribed length $L$ confined to the closed unit disk. It is classical that the bending energy of any closed curve in the disk is at least its length, with equality only for multiply covered unit circles. Such a circle is the energy limit of a stack of disjoint embedded circles, but not of a single embedded loop, and the size of the resulting defect $m_L-L$ has remained open. We prove that \[
  m_L \;=\; L+c_1\,L^{4/9}+O\bigl(L^{1/3}\bigr)\qquad\text{as }L\to\infty , \] with a sharp constant $c_1=5.223049\ldots$ given in closed form by two one-dimensional model problems. In particular $m_L-L\asymp L^{4/9}$, which improves the previously known upper bound of order $\sqrt{L}$ and provides the first lower bound beyond the trivial one.

</details>


### [136] [Sharp Lifespan Estimates for a Semilinear Wave Equation with Nonlinear Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10819)
*Firas Kaabi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究半线性波动方程 $u_{tt}-\Delta u=u|u|^{p-1}-u_t|u_t|^{q-1}$ 在Dirichlet边界条件与初始数据 $(\varrho f,\varrho g)$ 下解的最大存在时间 $T^*(\varrho)$，证明在 $1<q<p$、$\varrho$ 充分大时 $T^*(\varrho)\asymp\varrho^{-\vartheta(p,q)}$，其中 $\vartheta(p,q)=\min\{(p-1)/2,(p-q)/q\}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的凹性方法只给出上界 $T^*(\varrho)\leq C\varrho^{-(p-1)/2}$（当 $q\leq 2p/(p+1)$）或 $T^*(\varrho)\leq C\varrho^{-(p-q)/q}$（当 $q>2p/(p+1)$），而能量方法仅能给出阶为 $\varrho^{1-p}$ 的下界，两者指数不匹配。本文旨在填补这一空隙，证明与大振幅上界同指数的下界，从而得到最大存在时间关于振幅的精确幂律。

Method: 关键工具是双曲重标度：将大振幅转化为区域的伸缩，并在阻尼项前引入系数 $\varrho^{(q(p+1)-2p)/2}$；建立统一局部能量范数下的局部存在性理论，使其存在时间不依赖该系数；当该系数较大时，定量利用耗散项。阈值 $2p/(p+1)$ 正是阻尼项在该重标度下不变时对应的 $q$ 值。

Result: 对于非平凡 $f$ 与充分大的 $\varrho$，证明了与上界同指数的下界，使得 $T^*(\varrho)\asymp\varrho^{-\vartheta(p,q)}$，其中 $\vartheta(p,q)=\min\{(p-1)/2,(p-q)/q\}$。

Conclusion: 大振幅下最大存在时间的两个指数区间由 $q=2p/(p+1)$ 分隔：该阈值以下指数为 $(p-1)/2$，以上为 $(p-q)/q$。所得结果在指数层面是尖锐的，但常数未作优化。

Abstract: We study the maximal existence time $T^{*}(\varrho)$ of the solution of the semilinear wave equation $u_{tt}-Δu=u|u|^{p-1}-u_{t}|u_{t}|^{q-1}$ in a bounded domain, with Dirichlet boundary condition and initial data $(\varrho f,\varrho g)$, where $1<q<p$ and the amplitude $\varrho$ is large. For nontrivial $f$ and sufficiently large $\varrho$, the concavity method gives $T^{*}(\varrho)\leq C\varrho^{-(p-1)/2}$ for $q\leq2p/(p+1)$ and $T^{*}(\varrho)\leq C\varrho^{-(p-q)/q}$ for $q>2p/(p+1)$, whereas the energy method gives a lower bound of order $\varrho^{1-p}$ only. We prove lower bounds with the same exponents as the upper ones, so that $T^{*}(\varrho)\asymp\varrho^{-\vartheta(p,q)}$ with $\vartheta(p,q)=\min\{(p-1)/2,\,(p-q)/q\}$. The proof rests on a hyperbolic rescaling which converts the large amplitude into a dilation of the domain and a coefficient $\varrho^{(q(p+1)-2p)/2}$ in front of the damping term, on a local existence theory in uniformly local energy norms whose existence time does not depend on that coefficient, and on a quantitative use of the dissipation when the coefficient is large. The threshold $2p/(p+1)$ is the value of $q$ at which the damping term is invariant under the rescaling. No attempt is made to optimize the constants: sharpness is meant throughout at the level of the exponent.

</details>


### [137] [Heat equation with singular drift which has bounded solution discontinuous at an interior point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10828)
*N. V. Krylov*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文构造了一维带奇异漂移的热方程，其Cauchy问题在光滑初值下解有界但在所有解共有的内点处不连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究奇异漂移项对抛物型方程正则性的影响，寻找光滑初值下仍产生内部不连续性的反例。

Method: 构造一维热方程，引入奇异漂移项，分析Cauchy问题解的适定性与正则性。

Result: 该方程在光滑初值下解保持有界，但在某个固定内点处不连续，且该不连续点为所有解共有。

Conclusion: 奇异漂移可破坏解的连续性，即使在光滑初值条件下，表明此类方程的正则性理论存在本质限制。

Abstract: The goal of this paper is to present a one dimensional heat equation with a singular drift for which solutions of the Cauchy problem with smooth initial data are bounded and are discontinuous at an interior point (common to all solutions).

</details>


### [138] [Modified wave operators for nonlinear Schrödinger equations in the full subcritical long range regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11095)
*Jia Shen,Yifei Wu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在亚临界长程情形 $0<p<2/d$ 下，为非线性薛定谔方程构造修正波算子，统一处理 $1/d<p<2/d$ 与 $p\le 1/d$ 两种情形，并给出定量 $L^q$ 渐近与唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅对特定初值类建立大时间渐近，且 $p<1/d$ 时的精确渐近展开未知；文章旨在填补全亚临界长程范围的空白。

Method: 对 $1/d<p<2/d$，在解析空间中用半径递减的迭代构造波算子；对 $p\le 1/d$，由 Fuchsian 方程与输运方程耦合的有限截断构造 profile，并引入非线性末态正规形消除长程三角耦合，配合混合迭代。

Result: $1/d<p<2/d$ 时得到显式渐近公式；$p\le 1/d$ 时给出 profile 及 $2\le q\le\infty$ 的定量 $L^q$ 渐近，并在给定解析渐近类中获得唯一性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次覆盖完整亚临界长程区间，通过非线性末态正规形与混合迭代解决了终端积分不保持快衰减类的困难。

Abstract: We construct modified wave operators for the nonlinear Schrödinger equation $i\partial_tu+\frac12Δu=|u|^pu$ in the full subcritical long-range case $0<p<2/d$, with small, nonvanishing, analytic final data $W(x)$ with bounded logarithmic gradients. Previous results established large-time asymptotics for selected classes of Cauchy data. Moreover, the exact asymptotic expansion for $p<1/d$ remained unknown.
  When $1/d<p<2/d$, our result gives the approximation $$\frac{1}{(it)^{\frac{d}{2}}} e^{\frac{i|x|^2}{2t}} W\left(\frac{x}{t}\right) \exp\left[ -i\frac{t^{1-\frac{dp}{2}}-1}{1-\frac{dp}{2}} \left|W\left(\frac{x}{t}\right)\right|^p \right]$$ The wave operator is constructed by an iteration in the analytic spaces with decreasing radius.
  When $p\le 1/d$, we construct the profile from a finite truncation of a Fuchsian equation coupled with a transport equation. This profile still leaves a long-range triangular coupling whose terminal integral does not preserve the required fast decay class. The construction yields quantitative $L^q$ asymptotics for $2\le q\le\infty$ and uniqueness in the prescribed analytic asymptotic classes. The central new ingredients are a nonlinear final-state normal form that removes this long-range coupling and a mixed iteration in particular analytic spaces.

</details>


### [139] [Fractional Hardy--Maz'ya inequality on a half-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10832)
*Michał Kijaczko,Antoni Szczukiewicz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在半空间上建立了Maz'ya不等式的分数阶版本，并将结果推广至Sobolev–Bregman形式情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Maz'ya不等式在半空间上刻画了梯度积分与边界（及角点）奇异权积分之间的下界关系。本文旨在给出该不等式的分数阶对应物，即用Gagliardo型半范数（分数阶Sobolev半范数）替代梯度项，从而拓展其适用范围并揭示边界与角点奇性。

Method: 以半空间R^d_+上的Gagliardo型双积分（分数阶半范数）为左端，构造右端含两项奇异权积分的下界估计：第一项为分数阶Hardy型边界权x_d^{-sp}，最优常数取为半空间分数阶Hardy不等式的最优常数D_{d,s,p}；第二项为刻画角点奇性的权x_d^{τ-sp}(x_{d-1}^2+x_d^2)^{-τ/2}，系数为C_{d,s,p,τ}。进一步将该框架推广至Sobolev–Bregman形式的设定。

Result: 证明了对任意u成立的双权分数阶不等式，右端第一项系数为半空间分数阶Hardy不等式的最优常数，第二项给出角点奇性的额外增益项；并在Sobolev–Bregman形式下获得类似结论。

Conclusion: 所提不等式是经典Maz'ya不等式在半空间上的分数阶推广，且对分数阶Sobolev–Bregman形式同样成立。

Abstract: The main purpose of this article is to provide a fractional counterpart of the well-known Maz'ya inequality on the half-space, that is $$ \int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}_{+}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}_{+}}\frac{|u(x)-u(y)|^p}{|x-y|^{d+sp}}dy\,dx\ge\mathcal{D}_{d,s,p}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}_{+}}\frac{|u(x)|^p}{x_d^{sp}}dx+C_{d,s,p,τ}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}_{+}}\frac{|u(x)|^p}{x_{d}^{sp-τ}\left(x_{d-1}^2+x_d^2\right)^{τ/2}}dx, $$ where $\mathcal{D}_{d,s,p}$ stands for the sharp constant in the fractional Hardy inequality on a half-space $\mathbb{R}^{d}_{+}$. We also obtain a similar result in the setting of Sobolev--Bregman forms.

</details>


### [140] [Diffeomorphism Invariance of the Favre-Filtered Compressible Navier--Stokes System under Spacetime Dilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11566)
*Yuanya Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明Favre滤波可压缩Navier-Stokes方程组（含亚格子闭合项）在坐标(Lt,Lx)与(t,x)之间的变换由时空流形上的光滑微分同胚实现，并由此得出所有L>0参数下的解空间典范同构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清滤波后可压缩Navier-Stokes方程组在不同尺度坐标（(Lt,Lx)与标准坐标(t,x)）下表述的数学关系，说明参数L>0所诱导的方程组族是否仅是同一几何对象在微分同胚下的不同表示，从而为解空间的等价性提供严格依据。

Method: 采用无穷维流形与Fréchet流形框架处理光滑场构型空间；定义单参数伸缩群{φ_L}并验证其为微分同胚，计算其切与余切作用；证明构型丛截面上的拉回作用与全导数算子交换，从而φ_L的一阶jet延拓将方程子流形映到自身；再逐方程（连续性、动量、能量）验证残差。

Result: 每个方程的残差在变换坐标下均被同一非零因子L^{-1}相乘（或等价地恒为零），方程子流形在φ_L的一阶jet延拓下保持不变，故解集被保持；参数L>0的方程组族满足微分同胚变换式。

Conclusion: 该系统族在坐标伸缩变换下具有微分同胚不变性，所有L>0对应的解空间彼此典范同构。

Abstract: We prove that the system of Favre-filtered compressible Navier--Stokes equations with subgrid-scale closure terms, written in the coordinates $(Lt,Lx_1,Lx_2,Lx_3)$ for an arbitrary parameter $L>0$, is obtained from the system written in standard coordinates $(t,x_1,x_2,x_3)$ by the action of a smooth diffeomorphism of the underlying spacetime manifold. The proof begins with a self-contained treatment of infinite-dimensional manifolds and the Fréchet manifold of smooth field configurations. We then define the one-parameter dilation group $\{φ_L\}_{L>0}$, verify that each $φ_L$ is a diffeomorphism, and compute its tangent and cotangent actions. The induced pullback action on sections of the configuration bundle is shown to commute with the total derivative operator, which implies that the first-jet prolongation of $φ_L$ maps the equation submanifold of the system onto itself. Equation-by-equation verification---continuity, momentum, and energy---confirms that every residual is multiplied by the common nonzero factor $L^{-1}$ (or, equivalently, is identically zero in the transformed coordinates), so that the solution set is preserved. We conclude that the family of systems parameterised by $L>0$ satisfies the diffeomorphism transformation expression and that solution spaces are canonically isomorphic for all $L$.

</details>


### [141] [Stability Framework for the Singularity of the Euler Equations on $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10860)
*Valentin Duruisseaux,Adarsh Ganeshram,Robert J. George,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为Euler方程在R^3上高精度奇异自相似剖面建立非线性稳定性证明框架，将分析归结为有限个显式估计与可计算常数的验证，条件性完成稳定性证明并重构有限时间爆破解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随的数值研究发现了Euler方程在无界域R^3上的高精度奇异剖面，但缺乏严格的理论稳定性证明；本文旨在补充该研究，建立证明非线性稳定性的系统框架。

Method: 建立证明近似自相似剖面（非线性）稳定性的框架，将分析归结为大量但有限的显式估计和可计算常数的验证。

Result: 在估计与常数获得严格验证的条件下，框架可闭合稳定性证明，并能将稳定的重标度剖面重构为原始变量中有限物理时间内变奇异的可容许解。

Conclusion: 稳定性与重构机制建立后，剩余工作主要是定量性的：验证显式常数并在必要时收紧部分解析估计以完成稳定性证明。

Abstract: In a companion numerical study, we found a high-precision singular profile for the Euler equations on the unbounded domain $\mathbb{R}^3$. The present manuscript complements that study by establishing in detail a framework for proving (nonlinear) stability of the approximate self-similar profile, reducing the analysis to a large but finite collection of explicit estimates and computable constants. Conditional on rigorous certification of the estimates and constants appearing in the argument, the framework closes the stability proof and, crucially, allows the stable rescaled profile to be reconstructed as an admissible solution in the original variables that becomes singular in finite physical time. With the stability and reconstruction mechanisms established, the remaining work is largely quantitative: certifying the explicit constants and, where necessary, sharpening selected analytic estimates to close the stability proof.

</details>


### [142] [Stable Singularity of the Euler Equations on $\mathbb{R}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10867)
*Adarsh Ganeshram,Valentin Duruisseaux,Anima Anandkumar*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用物理信息神经网络(PINN)结合自相似假设，在无界域上找到了3D Euler方程临界爆破率0.5下的近似奇点剖面，并用样条表示加以验证，进而给出非线性稳定性证明框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Euler方程是否存在有限时间奇点（爆破）是流体力学与偏微分方程领域的核心未解难题，缺乏可信的数值或解析证据。作者希望借助机器学习方法构造出稳定奇点的可信证据。

Method: 采用带自相似假设的物理信息神经网络(PINN)求解Euler系统，得到临界爆破率0.5下的近似自相似剖面，并用样条表示进行认证；通过分析输运场证明线性阻尼在整个域上成立；建立将非线性稳定性归结为有限个显式估计与可计算常数的证明框架。

Result: 获得临界爆破率0.5下的近似奇异剖面，样条认证支持其有效性；输运场分析表明全域可建立线性阻尼，为候选剖面的整体稳定性提供强证据；同时给出完整的非线性稳定性证明框架，将分析约化为大量显式估计与可计算常数。

Conclusion: 该工作为3D Euler方程有限时间奇点的存在提供了强有力证据，并搭建了从近似剖面到严格非线性稳定性的桥梁，但仍需完成有限估计的验证以形成完整证明。

Abstract: We provide evidence of a stable finite-time singularity in the 3D Euler equations on the unbounded domain. Using a physics-informed neural network (PINN) with a self-similar ansatz, we find an approximate singular profile for the Euler system at the critical blowup rate of $0.5$ and certify it using a spline representation. The transport field associated with the obtained profile suggests that linear damping can be established throughout the domain, providing strong evidence for the overall stability of the candidate profile. We also establish a complete framework for proving nonlinear stability of the approximate self-similar profile, reducing the analysis to a large but finite collection of explicit estimates and computable constants.

</details>


### [143] [A $p$-Laplacian Schrödinger--Maxwell system with rapidly growing nonlinearities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10878)
*Genival da Silva*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究拟线性 Schrödinger–Maxwell 系统在 p-Laplace 算子下的可解性，在对数型可积条件下证明有限能量弱解存在，且该解为关联泛函的鞍点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受指数型非线性情形（\cite{BO2026}）工作的启发，将结论推广到一般 p-Laplace（1&lt;p&lt;∞）拟线性 Schrödinger–Maxwell 系统，处理具有一般凸偶非线性的耦合问题。

Method: 在 Ω⊂R^N（N≥2）有界域上考虑由 -Δ_p u 与 -Δ_p ψ 耦合的系统，G∈C^1(R) 为偶、凸、非负、非平凡且 G(0)=0，满足增长约束 G(t)≲1+|G'(t)|；对源项 f 假设 |f|ln(1+|f|)∈L^1(Ω)，据此构造并分析变分框架。

Result: 在 |f|ln(1+|f|)∈L^1(Ω) 下证明了有限能量弱解的存在性，并进一步证明该解是相应能量泛函的鞍点。

Conclusion: 表明该对数型可积性条件是保证 p-Laplace Schrödinger–Maxwell 系统弱解存在及其鞍点结构的充分条件，推广了指数情形的相应结果。

Abstract: Let $Ω\subset\R^N$, $N\ge2$, be a bounded domain and let $1<p<\infty$. Inspired by the exponential case treated in \cite{BO2026}, we study the quasilinear Schrödinger--Maxwell system \[ \begin{cases} -Δ_p u+ψG'(u)=f &\text{in }Ω,\\ -Δ_pψ=G(u) &\text{in }Ω,\\ u=ψ=0 &\text{on }\partialΩ, \end{cases} \] where $G\in C^1(\R)$ is even, convex, nonnegative and nontrivial, with $G(0)=0$, and satisfies \[ G(t)\lesssim 1+|G'(t)| \qquad\text{for large }|t|. \] Under the assumption \[ |f|\ln(1+|f|)\in L^1(Ω), \] we prove the existence of a finite-energy weak solution and show that the solution is a saddle point of the associated functional.

</details>


### [144] [Well-posedness of the two-dimensional unsteady Prandtl system in Sobolev space with degenerate critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10936)
*Shi-Yong Zhu,Ya-Guang Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究有限阶Sobolev空间中经典Prandtl方程的适定性，在初始数据含退化临界点且具有一般出流条件下，通过引入新的迭代格式与线性消去，证明了局部适定性，表明Oleinik单调性条件并非必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prandtl方程在Sobolev空间中的适定性通常依赖Oleinik单调性条件，该条件是否为必要条件尚不明确。作者希望放宽单调性假设，允许初始数据存在退化临界点，以探索更一般的适定性机制。

Method: 针对具有退化临界点和一般出流的初始数据，引入新的迭代格式（iteration scheme）并结合线性消去（linear cancelation）技术，在有限阶Sobolev空间中建立经典Prandtl方程解的构造。

Result: 证明了Prandtl方程在有限阶Sobolev空间中的局部时间存在性与唯一性，且不要求Oleinik单调性条件成立。

Conclusion: Oleinik单调性条件不是Prandtl方程在Sobolev空间中适定性的必要条件；同时为零剪应力不一定导致二维非定常边界层分离提供了证据。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the well-posedness of classical Prandtl equations in a finite order Sobolev space. For a initial data with degenerate critical points and general outflow, we obtain the local-in-time existence and uniqueness of the solution to the Prandtl equations in a Sobolev space, by introducing a new iteration scheme and linear cancelation. This result shows that Oleinik's monotonicity condition is not a necessary condition for the Prandtl equations to be well-posed in Sobolev spaces and provides evidence to demonstrate that zero shear stress does not necessarily lead to boundary layer separation in two-dimensional unsteady boundary layers.

</details>


### [145] [Global existence and time decay for a bipolar Euler-Poisson system with one pressureless and undamped fluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10959)
*Young-Pil Choi,Houzhi Tang,Weiyuan Zou*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究三维双极Euler–Poisson系统（一种流体无压力无阻尼、另一种带动量松弛），在无旋假设与小光滑扰动下证明光滑解整体存在唯一性及代数衰减估计，克服高频退化导致的regularity-loss结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双极Euler–Poisson系统中若同一流体同时缺失压力与阻尼，其速度仅通过Poisson耦合间接耗散，高频退化严重，形成regularity-loss，此前对该非对称情形的整体小数据理论尚不清楚。

Method: 结合精细Green函数估计、低–中–高频分解，以及适应非对称正则性层级的高阶非线性能量估计；对无压流体初速度施加无旋假设。

Result: 对常数平衡态的小光滑扰动，证明光滑解的整体存在性与唯一性，并获得代数时间衰减估计。

Conclusion: 建立了该非对称情形（同一流体同时无压力与阻尼）的整体小数据理论。

Abstract: We study the Cauchy problem for a three-dimensional bipolar Euler--Poisson system in which one fluid is pressureless and undamped, while the other is subject to momentum relaxation. For sufficiently small smooth perturbations of a constant equilibrium, we prove the global existence and uniqueness of smooth solutions under an irrotationality assumption on the initial velocity of the pressureless fluid, together with algebraic time-decay estimates. The main difficulty is that the velocity of the pressureless fluid is dissipated only indirectly through the Poisson coupling, and this mechanism degenerates strongly at high frequencies, leading to a regularity-loss structure. We overcome this difficulty by combining refined Green-function estimates, a low--middle--high frequency decomposition, and high-order nonlinear energy estimates adapted to the asymmetric regularity hierarchy. The result establishes a global small-data theory for this asymmetric regime, in which pressure and damping are simultaneously absent from the same fluid.

</details>


### [146] [Boundary layer of 2D Chemotaxis Navier-Stokes equations with logarithmic Sensitivity. II. viscous vanishing limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11027)
*Hui Wang,Lingling Zhao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是两部分工作的第二部分，研究二维半空间中趋化-纳维-斯托克斯耦合系统边界层解在滑移边界条件下随化学扩散-黏性参数 ε→0 的收敛性，证明 ε>0 的边界层解收敛到外解（ε=0）与内层解的叠加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一部分的遗留问题：需要证明边界层解关于参数 ε 的收敛性，并建立耦合趋化-流体方程在余法向 Sobolev 空间中的适定性。该研究有助于理解实验中观察到的好氧细菌向水-气表面趋化迁移现象，并丰富趋化流体模型边界层的理论结果。

Method: 以二维半平面上的奇异趋化-流体方程为对象，在滑移边界条件下，通过构造外解（ε=0）与内层解并分析其叠加，刻画 ε>0 时边界层解随 ε→0 的渐近结构；外解与内层剖面沿用第一部分的显式推导；并在附录中于余法向 Sobolev 空间框架下证明耦合趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统的适定性。

Result: 证明了当 ε→0 时，ε>0 的边界层解收敛到外解与内层解的叠加；给出了外解与内层剖面的显式表达；在附录中建立了耦合趋化-纳维-斯托克斯系统在余法向 Sobolev 空间中的适定性结果，回答了第一部分提出的问题。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了两部分研究中对边界层收敛性的刻画，揭示趋化流体边界层的黏性-扩散渐近结构，为理解细菌向水-气表面的趋化运动提供了理论支撑，并丰富了趋化流体模型边界层理论。

Abstract: This is the second part of a two-part work concerning boundary layer solutions to the coupled Chemotaxis-Navier-Stokes system in the two-dimensional half-space. In the present work, we address the convergence of boundary layer solutions to singular chemotaxis-fluid equations under slip boundary conditions with respect to the chemical diffusion-viscosity parameter $\varepsilon$ in the two-dimensional half-plane. More precisely, we show that the boundary layer for $\varepsilon>0$ (viscous convection coefficient) converges to the superposition of the outer layer (solution with $\varepsilon=0$) and the inner layer as $\varepsilon\rightarrow0$. The outer and inner profiles are explicitly derived as in the first part\cite{WWZ}. Furthermore, the well-posedness results of the coupled Chemotaxis-Navier-Stokes system in conormal Sobolev spaces will be presented in Appendix. They answer the question mentioned in the first part of the two-part work. This study could help the understanding of the chemotactic movement of aerobic bacteria to the water-air surface observed experimentally in fluids, and enrich the theoretical results of boundary layer in chemotactic fluid models.

</details>


### [147] [Viscosity and variational approaches in free boundary problems: A Volume in Honor of Sandro Salsa](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11205)
*Daniela De Silva,Fausto Ferrari*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文是献给Sandro Salsa 75岁诞辰的论文集编者按，回顾了自由边界问题（FBP）的研究进展，并介绍了2024年Palermo AMS-UMI联合会议上的自由边界专题会议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为庆祝自由边界问题领域的发展并致敬Sandro Salsa的开创性贡献，在Palermo（2024）AMS-UMI联合会议上组织了自由边界问题专题会议，并编辑此卷文集。

Method: 编者按性质的综述性介绍：概述自由边界问题的定义与应用，梳理经典例子（Bernoulli问题、障碍问题、Stefan问题）及变分与几何方法的发展脉络，特别是Alt、Caffarelli及其合作者提出的单调性公式与粘性解方法，以及向非线性算子、非齐次问题、低维情形（如thin Bernoulli问题）和演化问题（如Hele-Shaw流）的推广。

Result: 本文为编者按，无具体研究结果；其内容为总结自由边界问题领域的发展历程及研讨会背景。

Conclusion: 自由边界问题在变分与几何方法的推动下取得显著进展，涵盖从经典问题到非线性、低维及演化问题的广泛推广；本卷文集旨在致敬Sandro Salsa的贡献并汇集相关研究成果。

Abstract: Free boundary problems (FBPs) are differential equations where the domain depends on the solution, with applications in diverse fields such as flame propagation, financial mathematics, and tumor growth. Classical examples include the Bernoulli problem, the obstacle problem, and the Stefan problem. The field has advanced significantly through variational and geometric approaches, notably by Alt, Caffarelli, and collaborators, who developed monotonicity formulas and viscosity solutions to address free boundary regularity. Extensions have included nonlinear operators, inhomogeneous problems, and lower-dimensional cases like the thin Bernoulli problem. Evolution problems, such as the Hele-Shaw flow, also connect to FBPs. To celebrate these developments and honor Sandro Salsa's pioneering contributions, a special session on FBPs was held at the AMS-UMI joint meeting in Palermo (2024). This is the editorial of a volume dedicated to Sandro Salsa on the occasion of his 75th birthday.

</details>


### [148] [A Sharp Mass Threshold for Fractional Choquard Equations with Lower Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev and \(L^2\)-Critical Terms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11284)
*Yongpeng Chen,Zhipeng Yang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究分数阶Choquard方程在给定质量约束及下临界Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev与L²临界指数下的变分问题，给出临界质量a_*并刻画a≤a_*与a>a_*两种情形下的能量下确界与解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶Choquard方程在质量约束（归一化解）框架下的临界情形研究尚不完整，尤其涉及下临界HLS与L²指数时，临界质量的确定及能量下确界是否可达需要厘清。

Method: 基于质量约束变分方法，利用sharp Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev不等式与Choquard Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式确定显式临界质量a_*，并对约束能量泛函的下确界与Pohozaev集进行分析。

Result: 临界质量a_*由sharp HLS与Choquard Gagliardo-Nirenberg不等式显式给出；当0<a≤a_*时，约束能量的下确界被精确计算，且证明该下确界不可达、不存在归一化解；当a>a_*时，能量在质量球上无下界，同时Pohozaev集非空。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了临界质量附近归一化解的缺失与能量下确界行为，揭示了临界阈值a_*的相变现象。

Abstract: For \(\frac12\le s<1\), we study a fractional Choquard equation with prescribed mass and nonlinearities at the lower Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev and \(L^2\)-critical exponents. The sharp Hardy-Littlewood-Sobolev and Choquard Gagliardo-Nirenberg inequalities determine an explicit critical mass \(a_*\). For \(0<a\le a_*\), we compute the exact infimum of the constrained energy and prove that it is not attained and that no normalized solution exists. For \(a>a_*\), the energy is unbounded from below on the mass sphere, while the Pohozaev set is nonempty.

</details>


### [149] [A Lagrangian superposition principle for the continuity equation with reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11300)
*Stefano Almi,Giacomo Enrico Sodini*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在仅假设有限二次能量的条件下，为带反应项的连续性方程建立了拉格朗日叠加原理，并给出逆命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带反应项的连续性方程在最优传输与测度演化问题中具有基础地位，已有叠加原理通常需要反应项有界或测度支集具有某种紧性。本文旨在去除这些限制，在自然的一般性下建立叠加原理。

Method: 对三元组(v,w,μ)进行正则化，得到仅满足局部界的速度与反应场；随后在该局部假设下发展表示理论，核心工具是一个沿v的流将μ_s与μ_t联系起来的双时刻表示公式。

Result: 证明了对任意时间s,t成立的沿v流的双时刻表示公式；利用该公式在局部假设下建立表示理论，进而完成一般情形下叠加原理及逆命题的证明。

Conclusion: 在有限二次能量且不对反应项作有界性假设、不对测度支集作紧性假设的条件下，方程的解可由集中在特征系统x'=v(x)、k'=w(x)k解上的概率测度η经2齐次边缘表示，且逆命题亦成立。

Abstract: We study the continuity equation with reaction $\partial_tμ+ \operatorname{div}(vμ) = wμ$ on $[0,T]\times\mathbb{R}^d$, driven by Borel velocity and reaction fields $v$ and $w$. We provide the first version of a Lagrangian superposition principle for the equation in its natural generality, assuming only finite quadratic energy without imposing boundedness conditions on the reaction term neither any form of compactness for the support of the measure. More precisely, every solution $μ\in \mathcal{C}([0,T];\mathscr{M}_+(\mathbb{R}^d))$ with finite quadratic energy is represented by a probability measure $η$ on absolutely continuous curves in the geometric cone over $\mathbb{R}^d$, concentrated on the solutions of the characteristic system $x' = v(x)$, $k' = w(x)\,k$, through the $2$-homogeneous marginal $h^2_t(η)=μ_t$. We also establish the converse implication. The proof passes through a regularization of the triple $(v,w,μ)$ which produces fields satisfying only local bounds; the core of the argument is therefore a representation theory under such local assumptions, whose key tool, of independent interest, is a two-time representation formula relating $μ_s$ and $μ_t$ along the flow of $v$ for arbitrary times $s,t$.

</details>


### [150] [Sharp sign criterion for global existence and blow-up of strong solutions with arbitrarily large initial data in the 3D spherically symmetric compressible Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11304)
*Xiangdi Huang,Bingshu Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为3D球对称可压缩Navier-Stokes方程建立了强解全局存在与有限时间爆破的锐利判据，完全由初始有效速度的符号决定：边界上非负则对任意大初值全局存在（α>1超临界），边界上为负则可构造有限时间爆破解，爆破时真空恰出现在边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前高维强解的全局存在性结果仅限于α≤1（端点α=1对应粘性Saint-Venant浅水系统）。本文旨在将全局存在理论推广到超临界情形α>1，并寻找一个由初始有效速度符号决定的干净二分判据，统一全局正则性与奇性形成两种行为。

Method: 在Bresch-Desjardins结构μ=ρ^α, λ=(α-1)ρ^α下，以有效速度v=u+αρ^{α-2}ρ_r为核心。关键难点是推导密度的统一下界，需处理球心处的奇异估计：在球心附近构造若干新量，并利用精细的最大值原理首次建立速度在球心的Lipschitz连续性；随后对适当构造的函数导出Dini-Gronwall型不等式，从而封闭密度下界估计。爆破侧则构造边界上初始有效速度为负的一族初值。

Result: 当初始有效速度在球边界上非负时，证明了对任意大初值的全局强解存在性（α>1超临界）。反之，构造出一族边界上初始有效速度为负的初值，其强解在有限时间爆破，且真空恰在爆破时刻出现于边界。

Conclusion: 结果确立了全局正则性与奇性形成之间一个干净的二分（dichotomy），该二分完全由初始有效速度的符号所支配，将全局存在理论从α≤1拓展至α>1的超临界区域。

Abstract: We establish a sharp criterion for global-in-time existence versus finite-time blow-up of strong solutions to the 3D spherically symmetric compressible Navier-Stokes equations on a solid ball, based solely on the initial sign of effective velocity. The viscosity coefficients are assumed to satisfy the Bresch-Desjardins structure $μ=ρ^α$, $λ=(α-1)ρ^α$, with the effective velocity given by $v=u+αρ^{α-2}ρ_r$. Previously, global existence results for strong solutions in higher dimensions were restricted to the case $α\le 1$ with the endpoint $α=1$ corresponding to the viscous Saint-Venant (shallow water) system. In this paper, we extend the global existence theory beyond this threshold to the supercritical regime $α>1$. Specifically, whenever the initial effective velocity is nonnegative on the boundary of the ball, we prove the global-in-time existence of strong solutions for arbitrarily large initial data. The main difficulty lies in deriving a uniform lower bound for the density, which requires handling singular estimates at the center of the ball. To overcome this, we exploit several novel quantities near the center and employ a refined maximum principle to establish, for the first time, the Lipschitz continuity of the velocity at the center. Subsequently, we derive Dini-Gronwall-type inequalities for suitably constructed functions, which close the density lower bound estimate. Conversely, we can construct a family of initial data for which the initial effective velocity is negative on the boundary, such that the corresponding strong solutions blow up in finite time, with vacuum appearing exactly on the boundary at the blow-up time. Our results establish a clean dichotomy between global regularity and singularity formation, governed purely by the initial sign of the effective velocity.

</details>


### [151] [On an evolutionary equation involving the nonlocal infinity Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11311)
*Frida Fejne*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究非局部无穷拉普拉斯算子演化的方程∂u/∂t=(L∞)^αu，在柱形区域且0<α<1时用Perron方法证明Dirichlet问题解的存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类由非局部无穷拉普拉斯算子驱动的演化方程（含分数阶参数α）的适定性问题，特别是其Dirichlet问题的解的存在性与唯一性。

Method: 方法：采用Perron方法（上下解构造法）处理定义在柱形区域Ω⊂ℝ^{n+1}上的Dirichlet问题，针对0<α<1的情形。

Result: 对柱形区域Ω及0<α<1，证明了该Dirichlet问题解的存在性与唯一性。

Conclusion: 在该设定下（柱形域、0<α<1）方程的解存在且唯一；端点情形α=1等未涵盖，留待后续研究。

Abstract: We study the evolutionary equation $$\frac{\partial u}{\partial t} = (\mathcal{L}_{\infty})^αu \quad \text{in} \quad Ω,$$ where $(\mathcal{L}_{\infty})^αu$ denotes a nonlocal infinity Laplacian acting on the function $u$, $0<α\leq 1$ and $Ω$ is a bounded open set in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$. We prove existence and uniqueness using Perron's method for the Dirichlet problem when $Ω$ is a cylinder and $0<α<1$.

</details>


### [152] [Regularity of a free boundary determined by the value of the gradient. Part 1: Third order asymptotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11324)
*Frida Fejne*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究泛函 J(u)=∫_{B_1}F(∇u)dx 的极小元，其中 F 的二阶导数在 |x|=1 处可能跳跃。证明了在正则性与非退化假设下，极小元可展开为 u(x)=a+ν·x+δp(x)+δεq(x)，并得到余项 q^j 弱收敛到满足某 PDE 的 q^0，且 q^0 在 B_r^± 上为 C^3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由边界由函数梯度决定（Γ 为 |∇u| 跨越 1 的界面），此类问题在相变、最优设计等中常见。作者希望先建立两相平坦点附近的精细展开与余项的正则性，为后续证明自由边界正则性奠定基础。

Method: 考虑泛函 J(u)=∫_{B_1}F(∇u)dx 的极小元，F 一致凸且二阶导数在 |x|=1 处跳跃，导致 Euler-Lagrange 方程在自由边界 Γ 两侧不同。定义 Γ=∂{|∇u|>1}∩∂{|∇u|<1}，研究两相平坦点。在正则性与非退化假设下，对极小元作渐近展开 u=a+ν·x+δp(x)+δεq(x)，其中 p 为上半球与下半球各一个多项式拼接的 C^1 函数，q 为有界 L^2 余项；并对极小元序列 u^j 分析 q^j 的弱极限。

Result: 在正则性与非退化假设下，极小元可表示为 u(x)=a+ν·x+δp(x)+δεq(x)，其中 a∈R、ν∈R^n、0<δ,ε<<1，p 为上下半球各一多项式组成的 C^1 函数，q 为 L^2 有界余项。对极小元序列 u^j，q^j 弱收敛到 q^0，q^0 满足某 PDE，且在 B_r^+ 与 B_r^-（0<r<1）上分别为 C^3。

Conclusion: 论文给出两相平坦点处极小元的二阶展开及余项的极限 PDE 与 C^3 正则性，作为项目第一部分，为后续证明自由边界正则性提供关键工具。

Abstract: This is the first part of a project concerning the regularity of the free boundary of a function, where the free boundary depends on the gradient of the function. We study the minimizer of the expression \begin{equation*}
  J(u) : = \int_{B_1} F(\nabla u) \ dx, \end{equation*} where $F(x)$ is a uniformly convex function whose second derivatives might jump at $|x|=1$. This results in an Euler-Lagrange equation that varies over the free boundary, which we define as $$ Γ= \partial \{x \in B_1: |\nabla u| >1 \} \cap \partial \{x \in B_1: |\nabla u| <1 \}. $$ We consider two-phase flat points for which the second derivatives of $F$ jump over $Γ$. In this paper, we show that under some regularity and non-degeneracy assumptions, a minimizer $u$ can be expressed as $$ u(x)= a + ν\cdot x+ δp(x) + δεq(x), $$ where $a \in \mathbb{R}$, $ν\in \mathbb{R}^n$, $0 < δ, ε<< 1$. Here $p$ is a $C^1$ function, which consists of one polynomial in the upper half ball and another polynomial in the lower half ball. The function $q$ is a rest term with bounded $L^2$ norm. Furthermore, assuming that we have a sequence, $u^j$, of minimizers, we show that $q^j$ converges weakly to a function $q^0$ that satisfies a certain PDE. In addition, we show that $q^0$ is $C^3$ in $B_r^+$ and $B_r^-$, respectively, for $0<r<1$. This is the main result of this paper which is intended to be used to show regularity of the free boundary.

</details>


### [153] [Long-time dynamics of partially dissipative hyperbolic systems with non-autonomous coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11329)
*Timothée Crin-Barat,Ling-Yun Shou,Qimeng Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究非自治松弛系数的拟线性可对称化部分耗散双曲系统，在临界正则性框架下建立全局强解存在性，并给出最优代数衰减率与非自治抛物近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自治松弛理论假定耗散系数为常数，低频/高频分解阈值固定；当松弛系数显式依赖时间时，频阈随时间演化，原有Besov空间与hypocoercive估计不再适用，需处理"移动频率结构"以刻画时变耗散对长时间动力学的影响。

Method: 在满足Shizuta-Kawashima条件与熵条件的系统中，采用临界正则性框架证明全局强解；当初始数据额外有低正则性界时证明解以最优代数速率收敛到平衡态。引入一类适配时变阈值的混合Besov空间，在各频率区制推导hypocoercive估计，并利用非自治抛物方程逼近守恒部分。

Result: 对满足SK条件与熵条件的系统，在临界正则性下得到全局强解；低正则性初值下解以最优代数速率趋向平衡；守恒部分的渐近行为由非自治抛物方程描述。自治理论的低/高频分解在非自治情形下保持，但频阈随时间演化。结果适用于速度方程含时变阻尼系数K/(1+t)^α（α<1,K>0；或α=1,K≫1）的可压缩Euler系统。

Conclusion: 揭示了时变松弛系数对耗散与长时间动力学的定性与定量影响，发展的时变频阈混合Besov框架可用于更一般的非自治耗散双曲问题。

Abstract: We study quasilinear symmetrizable partially dissipative hyperbolic systems with non-autonomous relaxation coefficients in $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($d\geq1$). The existence of global strong solutions is established in a critical regularity setting for systems satisfying the so-called Shizuta-Kawashima (SK) and entropy conditions. When the initial data are additionally bounded in a lower-regularity norm, we prove that the corresponding solutions converge to equilibrium at optimal algebraic decay rates. Furthermore, we show that the conservative part of the solution behaves asymptotically as the solution of a non-autonomous parabolic equation. Our results apply to the compressible Euler system with the time-dependent damping coefficient $\frac{K}{(1+t)^α}$ ($α<1$, $K>0$ or $α=1$, $K\gg 1$) in the velocity equation. The natural low/high-frequency splitting of the autonomous theory persists in the non-autonomous setting, but with a frequency-threshold that evolves in time. To handle this moving frequency structure, we introduce a new class of hybrid Besov spaces adapted to time-dependent thresholds and derive hypocoercive estimates in each frequency regime. Our results reveal the qualitative and quantitative effects of general time-dependent relaxation coefficients on dissipation and large-time dynamics.

</details>


### [154] [Cartesian PML truncation for the Helmholtz equation is exponentially accurate at high frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11343)
*Martin Averseng,Jeffrey Galkowski,Euan A. Spence*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在Sjöstrand–Zworski黑箱框架下的一类Helmholtz散射问题中，笛卡尔PML近似辐射条件的误差随PML宽度、PML缩放强度和频率呈指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值求解Helmholtz散射问题时，需用人工边界层（如PML）近似无穷远处的辐射条件，但该近似的误差随PML参数与频率的衰减机制尚缺乏一般的严格理论刻画，尤其对包含复杂障碍或非均匀介质的黑箱类问题。

Method: 在Sjöstrand–Zworski黑箱散射框架下，考虑一类广泛的Helmholtz散射问题，用笛卡尔完美匹配层（PML）替代精确辐射条件，并对由此产生的近似误差进行严格的理论分析。

Result: 证明PML近似辐射条件的误差关于三个量——PML宽度、PML缩放强度以及频率——均呈指数衰减。

Conclusion: 笛卡尔PML是该类Helmholtz散射问题中辐射条件的一种高精度近似，其误差随PML宽度、缩放强度和频率指数下降，为黑箱框架下PML的适用性与收敛速度提供了严格的数学依据。

Abstract: For a wide class of Helmholtz scattering problems (namely, those fitting in the black-box framework of Sjöstrand--Zworski) we prove that the error incurred by approximating the radiation condition by a Cartesian perfectly-matched layer (PML) decreases exponentially in the width of the PML, the strength of the PML scaling, and the frequency.

</details>


### [155] [Emergence of a convex strange term via homogenization of non-local energies at the critical exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11371)
*Giuseppe Cosma Brusca,Giuliana Fusco*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在临界指数下，对周期穿孔区域上带Dirichlet边界条件的卷积型与离散能量，推导了Γ-极限，并证明尺度分离现象及"strange term"能量密度的凸性（即使在向量值情形下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期穿孔区域上非局部（卷积型）与离散能量在临界指数处的渐近行为，需克服能量尺度不变性带来的技术困难，并揭示多尺度分析中新现象。

Method: 假设非局部相互作用尺度远小于立方穿孔边长，利用卷积型与离散变分框架之间的类比，采用统一论证方法推导Γ-极限，进行多尺度分析。

Result: 证明临界指数下发生尺度分离，得到相应的Γ-极限；并发现"strange term"的能量密度是凸的，该结论与泛函的非局部性质无关，而源于临界指数的分析，且在向量值情形下同样成立。

Conclusion: 该工作统一处理了卷积型与离散能量在穿孔区域临界指数下的Γ-收敛，揭示了尺度分离与strange term密度凸性这一新观察。

Abstract: We derive the $Γ$-limit of convolution-type and discrete energies subject to Dirichlet boundary conditions on periodically perforated domains at the critical exponent. We assume that the length-scale of the non-local interactions is much smaller than the side-length of the cubic perforations and prove that a separation of scales occurs. Exploiting the analogies between the variational frameworks of our interest, we employ a unified argument to overcome the technical difficulties that arise from the scaling invariance of the energies. Our multiscale analysis yields a novel observation that is not related to the non-local nature of the functionals, but rather to the analysis at the critical exponent: we prove that the energy density of the $\textit{strange term}$ is convex, even in the vector-valued setting.

</details>


### [156] [A sharp lower bound for the nodal volume of harmonic functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11444)
*Zhehui Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了调和函数零集（nodal set）在$B_2$内的$(n-1)$维Hausdorff测度被doubling index $\mathcal{N}$线性下界控制，且线性依赖是最优的，从而证实了关于调和函数nodal体积的一个民间猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和函数的nodal set（零集）测度与其频率/倍增指标的关系存在一个民间猜想：nodal体积应与doubling index $\mathcal{N}$成线性下界。此前该猜想缺乏严格证明，本文旨在建立这一最优线性估计。

Method: 在$B_4\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ ($n\geq3$)上考虑非零实值调和函数$u$，$u(0)=0$。引入doubling index $\mathcal{N}=\log_2\frac{\sup_{B_1}|u|}{\sup_{B_{1/2}}|u|}$，通过分析零点集$\{u=0\}$在$B_2$中的$(n-1)$维Hausdorff测度，建立其与$\mathcal{N}$的定量关系。

Result: 证明了$\mathcal{H}^{n-1}(\{u(x)=0\}\cap B_2)\ge C_n\mathcal{N}$，其中$C_n>0$仅依赖于维数$n$。同时指出对$\mathcal{N}$的线性依赖是最优的。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了关于调和函数nodal体积的民间猜想，给出了nodal set测度关于doubling index的最优线性下界估计。

Abstract: Let $u$ be a non-zero real-valued harmonic function in $B_4\subset\mathbb{R}^n$ with $n\geq3$ and $u(0)=0$. We prove that $$\mathcal{H}^{n-1}\bigl(\{u(x)=0\}\cap B_2\bigr)\ge C_n\mathcal{N},$$ where $C_n$ is a positive constant depending only on $n$, and $\mathcal{N}$ is the doubling index defined by $$\mathcal{N}=\log_2\frac{\sup_{B_1}|u|}{\sup_{B_{\frac{1}{2}}}|u|}.$$ The linear dependence on $\mathcal{N}$ is optimal. This estimate confirms a folklore conjecture on the nodal volume of harmonic functions.

</details>


### [157] [Parabolic-hyperbolic splitting in support propagation for stochastic porous media equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11468)
*Max Sauerbrey,Joshua Utley*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文提出随机局部化能量法，用随机输运方程的解作权重，研究含线性保守噪声的随机多孔介质方程解的支撑传播性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究用空间截断函数对能量进行局部化，但在含保守噪声时能量方法失效，因此需要寻找新的局部化方式以恢复能量论证。

Method: 将能量以随机输运方程的解作为权重进行局部化（而非空间截断函数），从而抵消保守噪声项对支撑传播的影响。

Result: 证明了有限传播速度，并给出（模随机输运意义下）等待时间现象存在的充分必要条件。

Conclusion: 在短时间尺度上，支撑传播可分解为两部分：多孔介质方程演化引起的一部分与随机输运引起的一部分，二者相互独立。

Abstract: We develop a randomly-localized energy method to derive qualitative results on the support propagation of stochastic porous media equations with linear conservative noise. Unlike in previous works, where energies are localized by weighting them with a spatial bump function, we weight them with profiles which are solutions to stochastic transport equations. This modulates out the support propagation due to the conservative noise term, and energy arguments---which otherwise fail in this setting---are again applicable. As a result, finite speed of propagation along with sufficient and necessary conditions on the existence of waiting time phenomena (modulo stochastic transport) are proven. These methods and results demonstrate, on short time scales, that the support propagation may be disintegrated into two independent parts, one due to the evolution of the porous media equation and one due to stochastic transport.

</details>


### [158] [An Anisotropic Onsager Criterion for the Two-Dimensional Navier--Stokes Equations with Horizontal Viscosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11488)
*Siyu Liang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究水平粘性二维Navier–Stokes方程，证明Lions可积性与非耗散方向Onsager临界正则性可推出水平耗散正则性，并使解满足能量等式与L²连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对带水平粘性的二维Navier–Stokes方程，通常把∂₁u∈L²ₜL²ₓ作为解的定义的一部分。本文希望放松这一假设，考察耗散正则性能否由方程本身与较弱条件推出，即解的正则性是否不必预先纳入定义。

Method: 在T²或R²上考虑L∞ₜL²ₓ分布弱解，不加∂₁u∈L²ₜL²ₓ。利用Lions可积条件u∈L⁴ₜL⁴ₓ与非耗散方向的Onsager临界正则性u∈L³ₜB^{1/3,v}_{3,∞}（Besov正则性只施加在垂直方向），结合吸收法将水平能量通量并入耗散项，并对垂直能量通量使用一维交换子估计。

Result: 在该条件下证明∂₁u∈L²ₜL²ₓ，并给出水平耗散的定量上界。进一步，若u∈L³ₜB^{1/3,v}_{3,c₀}，则u属于各向同性临界Onsager空间L³ₜB^{1/3}_{3,c₀}，满足能量等式，并在L²中连续。其中c₀表示相应二进Besov序列在高频趋于零。

Conclusion: 耗散正则性可作为方程的结果而非解定义的一部分；较弱的方向性Onsager正则性足以升级为各向同性临界正则性、能量等式与L²连续性。

Abstract: We consider distributional weak solutions $u\in L^\infty_tL^2_x$ of the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations with horizontal viscosity on $\mathbb T^2$ or $\mathbb R^2$, without assuming $\partial_1u\in L^2_tL^2_x$. We show that the Lions integrability condition $u\in L^4_tL^4_x$ together with Onsager-critical regularity in the nondissipative direction, $u\in L^3_tB^{1/3,v}_{3,\infty}$ with the Besov regularity imposed only in the vertical direction, implies $\partial_1u\in L^2_tL^2_x$, with a quantitative bound on the horizontal dissipation. Thus the dissipative regularity is a consequence of the equation and need not be part of the definition of the solution. If moreover $u\in L^3_tB^{1/3,v}_{3,c_0}$, then $u$ belongs to the isotropic critical Onsager space $L^3_tB^{1/3}_{3,c_0}$, satisfies the energy equality, and is continuous in $L^2$. Here $c_0$ indicates that the corresponding dyadic Besov sequence tends to zero at high frequencies. The proof combines absorption of the horizontal energy flux into the dissipation with a one-dimensional commutator estimate for the vertical energy flux.

</details>


### [159] [Existence and Asymptotic Behavior of Normalized Ground States for a Weighted Elliptic System with Caffarelli--Kohn--Nirenberg Critical Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11494)
*Asmita Rai,Divya Goel*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带奇异权与CKN临界指数的耦合椭圆系统在质量约束下的正规化基态，证明β≤0无基态、β>0在次临界/临界/超临界区域存在正基态，并刻画β→0⁺与β→+∞的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caffarelli–Kohn–Nirenberg不等式的临界指数与奇异权位势在非线性分析中具有基本地位，耦合系统与质量约束（归一化解）结合后，基态的存在性、阈值现象及渐近行为尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其是质量超临界情形下的阈值β₀。

Method: 采用变分方法处理带L²（加权）质量约束的椭圆系统：引入Lagrange乘子λ₁,λ₂将约束极值问题化为Euler–Lagrange方程；利用CKN不等式与紧嵌入/集中紧性分析处理临界指数2^♯；分质量次临界、临界、超临界三种情形讨论，并通过阈值分析确定β₀的存在性条件。

Result: β≤0时基态能级不可达；β>0时在质量次临界、临界、超临界区域对显式β范围得到正正规化基态且Lagrange乘子为正。超临界情形存在阈值β₀≥0：β>β₀有基态，0<β<β₀无基态；给出β₀=0的p,q条件，并证明p,q≥2时β₀>0。

Conclusion: 在β→0⁺与β→+∞的极限中，经伸缩变换后基态收敛到无临界项的极限系统的基态，或一个分量消失而另一分量在CKN不等式的极值函数上集中，完整刻画了耦合奇异系统基态的渐近结构。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the coupled singular weighted elliptic system
  $$\left\{
  \begin{aligned}
  -\operatorname{div}(|x|^{-2a}\nabla u)+λ_1 \frac{u}{|x|^{2a}}&=βp\frac{|v|^q|u|^{p-2}u}{|x|^{b(p+q)}}+\frac{|u|^{2^{\sharp}-2}u}{|x|^{b2^{\sharp}}},\quad\text{in}~\mathbb{R}^N,
  -\operatorname{div}(|x|^{-2a}\nabla v)+λ_2\frac{v}{|x|^{2a}}&=βq\frac{|u|^p|v|^{q-2}v}{|x|^{b(p+q)}}+\frac{|v|^{2^\sharp-2}v}{|x|^{b{2^\sharp}}},\quad\text{in}~\mathbb{R}^N,
  \displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^N}\frac{|u|^2}{|x|^{2a}} dx=ρ_1^2,\quad &\int_{\mathbb{R}^N}\frac{|v|^2}{|x|^{2a}}dx=ρ_2^2.
  \end{aligned}
  \right.
  $$
  where $N\ge3$, $β\in\mathbb{R}$, $ρ_1,ρ_2>0$, $\max\{0,\tfrac{N-4}{2}\}\le a<\tfrac{N-2}{2}$, $a<b<a+1$, $2^{\sharp}:=\frac{2N}{N-2(1+a-b)}$, $p,q>1$ and $2<p+q<2^{\sharp}$. Here $2^\sharp$ is the critical exponent of the Caffarelli--Kohn--Nirenberg inequality, and $λ_1,λ_2\in\mathbb{R}$ are unknown, since the two weighted masses are prescribed. We show that the ground state level is not attained for $β\le0$. For $β>0$ we obtain positive normalized ground states, with positive Lagrange multipliers, in the mass subcritical, mass critical, and mass supercritical regimes, for explicit ranges of $β$. In the mass supercritical regime, a threshold $β_0\ge0$ appears: a ground state exists for every $β>β_0$ and does not exist for $0<β<β_0$. We give conditions on $p,q$ under which $β_0=0$, and we prove that $β_0>0$ when $p,q\ge2$. Finally, we describe the ground states as $β\to0^+$ and as $β\to+\infty$: after a dilation, they converge to the ground states of the limit system without critical terms, or one component vanishes and the other concentrates on an extremal of the Caffarelli--Kohn--Nirenberg inequality.

</details>


### [160] [Quantitative Diffusive Limits for Singular Nonlocal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11837)
*Andrea Agazzi,Giuseppe Bruno,Federico Pasqualotto,Philippe Rigollet*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究闭连通Riemann流形上的非局部连续性方程，证明b→0时其整体解以尖锐且时间一致的速率Cb²收敛到热流，并通过b加权高阶预解能量的均匀耗散获得指数弛豫；在圆周上分析N粒子动力学，得到W₁误差界与热流逼近速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局部连续性方程与热流之间的关系尚缺乏时间一致的定量刻画；同时该方程缺少Wasserstein梯度流结构，使得常规能量方法难以直接给出指数弛豫与b→0极限速率。

Method: 对闭连通Riemann流形上的方程，构造并证明b加权高阶预解能量的均匀耗散估计；在圆周情形，对相应确定性N粒子动力学采用弱—强调制能量论证。

Result: 对光滑严格正初值，得到sup_{t≥0}‖μ_b(t)-μ(t)‖_{L¹}≤Cb²；圆周上对iid初值得到E[sup_{t≥0}W₁(μ_b^N(t),μ_b(t))]≤C(Nb)^{-1/2}，取b≍N^{-1/5}时以N^{-2/5}速率时间一致逼近热流。

Conclusion: 非局部方程在b→0时以尖锐、时间一致速率收敛到热流，即使无Wasserstein梯度流结构仍具指数弛豫；粒子近似给出了最优的b-N权衡与热流逼近速率。

Abstract: We study the nonlocal continuity equation \[ \partial_tμ_b =\operatorname{div}\!\left( μ_b\nabla\log\bigl((I-b^2Δ)^{-1}μ_b\bigr) \right) \] on a closed connected Riemannian manifold. For smooth strictly positive initial data, we prove that as $b \to 0$, its global solution converges to heat flow $μ(t)$ at the sharp, uniform-in-time rate \[ \sup_{t\ge0}\|μ_b(t)-μ(t)\|_{L^1}\le Cb^2. \] The key estimate is the uniform dissipation of a $b$-weighted higher-order resolvent energy, which yields exponential relaxation despite the absence of a Wasserstein gradient-flow structure.
  On the circle, we also analyze the corresponding deterministic $N$-particle dynamics. A weak--strong modulated energy argument gives \[ \mathbb E\!\left[ \sup_{t\ge0}W_1(μ_b^N(t),μ_b(t)) \right] \le C(Nb)^{-1/2} \] for iid initialization. Consequently, the choice $b\asymp N^{-1/5}$ approximates heat flow uniformly in time at rate $N^{-2/5}$.

</details>


### [161] [A sharp curvature lower bound for the first exterior p-harmonic Steklov eigenvalue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11517)
*Yating Niu,Tao Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究外区域上p-Laplace方程的第一变分Steklov特征值，在全凸条件下给出由边界主曲率的p-对数平均刻画的锐下界，并给出由边界等容常数刻画的上下界，p=2时退化为已有对数平均结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外区域上的Steklov特征值问题在势论与几何分析中具有基础意义，但p-Laplace（非线性）情形下的变分Steklov特征值缺乏精细的几何下界，特别是与边界曲率（主曲率的对数型平均）之间的锐利联系尚不清楚，且需要合适的外区域有限能量理论支撑。

Method: 在1<p<n、外区域Ω^ext上定义并研究第一变分Steklov特征值；当Ω凸且∂Ω∈C^{1,1}时，利用已建立的外区域有限能量理论以及p-调和延拓的衰减估计，结合边界曲率几何量进行分析；通过p-对数平均对边界主曲率进行刻画来导出下界，并通过边界等容常数导出上界。

Result: 证明了第一变分Steklov特征值的一个锐下界，其显式地由边界主曲率的p-对数平均给出；同时得到了以边界等容常数为参数的上下界估计；当p=2时该下界退化为Bundrock等人（arXiv:2511.09490）的对数平均界。

Conclusion: 结果将外区域Steklov特征值的曲率型锐估计从线性情形推广到p-Laplace非线性情形，并借助外区域有限能量理论与p-调和延拓衰减估计建立了相应的分析框架，为后续研究提供了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the first variational Steklov eigenvalue of the $ p$-Laplace equation on exterior domain $ Ω^{\text{ext}}$ for $ 1< p <n$. If $ Ω$ is convex and $ \partial Ω\in C^{1,1}$, we prove a sharp lower bound in terms of $p$-logarithmic mean of the principal curvatures of $ \partial Ω$. For the linear case $ p=2$, our estimate reduces to the logarithmic mean bound of Bundrock et al. (arXiv:2511.09490). We also derive an upper bound in terms of a boundary isocapacitary constant. The analysis relies on the established finite energy theory on exterior domains, together with a decay estimate for $p$-harmonic extensions.

</details>


### [162] [The Singular Set of Minima of Multiple Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11546)
*Cristiana De Filippis,Jan Kristensen,Giuseppe Mingione,Aidan Strong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究拟凸积分的局部极小元，证明其奇异集的Hausdorff维数严格小于环境空间维数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟凸积分是变分法与非线性弹性中的重要模型，其局部极小元可能在某些点处正则性失效（即奇异集非空）。理解奇异集的测度与维数是正则性理论的核心问题，此前的部分正则性结果多给出奇异集的测度估计，而刻画其Hausdorff维数的上界更为精细。

Method: 论文针对拟凸（quasiconvex）变分积分的局部极小元，结合部分正则性理论与奇异集维数估计技术，通过建立奇异点附近的衰减/分层（stratification）论证，给出奇异集Hausdorff维数的严格上界。

Result: 证明了拟凸积分局部极小元的奇异集，其Hausdorff维数严格小于环境空间维数n，即奇异集为维数小于n的集合。

Conclusion: 该结果将拟凸情形下奇异集的小测度结论加强为维数意义上的精细刻画，表明奇异集在Hausdorff维数下是"小"的，深化了拟凸变分问题的部分正则性理论。

Abstract: The Hausdorff dimension of the singular set of local minimizers of quasiconvex integrals is strictly less than the ambient dimension.

</details>


### [163] [Solvability of the \(\mathrm L^2\) Dirichlet problem for parabolic operators with spatially periodic coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11626)
*Kaj Nyström*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明上半空间中标量散度型抛物算子Poisson核的全局L²反向Hölder估计，并由此得到L² Dirichlet问题的唯一可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究空间周期、时间非周期的散度型抛物算子，在边界迹具有时间一致连续模与横向square-Dini收敛条件下，建立尺度一致的Poisson核RH₂估计及L² Dirichlet问题可解性，并将结果推广至非对称A∞定理、Lipschitz图区域与周期均匀化族。

Method: 对上半空间实对称、各空间变量周期（时间无周期假设）的系数矩阵，在边界迹A⁰具有定量一致时间连续模、且横向收敛受square-Dini模控制时，证明周期以下尺度的RH₂估计；利用完全空间周期性将估计传播到所有尺度；核心新工具是正伴随高度坐标Φ*=λ+O(1)，通过边界比较得到Green函数的大尺度衰减，进而获得全局RH₂估计；时间连续性可用D_t^{1/2}A⁰∈L∞_x(BMO_t)作为充分判据。

Result: Poisson核在周期以下尺度满足一致RH₂估计，并经周期性传播至所有尺度，从而Green函数具大尺度衰减；由此L² Dirichlet问题在N_*u∈L²的弱解类中唯一可解。对完全空间周期系数，在横向无关边界迹的square-Dini收敛（时间仅需可测）下建立非对称A∞定理；结果可迁移至时间无关Lipschitz图区域，并给出周期均匀化族的尺度一致边界估计。

Conclusion: 建立了空间周期抛物算子Poisson核的全局RH₂估计及其L² Dirichlet问题可解性，提出正伴随高度坐标Φ*与square-Dini条件等关键工具，并将结论推广到非对称A∞、Lipschitz图区域与均匀化族。

Abstract: We prove global reverse Hölder estimates in \(\mathrm L^2\) for the Poisson kernel of scalar divergence-form parabolic operators in the upper half-space. The coefficient matrix is real, symmetric, and periodic in every spatial variable \(X=(λ,x)\), while no periodicity in time is assumed. If its boundary trace \(A^0=A^0(x,t)\) has a quantitative uniform modulus of continuity in time and its convergence to \(A^0\) in the transverse variable is controlled by a square-Dini modulus, then the Poisson kernel satisfies a uniform \(\mathrm{RH}_2\) estimate at scales below the period. Full spatial periodicity is then used to propagate this estimate to every scale. The main new ingredient in the large-scale argument is a positive adjoint height coordinate \(Φ^*=λ+O(1)\). Boundary comparison with \(Φ^*\) yields the required large-scale decay of the Green function and hence the global \(\mathrm{RH}_2\) estimate. Consequently, the \(\mathrm L^2\) Dirichlet problem is uniquely solvable among weak solutions \(u\) satisfying \(N_\ast u\in\mathrm L^2\). We also establish, for fully spatially periodic coefficients, a nonsymmetric \(A_\infty\) theorem under square-Dini convergence to a transverse-independent boundary trace, with no temporal regularity beyond measurability. We explain how the results transfer to time-independent Lipschitz graph domains under the stated coefficient and periodicity-compatibility hypotheses, and we derive scale-uniform boundary estimates for the periodic homogenization families considered below. The condition \(D_t^{1/2}A^0\in\mathrm L^\infty_x(\mathrm{BMO}_t)\) provides a natural sufficient criterion for the temporal continuity required in the symmetric theorem.

</details>


### [164] [Gaussians Do Not Always Maximize Mixed-Norm Strichartz Inequalities for the Schrödinger Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11644)
*Felipe Gonçalves,Giuseppe Negro,Diogo Oliveira e Silva*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Schrödinger方程混合范数Strichartz不等式的最值问题，证明在低维（1≤d≤5）下高斯函数作为局部极大值点的稳定性阈值ρ_d，并在特定情形下建立全局稳定性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨混合范数Strichartz不等式的最佳常数及极值函数（高斯函数）是否为局部/全局极大值点，是调和分析与偏微分方程中的核心问题，现有结果在高维或全局稳定性方面尚不完整。

Method: 基于谱隙（spectral gap）分析，精确计算了相应算子的谱隙，并据此分析高斯函数在L_t^qL_x^r范数下作为局部极大值点的稳定性；对特定参数情形推导全局稳定性不等式并给出有效的稳定性常数。

Result: 在1≤d≤5时，给出阈值ρ_1=10, ρ_2=6, ρ_3=4√7−6≈4.583, ρ_4=2√15−4≈3.746, ρ_5=10/3≈3.333：当2<r<ρ_d时高斯函数为稳定的局部极大值点，当ρ_d<r≤2d/(d−2)时不再是局部极大值点。在(q,r,d)∈{(6,6,1),(8,4,1),(4,4,2)}三种情形下建立了带有效稳定性常数的全局稳定性不等式。

Conclusion: 高斯函数在低维混合范数Strichartz不等式中的局部极大值稳定性存在依赖于维数的阈值ρ_d；谱隙的精确计算是证明的关键工具，且部分情形可得全局稳定性结果。

Abstract: We investigate the maximization problem for the family of mixed-norm Strichartz inequalities for the Schrödinger equation, $\|e^{-itΔ/2}f\|_{L_t^qL_{\boldsymbol{x}}^r(\mathbb{R}^{1+d})}\le C_{q,r}\lVert f\rVert_{L^2(\mathbb{R}^d)}$, with $2/q+d/r=d/2$, $q,r\geq 2$, and thus $r\leq 2d/(d-2)$ if $d\geq 3$. We show that, in low dimensions $1\leq d\leq 5$, the thresholds $ρ_1=10$, $ρ_2=6$, $ρ_3=4\sqrt{7}-6\approx 4.583$, $ρ_4=2\sqrt{15}-4\approx 3.746$, and $ρ_5=10/3\approx 3.333$ are such that gaussians are stable local maximizers for $2<r<ρ_d$, and fail to be local maximizers for $ρ_d<r\leq 2d/(d-2)$ (with the conventions there is no upper bound on $r$ when $d\in\{1,2\}$ and that $r=\infty$ is excluded when $d=2$). In the cases $(q,r,d)\in\{(6,6,1),(8,4,1),(4,4,2)\}$, we establish global stability inequalities with effective stability constants. Both proofs hinge on spectral gaps which we compute exactly.

</details>


### [165] [Stable solutions in the abelian Higgs model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11647)
*Marco Badran*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文完整分类了四维阿贝尔希格斯模型中具有二次能量增长的整稳定解，证明它们全纯并满足Bradlow一阶方程，其零点集为全纯曲线；并构造出零点集为任意具二次面积增长的全纯曲线的解。作为推论，三维中具线性能量增长的稳定解皆为二维Bogomolnyi涡旋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定解的分类是变分与几何分析中的核心问题，而能量增长阶往往决定了正则性结构与可能的解类型。作者希望刻画四维阿贝尔希格斯模型中二次能量增长的整稳定解，揭示其背后的复几何/全纯结构，并借此统一理解低维涡旋现象。

Method: 采用变分与几何分析手段：利用稳定性（第二变分非负）结合二次能量增长的量化估计，运用单调性公式、Bernstein型论证及blow-down分析，证明解必满足Bradlow引入的一阶（全纯）方程；同时通过构造性方法，给定任意具二次面积增长的全纯曲线，构造以其为零点集的解。

Result: 证明了此类整稳定解关于某复结构全纯，满足Bradlow一阶方程，因而其零点集为全纯曲线；反向地，对任意具二次面积增长的全纯曲线，均构造出以其为零点集的解。并据此推出$\mathbb{R}^3$中具线性能量增长的稳定解都是二维Bogomolnyi涡旋。

Conclusion: 稳定性与能量增长阶强约束了解的结构：四维二次能量增长的稳定解被完全分类为全纯（Bradlow）解，三维线性增长情形退化为二维Bogomolnyi涡旋，从而建立起高维稳定解与低维涡旋之间的对应。

Abstract: We classify entire stable solutions with quadratic energy growth in the $4$-dimensional abelian Higgs model. More precisely, we prove that such solutions are holomorphic with respect to some complex structure, namely they satisfy the first order equations introduced by Bradlow, and consequently their nodal sets are holomorphic curves. Conversely, we construct solutions whose nodal set is any prescribed holomorphic curve with quadratic area growth. As a corollary, we show that all stable solutions with linear energy growth in $\mathbb{R}^3$ are two-dimensional Bogomolnyi vortices.

</details>


### [166] [The Schrödinger-Klein-Gordon System Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11658)
*Christophe Cheverry,Zied Ammari*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文引入 Schr{ö}dinger-Klein-Gordon 系统的微局部分析表述：用 Fourier-Wigner 变换表示量子分量，将 Klein-Gordon 方程改写于复 Fourier 变量，消去场变量后得到相空间上的非线性 Fourier-Moyal 方程，并建立其适定性与弱解理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究非相对论量子粒子与 Klein-Gordon 场经 Yukawa 耦合的相互作用提供替代性分析框架，避免直接处理 Schrödinger 波函数，并在微局域层面细化粒子-场相互作用描述，架起非线性色散方程与时频/相空间方法之间的桥梁。

Method: 以 Fourier-Wigner 变换表示量子分量，将 Klein-Gordon 方程用复 Fourier 变量改写，消去场变量得到以 Fourier-Wigner 分布为未知量的闭非线性 Fourier-Moyal 方程；原立方耦合转化为由显式双线性算子控制的二次自相互作用。随后在涉及 Wiener 型范数与指定连续模的各向异性空间中建立适定性理论，并利用 Fourier-Wigner 变换的紧性构造弱 L2 解。

Result: 在紫外截断的一般假设下，建立了 Fourier-Moyal 方程的局部存在唯一性及微局域正则性传播；通过 Fourier-Wigner 变换的紧性引入新的弱 L2 解构造，并对“预备数据”（prepared data）得到全局存在性。

Conclusion: 该微局域表述将原始立方耦合化为二次自相互作用，使时频分析技术得以应用，为 Yukawa 型相互作用研究提供了替代分析框架，并在非线性色散方程与相空间方法之间建立了联系。

Abstract: We introduce a microlocal formulation of the Schr{ö}dinger-Klein-Gordon system describing the interaction between a non-relativistic quantum particle and a Klein-Gordon field through Yukawa coupling. Instead of working directly with the Schr{ö}dinger wave function, we represent the quantum component by its Fourier-Wigner transform, and we rewrite the Klein-Gordon equation in terms of a complex Fourier variable. Eliminating the field variable yields a closed nonlinear Fourier-Moyal equation on phase space whose unknown is the Fourier-Wigner distribution associated with the quantum component. The resulting formulation provides a refined description of the particle-field interaction at the microlocal level. In particular, the original cubic coupling is transformed into a quadratic self-interaction governed by an explicit bilinear operator. This new representation permits the use of techniques from time-frequency analysis. Building on this, we develop a well-posedness theory for the Fourier-Moyal equation in anisotropic spaces involving Wiener-type norms and prescribed moduli of continuity. Local existence and uniqueness are established under general assumptions on the ultraviolet cutoff, together with propagation of microlocal regularity. We then introduce a new construction of weak L 2 -solutions by exploiting compactness properties of Fourier-Wigner transforms and obtain global existence for prepared data. The aim is to provide an alternative analytical framework for the study of Yukawa-type interactions and to establish a bridge between nonlinear dispersive equations and phase-space methods.

</details>


### [167] [Numerical Study of Reported Multiple Solutions to a Cauchy Problem for the 2d Incompressible Euler Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11694)
*Allen Tesdall,Richard Sanders*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过精心设计的数值方法，为Bressan-Shen（2021）与Bressan-Murray（2020）关于二维不可压缩Euler方程弱解非唯一性的猜想提供数值证据，发现众多参数对中仅有一组可能支持该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bressan等人提出了一个依赖两个参数的二维Cauchy问题例子，声称对其初始数据施加两种特定近似并取极限可得到两个不同的弱解，从而说明不可压缩Euler方程弱解非唯一。该猜想缺乏严格证明，需要数值证据来判断其是否成立、以及在何种条件下成立。

Method: 针对该问题的四个数值难点（初始数据在原点邻域无界、问题定义在整个R^2上、需捕捉真解的多重对称性、两参数可能存在临界耦合），采用嵌套网格（nested-grid）策略：用极大计算域以捕捉自由空间问题的核心对称性，并在精度最关键区域实现极端网格加密；在嵌套网格框架内设计高阶、稳健且快速的离散格式；对广泛的允许参数对进行系统计算。

Result: 数值结果表明，为捕捉精确自由空间问题的核心对称性，数学上必须采用极大的计算域；在考察的众多允许参数对中，仅有一组参数可能满足所提出的非唯一性猜想，其余参数对均不能支持该主张。

Conclusion: 该猜想并非对参数空间中任意取值都成立，而是（若有成立）仅在极其特殊的一组参数下可能实现；研究同时表明，此类问题的数值验证对计算域规模和局部网格分辨率有极强要求，嵌套网格加高阶离散是可行且有效的途径。

Abstract: Bressan and Shen (2021) jointly with Bressan and Murray (2020) proposed a simple example of a Cauchy problem on $\mathbb{R}^2$, characterized by two key parameters, intended to yield nonunique weak solutions of the incompressible Euler equations. They suggest that a limiting process applied to two specific approximations of their example initial data yields two distinct solutions. Here we provide evidence coming from a carefully designed numerical procedure to support if, or when, this intriguing claim is valid. Some of the numerical challenges encountered in the present study are: (i) the proposed initial data is unbounded in a neighborhood of the origin, (ii) the proposed problem is posed on all of $\mathbb{R}^2$, (iii) several essential symmetries present in the true solution must be captured, (iv) the proposed example depends on two parameters which may be critically linked. We demonstrate that an extremely large computational domain is mathematically necessary to capture the core symmetries of the exact free-space problem. Accordingly we implement a nested-grid strategy specifically designed for its particular suitability and efficacy for the problem at hand. Our strategy also allows us to achieve extreme grid refinement in the region where solution accuracy is most critical. Within our nested-grid framework, we design an underlying discretization scheme that is high-order, robust, and fast.
  We consider a broad collection of allowable parameter pairs, but among all of them we find only one that may satisfy the suggested claim.

</details>


### [168] [Limiting Pointwise Decay for the compressible isentropic Navier-Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11707)
*Rui Li,Xu Song,Guanghui Wang,Lingda Xu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维可压缩等熵Navier-Stokes方程常状态小局部扰动解的长时间逐点行为，证明扣除二阶Burgers扩散波及高阶扩散波、跨族粘性修正后的物理余项具有保锥逐点估计，在可积对数损失意义下达到极限衰减率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Navier-Stokes方程小扰动解的长时间渐近行为通常由扩散波描述，但物理余项的逐点在锥内的精细衰减估计，尤其是达到极限衰减率与保锥性质，是难点。已有方法难以同时处理同族一阶临界反馈、跨族耦合和锥内点态衰减。作者希望建立余项的最优（极限率）逐点估计。

Method: 核心是对余项空间原函数施加族对族（familywise）Cole-Hopf变换，精确消去临界同族一阶反馈；同时构造适应两条特征族的近似Green函数，结合Gaussian模提取与Kawashima型能量方法，进行保锥逐点加权估计。

Result: 证明余项满足保锥逐点估计 |R_i(x,t)| ≤ C E_N log(2+t) Ψ_i(x,t)，其中 sup_x Ψ_i(x,t) ≤ C(1+t)^{-1}，从而 ‖R_i(t)‖_{L^∞} ≤ C E_N (1+t)^{-1} log(2+t)。即在可积对数损失下达到极限衰减率(1+t)^{-1}，E_N为初值大小。

Conclusion: 通过族对族Cole-Hopf变换与特征族适应的近似Green函数方法，成功给出NS方程小扰动余项的保锥逐点渐近，达到极限衰减率（对数损失可积），为一维可压缩NS的逐点渐近分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the long-time pointwise behavior of small localized perturbations of a constant state for the one-dimensional compressible isentropic Navier-Stokes equations. After subtracting the two Burgers diffusion waves, the convergent sum of all higher-order diffusion waves, and the cross-family viscous corrections, we prove a cone-preserving pointwise estimate for the exact physical remainder and, in particular, \[
  |R_i(x,t)|\leq C E_N\log(2+t)Ψ_i(x,t),
  \qquad
  \sup_{x\in\mathbb R}Ψ_i(x,t)\leq C(1+t)^{-1}. \] Here \(E_N\) measures the size of the initial data and \(Ψ_i\) is the cone-resolved weight; both are defined precisely in the main theorem below. Thus $\|R_i(t)\|_{L^\infty}\leq C E_N(1+t)^{-1}\log(2+t)$. The key new idea is to apply a familywise Cole--Hopf transformation to the spatial antiderivative of the remainder, which exactly eliminates the critical same-family first-order feedback. We further construct an approximate Green function adapted to the two characteristic families and combine it with Gaussian-mode extraction and a Kawashima-type energy argument. This yields a cone-preserving estimate at the limiting decay rate, up to a logarithmic loss.

</details>


### [169] [Global well-posedness of the primitive equations with stochastic wind-driven boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11734)
*Tarek Zoechling*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了由有限个布朗运动驱动的Neumann风应力下三维原始方程在任意光滑空间风剖面下的全局路径存在唯一性，无需假设初值具有垂直导数，推广了此前仅有的局部理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维原始方程在随机风应力驱动下的适定性研究通常需要初值的垂直导数等额外正则性假设，而已有的相关理论（如Binz、Hieber、Hussein和Saal的工作）对此类数据和风剖面只能给出局部解，因此需要建立全局路径存在唯一性并放宽初值正则性要求。

Method: 结合局部最大L^2正则性构造与边界随机卷积的加权估计。

Result: 对任意光滑空间风剖面，在初值不假设垂直导数的条件下，证明了三维原始方程的全局路径存在唯一性。

Conclusion: 该结果将Binz、Hieber、Hussein和Saal关于此类数据与风剖面的局部理论推广至全局情形，并放宽了对初值正则性的要求。

Abstract: Consider the three-dimensional primitive equations subject to Neumann wind stress driven by finitely many Brownian motions. For arbitrary smooth spatial wind profiles, global pathwise existence and uniqueness without assuming a vertical derivative of the initial datum is proved, extending the local theory of Binz, Hieber, Hussein, and Saal~\cite{BHHS-24} for this class of data and wind profiles. The proof combines a local maximal $\rL^2$ regularity construction with weighted estimates for the boundary stochastic convolution.

</details>


### [170] [Positive radial ground states for nonlinear biharmonic equations: maximum principles and expanding-domain approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11773)
*Daniele Cassani,Zhisu Liu,Giuulio Romani,Antonio Tarsia*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明一类$\mathbb R^N$上非线性双调和方程非负径向基态解的存在性，涵盖次临界与临界非线性情形，并给出正性结论与反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性双调和方程径向基态解的存在性与正性，特别是临界非线性下解在原点的正则性/正性问题，并厘清 Cassani-Tarsia 齐次最大值原理的可推广边界。

Method: 以扩张球上夹紧（clamped）基态解取极限构造解；用 Cassani-Tarsia 齐次最大值原理保证逼近问题解的正性；在径向 Nehari 流形上作变分分解以排除任意正半径球面上的零点；当四阶算子可分解为两个具正预解式的二阶算子时进一步提升原点处正性；并构造显式反例。

Result: 得到 $\mathbb R^N\setminus\{0\}$ 上为正的极限基态解；在算子分解情形下证得全空间 $u>0$（含原点）；并给出反例表明齐次最大值原理不能推广到带任意非齐次边界数据的超解，即使零阶系数任意大也成立。

Conclusion: 所给变分-极值原理结合的方法可建立双调和径向基态解的非负性与正性，并明确了齐次最大值原理的适用范围限制。

Abstract: We prove the existence of nonnegative radial ground states for a class of nonlinear biharmonic equations in $\mathbb R^N$, covering subcritical and critical nonlinearities. The solutions are obtained as limits of clamped ground states on expanding balls. The Cassani-Tarsia homogeneous maximum principle in [D. Cassani. A. Tarsia, Adv. Nonlinear Anal. 11 (2022)] makes the approximating states positive. A variational decomposition on the radial Nehari manifold then excludes every zero sphere of positive radius, so the limiting ground state is positive on $\mathbb R^N\setminus\{0\}$. When the fourth order operator factorises into two second-order operators with positive resolvents, the conclusion upgrades to $u>0$ throughout $\mathbb R^N$, including at the origin. We also give an explicit counterexample showing that the tempting extension of the homogeneous maximum principle to super-solutions with arbitrary nonhomogeneous boundary data is false, even with an arbitrarily large positive zeroth-order coefficient.

</details>


### [171] [Isolation of scalar Allen-Cahn local minimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11797)
*Nam Q. Le*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明有界Lipschitz区域上带双势阱与齐次Neumann边界条件的Allen-Cahn泛函的标量局部极小元在L¹拓扑下是孤立的，肯定回答了Kohn-Sternberg(1989)的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn与Sternberg于1989年提出该问题，即此类局部极小元是否在L¹拓扑下孤立。这一问题关系到Allen-Cahn型相变模型中极小元的稳定性与结构，具有重要理论意义。

Method: 借鉴Jiang-Yu与Hirsch-Smith关于单调动力系统理论的思想，在椭圆型框架下实施解析的Lyapunov-Schmidt约化方法。

Result: 证明了Allen-Cahn泛函的标量局部极小元在L¹拓扑下是孤立的。

Conclusion: 肯定回答了Kohn-Sternberg提出的问题，表明该类局部极小元具有孤立性质，为相关变分问题的稳定性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We show that scalar local minimizers of the Allen-Cahn functional with the double-well potential and homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions on a bounded Lipschitz domain are isolated in the $L^1$-topology. This affirmatively answers a question raised by Kohn and Sternberg (Local minimisers and singular perturbations, Proc. Roy. Soc. Edinburgh Sect. A 111 (1989)). The proof implements an analytic Lyapunov-Schmidt reduction inspired by the theory of monotone dynamical systems in works of Jiang-Yu and Hirsch-Smith in an elliptic setting.

</details>


### [172] [Global $W^{2,p}$ and $C^{2,α}$ regularity for the sigma-$2$ equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11909)
*Yasheng Lyu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了σ₂方程连续2-凸解在严格均值凸C³域及C³边界条件下的正则性结果，并给出两个反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究σ₂方程解的正则性问题，特别是探究经典C^{2,α}正则性结论所需的最优光滑性假设。}

Method: 假设密度为正连续、域为有界严格均值凸C³域、边界值为C³，证明连续2-凸解属于每个W^{2,p}（1<p<∞），任意维数；若密度为C^α，则解属于C^{2,α}。并构造两个反例。

Result: 在给定假设下，解具有W^{2,p}（任意1<p<∞）正则性；密度为C^α时提升至C^{2,α}。反例表明域与边界值的C³假设一般不能弱化为C^{2,1}。

Conclusion: σ₂方程解的正则性依赖于域与边界值的C³光滑性，且该假设在一般情况下是最优的（不能放松到C^{2,1}）。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove that continuous $2$-convex solutions of the $σ_{2}$ equation with positive continuous density on bounded strictly mean-convex $C^{3}$ domains with $C^{3}$ boundary values belong to $W^{2,p}$ for every $1<p<\infty$ in every dimension. If, in addition, the density is $C^α$, $0<α<1$, the solutions belong to $C^{2,α}$. Moreover, two counterexamples show that neither the $C^{3}$ assumption on the domain nor that on the boundary values can in general be weakened to $C^{2,1}$.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [EVTradeMatch: A Mobility-Aware Multi-Objective Matching Framework for EV--EV Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10551)
*Md. Mahfujur Rahman,Alistair Barros,Raja Jurdak,Darshika Koggalahewa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出EVTradeMatch，一个预测引导的多目标优化框架，用于移动感知的电动汽车（EV）间能量交易，通过NSGA-II在广域动态场景下近似Pareto高效解，显著提升转移能量、充电节点适配度与匹配覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限充电基础设施下EV间能量交易可提升充电灵活性，但有效的EV–EV交易需要在行程特定条件下协调供给者–消费者匹配。现有邻近法与拍卖法等SOTA方法难以同时兼顾匹配覆盖、转移能量与充电节点适配度等多目标。

Method: 基于先前EVNextTrade的learning-to-rank充电节点推荐模型，定义充电节点适配度为预测得分（反映将供给–消费对分配到候选节点在移动性、能量与上下文交易特征上的适宜性），并作为引导信号与显式优化目标而非硬选择规则。将EV–EV匹配建模为多目标混合整数线性规划（MILP），在空间、时间、一对一匹配与节点容量约束下最大化匹配覆盖、转移能量与节点适配度并最小化移动成本。为在广域动态场景近似Pareto高效解，设计定制的非支配排序遗传算法II（NSGA-II）。

Result: 相比邻近法与拍卖法SOTA，转移能量提升74.2–82.8%，充电节点适配度提升8.3–84.4%，匹配覆盖率提升3.74–25.07个百分点。均衡NSGA-II解实现匹配覆盖率53.07±0.76%，转移能量1701.94±17.03 kWh，移动成本相对最小化行程法更高，作为显式权衡。Pareto前沿分析显示可按运营优先级在高覆盖、高能量、低移动成本与高适配度解之间灵活选择。

Conclusion: EVTradeMatch将预测引导信号与多目标优化结合，有效支撑移动感知的EV–EV能量交易，并提供Pareto前沿以支持面向运营优先级的灵活决策。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer energy trading among electric vehicles (EVs) can improve charging flexibility under limited charging infrastructure, but effective EV--EV trading requires coordinated provider--consumer matching under journey-specific conditions. This paper proposes EVTradeMatch, a prediction-guided multi-objective optimization framework for mobility-aware EV--EV energy trading. Building on the EVNextTrade study, a prior learning-to-rank model for charging-node recommendation, we define charging-node suitability as a prediction-derived score reflecting the appropriateness of assigning a provider--consumer pair to a candidate charging node based on mobility, energy, and contextual trading features. This score is used as a guidance signal and as an explicit optimization objective rather than as a hard selection rule. The EV--EV matching problem is formulated as a multi-objective mixed-integer linear program that maximizes matching coverage, transferred energy, and charging-node suitability while minimizing mobility cost under spatial, temporal, one-to-one matching, and charging-node capacity constraints. To approximate Pareto-efficient solutions in wide-area dynamic settings, we develop a tailored non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II). Experimental results show that EVTradeMatch improves transferred energy by 74.2--82.8% and charging-node suitability by 8.3--84.4% compared with proximity- and auction-based state-of-the-art methods, while improving matching coverage by 3.74--25.07 percentage points. Balanced NSGA-II solutions achieve 53.07$\pm$0.76% matching coverage and transfer 1701.94$\pm$17.03 kWh, with higher mobility cost as an explicit trade-off against travel-minimizing methods. Pareto-front analysis shows that the framework supports flexible selection among high-coverage, high-energy, low-mobility-cost, and high-suitability solutions according to operational priorities.

</details>


### [174] [Disturbance rejection for classes of nonlinear systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10553)
*Saverio Messineo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两类非线性系统的非自适应全局鲁棒抗扰问题，基于高增益与滑模范式提出控制架构，实现闭环ISS与可任意缩小的吸引子收敛，并延伸至输出反馈情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对严格反馈型系统及最小相位不确定非线性系统的全局鲁棒抗扰，特别是存在不可测零动态、不可匹配扰动、输出相关非线性及未知扰动下的输出反馈问题，仍缺乏非自适应解决方案；经典动态扩展因未知扰动不可实现。

Method: 对C1类（严格反馈形式、线性Hurwitz零动态不可测、含不可匹配加性扰动）采用高增益范式控制架构，证明闭环关于扰动输入到状态稳定（ISS），并加入基于滑模范式的均匀有界附加控制单元以降低高增益开销。对C2类（最小相位、不确定、相对阶大于一、输出相关非线性可能无界及其导数无界、含匹配加性扰动），用开环观测器替代经典动态扩展，并将C1结果适配到C2，构造输出反馈动态控制器。

Result: C1类系统在不可匹配扰动下实现闭环ISS及全局渐近收敛到可任意缩小的吸引子，且控制作用一致有界；C2类系统实现闭环全局一致有界，并渐近调节到可任意缩小的吸引子。

Conclusion: 所提高增益加滑模、以及开环观测器加动态输出的非自适应控制架构，可统一处理两类非线性系统的全局鲁棒抗扰，兼具ISS/一致有界性与可调精度收敛。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of non-adaptive global robust disturbance rejection for two distinct classes of nonlinear systems. The first class, denoted by C1, consists of nonlinear systems in strict-feedback form, with linear and Hurwitz zero-dynamics (whose states are unavailable for feedback), and enhanced - within this work - by forcing, unmatched, additive disturbances. Nonlinear tools are herein employed to demonstrate that the proposed control architecture - based on the high-gain paradigm - achieves closed-loop input-to-state stability with respect to the forcing disturbances, along with global asymptotic convergence towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired. Then, owing to the established input-to-state stability property, a uniformly bounded control action is additionally embedded within the control architecture. The additional unit, designed following the sliding-mode paradigm, is aimed at improving the disturbance rejection task, by potentially lowering the required high-gain control expenditure. The second class of systems, denoted by C2, is constituted by minimum-phase, uncertain, nonlinear systems with relative degree greater than one, featuring possibly unbounded, with possibly unbounded derivatives, output-dependent nonlinearities, with matched additive forcing disturbances. To solve the problem of output-feedback, non-adaptive, global robust disturbance rejection for systems within C2, first, an open-loop observer is employed in lieu of a classic dynamic extension adopted in earlier works, as the latter is no longer implementable due to the presence of unknown forcing disturbances. Subsequently, the results derived for C1 are then adapted to C2, to yield an output-feedback dynamic controller providing closed-loop global uniform boundedness, along with asymptotic regulation towards an attractor which can be rendered as small as desired.

</details>


### [175] [Constructing Pareto Sets Using Noether's Second Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10555)
*Evgeny Nikulchev*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文基于Noether第二定理提出一种构造多目标变分控制问题Pareto集的分析方法，揭示系统规范对称性与Pareto集结构之间的基本联系，并提出规范正则化以约简解空间维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标变分控制问题中Pareto集的构造通常计算量大、解空间维度高，缺乏将系统对称性与优化结构相联系的解析工具。本文旨在建立动力学系统对称性与Pareto前沿之间的理论桥梁，以简化Pareto集的求解。

Method: 采用基于Noether第二定理的解析方法：建立描述被控对象的动力学系统的规范对称性与Pareto集结构之间的基本联系；将Pareto前沿解释为由系统及质量准则关于某对称群的不变性所导出的守恒律；提出"规范正则化"概念，在保持Pareto集不变的前提下消除不影响准则的变量，从而降低解空间维度。

Result: 理论上证明了Pareto前沿可视为系统不变性对应的守恒律；规范正则化能够在不改变Pareto集的情况下剔除冗余变量、降低解空间维度。数值上，在具有两个冲突准则的线性二次调节器（LQR）问题和标准ZDT1测试问题上进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作表明Noether第二定理可为多目标变分控制问题提供解析化的Pareto集构造框架，规范对称性与Pareto前沿存在内在对应关系，规范正则化是一种有效的解空间降维手段，并具有推广至更一般多目标优化问题的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents an analytical approach to constructing the Pareto set in multiobjective variational control problems, based on Noether's second theorem. A fundamental connection is established between the gauge symmetries of the dynamical system describing the plant and the structure of the Pareto set. It is shown that the Pareto front can be interpreted as a conservation law arising from the invariance of the system and the quality criteria with respect to a symmetry group. A gauge regularization concept is proposed, which allows eliminating variables that do not affect the criteria without changing the Pareto set, thereby reducing the dimension of the solution space. Numerical examples are provided for the linear-quadratic regulator with two conflicting criteria and for the standard ZDT1 test problem.

</details>


### [176] [PEJWAK: A Profile-Echoing Self-Anchored Aggregation Architecture for Multi-Criteria Decision Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10562)
*Seyyed Ahmad Edalatpanah*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出PEJWAK——一种"剖面回显式自锚定"聚合架构，用于多准则决策分析；每个准则在聚合前与来自同一备选方案的内生参考进行几何耦合，从而在保留准则身份的同时让贡献受方案剖面调节，并给出理论性质与确定性审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接加权聚合过于简单、无法刻画准则间的相互依赖，而参数密集的非可加交互模型（如模糊积分类）又难以标定与解释。本文试图在两者之间提供一种可审计、剖面条件化的聚合机制，兼顾准则身份保留与交互表达能力。

Method: 提出通用形式化框架，将锚函数、锚定核、保留指数、贡献系数与外层聚合规则分离；典型成员采用加权算术自锚、以准则重要性作为保留指数、并以归一化平方根映射导出贡献系数。理论上推导连续性、单调性、对角标定、内部性、全定义域联合连续性、精确边界行为、不可分性、等权二元限制下双对称性失效，以及精确对数保留律。同时给出确定性MCDA审计流程，包含准则级完全分解、精确权重稳定区间与秩跃迁、贡献规则秩相位、集合依赖性诊断及基于排列的精确秩亲和度分析。

Result: 典型PEJWAK无需ε正则化即可处理精确零值：当其余活跃得分可支撑正锚时，活跃零值仅移除其自身准则贡献。理论上明确了该架构的非可加性特征（不可分、双对称性失效）与保留律；审计工具可输出权重稳定区间、秩跃迁与排列秩亲和度等精确诊断结果。

Conclusion: PEJWAK在直接加权聚合与参数密集型非可加交互模型之间，提供了一种可审计、剖面条件化的聚合机制，兼具准则身份保留、内生锚定与精确零值鲁棒性。

Abstract: PEJWAK is introduced as a profile-echoing self-anchored aggregation architecture for multi-criteria decision analysis. Each criterion is coupled geometrically with an endogenous reference derived from the same alternative before final aggregation, so criterion identity is preserved while its expressed contribution is conditioned by the alternative's profile. A general formulation separates the anchor function, anchoring kernel, retention exponents, contribution coefficients, and outer aggregation rule. The canonical member uses a weighted-arithmetic self-anchor, sets each retention exponent equal to the corresponding criterion-importance value, and derives contribution coefficients through a normalized square-root map. Analytical results establish continuity, monotonicity, diagonal calibration, internality, full-domain joint continuity, exact boundary behavior, nonseparability, failure of bisymmetry in the equal-weight binary restriction, and an exact logarithmic retention law. A deterministic MCDA audit provides complete criterion-level decomposition, exact weight-stability intervals and rank transitions, contribution-rule rank phases, set-dependence diagnostics, and exact permutation-based rank-affinity analysis. Canonical PEJWAK handles exact zeros without epsilon regularization: an active zero removes only its own criterion contribution whenever the remaining active scores sustain a positive anchor. The architecture therefore supplies an auditable profile-conditioned aggregation mechanism between direct weighted aggregation and parameter-intensive nonadditive interaction models.

</details>


### [177] [Supply Chain Analytics: A Data-Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10563)
*Elioth Sanabria*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将统计学习与鲁棒决策框架融合的供应链分析体系，涵盖需求预测、库存与网络控制、分布鲁棒优化（DRO）及车辆路径列生成等方法，为数据驱动的韧性供应链系统提供理论与算法蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链网络依赖实时数据应对结构不确定性、市场波动与运营中断，然而统计型数据驱动学习与鲁棒决策框架之间存在脱节，需弥合二者鸿沟。

Method: 以数学严谨的方式系统处理供应链分析：从经验需求预测出发，结合样本最小化、时变库存补货的动态规划递推、网络履约框架，以及基于传输理论的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）以对冲稀有事件；并整合车辆路径的列生成与非齐次排队等方法。

Result: 构建了将预测性统计模型与规范性控制算法相整合的方法体系，形成面向不确定性的库存与网络控制工具集。

Conclusion: 该研究可作为在机器学习、数学优化与应用概率交叉领域的研究者与从业者的理论蓝图与算法指南，支撑韧性与数据驱动的自动化系统设计。

Abstract: Modern supply chain networks increasingly rely on real-time data to navigate structural uncertainties, market volatility, and operational disruptions. This manuscript bridges the gap between statistical data-driven learning and robust decision-making frameworks in logistics and operations management. We present a comprehensive, mathematically rigorous treatment of supply chain analytics, moving from empirical demand forecasting to optimal inventory and network control under uncertainty.
