Key topics explored include sample minimization, dynamic programming recursions for time-varying inventory replenishment, network fulfillment frameworks, and advanced distributionally robust optimization (DRO) via transport theory to hedge against rare events. By integrating predictive statistical models with prescriptive control algorithms, such as column generation for vehicle routing and non-homogeneous queueing regimes, this text provides the foundational tools necessary for designing resilient, data-driven automated systems. It serves as both a theoretical blueprint and an algorithmic guide for researchers and practitioners operating at the intersection of machine learning, mathematical optimization, and applied probability.

</details>


### [178] [Operator Splitting Methods with Online Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10732)
*Wanyu Zhang,Wenzhi Gao,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个自动调优算子分裂方法预条件子的框架，将前向-后向分裂、Douglas-Rachford分裂和ADMM解释为在其包络函数上的预条件梯度下降，并用在线学习自适应学习预条件子，从而获得渐近更快的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算子分裂方法的性能高度依赖预条件子的选择，而人工调参困难且缺乏原则性指导，因此需要一个能自动、自适应调优预条件子的通用框架。

Method: 将前向-后向分裂、Douglas-Rachford分裂与ADMM统一刻画为对相应包络函数的预条件梯度下降，并借助在线学习在优化过程中自适应地学习预条件子。

Result: 理论上获得渐近更快的收敛速率；在Lasso、ℓ1正则化逻辑回归和二次规划等问题上加速求解；大量数值实验验证了理论结果与实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为算子分裂方法中的预条件子自动调优提供了原则性方案，兼具理论收敛保证与实际加速效果。

Abstract: This paper develops a principled framework for automatically tuning preconditioners in operator splitting methods, including forward-backward splitting, Douglas-Rachford splitting, and the alternating direction method of multipliers. We interpret these splitting methods as preconditioned gradient descent applied to their corresponding envelope functions and use online learning to adaptively learn the preconditioner during optimization. Our framework achieves asymptotically faster convergence rates and accelerates the solution of important optimization problems, including Lasso, $\ell_1$-regularized logistic regression, and quadratic programming. Extensive numerical experiments validate our theoretical results and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [179] [Method for Constrained Computational Guidance and Control with Application to Hypersonic Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10813)
*Emily M. Palmer,Anil V. Rao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于路径约束制导与控制的约束计算制导方法：通过周期性重解约束最优控制问题实现制导更新，并引入基于平滑最大值近似的惩罚项来提高扰动系统在约束边界附近的可行性与安全裕度，在可重复使用运载器最大横向航程再入问题上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在制导与控制中周期性重解约束最优控制问题时，系统受扰动后容易导致约束被违反，从而破坏最优控制问题的可行性。因此需要一种机制，在扰动系统接近约束极限时主动扩大约束与其极限之间的裕度，以保证制导方案的可行性和安全性。

Method: 采用周期性重解约束最优控制问题的方式进行制导更新；在最优控制问题中额外增加一个惩罚项，当扰动系统进入约束极限的特定邻域时，该惩罚项用于增大约束与极限之间的裕度以防止约束违反；该惩罚项基于平滑最大值（smooth maximum）近似构造；并在最大横向航程的可重复使用运载器（RLV）再入问题上进行验证，其参考解包含长时间约束弧。

Result: 在RLV最大横向航程再入问题上（参考解含长时间约束弧）的仿真结果表明，所提方法能够有效提升约束满足能力，对于路径约束下的最优制导与控制是可行的。

Conclusion: 通过在约束最优控制问题中引入基于平滑最大值近似的惩罚项来主动增大约束裕度，可改善扰动系统下最优控制问题的可行性，该方法适用于路径约束最优制导与控制。

Abstract: A method for constrained computation guidance and control is described. The method employs guidance updates by periodically re-solving a constrained optimal control problem. Feasibility of the optimal control problem is improved by preventing constraint violations in the perturbed system. Constraint violation is prevented by augmenting the optimal control problem with a penalty term intended to increase the margin between a constraint and its limit when the perturbed system is within certain proximity of the constraint limit. The additional penalty term employs a smooth maximum approximation to increase the margin between the constraint and its limit. The method developed in this paper is demonstrated on the maximum cross-range reusable launch vehicle (RLV) entry problem, for which the reference solution contains a long-duration constrained arc. The results demonstrate that the method developed in this paper is viable for path-constrained optimal guidance and control.

</details>


### [180] [A Simple Complexity Lower Bound for Solving $Ax=b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10874)
*Ohad Shamir*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出一个简短直接的证明：在最坏情况下，用相对误差 ε 近似求解 Ax=b（条件数为 κ、维度不限）需要 Ω(κ log(1/ε)) 次矩阵-向量乘法，即使对随机算法亦然；并由此推出强凸二次优化问题的经典 Ω(√κ log(1/ε)) 下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dereziński、Epperly 与 Meyer [2026] 提出了更一般的优雅方法并给出了该下界，但其证明不够简短直接。作者受此启发，希望提供一个更简洁、直接的证明。

Method: 给出一个简短且直接的证明，证明在最坏情况下、相对误差 ε、条件数 κ、维度不受限时，近似求解 Ax=b 需要 Ω(κ log(1/ε)) 次矩阵-向量乘法，且该下界对随机算法同样成立；再通过一个直截了当的归约，推广到强凸二次函数优化。

Result: 约简地重现了 Dereziński 等人 [2026] 关于该情形的下界，即 Ω(κ log(1/ε)) 次矩阵-向量乘法；同时通过直接归约得到强凸二次优化问题的经典 Ω(√κ log(1/ε)) 下界，且适用于随机算法。

Conclusion: 该文用一个简短直接的证明恢复了已有的一般性下界，并说明其可归约至强凸二次优化的经典下界，且结论对随机算法成立。

Abstract: In this note, we provide a short and direct proof that approximately solving $Ax=b$ to relative error $\varepsilon$, where $A$ has condition number $κ$ and unrestricted dimension, requires $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix-vector multiplications in the worst case, even for randomized algorithms. This essentially recovers the lower bound of Dereziński, Epperly and Meyer [2026] for this setting, whose elegant and more general approach inspired us to seek a short direct proof. A straightforward reduction implies the classical $Ω(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ lower bound for optimizing strongly convex quadratic functions, applicable to randomized algorithms.

</details>


### [181] [The Polarity Process for the Maximum-Volume Inscribed Ellipsoid Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10888)
*Kaizhao Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究针对多面体的最大体积内切椭球（MaxIE）问题，基于极性的几何迭代展开分析：给定内点，构造平移极多面体，求其最小体积覆盖椭球（MinCE），再将其极化回内切椭球。作者以极MinCE的对数体积作为势函数，证明其凸性并给出显式梯度，建立势差的全局线性收缩，从而得到独立的收敛证明与有限体积比迭代界，并进一步分析非精确极性过程与算数复杂度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Khachiyan与Todd（1993）提出的极性迭代过程此前仅有渐近收敛论证，缺少体积比意义上的收敛速率。本文旨在弥补这一空白，为该精确过程提供显式的收敛速率与有限迭代界，并延伸至子问题近似求解的实际情形。

Method: 以极化后MinCE的对数体积为势函数，证明其凸性并推导显式梯度公式，沿每条迭代轨迹建立势差的全局线性收缩（收缩因子为存在性、依赖于实例）。同时分析非精确极性过程：每个MinCE子问题仅近似求解，推导出达到指定体积精度所需的预言机容差。结合路径跟踪牛顿法、重心坐标下降法与离线步Frank-Wolfe法等MinCE算法，给出条件算数复杂度估计。

Result: 给出了独立于原有渐近论证的新收敛证明，并得到有限体积比迭代界；对非精确过程建立了充分的预言机精度条件；结合三种MinCE求解器给出算数复杂度估计；数值实验将不同预言机选择与两个MaxIE基线对比，表明性能取决于MinCE求解器与实例几何的匹配程度。

Conclusion: 该工作为MaxIE的极性迭代提供了基于势函数的收敛速率理论，将渐近收敛强化为线性收缩与有限迭代界，并拓展到非精确求解与算数复杂度分析；实验表明求解器与实例几何的匹配是影响实际性能的关键。

Abstract: We study the maximum-volume inscribed ellipsoid (MaxIE) problem for a polytope through a geometric iteration based on polarity. Given an interior point, the method forms the shifted polar polytope, computes its minimum-volume covering ellipsoid (MinCE), and then polarizes this covering ellipsoid back to obtain a new inscribed ellipsoid. This procedure was suggested by Khachiyan and Todd in 1993. Prior work studied basic polarity identities and gave an asymptotic-convergence argument for the exact process, but did not furnish a volume-ratio rate. Independently of that asymptotic argument, we develop a new analysis based on the log-volume of the polar MinCE as a potential function. We prove its convexity with an explicit gradient formula and establish global linear contraction of the potential gap along each trajectory. The contraction factor is existential and instance-dependent. This gives both a separate convergence proof and a finite volume-ratio iteration bound. We further analyze an inexact polarity process in which each MinCE subproblem is solved only approximately, and derive sufficient oracle tolerances for producing a prescribed volume approximation. Combining this outer analysis with existing algorithms for MinCE gives conditional arithmetic estimates based on the path-following Newton method, the barycentric coordinate descent, and the away-step Frank-Wolfe method. Numerical experiments compare these oracle choices with two MaxIE baselines and illustrate that performance depends on matching the MinCE solver to the instance geometry.

</details>


### [182] [Deterministic and Random Bipartite Matching on General Networks: Convex Flow Reformulation, Asymptotic Properties, and Fast Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10938)
*Yuhui Zhai,Yanfeng Ouyang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究一般网络上最小距离二分匹配问题，提出确定性问题的精确凸流重构与近似求解方法，并分析随机问题的期望匹配距离标度律与算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般网络上的最小距离二分匹配应用广泛，但精确求解计算量大，需要高效的精确重构与近似估计方法；同时随机供需分布下的匹配距离标度规律和最优流结构尚需理论刻画。

Method: 针对确定性问题，给出边可分离的凸流精确重构；引入边不平衡剖面的光滑单调重排近似以高效求解该凸规划；进一步采用基于阻力的一阶近似，导出闭式的一步拉普拉斯估计量。针对随机问题，假设供需点随机分布，分析期望最优匹配距离的标度，并利用极限阻力网络刻画供需失衡的再分配，据此设计快速近似最优流的算法。

Result: 证明当供需点分布相同时，期望最优匹配距离按点数平方根标度；分布不同时呈线性标度。分布相同时最优流被证明具有中心化、对称和次高斯性质；分布不同时，极限阻力网络刻画供需失衡再分配。数值实验表明所提估计量能较好逼近精确匹配代价并显著减少计算时间；大规模蒙特卡洛仿真验证了随机匹配解的理论性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为一般网络最小距离二分匹配提供了可高效求解的凸流框架、闭式估计量及随机标度理论，兼顾理论刻画与计算效率。

Abstract: Minimum-distance bipartite matching on general networks has numerous applications various fields. This paper first focuses on deterministic problems and presents an exact edgewise-separable convex-flow reformulation. By introducing a smooth monotone rearrangement approximation of the edge-wise imbalance profiles, the convex-flow reformulation's can be solved efficiently. If we further conduct a first-order resistance-based approximation of the convex program, a one-step Laplacian-based estimator can be analytically derived in closed forms. The paper also studies random problems where supply and demand points are randomly distributed. We show that the expected optimal matching distance scales with the square root of the number of points if the supply/demand point distributions are identical, or linearly otherwise. In the former case, the optimal flow is proven to be centered, symmetric, and sub-Gaussian. In the latter case, the limiting resistance network characterizes how supply-demand imbalance is redistributed and motivates a fast algorithm that approximate the optimal flow based on the limiting resistance. Numerical experiments show that the proposed estimators closely approximate the exact matching cost while substantially reducing computation time. The proven theoretical properties of the random matching solution are numerically verified by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [183] [Conformal-DRO: Distributionally Robust Optimization with Conformalized Ambiguity Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11073)
*Luhao Zhang,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Conformal-DRO，用嵌套共形区域为未来潜在分布构造模糊集，在有限样本下以至少 1-α 概率覆盖潜在法则，规避了对潜在法则及其混合机制的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据驱动分布鲁棒优化把条件结果法则视为固定，仅用模糊集刻画估计误差。本文关注潜在分布异质性：每个样本有未观测的法则却只贡献一个观测，因此即使混合法则已知，不确定性仍存在，需要有别于估计误差的稳健化工具。

Method: 提出 Conformal-DRO：利用嵌套共形区域为未来潜在法则构造模糊集；在可交换性假设下该集合以至少 1-α 概率覆盖该法则，无需估计潜在法则或混合机制；共形路径诱导数据驱动的传输几何，α 决定半径；最坏情况问题约化为共形层上的有限线性规划，并具有稀疏对抗解。

Result: 在可交换性下，所构造的模糊集对潜在法则具有有限样本覆盖保证（≥1-α）；最坏情况问题可归约为有限线性规划且对抗解稀疏；所得稳健值给出所选决策期望代价的有限样本证书。

Conclusion: Conformal-DRO 将共形推断与分布鲁棒优化结合，在潜在分布异质性且单观测限制下提供有限样本稳健决策，无需估计潜在法则及混合机制，并保持计算可处理性与稀疏结构。

Abstract: Data-driven distributionally robust optimization (DRO) typically treats the conditional outcome law as fixed and uses ambiguity sets to capture estimation error. This paper studies latent distributional heterogeneity, where each instance has an unobserved law but contributes only one observation, so uncertainty persists even if the mixture law is known. We propose Conformal-DRO, which uses nested conformal regions to construct an ambiguity set for the future latent law. Under exchangeability, the set covers this law with probability at least $1-α$ in finite samples, without estimating underlying latent laws or their mixing mechanism. The conformal path induces a data-driven transport geometry, while $α$ determines the radius. The worst-case problem reduces to a finite linear program over conformal shells and admits sparse adversarial solutions. The resulting robust value provides a finite-sample certificate for the selected decision's expected cost.

</details>


### [184] [The best approximation tuple: an extension of the Cheney-Goldstein algorithm and results to the multiple sets case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11105)
*Yair Censor,Daniel Reem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文推广Cheney-Goldstein最佳逼近对(BAP)问题：处理多于两个集合，并允许每个集合为有限族集合的交，提出"最佳逼近元组(BAT)"问题；通过区分"枢轴集"与"卫星集"避免循环与泛函极小化障碍，在卫星集严格凸紧的欧氏情形下证明算法收敛到唯一解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典BAP问题仅涉及两个集合，且已有算法依赖对目标泛函极小化(循环)的刻画，难以直接推广到多集合或交集结构；作者希望突破这一理论障碍，将问题扩展为多集合及集合族交形式的BAT问题。

Method: 核心思想是不再寻求作为目标泛函极小值的循环，而是把其中一个集合视为"枢轴集"，其余视为"卫星集"，基于正交投影的非扩张性中关于等式与不等式的性质(非标准性质)设计并分析算法，处理缺乏Fejér单调性的情形。

Result: 在欧氏空间、卫星集严格凸且紧的假设下，证明所提算法收敛到BAT问题的唯一解；收敛分析因缺少Fejér单调性而非常规，依赖于正交投影在非扩张性等式/不等式刻画下的若干鲜为人知的性质。

Conclusion: 该工作从两方向推广了Cheney-Goldstein的BAP理论，引入并求解BAT问题，提供了新的收敛性分析工具，克服了循环与泛函极小化相关的理论障碍。

Abstract: In this paper we extend the algorithm and several results published in the celebrated 1959 paper of Cheney and Goldstein about the best approximation pair (BAP) problem in two separate directions. One is the consideration of more than two sets. The other is the ability to handle each set as an intersections of a finite family of sets. We call the resulting problem the "Best Approximation Tuple (BAT) problem". The fundamental observation that leads to this generalizations is to recognize and handle one set (the "pivot set") as different from the remaining sets (the "satellite sets") instead of seeking cycles as the minimizers of a target functional. This enable us to overcome a certain theoretical obstacle related to cycles and minimizers of general functionals. We prove the convergence of the algorithm to the unique solution of the problem in the Euclidean case with strictly convex and compact satellite sets. Because of the lack of Fejér monotonicity, our convergence analysis is not standard, and is based on almost unknown properties of orthogonal projections regarding equality and inequality in the definition of nonexpansiveness.

</details>


### [185] [Cooperative Integer Programming Games: Core Stability and Optimal Coalition Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11116)
*Hyunwoo Lee,Robert Hildebrand,I. Esra Buyuktahtakin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出合作整数规划博弈(CIPG)，用整数规划刻画联盟特征函数，给出求最优且稳定联盟结构的MIP模型与割平面算法，在合作背包博弈上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合作博弈研究多假设特征函数可解析或易计算，而在预算汇集、不可分割任务的现实场景中，特征函数本身是一个整数规划最优值，导致联盟价值计算困难、联盟结构求解与稳定性判定复杂。因此需要针对这类CIPG建模并设计可扩展的求解方法。

Method: 1) 定义CIPG：各代理汇集预算约束共同完成不可分任务，特征函数为每个联盟对应的汇集整数规划最优值；目标是求最优联盟结构(OCS)与稳定联盟结构(OSCS)。2) 推导稳定性不等式，使每个已形成联盟相对自身处于Core中。3) 提出聚合与解聚合两种混合整数OCS模型，证明解聚合模型整数等价且LP松弛更紧。4) 基于稳定性不等式构造提升稳定性割(lifted stability cuts)、割平面算法中的多种分离策略、保证稳定性的SCS可行原始启发式用于热启动，并加入收益精化步骤计算各联盟的Shapley值与nucleolus。

Result: 在基准合作背包博弈上，方法可对至多16个参与者证明最优性；30个参与者时MIP间隙低于1%，且仅评估约10^9个联盟值中的766个。

Conclusion: CIPG框架将联盟结构优化与稳定性分析统一到整数规划与割平面求解体系中，解聚合模型与提升割有效提升松弛质量，算法在计算量与规模上具备良好可扩展性，为预算汇集型合作问题提供了可证明最优且稳定的求解途径。

Abstract: We introduce cooperative integer programming games (CIPGs), in which agents pool budget constraints to accomplish indivisible tasks jointly and the characteristic function maps every coalition to the optimal value of a pooled integer program. Our goal is to identify an optimal coalition structure (OCS) and a stable one (OSCS). We derive a stability inequality that keeps each formed coalition in the Core with respect to itself, and present two mixed-integer OCS formulations, aggregated and disaggregated, proving that the disaggregated formulation is integer-equivalent yet yields a tighter LP relaxation. Building on the stability inequality we develop lifted stability cuts, several separation strategies inside a cutting-plane algorithm, an SCS-feasible primal heuristic that constructs warm starts with guaranteed stability, and a payoff-refinement step computing the Shapley value and the nucleolus of every formed coalition. On benchmark cooperative knapsack games, the method certifies optimality with up to 16 players and reaches MIP gaps below 1% at 30 players while evaluating 766 of the roughly $10^9$ coalition values.

</details>


### [186] [Minimal null control time of some 1D linear hyperbolic balance laws with constant coefficients and properties of related kernel equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11118)
*Long Hu,Guillaume Olive*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究一维常系数双曲平衡律的单侧边界零可控性，证明最小零控制时间由特定序列的正交条件决定，并给出需检验正交条件数目的显式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画一维双曲平衡律在单侧边界控制下可达零可控所需的最小时间，揭示其与系统系数所决定的正交条件之间的联系，并给出可操作的判定准则。

Method: 对系统相关的核方程进行细致分析，建立新的适定性结果；利用由非线性递推关系显式定义的序列，分析其正交条件对最小零控制时间的影响。

Result: 当系统仅含一个负速度或正速度时，最小零控制时间取决于特定序列的正交条件；在两类非平凡情形下给出了必须检验的正交条件数目的显式界。

Conclusion: 所得结果也可用于有限时间镇定问题，为单侧边界控制下双曲平衡律的零可控与镇定提供了新的理论依据与可验证条件。

Abstract: In this work, we study the null controllability by one-sided boundary controls of one-dimensional hyperbolic balance laws with constant coefficients. Our first result shows that, when the system has only one negative or positive speed, the minimal null control time of such systems depends on some orthogonality conditions for a particular sequence. This sequence is explicit in function of the coefficients of the system but it is defined by a nonlinear recurrence relation. Our second result then completes the previous one by giving explicit bounds on the number of orthogonality conditions that have to be checked in two nontrivial situations. The proofs rely on a careful analysis of the so-called kernel equations associated with the system, including a new well-posedness result. Our results are also valid for the finite-time stabilization property.

</details>


### [187] [A sequential smoothing majorant stochastic approximation method for nonconvex nonconcave minimax problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11171)
*Zhouxing Luo,Wei Liu,Xin Liu,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出序贯平滑优majorant随机逼近(SMSA)方法求解非凸-非凹minimax优化，通过新的majorant随机逼近将精度界从O(β_N)改进到O(β_N²)，并建立全局最优值与极小元集的非渐近界及Clarke稳定点的相合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸-非凹minimax优化中，内层最大化问题非凹，标准随机逼近的近似精度仅O(β_N)，逼近保真度不足，亟需更精确的majorant随机逼近模型与相应算法。

Method: 提出序贯平滑优majorant随机逼近(SMSA)方法，引入新的majorant随机逼近，其精度界为O(β_N²)；建立全局最优值与极小元集的非渐近界，证明Clarke稳定点的相合性(当β_N↓0几乎必然)；证明生成序列有界，返回点为majorant随机逼近模型的近似Clarke稳定点。

Result: 精度界由O(β_N)改进为O(β_N²)；得到全局最优值与极小元集的非渐近界并证明Clarke稳定点相合性；序列有界且返回点为近似Clarke稳定点；在合成示例与两个UCI数据集的鲁棒logistic回归上，相比标准采样逼近具有更高逼近保真度与更低平均鲁棒测试损失。

Conclusion: SMSA通过O(β_N²)精度的majorant随机逼近有效克服内层非凹性，理论保证完备，实验验证其在逼近保真度与鲁棒测试损失上优于标准采样逼近。

Abstract: We propose a sequential smoothing majorant stochastic approximation (SMSA) method for nonconvex-nonconcave minimax optimization problems. To overcome the nonconcavity of the inner maximization problem, we introduce a new majorant stochastic approximation with an $O\left(β_N^2\right)$ accuracy bound, where $β_N$ is the sample coverage radius. The accuracy bound significantly improves upon the $O\left(β_N\right)$ approximation bound for the standard stochastic approximation. Moreover, we establish nonasymptotic bounds for both global optimal values and minimizer sets, and prove consistency for the Clarke stationary points, as $β_N \downarrow 0$ almost surely. We show that the generated sequence by the SMSA method is bounded, and that the returned point is an approximate Clarke stationary point of the majorant stochastic approximation models. Numerical experiments on a synthetic toy example and robust logistic regression on two UCI datasets demonstrate improved approximation fidelity and lower mean robust test losses relative to the standard sampled approximation.

</details>


### [188] [Single-Loop Gradient Algorithms for Pessimistic Bilevel Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11183)
*Qichao Cao,Bo Zeng,Shangzhi Zeng,Jin Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对研究较少的悲观双层优化（PBO），通过重构、惩罚与正则化构造光滑近似，并建立极小点与稳定性意义下的收敛保证；进而提出分别面向确定性与随机PBO的单环、纯一阶梯度算法，避免二阶导与内层子问题求解，给出非渐近收敛速率。合成与实际问题实验（含垃圾邮件分类与SPO）表明算法高效，并显示PBO相对经典乐观双层优化的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乐观双层优化已有大量进展，而悲观双层优化（PBO）研究明显不足，尤其缺乏完全一阶、单环、基于梯度的方法。该空白限制了PBO在实际建模中提供稳健解的能力。

Method: 通过对PBO进行重构、惩罚与正则化，构造其光滑近似，并在极小点与稳定性两类准则下建立收敛保证；在此基础上设计两个单环算法，分别处理确定性PBO与随机PBO。算法仅使用一阶梯度信息，不依赖二阶导数，也不需求解内层子问题。

Result: 为所提算法建立了非渐近收敛速率，提供理论保证；在合成与实际问题上进行系统实证研究，验证算法的高效性与有效性；在垃圾邮件分类和Smart Predict-then-Optimize任务上，PBO相较经典乐观双层优化对应方法表现出优势，并给出更稳健的解。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了PBO在完全一阶、单环梯度方法上的空白，证明PBO在实践建模中具有较强潜力，可产生比乐观双层优化更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Bilevel optimization has recently attracted growing attention, particularly in the development of efficient numerical methods. Despite substantial progress on optimistic bilevel optimization, pessimistic bilevel optimization (PBO) remains much less explored, especially the design of fully first-order, single-loop gradient-based methods. To address this gap, we propose a smooth approximation of PBO through reformulation, penalization and regularization, and establish convergence guarantees in terms of both minimizers and stationarity. Building on this framework, we then develop two single-loop algorithms for deterministic and stochastic PBOs, respectively. Both use only first-order gradient information and avoid second-order derivatives and inner-loop subproblem solves. Non-asymptotic convergence rates for the proposed algorithms are established to provide theoretical guarantees. Through a systematic empirical study of both synthetic and practical problem instances, we demonstrate that our algorithms are highly effective and efficient. In particular, our results on spam classification and Smart Predict-then-Optimize further illustrate PBO's advantages over its classical optimistic bilevel counterpart, highlighting its strong potential for practical modeling and the delivery of robust solutions.

</details>


### [189] [Optimal Control for Cancer Chemotherapy Using Hybrid Quantum Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11197)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于正则化的混合量子粒子群优化（QPSO）方法，用于求解癌症化疗最优控制问题，以克服经典庞特里亚金极大值原理（PMP）对协态方程初值猜测的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肿瘤异质性与突变使化疗治疗的有效性难以保证；传统方法（如PMP）因依赖协态方程的初始猜测，在精度与收敛性上存在不足。

Method: 采用QPSO进行全局搜索以逼近最优控制轨迹，随后进行基于正则化的精细化处理以保证轨迹光滑性并满足最优性条件；再利用哈密顿函数进行一阶与二阶最优性检验以验证解的质量。

Result: 数值案例研究考察了多种药物效力函数，结果表明周期性给药与局部化给药在实现稳健的肿瘤抑制中具有作用，并揭示了不同药物组合对最优化疗策略的影响。

Conclusion: 该正则化混合QPSO方法为化疗最优控制提供了一种规避PMP初值难题的可行途径，并为给药方式与药物组合策略设计提供了洞见。

Abstract: Optimal control in cancer chemotherapy is challenged by tumor heterogeneity and mutations, which complicate treatment effectiveness. Traditional methods, such as Pontryagin's maximum principle (PMP), are often hindered by their reliance on an initial guess for the costate equation, affecting accuracy and convergence. To address these limitations, this work introduces a hybrid quantum particle swarm optimization (QPSO) method based on regularization. QPSO is employed for global exploration to approximate the optimal control trajectory, followed by a regularization-based refinement to ensure smoothness and consistency with optimality conditions. The Hamiltonian function is used for first- and second-order optimality checks, verifying solution quality. Numerical case studies explore various drug effectiveness functions, demonstrating the role of periodic and localized drug delivery in achieving robust tumor suppression and providing insights into the impact of different drug combinations on optimal chemotherapy strategies.

</details>


### [190] [Optimal chemo-immunotherapy scheduling: A hybrid QPSO-SQP approach with Michaelis-Menten pharmacodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11213)
*Bereket Sitotaw Kidane,Md Samiul Haque Motayed,Shuo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 将Michaelis-Menten饱和药效直接嵌入治疗通道，使动力学变为非控制仿射型，得到显式三区间逐点极小化律；采用QPSO-SQP混合算法求解非凸转录问题，并用协态重构验证PMP结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联合化疗-免疫治疗的最优调度被建模为控制仿射最优控制问题，通常产生边界选择型（bang-bang型）方案，这种结构虽便于处理，却忽略了高剂量率下的饱和药效动力学，导致方案不够真实。

Method: 将Michaelis-Menten饱和直接纳入治疗通道，使系统动力学非控制仿射、哈密顿量对每个控制呈非线性；在严格正暴露惩罚下推导哈密顿量的显式三区间逐点极小化律（下界、上界、唯一内点极小值）。内点区间满足严格Legendre条件，连续调制给药作为正则内点极值出现而非奇异弧或平滑伪迹。因转录问题非凸，采用QPSO-SQP混合流程：QPSO提供约束感知的暖启动，SQP在配点网格上保证可行性与KKT最优性；并通过协态重构验证PMP结构。

Result: 哈密顿量存在唯一内点极小值，内点区间满足严格Legendre条件，连续调制给药是正则内点极值；协态重构证实了PMP结构及预测的边界/内点区间切换；混合QPSO-SQP流程可在非凸转录下获得满足KKT最优性的解。

Conclusion: 考虑饱和药效动力学会从根本上改变最优联合治疗方案的结构，使其从bang-bang型转变为包含连续调制给药的正则内点极值；所提出的QPSO-SQP混合算法结合协态重构，能够有效求解并验证此类非控制仿射、非凸最优控制问题。

Abstract: Optimal scheduling of combined chemo-immunotherapy is often formulated as a control-affine optimal control problem, which generically yields boundary-selected (bang-bang-type) protocols unless singular arcs occur. This structure, while convenient, neglects saturating pharmacodynamics at high dose rates. We incorporate Michaelis-Menten saturation directly into the therapy channels, making the dynamics non-control-affine and the Hamiltonian nonlinear in each control. With strictly positive exposure penalties, the Hamiltonian admits an explicit three-regime pointwise minimization law: each input is chosen at the lower bound, the upper bound, or as a unique interior minimizer. On interior intervals the strict Legendre condition holds, so continuously modulated dosing arises as a regular interior extremal rather than a singular-arc or smoothing artifact. The resulting transcription is nonconvex; we therefore use a hybrid Quantum Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO)-Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) pipeline, where QPSO provides a constraint-aware warm start and SQP enforces feasibility and Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) optimality on a collocation grid. Costate reconstruction corroborates the Pontryagin Minimum Principle (PMP) structure and the predicted boundary/interior regime transitions.

</details>


### [191] [Sequential Extremal Principle: Refinements and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11232)
*Nguyen Duy Cuong,Alexander Kruger,Nguyen Hieu Thao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究序贯极值性与平稳性性质（尤其对应固定平移序列者），给出精确的定量刻画，并建立相应的对偶必要条件及序贯扩展极值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典极值原理与平稳性条件通常假设极值点存在，但在标准极值原理不适用或极小值不被达到的情形下需要推广，因此本文借助固定序列平移引入序贯（近似）极值性与平稳性的研究。

Method: 通过精确的定量刻画分析序贯极值性与平稳性，借助广义分离条件 $(GS)$、$(GS_\alpha)$ 及原始-对偶互补条件 $(PD)$，对固定与非固定平移序列分别建立对偶必要条件，并给出序贯扩展极值原理的一种形式。

Result: 序贯（以及经典）极值性与近似平稳性具有某种稳定性，而非近似平稳性不具备该稳定性；得到固定与非固定平移序列下序贯极值性与平稳性的对偶必要条件；并证明了一个约束极小化问题（其极小值不一定被达到）的对偶最优性/平稳性条件。

Conclusion: 研究表明序贯框架可有效处理极小值不被达到的情形，稳定性差异凸显近似与严格平稳性的本质区别，广义分离与互补条件为分析提供了统一工具。

Abstract: Sequential extremality and stationarity properties are discussed with the emphasis on those corresponding to fixed sequences of translations. Exact quantitative characterisations of the properties are provided. We show, in particular, that the sequential (as well as conventional) extremality and approximate stationarity properties possess certain stability, while the (non-approximate) stationarity does not. Dual necessary conditions for the sequential extremality and stationarity properties with fixed and non-fixed sequences of translations are established. A version of the sequential extended extremal principle is formulated. In the statements, we employ certain generalised separation conditions $(GS)$ and $(GS_α)$ as well as a complementary primal-dual condition $(PD)$. To illustrate the model, we prove dual optimality/stationarity conditions for a constrained minimisation problem in which the minimal value is not necessarily attained.

</details>


### [192] [pqSEDMD: Subspace Methods for Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11292)
*Camilo Garcia-Tenorio,Alan Vande Wouwer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出 pqSEDMD 算法，将 pqEDMD 的 p-q 拟范数正交多项式基降维与子空间辨识方法结合，用于含过程噪声与测量噪声的非线性系统建模，并以 Duffing 振子为基准验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMD/EDMD 虽能从测量数据提取(非)线性动力系统的时空特征，但现实数据存在过程噪声与测量噪声两类不确定性，是分解类方法的关键瓶颈；而子空间辨识方法能从输入输出数据直接给出多变量线性系统的精确状态空间模型且具鲁棒性。因此作者希望融合二者优势，提升含噪条件下非线性系统的辨识鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 p-q 拟范数子空间扩展动态模式分解(pqSEDMD)。该方法在正交多项式基上采用 p-q 拟范数降维(即前期工作 pqEDMD)以增强 EDMD 的鲁棒性，再与子空间辨识方法结合，从而在线性函数空间中稳健地逼近非线性系统。文中以 Duffing 振子作为基准问题，验证算法有效性并讨论相关开发要点。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅指出该方法为非线性系统提供了鲁棒的线性函数空间近似，兼具两种方法学的优势；并通过 Duffing 振子基准算例展示了算法的有效性及若干与算法开发相关的重要方面(如噪声处理、降维与辨识效果等)。

Conclusion: pqSEDMD 通过融合 pqEDMD 的拟范数降维与子空间辨识，为含过程与测量噪声的非线性系统辨识提供了一种鲁棒且有效的建模途径。摘要未给出具体数值或对比结论，仅声明其有效性由 Duffing 振子实验支持，后续工作可进一步验证其泛化能力与噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: The dynamic mode decomposition (DMD), along with its variant for nonlinear systems, the extended DMD (EDMD), are powerful tools for the extraction of meaningful spatio-temporal characteristics of (non)linear dynamical systems from measurement data. Despite some efforts to handle the identification task when dealing with real-world data, the decomposition based methods face a critical challenge: real-world data has two inherent sources of uncertainty, the process and measurement noise. Subspace identification methods, are robust tools able to provide accurate state-space models for multi-variable linear systems directly from input-output data. Combining these two methods, we introduce the p-q quasi-norm Subspace Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (pqSEDMD). An algorithm that uses our previous improvements to the EDMD by the use of a p-q-quasi-norm reduction on an orthogonal polynomial basis, the pqEDMD algorithm, along with subspace identification methods. The result is a robust approximation of nonlinear systems in a linear function space, combining the strengths of the two methodologies. Throughout the paper we will use the Duffing oscillator as a benchmark problem to show the effectiveness of the algorithm and illustrate many important aspects related to the development.

</details>


### [193] [InFlow: entropic stochastic dual dynamic programming with HJB cross-certification for seasonal energy storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11339)
*Steeven B. Affognona,Babacar M. Ndiaye,Pierre Mendy,Cheikh M. F. Kebe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出面向验证的水库调度框架 InFlow，融合周期 SDDP、熵鲁棒性、Bellman 退火与独立离散 HJB 基准，并以多情景仿真验证其精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性水库调度需在强周期性、不确定来水条件下保持可靠，现有优化方法缺乏透明且可独立核验的基准。

Method: 周期性随机对偶动态规划（SDDP）结合熵鲁棒算子（解释为 KL 惩罚的最坏情况期望，并经 Lagrange 对偶联系固定半径 KL 模糊集）、控制空间 Bellman 退火，以及独立离散的 HJB 基准（基于归一化季节 CIR 来水过程，处理最干周正性条件失效的退化边界）；证明均匀软 Bellman 近似界与 Gibbs 倾斜 SDDP 割的有效性。

Result: HJB 蓄量梯度与 HiGHS 蓄量平衡对偶高度一致，水价值相关 0.995/0.996；网格由 61² 细化为 81² 时年均水价值变化 0.6%、峰值变化小于 0.1%。3000 条样本外轨迹显示 gamma=5 使名义均值成本增 4.6%、CVaR90 降 11.2%；枯季来水减 40% 时均值成本差异不显著而 CVaR90 改善 11.5%；鲁棒性扫描呈单调性。两库梯级复现网格 oracle 边际水价值相关 0.999，并恢复上下游价值比 2。

Conclusion: InFlow 作为透明的方法学基准而非标定运行模型，可在周期性不确定来水下兼顾风险鲁棒性与可核验精度。

Abstract: Seasonal reservoir operation requires optimization methods that remain reliable under strongly periodic and uncertain inflow dynamics. We present InFlow, a verification-oriented framework combining periodic stochastic dual dynamic programming (SDDP), entropic robustness, control-space Bellman annealing, and an independently discretized Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) benchmark. The entropic transition operator is interpreted as a KL-penalized worst-case expectation and linked to fixed-radius KL ambiguity sets through Lagrange duality. We establish a uniform soft-Bellman approximation bound and prove the validity of Gibbs-tilted SDDP cuts. The benchmark relies on a normalized seasonal Cox-Ingersoll-Ross inflow process whose positivity condition fails during the driest weeks, requiring explicit treatment of the degenerate boundary. Independent verification shows strong agreement between HJB storage gradients and HiGHS storage-balance duals, with water-value correlations of 0.995 and 0.996 for the standard and exact-cut-only implementations. Refining the HJB grid from 61^2 to 81^2 states changes the annual mean water value by only 0.6% and the peak value by less than 0.1%. Out-of-sample simulations based on 3,000 independent trajectories show that a robustness level of gamma=5 increases nominal mean cost by 4.6% while reducing CVaR90 by 11.2%. Under a dry-season stress scenario with inflows reduced by 40%, the mean-cost difference becomes statistically insignificant whereas CVaR90 improves by 11.5%. A robustness sweep confirms monotonicity of the risk-adjusted value. Finally, a two-reservoir cascade reproduces grid-oracle marginal water values with correlation 0.999 and recovers the predicted upstream-to-downstream value ratio of two. InFlow is intended as a transparent methodological benchmark rather than a calibrated operational reservoir model.

</details>


### [194] [Time-optimal elevator control with higher-order state constraints: analysis and computation of boundary contacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11348)
*Dmitry Karamzin,Aleksandra Zhukova,Roman Chertovskih,Pedro A. Aguiar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带高阶状态约束的时间最优电梯问题，提出基于高阶约束系统庞特里亚金最大值原理的构造性间接求解框架，将无穷维最优控制问题转化为有限维非线性代数方程组，并刻画接触-颤振现象，在四阶电梯模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典时间最优电梯问题在存在高阶状态约束时的求解具有挑战性：高阶约束使最优轨迹结构复杂，并可能引发接触-颤振现象，现有方法缺乏系统的构造性间接求解框架与几何刻画。

Method: 1) 针对高阶约束系统建立庞特里亚金最大值原理形式，推导专门的最优性条件并分析极值轨迹结构；2) 将无穷维最优控制问题转化为含切换时刻、边界接触时刻与乘子参数的有限维非线性代数方程组，用标准非线性方程求解器计算候选最优轨迹；3) 刻画边界接触几何并分析高阶约束系统中接触-颤振的产生机制。

Result: 证明正规极值不能在约束边界上持续正时间演化，尽管边界接触序列可能无限累积；在四阶电梯模型上演示该方法，所得解通过基于IPOPT的直接优化方法独立验证。

Conclusion: 所提间接框架为高阶状态约束下的时间最优电梯问题提供了构造性、计算高效的求解途径，并揭示边界接触几何与接触-颤振特性，数值结果与直接法一致。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the classical time-optimal elevator problem in the presence of higher-order state constraints. The main contribution is a constructive indirect solution framework based on a Pontryagin Maximum Principle specifically formulated for higher-order constrained systems. First, we derive specialized optimality conditions and analyze the resulting structure of extremal trajectories. Second, we show that the infinite-dimensional optimal control problem can be transformed into a finite-dimensional system of nonlinear algebraic equations involving switching times, boundary-contact times, and multiplier parameters. This provides a computationally efficient procedure for calculating candidate optimal trajectories using standard nonlinear equation solvers. Third, we characterize the geometry of boundary contacts and explain the emergence of contact-chattering phenomena in higher-order constrained systems. In particular, we show that normal extremals cannot evolve along the constraint boundary for a positive amount of time, although sequences of boundary contacts may accumulate indefinitely. The proposed approach is demonstrated on a fourth-order elevator model, and the computed solutions are independently verified using a direct IPOPT-based optimization method.

</details>


### [195] [Fully Coupled Nonlinear Forward-Backward Stochastic Difference Equations: Spectral Contraction and Infinite-Horizon Maximum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11354)
*Rui Chen,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出谱压缩方法研究无限时域全耦合非线性前向-后向随机差分方程，并给出显式折扣阈值与随机最大值原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限时域全耦合非线性前向-后向随机差分方程的解耦与适定性分析缺乏显式且可计算的压缩性判据，限制其在随机控制中的应用。

Method: 将全耦合系统的估计整合为显式二维非负矩阵，利用其谱半径给出显式充分折扣阈值，并构造等价加权乘积范数以建立压缩性质；对带凸控制约束和累积折扣运行成本的无限时域控制问题，推导Pontryagin型随机最大值原理、等价的逐点法锥形式及验证定理。

Result: 得到显式折扣阈值与压缩性判据，建立了相应的随机最大值原理及其等价逐点法锥刻画和验证定理；通过递归风险调整投资组合实例展示理论应用，并导出该实例的投影型充分最优性条件。

Conclusion: 显式谱压缩框架为无限时域全耦合前向-后向随机差分方程及其随机控制问题提供了可计算的理论工具与最优性条件。

Abstract: This paper develops an explicit spectral-contraction approach to studying fully coupled nonlinear forward--backward stochastic difference equations on infinite horizon and their applications to stochastic control. The estimates for this fully coupled system are assembled into an explicit two-dimensional nonnegative matrix. Its spectral radius yields an explicit sufficient discount threshold, and a corresponding equivalent weighted product norm is constructed to establish the contraction property. Moreover, for an infinite-horizon control problem with convex control constraints and an accumulated discounted running cost, we derive a Pontryagin-type stochastic maximum principle, its equivalent pointwise normal-cone formulation, and a verification theorem. Finally, a recursive risk-adjusted portfolio example is given to demonstrate the applications of our theoretical results, and a projection-type sufficient optimality condition for this example is derived.

</details>


### [196] [Control Strategies for Multi-Species Wasserstein Gradient Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11422)
*Dante Kalise,Lucas M. Moschen,Grigorios A. Pavliotis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将单密度的 Wasserstein Hessian 与 Riccati 方程谱方法推广到多物种耦合 McKean–Vlasov 方程，研究仅驱动单一物种时的反馈镇定问题，给出局部指数镇定的充要条件及最小控制输入数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合多物种 McKean–Vlasov 方程的稳态镇定在单密度情形已有 Wasserstein Hessian 与 Riccati 方程的谱方法，但多物种耦合系统尚无相应理论，且实际中往往只能对其中一个物种施加控制，因此需将理论推广至 Wasserstein 空间之积并刻画单物种驱动的可镇定性。

Method: 在 Wasserstein 空间之积上建立谱方法，结合 Wasserstein Hessian 与 Riccati 方程进行反馈控制设计；推导以指定速率实现局部指数镇定的充要条件，并确定所需最小控制输入数目；对两物种情形，将条件化为交叉交互核的傅里叶系数在未驱动物种线性化动力学特征空间上的秩条件。

Result: 给出了单物种驱动下指定速率局部指数镇定的充要条件与最小控制输入数；两物种情形下该条件约化为交叉交互核傅里叶系数在未驱动物种线性化特征子空间上的秩条件；以两社区含噪 Kuramoto 模型与细胞分选模型验证了结论。

Conclusion: Wasserstein Hessian–Riccati 谱框架可有效推广至多物种耦合 McKean–Vlasov 方程的反馈镇定，单物种驱动的可镇定性由交叉耦合的谱结构（秩条件）决定，为多物种平均场系统的控制设计提供了可操作的判据。

Abstract: We stabilize stationary states of coupled multi-species McKean--Vlasov equations by feedback control, extending a spectral approach based on the Wasserstein Hessian and a Riccati equation from a single density to a product of Wasserstein spaces. We give necessary and sufficient conditions for local exponential stabilization at a prescribed rate when only one species is actuated, together with the minimum number of control inputs required. For two species, the condition reduces to a rank condition on the Fourier coefficients of the cross-interaction kernel over the eigenspaces of the unactuated species' linearized dynamics. A two-community noisy Kuramoto model and a cell-sorting model illustrate the results.

</details>


### [197] [Sparsity Regularized and Robust Mean Variance Portfolio Selection Under Ellipsoidal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11749)
*Deniz Akkaya,Emre Can Yayla,Buse Şen,Mustafa Ç. Pınar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究带ℓ0惩罚的均值-方差投资组合选择问题，通过椭球不确定性集引入收益均值的不确定性，构建鲁棒稀疏优化框架，刻画局部与全局最优解结构，并设计定制分支定界算法及高效剪枝规则求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在投资组合优化中，稀疏性有助于降低交易成本与管理复杂度，而均值收益向量往往存在估计误差。因此需要同时处理稀疏性（ℓ0惩罚）与收益不确定性（鲁棒优化），但二者结合会带来非凸、非光滑的困难优化问题，现有方法求解效率有限。

Method: 在均值-方差框架中引入ℓ0惩罚以促进持仓稀疏，并用椭球不确定性集刻画均值收益的不确定性，形成鲁棒稀疏优化模型；刻画局部与全局最优解的结构性质，并将其应用于风险最小化与收益最大化两种表述；基于该结构性质设计定制分支定界算法，并提出可在单步内排除指数级数量候选组合的新型剪枝规则。

Result: 在真实市场数据上进行了大量计算实验，并与混合整数二阶锥规划（MISOCP）求解器进行对比，结果表明所提方法有效且具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过挖掘鲁棒稀疏投资组合问题的解结构，可显著提升分支定界求解效率，所提算法在真实数据上优于或媲美通用MISOCP求解器，为稀疏鲁棒投资组合选择提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We investigate mean-variance portfolio selection with an $\ell_0$-penalty to promote sparsity in asset allocations. Uncertainty in the mean return vector is incorporated through an ellipsoidal uncertainty set, yielding a robust sparse optimization framework. We characterize the structure of both local and global minimizers and exploit these properties in the risk minimization and return maximization formulations. Building on this structural insight, we develop a branch-and-bound algorithm tailored to the resulting robust sparse portfolio problems, together with a new pruning rule that can discard exponentially many candidate portfolios in a single step. Extensive computational experiments on real market data, together with comparisons against a mixed-integer second-order cone programming solver, demonstrate the effectiveness and competitiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [198] [Asynchronous Replanning in Two Population Linear Quadratic Mean Field Games: Information Requirements and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11424)
*Yuxin Jin,Wang Yao,Xiao Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究双种群线性二次平均场博弈中的异步重规划问题，刻画重规划所需信息、可恢复性条件、局部算法性质及有限种群误差界与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个种群可能从不同信念出发并采用不同计划，每个种群仅观测自身聚合轨迹与公开的修订记录，其延续最优响应依赖对手当前计划，因此需要明确异步重规划所需的信息及其可恢复性。

Method: 将重规划所需信息识别为初始观测区间末端的聚合状态与对手活跃延续计划的"状态-计划对"；对线性观测，用核包含刻画该对的可恢复性，并用有界分解量化对观测误差的敏感性；利用公开事件记录与共同最优响应映射递归确定后续对手计划，构造局部算法；对有限种群导出逐事件的采样误差线性递推，并在正决策边际下证明自主死区规则的记录匹配；区分相互延续响应的唯一可解性与交替响应的稳定性。

Result: 所需状态-计划对即使在完整隐藏信念不可恢复时仍可能可恢复；局部算法在每个有限机会前缀上复现理想基准，且实现的修订因最优响应持续性而交替出现；获得有限前缀误差界与记录匹配结果；在预终端Zeno累积处，谱稳定性结合移动边界估计使延续计划收敛到从实际极限状态重启的均衡。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了异步重规划所需信息的最小刻画，明确了可恢复性与误差敏感性，并建立了交替响应算法的有限样本误差界、记录匹配及Zeno点处的均衡收敛性，同时将相互延续响应唯一可解性与交替响应稳定性分离。

Abstract: We study asynchronous replanning in a two population linear quadratic mean field game in which the populations may begin from different beliefs and hence use different plans. Each population observes its own aggregate trajectory and a public record of implemented revisions, while its continuation best response depends on the opponent's current plan. We identify the information required for replanning as the aggregate state at the end of the initial observation interval together with the opponent's active continuation plan. For linear observations, recoverability of this state-plan pair is characterized by a kernel inclusion, and a bounded factorization quantifies sensitivity to observation error. In particular, the required pair may be recoverable even when the full hidden belief is not. Once initialized, the public event record and the common best-response map recursively determine subsequent opponent plans, and the resulting local algorithm reproduces an ideal benchmark on every finite opportunity prefix; implemented revisions alternate as a consequence of best-response persistence. For finite populations, we derive an eventwise linear recursion for sampling errors, obtain finite prefix error bounds, and prove record matching for an autonomous deadband rule under a positive decision margin. Finally, we separate unique solvability of mutual continuation responses from stability of alternating responses, and show that at a pre-terminal Zeno accumulation, spectral stability together with a moving-boundary estimate yields convergence of the continuation plans to the equilibrium restarted from the actual limiting state.

</details>


### [199] [Efficient Algorithms for Energy-Aware Single-Machine Scheduling with Battery Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11455)
*Luca Forte,Stijn De Vuyst,Pieter Leyman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文研究单机、固定时域下，结合分时电价（TOU）与电池储能系统（BESS）的能耗成本最小化调度问题，并提出两种数学启发式（matheuristic）算法变体以协同优化生产调度与BESS充放电决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造业面临降低运营成本与碳排放的压力，将生产活动与电价对齐十分关键；引入BESS虽能进一步降低购电成本，但也使作业调度与储能管理产生复杂耦合，显著提升问题求解难度。

Method: 针对单机、固定时间范围内执行一组作业、电价遵循TOU tariff、并配置可从电网充电并在高价时段放电的BESS的成本最小化问题，设计并分析两种matheuristic算法变体，用于协同生产调度与BESS使用决策。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果；作者宣称所提出的两种matheuristic算法变体能够有效协调生产调度与BESS使用决策，从而高效求解该问题。

Conclusion: 该研究强调BESS与TOU电价下生产调度耦合问题的重要性，并以两种matheuristic变体作为有效求解途径。

Abstract: Energy-aware scheduling has become a central challenge in modern manufacturing environments. As industries increasingly aim to reduce operational costs and carbon emissions, aligning production activities with electricity tariffs is increasingly important. The integration of battery energy storage system (BESS) further enhances the potential to reduce energy purchase costs; however, incorporating such systems introduces complex interdependencies between job scheduling and BESS management decisions, significantly complicating the problem structure.\\In this work, we study the problem of minimizing the energy cost of executing a set of jobs on a single machine within a fixed time horizon, where electricity prices follow a Time-of-Use (TOU) tariff. In addition, we consider the presence of a BESS that can be charged from the grid and discharged during high-price periods to reduce overall energy costs. To efficiently address this problem, we propose and analyze two matheuristic algorithm variants designed to effectively coordinate production scheduling and BESS usage decisions.

</details>


### [200] [Global Linear Convergence of the Proximal Bundle Method under Unknown Piecewise Smoothness and Quadratic Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11480)
*Zhenwei Lin,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解释了近端束方法(PBM)在保留足够多切平面时表现更优的原因：保留足够多切平面使PBM能够利用目标函数的分片光滑结构，从而表现得像在优化光滑函数，并为其线性收敛提供理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中PBM保留更多切平面时表现更好，但缺乏理论解释。作者希望为这一现象提供理论依据。

Method: 研究具有二次增长且分片光滑结构未知的凸目标函数，分析PBM保留足够多切平面时所利用的信息结构。

Result: 关键观察：保留足够多切平面使PBM能够利用目标函数的分片光滑结构，表现得仿佛在优化光滑函数。作者为PBM在分片光滑目标上保留足够多切平面时的线性收敛提供了理论解释。

Conclusion: 保留足够多切平面可让PBM利用分片光滑结构并实现线性收敛，从而理论性地解释了其实际中的优越表现。

Abstract: We study why the proximal bundle method (PBM) can perform better in practice when it retains more cutting planes. We consider convex objectives with quadratic growth and an unknown piecewise-smooth structure. Our key observation is that retaining sufficiently many cutting planes allows PBM to exploit the objective's piecewise-smooth structure and behave as if it were optimizing a smooth function. We provide a theoretical explanation for the observed linear convergence of PBM on piecewise-smooth objectives when it retains sufficiently many cutting planes.

</details>


### [201] [Non-monotone direct-search methods for deterministic and stochastic derivative-free optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11567)
*Anjie Ding,Trang H. Tran,Luis Nunes Vicente*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为max-M非单调直接搜索法在确定性与随机无导数优化中建立了完整的复杂度理论，三类情形均达到O(ε⁻²)的迭代复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准直接搜索法要求试探点使目标函数下降量与步长平方成正比，在复杂地形中易陷入次优解的邻域；采用非单调接受准则可允许目标函数暂时上升，从而穿越狭窄弯曲的谷地，但其缺乏单调下降性，理论分析极为困难，此前缺少相应的复杂度理论。

Method: 研究max-M非单调直接搜索：将试探函数值与最近M个互异迭代点中最大的目标函数值进行比较。为克服非单调性带来的分析困难，引入一类新的价值函数（merit function），按有序倍数对存储的目标值进行步长平方修正；并针对概率性方法采用更新-回报（renewal-reward）停时论证。分别分析了基于正生成集的完整poll（确定性）、概率下降poll（期望界）以及使用独立函数估计的随机变体（在误差尾界假设下）。

Result: 确定性情形得到完整poll的最坏情况迭代界与概率下降poll的期望迭代界；随机情形在随机误差尾界假设下得到期望迭代复杂度。三者的复杂度均为O(ε⁻²)，与单调直接搜索法的迭代复杂度相匹配。

Conclusion: max-M非单调接受准则在获得更强逃离次优区域能力的同时，并不损失最坏情况/期望意义上的迭代复杂度，达到与单调方法相同的O(ε⁻²)阶；所提出的价值函数族与停时论证为分析非单调直接搜索提供了通用工具。

Abstract: In derivative-free optimization (DFO), one minimizes functions for which the gradient is unavailable or expensive to compute. In many applications, objective function values and gradients are noisy due to simulations or system randomness. A class of standard direct-search methods for DFO accept a trial point when it decreases the objective function by an amount proportional to the squared stepsize. However, when applied to complex landscapes, such a requirement may trap the algorithm in a neighborhood of sub-optimal solutions. We study a non-monotone direct-search alternative where the trial function value is compared with the largest objective function obtained through the $M$ most recent distinct iterates. This max-$M$ non-monotone condition permits temporary increases in the objective function and can help navigate narrow curved valleys; however, its theoretical analysis is significantly more challenging due to the lack of monotonic decrease. In this paper, we develop a comprehensive complexity theory for the max-$M$ non-monotone direct-search in both deterministic and stochastic DFO problems. For deterministic objectives, we establish a worst-case iteration bound for a complete poll based on a positive spanning set and an expected iteration bound for a probabilistic-descent poll. We then analyze a stochastic variant using independent function estimates and show the expected iteration complexity under tail-bound assumptions of the stochastic errors. All three results have the standard complexity of $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})$, which matches the iteration complexity of monotone direct-search methods. Our theory is enabled by a new family of merit functions that correct the stored objective values by ordered multiples of the squared stepsize, together with a renewal-reward stopping-time argument for the probabilistic methods.

</details>


### [202] [Values of Absorbing Recursive Games Express All Real Algebraic Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11583)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Oliu-Barton 与 Vigeral 关于吸收博弈无理值的猜想：任意 $\mathbb{Q}$ 上次数为 $m\ge 1$ 的实代数数 $\alpha$ 都是某个有理 $m\times m$ 吸收博弈的未折扣值，且可在严格吸收且递归的子类中实现；从而有理 $m\times m$ 吸收博弈的未折扣值集恰为次数不超过 $m$ 的实代数数集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多二人零和随机博弈具有序域性质：若所有收益与转移概率属于 $\mathbb{R}$ 的某子域，则未折扣值也属于该子域。吸收博弈不满足此性质，Oliu-Barton 与 Vigeral 据此猜想其失效范围可被精确刻画，即每个实代数数都可由一个尺寸匹配的有理吸收博弈实现。本文旨在完全证明该猜想并给出更强结论。

Method: 将待实现的实代数数 $\alpha$（$\mathbb{Q}$ 上次数 $m$）构造为特殊子类吸收博弈的未折扣值：构造的博弈为严格吸收且递归，即每个行动对均以正概率吸收、所有非吸收阶段收益为零；当 $\alpha>0$ 时进一步构造为正递归博弈，吸收收益为正。随后由此构造推导出值集的完整刻画。

Result: 猜想成立并被证明，且在更强的子类中实现：对每个次数 $m\ge 1$ 的实代数数 $\alpha$，存在严格吸收且递归的有理 $m\times m$ 吸收博弈以其为未折扣值；当 $\alpha>0$ 时还可取为正递归博弈。作为推论，有理 $m\times m$ 吸收博弈的未折扣值集恰好等于所有次数不超过 $m$ 的实代数数构成的集合。

Conclusion: 吸收博弈未折扣值的代数次数由博弈矩阵规模精确控制：$m\times m$ 有理吸收博弈的值域恰为次数 $\le m$ 的实代数数。这证实了 Oliu-Barton 与 Vigeral 的猜想，并加强了其结论（严格吸收、递归甚至正递归的可实现性），刻画了吸收博弈破坏序域性质的确切边界。

Abstract: Many classes of two-player zero-sum stochastic games have the orderfield property: if all payoffs and transition probabilities lie in a subfield of $\mathbb{R}$, so does the undiscounted value. Absorbing games fail this property, and Oliu-Barton and Vigeral [\emph{Absorbing games with irrational values}, Oper.\ Res.\ Lett.\ 51 (2023) 555--559] conjectured the precise extent of the failure: every real algebraic number $α$ of degree $m\geq 1$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ is the undiscounted value of a rational $m\times m$ absorbing game. We prove this conjecture, and in fact within a special subclass of absorbing games: for every such $α$, the game realizing it is \emph{strictly absorbing} and \emph{recursive}, i.e., every action pair is absorbing with positive probability and all non-absorbing stage payoffs are zero; when $α>0$ it can moreover be taken \emph{positive recursive}, with positive absorbing payoffs. As a corollary, the set of undiscounted values of rational $m\times m$ absorbing games is exactly the set of real algebraic numbers of degree at most $m$

</details>


### [203] [Verified Linear Programming through Tolerance-Aware Precision Boosting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11721)
*Ernesto Casablanca,Martin Sidaway,Paolo Zuliani,Sadegh Soudjani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究单纯形算法的数值稳定性，给出精度提升浮点实现与精确有理实现产生相同枢轴决策和最终基的条件，并提出带容差感知的精度提升单纯形算法与delta-完备终止准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性规划在优化、形式验证、SMT求解等领域应用广泛。精确性要求下，浮点运算的数值误差会破坏可靠性，而精确有理运算又会带来显著计算开销，因此需要兼顾正确性与效率的折中方案。

Method: 分析单纯形算法的数值稳定性，建立精度提升浮点实现与精确有理实现在枢轴决策及最终基上一致的条件；指出正确性同时依赖算术精度与数值容差的谨慎处理；据此设计容差感知、精度提升的单纯形算法并提供形式化正确性保证；引入delta-完备终止准则。

Result: 证明正确性取决于算术精度与数值容差处理；所提算法在给定条件下可产生与精确有理实现相同的枢轴决策和最终基，并可由浮点结果精确重构与认证最终解；delta-完备准则使算法在最优目标值的认证上下界之差不超过用户指定阈值delta时终止。

Conclusion: 该工作为精度提升浮点单纯形算法提供了形式化正确性保证，实现了精确结果重构与认证，并通过delta-完备终止准则提供用户可控的最优性间隙，平衡了数值可靠性与计算效率。

Abstract: Linear programming plays a fundamental role in computer science, with applications in optimization, formal verification, SMT solving, and numerous other domains. When exactness guarantees are required, numerical inaccuracies arising from floating-point arithmetic can compromise soundness, whereas exact rational arithmetic often incurs significant computational overhead. In this paper, we investigate the numerical stability of the simplex algorithm and establish conditions under which a precision-boosting floating-point implementation provably produces the same pivot decisions and final basis as an exact rational implementation, from which the final result is reconstructed and certified exactly. Our analysis shows that correctness depends on both arithmetic precision and a careful handling of numerical tolerances. Based on these results, we develop a tolerance-aware, precision-boosting simplex algorithm with formal correctness guarantees. Finally, we introduce a delta-complete termination criterion that allows the algorithm to terminate once certified upper and lower bounds on the optimal objective differ by at most a user-specified threshold delta, providing a certified, user-controlled optimality gap.

</details>


### [204] [Stochastic Gradient Methods with Online Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11751)
*Wanyu Zhang,Wenzhi Gao,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出随机在线缩放梯度方法（SOSGM），将自适应预条件框架推广到随机优化，并在标准假设下给出收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将近期发展的自适应预条件框架（arXiv:2505.23081、arXiv:2509.11007）从确定性/在线设置推广到随机优化场景。

Method: SOSGM 采用随机优化形式，借助大 batch size 或方差缩减技术建立收敛性；兼容对角及/或低秩预条件器与重球动量，内存与计算开销与 Adam 相当。

Result: 在标准假设下证明其收敛性；大量数值实验显示其经验性能强。使用对角预条件器时，SOSGM 及其变体在多种统计学习任务上显著优于现有自适应一阶方法。

Conclusion: SOSGM 是一种高效、通用的随机自适应预条件优化方法，兼具理论收敛保证与实证优势。

Abstract: This paper introduces Stochastic Online Scaled Gradient Methods (SOSGM), a generalization of the recently developed adaptive preconditioning framework in arXiv:2505.23081 and arXiv:2509.11007 to stochastic optimization. Under standard assumptions, we establish convergence guarantees for SOSGM using large batchsize or variance reduction. SOSGM is compatible with popular diagonal and/or low-rank preconditioners as well as heavy-ball momentum, while maintaining memory and computation cost comparable to Adam. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the strong empirical performance of SOSGM. Using a diagonal preconditioner, SOSGM and its variants substantially outperform existing adaptive first-order methods across a range of statistical learning tasks.

</details>


### [205] [Constant Steps Are s-Composable: An Exact Interpolation Certificate for Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11784)
*Jinze Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了Grimmer、Shu和Wang提出的关于平衡常数步长调度在任意时间步数下$s$-可组合性的猜想，对所有$n\geq1$给出完整证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grimmer、Shu和Wang提出：平衡常数步长调度是否在每个时间步数下都是$s$-可组合的。当$n=1,2$时已知成立，但一般情形$n\geq3$尚未解决，因此本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对每个$n$构造显式、无维数依赖的非负线性组合，即光滑凸插值不等式的证书。该证书由支撑在连续指标区间上的矩阵拼装而成：非对角元自动为正，二次部分为对角，其余乘子归结为两族标量。作者推导这两族的闭式表达，并利用严格凹性与初等有理不等式证明其正性。所有例外时间步长与边界指标均单独处理。

Result: 对每个$n$证明了该猜想。由此，对任意$L$-光滑凸函数，常数调度满足原论文所猜测的尖锐混合终端Lyapunov不等式，并同时给出相应的目标间隙与梯度范数界。

Conclusion: 平衡常数调度在每个时间步数下均为$s$-可组合，原论文的尖锐混合终端Lyapunov不等式及其目标间隙和梯度范数界对任意$L$-光滑凸函数成立。

Abstract: Grimmer, Shu, and Wang asked whether a balanced constant schedule is $s$-composable at every horizon. More precisely, for an integer $n\geq 1$, let $\bar{h}=1+r$, where $r\in(0,1)$ is the unique solution of $$r^n\bigl(1+n(1+r)\bigr)=1,$$ and run gradient descent for $n$ steps with normalized stepsize $\bar{h}$. The cases $n=1,2$ were known, while the general case $n\geq3$ was left open. We prove the conjecture for every $n$. Our proof gives an explicit, dimension-free nonnegative linear combination of the smooth convex interpolation inequalities. The certificate is assembled from matrices supported on contiguous index intervals. Its off-diagonal entries are automatically positive, its quadratic part is diagonal, and its remaining multipliers reduce to two scalar families. We derive closed forms for those families and prove positivity using strict concavity and elementary rational inequalities. Consequently, for every $L$-smooth convex function, the constant schedule satisfies the sharp mixed terminal Lyapunov inequality conjectured in the original paper, together with the associated simultaneous objective-gap and gradient-norm bounds. All exceptional horizons and boundary indices are treated explicitly.

</details>


### [206] [Optimal Recursive Composition and Dyadic Phase Laws for Gradient Descent with Predetermined Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11788)
*Yu Liu,Kang Chen,Rujun Jiang,Tianyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文刻画了通过递归组合构造的优化步长策略在光滑凸函数上的收敛性，揭示了收敛速率中存在非常值的对数周期调制，并证明了平衡分割在对称递归框架下每个时域均最优，从而解决了Zhang和Jiang的猜想；同时指出非对称框架下最优分割未必平衡，但Silver指数渐近保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预设的、精心选择长步的步长策略已被证明能加速光滑凸函数上的梯度下降，其中递归组合（recursive composition）构造是一类重要方法。然而，这类优化递归策略的精确收敛行为尚不清楚，尤其是收敛速率是否仅为常数阶、是否存在周期性调制结构，以及对称与非对称框架下最优分割的性质（如平衡分割是否最优）仍待解决。作者希望刻画这些优化递归策略的收敛性，并验证相关猜想。

Method: 作者从递归组合框架出发，分别研究对称递归框架（primitive和OBS-S构造）与非对称框架（OBS-F构造）。通过分析每个时域（horizon）下递归分割的最优性，证明对称框架中平衡分割（balanced splitting）在每个时域都是最优的，从而得到优化策略的显式收敛上界；对非对称框架则分析最优分割结构及渐近行为。

Result: 对对称递归框架（primitive和OBS-S），证明对任意N≥1，优化步长策略满足 f(x_{N-1})-f* ≤ [1/(2N^p Φ(log2 N)-1)]·(L/2)||x0-x*||²，其中p=log2(1+√2)（Silver指数），Φ为正的、Lipschitz的、非常值1-周期函数，表明收敛速率存在非常值的对数周期调制。该结果通过证明平衡分割在每个时域最优得到，解决了Zhang和Jiang的猜想。对非对称框架（OBS-F），证明最优分割不一定平衡，但同样的Silver指数渐近保持，并伴随一个不同的对数周期调制。

Conclusion: 递归组合构造的优化步长策略在光滑凸函数上实现了带有对数周期调制的收敛速率，其主导指数为Silver指数p=log2(1+√2)。对称框架（primitive、OBS-S）中平衡分割在每个时域最优，收敛上界含非常值对数周期函数Φ；非对称框架（OBS-F）中最优分割未必平衡，但Silver指数渐近持续存在，仅调制函数不同。该工作刻画了递归步长策略的精细收敛行为并解决了相关猜想。

Abstract: Predetermined stepsize schedules featuring carefully chosen long steps have recently been shown to accelerate gradient descent (GD) on smooth convex functions. A prominent class of such schedules is built through recursive composition. In this paper, we characterize the convergence of these optimized recursive schedules, revealing a non-constant log-periodic modulation across prescribed horizons. Specifically, for symmetric recursive frameworks (primitive and OBS-S constructions), we prove that for every $N \geq 1$, the corresponding optimized schedules satisfy $f(x_{N-1})-f^\ast \le \frac{1}{2N^p Φ(\log_2N)-1} \frac{L}{2}\|x_0-x^\ast\|^2$, $ p=\log_2(1+\sqrt2)$, where $Φ$ is a positive, Lipschitz, nonconstant $1$-periodic function. We derive this by proving that balanced splitting is optimal at every horizon for these constructions, resolving a conjecture of Zhang and Jiang. Furthermore, for the asymmetric framework (the OBS-F construction), we show that although optimal splits are not necessarily balanced, the same Silver exponent asymptotically persists alongside a distinct log-periodic modulation.

</details>


### [207] [Constrained Deep Inventory Management Using Forward-Backward SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11817)
*Keunwoo Lim,Michael Bloem*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将带总量运营限制的多产品补货问题建模为安全随机最优控制问题，用FBSDE求解HJB方程并结合控制屏障函数（CBF）导出二次规划，处理在途约束（相对度1）与在手约束（相对度2）混合的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统补货问题中的运营限制多以期望形式施加，而本文希望以逐路径（pathwise）形式施加总量运营限制，从而更严格地保证安全性与环保要求。

Method: 将HJB方程表示为前向-后向随机微分方程组（FBSDEs），通过学习值函数及其梯度求解；每个决策时刻求解由控制屏障函数（CBF）导出的二次规划。由于采购订单仅进入在途管道，在途约束相对度为1，在手约束相对度为2。作者将Clark（2021）的单屏障高相对度随机构造扩展为四个混合相对度的耦合总量约束，并通过设计确定其初始条件要求。

Result: 提出了一种可处理混合相对度、多约束耦合的安全随机最优控制框架，并明确了初始条件设计要求。

Conclusion: 该框架为逐路径总量限制下的多产品补货提供了可行求解途径，扩展了高相对度CBF方法在随机系统中的适用范围。

Abstract: We study multi-product replenishment subject to aggregate operating limits imposed pathwise in place of expectation. On-hand and in-transit inventory, warehouse capacity and an emission allowance define a safe set, and the resulting multi-product lost-sales problem is posed as a safe stochastic optimal control problem. The Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman (HJB) equation is represented as a system of forward--backward stochastic differential equations (FBSDEs) and solved by learning the value function and its gradient, with a quadratic program derived by control barrier function (CBF) is solved at each decision epoch. Because purchase orders enter the in-transit pipeline only, the in-transit constraints have relative degree one while the on-hand constraints have relative degree two. We extend the stochastic high-relative-degree construction of (Clark, 2021) from a single barrier to four coupled aggregate constraints of mixed relative degree, and we establish its initial-condition requirement by design.

</details>


### [208] [Conformal Margins for Electrical Transmission Capacity Under Fixed Balancing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11823)
*Sushanth Balaraman,Shriram Srinivasan,Kaarthik Sundar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动方法，在固定AGC策略下最小化调整基于预测的稳态发电调度，以应对需求与可再生能源不确定性引发的输电拥塞，并为线路安全和AGC非饱和运行提供有限样本概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求与可再生能源出力的不确定性会导致输电网络拥塞，而基于预测的调度难以应对；需要在保持较低运行成本的同时，可靠地调整调度以预留足够的输电容量与AGC调节余量。

Method: 首先证明在固定且不饱和的AGC策略下，AGC后残余注入分布与计划发电调度无关，从而将线路负载分解为调度相关的基准分量与不确定性引发的残余分量；随后利用历史预测误差，采用分裂共形预测构造具有无分布、有限样本逐线路或联合覆盖率保证的线路裕度；并校准AGC前总不匹配量以确定所需发电余量；最后将这些校准量嵌入凸安全再调度问题中，最小化调整标称调度。

Result: 在RTS-GMLC系统上的案例研究表明，所得到的修正调度在仅带来适度运行成本增加的情况下，显著降低了样本外线路越限违规次数。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在固定AGC策略下，以最小调度调整为代价，为逐线路安全与AGC非饱和运行提供有限样本概率保证，并有效减少线路越限，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents a data-driven approach to minimally adjust forecast-based steady-state generation dispatch in electrical transmission networks. The goal is to manage congestion caused by uncertainty in demand and renewable generation under a fixed automatic generation control (AGC) policy operating without saturation. Under this policy, we first show that the post-AGC residual injection distribution is invariant to the scheduled generation dispatch. This result separates line loading into a dispatch-dependent baseline and an uncertainty-induced residual component. Using historical forecast errors, we then apply split conformal prediction to construct transmission-line margins with distribution-free, finite-sample linewise or joint coverage guarantees. We also calibrate the aggregate pre-AGC mismatch to determine the generation headroom required for the prescribed AGC response. We incorporate these calibrated quantities into a convex secure re-dispatch problem that minimally adjusts the nominal schedule while reserving sufficient transmission capacity and AGC headroom. The resulting corrected dispatch provides finite-sample probabilistic guarantees on per-line security and unsaturated AGC operation. Case studies on the RTS-GMLC system demonstrate substantial reductions in out-of-sample line-limit violations with modest operating-cost increases.

</details>


### [209] [WFDroneBench: A Benchmark for Sensor Placement and Drone Routing for Wildfire Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11829)
*Romain Puech,Danique de Moor,Ana Trisovic,Dimitris Bertsimas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出WFDroneBench，一个开源Python基准测试库，将机器学习风险图与基于优化的传感器/充电站/无人机部署策略整合，用于早期野火检测，涵盖7746个场景、49个地点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火日益频繁且严重，威胁生态系统、公共健康与基础设施；早期检测至关重要但受限于现有监测系统，无人机虽能提供移动实时覆盖，但在动态火区中优化传感器布设与无人机路径仍具挑战。

Method: 构建WFDroneBench开源基准库，整合机器学习生成的风险图与基于优化的部署策略（传感器、充电站、无人机），使用标准化指标与真实野火模拟评估风险图、优化策略和监测设备；数据集包含49个地点的7746个场景，来源于历史火点、真实风险图与模拟火势蔓延，并包含2种地面检测器与3种无人机路由策略。

Result: 实验表明，当风险图足够准确时，风险感知策略Max-Coverage显著优于其他基线，在最困难的火情上实现最快检测；风险感知静态基础设施在风险图不完美时仍有帮助，无人机检测优于地面传感器。

Conclusion: 揭示两个关键开放挑战：(i) 快速可靠地检测小火情；(ii) 改进风险图预测——真实点火模式与现有风险图之间的差距为ML创新提供了重要机会。代码、数据与文档全部开源。

Abstract: Increasingly frequent and severe wildfires threaten ecosystems, public health, and infrastructure. Early detection is vital but limited by existing monitoring systems. Drones offer mobile, real-time coverage, but optimizing sensor placement and drone routing in dynamic fire zones remains challenging. To address this, we introduce WFDroneBench, an open-source Python benchmarking library for early wildfire detection that integrates machine-learned risk maps with optimization-based deployment strategies for sensors, charging stations, and drones. It evaluates risk maps, optimization strategies, and monitoring equipment using standardized metrics and realistic wildfire simulations. The framework supports benchmarking across predictive and decision-making components: machine learning researchers can assess risk models and compare routing strategies. WFDroneBench includes 7746 scenarios across 49 locations, built from historical ignitions, real-world wildfire risk maps, and simulated fire spread, along with two ground detector and three drone routing strategies. Our experiments show that the risk-aware strategy Max-Coverage significantly outperforms other baselines when risk maps are sufficiently accurate, achieving the fastest detection on the most difficult fires. We further find that risk-aware static infrastructure helps even under an imperfect risk map and drone-based detection outperforms ground sensors. Finally, our results reveal two key open challenges: (i) detecting small fires rapidly and reliably, and (ii) improving risk-map prediction, where the gap between ground-truth ignition patterns and available risk maps highlights a significant opportunity for ML innovation. We openly release all code, data, and documentation.

</details>


### [210] [Strong Consistency and Optimal Tracking of the Åström-Wittenmark Self-Tuning Regulator with Unknown Input Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11831)
*Zhaobo Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究未知输入增益下 Åström-Wittenmark 自校正调节器的强一致性与最优跟踪问题。针对最小相位线性系统与鞅差噪声，作者证明：在无需参考激励、增益调整或附加探测信号的条件下，普通最小二乘配合确定性等价控制即可同时获得稳定性、最优跟踪与参数强一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果要么需对参考信息施加增长条件才能获得稳定性、最优跟踪与参数一致性，要么通过修改反馈中的估计值或加入衰减探测信号来获得性能保证。本文旨在去除这些额外要求，在无参考激励、无增益调整、无附加探测的情形下同时建立三类保证。

Method: 针对一类带鞅差噪声的最小相位线性系统，采用普通最小二乘（OLS）参数估计与确定性等价控制。一致性证明的关键是构造辅助最小二乘噪声预测与参考之间累积平方差的对数下界：若增益误差持续存在，则噪声信息与真实最小二乘递推对同一加权平方和给出不相容的上下界，由此先推出增益收敛，再得到稳定性与全参数一致性。

Result: 对每个有界参考信号，平均输入、输出能量几乎必然有界；平均平方跟踪误差几乎必然收敛到噪声方差；只要估计参数不少于两个，所有参数估计均强一致。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在所设定系统类中，未经修改的 Åström-Wittenmark 自校正调节器可在最弱条件下实现稳定性、最优跟踪与参数强一致的联合保证，突破了以往对参考激励、增益调整或探测信号的依赖。

Abstract: We study strong consistency and optimal tracking of the Åström-Wittenmark self-tuning regulator with unknown input gain. Existing results establish stability, optimal tracking and parameter consistency for the unmodified recursion under growth conditions on reference information. Other approaches obtain performance guarantees by adjusting the estimates used in feedback or adding decaying probing signals. For a class of minimum-phase linear systems with martingale difference noise, we establish joint guarantees for ordinary least squares with certainty-equivalent control without reference excitation, gain adjustment or added probing. For each bounded reference, average input and output energy are almost surely bounded, average squared tracking error converges almost surely to the noise variance, and all parameter estimates are strongly consistent whenever at least two parameters are estimated. For the consistency result, the key is a logarithmic lower bound on the cumulative squared difference between an auxiliary least-squares prediction of the noise and the reference. If the gain error persisted, this noise information and the actual least-squares recursion would give incompatible lower and upper bounds on the same weighted squared sum. This contradiction establishes gain convergence before stability and full parameter consistency.

</details>


<div id='math.AG'></div>

# math.AG [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Long range divided differences, clusters, and Graham-positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10735)
*Hunter Spink,Vasu Tewari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究A型完全旗簇中与正簇扇锥对应的环面轨道闭包及其左 $S_n$-平移，通过由非交叉交错森林编码的长程除差复合计算环面等变度映射，给出算法性组合展开，得到多种 Graham-正 Schubert 循环展开及 Grassmann 情形结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于理解正簇扇中锥所自然对应的A型完全旗簇环面轨道闭包（及其左 $S_n$-平移），并寻求其环面等变上同调/同调类的显式、正性展开。既有工作如 AJS-Billey 公式仅处理环面不动点，缺乏对环面轨道闭包、环面不变曲线及若干 Richardson 簇的统一组合算法与 Graham-正性结果，因此需要新方法推广。

Method: 方法上，环面等变度映射通过由非交叉交错森林（noncrossing alternating forests）编码的长程除差算子（long-range divided difference operations）之复合来计算；并给出组合算法，将环面等变同调类展开为 Schubert 循环的 Graham-正组合。进一步通过投影到 Grassmannian，处理置换基集上格路拟阵对应的环面轨道闭包。

Result: 主要结果包括：对所有环面不变曲线得到 Graham-正 Schubert 循环展开（推广环面不动点的 AJS-Billey 公式）；对一般环面轨道闭包得到同类展开；对 Bruhat 区间 $[w,wc']$（其中 $c'\le s_{n-1}s_{n-2}\cdots s_1$）的 Richardson 簇得到 Graham-正展开；在投影到 Grassmannian 后，得到置换基集上格路拟阵对应环面轨道闭包的 Graham-正 Grassmannian Schubert 循环分解。

Conclusion: 结论是，非交叉交错森林与长程除差复合为计算环面等变度映射提供了统一的组合框架，并由此系统性地获得多类环面轨道闭包及相关簇的 Graham-正 Schubert 展开，显著推广了 AJS-Billey 公式并覆盖 Grassmannian 中的格路拟阵情形。

Abstract: We study torus-orbit closures in the type $A$ complete flag variety naturally associated to cones in the positive cluster fan, together with their left $S_n$-translates. The torus-equivariant degree maps can be computed via composites of long-range divided difference operations encoded by noncrossing alternating forests, and we give combinatorial algorithms to expand the torus-equivariant homology classes into Graham-positive combinations of Schubert cycles. As applications we obtain combinatorial Graham-positive Schubert cycle expansions for all torus-invariant curves (generalizing the AJS-Billey formula for torus-fixed points), generic torus-orbit closures, and Richardson varieties for Bruhat intervals $[w,wc']$ where $c'\le s_{n-1}s_{n-2}\cdots s_1$. Projecting to Grassmannians we also obtain Graham-positive Grassmannian Schubert cycle decompositions of torus-orbit closures associated to lattice path matroids on permuted ground sets.

</details>


### [212] [The Reye geometry inside the 64 lines of the Schur quartic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10751)
*Paweł Nurowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 在Schur四次曲面上的Naskręcki--Pokora $(24_4,32_3)$构型中揭示了经典Reye构型，并确定了完整64线关联几何的自同构群（阶4608），同时将64线典范扩展为六个射影等价Schur四次曲面承载的176线构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别Naskręcki--Pokora $(24_4,32_3)$构型中隐藏的经典几何结构，并刻画相关关联构型的对称性及扩展结构。

Method: 利用Höhn的$D_4$标记；考察24个根上的对径对合诱导的无不动点商；计算完整64线关联几何的自同构群（包括构型轨道与稳定子）；分析64线到176线构型的典范扩展及其诱导曲面置换群。

Result: 对径对合的无不动点商恰为经典Reye构型；完整64线关联几何的自同构群阶为4608，两个Naskręcki--Pokora构型构成单一轨道，其稳定子阶为2304（射影意义下576）；64线典范扩展为六个射影等价Schur四次曲面承载的176线构型，176=16+16+9·16，诱导曲面置换群为$S_3\times S_3$。

Conclusion: Naskręcki--Pokora构型与经典Reye构型之间存在自然的商关系，完整线构型的对称性被完全刻画；此外该几何在六个射影等价的Schur四次曲面上具有规范的176线扩展结构，具有$S_3\times S_3$对称。

Abstract: We identify the classical geometry hidden in the Naskręcki--Pokora $(24_4,32_3)$ configuration on the Schur quartic. In Höhn's $D_4$ labelling, the antipodal involution on the $24$ roots induces a fixed-point-free quotient of the incidence configuration, and this quotient is precisely the classical Reye configuration. We also determine the symmetry of the complete $64$-line incidence geometry: its automorphism group has order $4608$, the two Naskręcki--Pokora configurations form a single orbit, and the stabilizer of either has order $2304$ (projectively, $576$). Finally, the $64$ lines extend canonically to a $176$-line arrangement carried by six projectively equivalent Schur quartics, with $176=16+16+9\cdot16$ and induced surface permutation group $S_3\times S_3$.

</details>


### [213] [Torelli theorem for the product of moduli spaces of vector bundles over a curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10764)
*Indranil Biswas,Arijit Dey,Anubhab Pahari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了光滑射影曲线（g≥4）上稳定向量丛模空间及模栈的乘积的Torelli型定理，利用Picard秩为1且Picard群离散的正规射影簇乘积的分解定理，并计算了其自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对模空间乘积的Torelli型问题的刻画，以及通过分解定理研究此类乘积的自同构结构。

Method: 采用正规射影簇（Picard秩为1、Picard群离散）乘积的分解定理来证明Torelli型结果。

Result: 得到半稳定向量丛模空间乘积与模栈乘积的Torelli型定理。

Conclusion: 作为应用，计算了模空间乘积与模栈乘积的自同构群。

Abstract: We prove a Torelli-type theorem for a product of the moduli spaces of semistable vector bundles over smooth projective curves of genus $g\,\ge\, 4$. A similar result is proved for the product of the moduli stacks of semistable vector bundles. This is proved using a decomposition theorem for the product of the normal projective varieties with Picard rank one and discrete Picard group. As an application, we compute the automorphism group of the product of the moduli spaces and moduli stacks.

</details>


### [214] [Perverse Euler Characteristics of Hermitian Locally Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10946)
*Mert Akdenizli,Quoc-Anh Tran*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了有限体积非紧型局部Hermitian对称空间的perverse Euler示性数非负，并给出满支撑情形的严格不等式及若干模空间应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局部Hermitian对称空间上perverse Euler示性数的符号性质，并将该非负性推广到各类模空间。

Method: 证明光滑环面紧化的对数余切丛的nef性，并结合perverse层Euler示性数的正性判别准则进行推导。

Result: 得到有限体积非紧型局部Hermitian对称空间perverse Euler示性数非负；对满支撑perverse层该不等式严格成立；并得到多种模空间上perverse Euler示性数的非负性结果。

Conclusion: 对数余切丛的nef性为perverse Euler示性数的非负性提供了有效工具，可推广至多类模空间。

Abstract: We prove that finite-volume locally Hermitian symmetric spaces of noncompact type have nonnegative perverse Euler characteristics. To show this, we obtain a nefness result for the logarithmic cotangent bundle of a smooth toroidal compactification. Combining this with a positivity criterion for Euler characteristics of perverse sheaves, we deduce the nonnegativity result. We further prove that the inequality is strict for perverse sheaves with full support. As applications, we get nonnegativity results for perverse Euler characteristics on various moduli spaces.

</details>


### [215] [Coupled Pklt Tuples and Varieties of Pklt Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11107)
*Donghyeon Kim,Dae-Won Lee*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文引入与射影klt对上伪有效除子组相关的渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值，证明耦合潜在klt组的阈值由准单项赋值计算，并给出潜在klt型簇上大除子的双有理Zariski分解、多重截面环有限生成及Mori梦空间判别准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将乘子理想层与对数典范阈值理论从单除子推广到除子组，并研究潜在klt型簇的几何性质，为双有理几何中Zariski分解与Mori梦空间提供新工具。

Method: 引入渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值（针对伪有效除子组）；使用准单项赋值刻画耦合潜在klt组的阈值；对潜在klt型簇构造大除子的双有理Zariski分解并研究其正部的半丰性。

Result: 证明耦合潜在klt组的阈值由准单项赋值计算；证明潜在klt型簇上每个大除子存在半丰正部的双有理Zariski分解；证明大除子多重截面环有限生成；给出潜在klt型簇为Mori梦空间的判别准则。

Conclusion: 该工作把乘子理想层与阈值理论拓展到除子组情形，并刻画了潜在klt型簇上大除子的双有理结构与Mori梦空间性质。

Abstract: We introduce asymptotic multiplier ideal sheaves and log canonical thresholds associated with tuples of pseudoeffective divisors on a projective klt pair. We prove that the threshold of a coupled potentially klt tuple is computed by a quasi-monomial valuation. For varieties of potentially klt type, we prove that every big divisor admits a birational Zariski decomposition with semiample positive part. We also prove finite generation of multisection rings of big divisors and give a criterion for a variety of potentially klt type to be a Mori dream space.

</details>


### [216] [Graded Betti numbers of general curves of large degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11161)
*JeongDon Lee,Li Li,Jinhyung Park*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文给出了亏格g、gonality k的光滑射影复曲线C上very ample线丛L的Koszul上同调群K_{p,q}(C,L)所有分次Betti数κ_{p,q}(C,L)的显式闭公式，并确定了Betti表及Boij–Söderberg系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当L次数足够大时，Koszul上同调群的消失与非消失已被确定，但分次Betti数κ_{p,q}(C,L)的确切值仍大部分未知；本文旨在给出其显式公式并刻画相关性质。

Method: 在Brill–Noether轨迹W_k^1(C)具有预期维数且H^1(C, L⊗ω_C^{-1})=0的条件下，推导所有分次Betti数κ_{p,q}(C,L)的显式闭公式；并计算控制渐近纯性的section ring R(C,L)的Boij–Söderberg系数。

Result: 得到所有κ_{p,q}(C,L)的显式闭公式；确定了一般曲线在°L≥4g-3或°L≥3g-3且L一般时的完整Betti表；显式计算出R(C,L)的Boij–Söderberg系数，并证明其余系数的最终单调性：超椭圆曲线情形递减，在相关Brill–Noether轨迹的自然一般既约性假设下递增。

Conclusion: 本文在给定条件下完全确定了曲线Koszul上同调的分次Betti数，给出了一般曲线的完整Betti表，并刻画了section ring的Boij–Söderberg系数及其渐近单调性。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a smooth projective complex curve of genus $g$ and gonality $k$, and $L$ be a very ample line bundle on $C$. When $L$ has sufficiently large degree, the vanishing and nonvanishing of the Koszul cohomology groups $K_{p,q}(C,L)$ have been determined previously, but the exact values of the graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ remain largely unknown. In this paper, we give explicit closed formulas for all graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ when the Brill--Noether locus $W_k^1(C)$ has the expected dimension and $H^1(C, L \otimes ω_C^{-1})=0$. Consequently, we determine the complete Betti table for a general curve when $°L \geq 4g-3$ or when $°L \geq 3g-3$ and $L$ is general. We also explicitly compute the Boij--Söderberg coefficient of the section ring $R(C, L)$ governing asymptotic purity, and show eventual monotonicity of the remaining coefficients: they decrease for hyperelliptic curves and increase under a natural generic reducedness assumption on the relevant Brill--Noether loci.

</details>


### [217] [Divisibility and torsion in higher Chow groups over arithmetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11178)
*Toshiro Hiranouchi,Rin Sugiyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维Chow群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的交换群结构，给出了整除性与无挠性结果，并应用于有限域、局部域和整体域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光滑方案上高维Chow群（高阶Chow群）的交换群结构，包括其整除性、挠性及推前映射的核，是代数K理论与代数几何中的重要问题。作者希望在特定余维数条件下揭示其群结构性质并推广到经典数域情形。

Method: 设 $X$ 为域 $F$ 上维数 $d$ 的光滑方案。对与 $F$ 特征不同的素数 $l$，在 $i$ 不小于 $F$ 的 $l$-上同调维数时，证明高维Chow群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的整除性与无挠性结果；对光滑真且几何不可约的 $X$，研究推前映射 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 的核。

Result: 在 $i\ge l\text{-cd}(F)$ 的条件下，得到关于 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的整除性与无挠性结论；并对光滑真几何不可约情形刻画了推前映射的核。上述结果应用于有限域、局部域与整体域。

Conclusion: 高维Chow群在足够大的余维数下呈现良好的交换群结构（整除性与无挠性），且其在数域类域情形下有具体应用。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth scheme of dimension $d$ over a field $F$. We study the abelian-group structure of the higher Chow groups $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$. For a prime $l$ different from the characteristic of $F$, we prove divisibility and torsion-freeness results when $i\ge$ the $l$-cohomological dimension of $F$. If $X$ is smooth proper and geometrically irreducible, we also study the kernel of the push-forward $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$. We apply these results to finite fields, local fields, and global fields.

</details>


### [218] [Openness of local properties in flat families of superschemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11287)
*Ibrahim Ahmad*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明平坦真态射下超概形的正规性（GFRR）、正则性与Cohen-Macaulay性质在底上构成开集，并由此得到从特殊纤维到一般纤维的性质提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jankowski借助Serre判别法引入超概形的正规性与既约性（即generic fermionic regularity与reducedness），但关于这些性质在族中的开性及退化提升问题尚不清楚，本文旨在建立相应的开性定理与提升结论。

Method: 设f:X→Y为平坦真态射，X为超概形，Y为特征0域上有限型的Dedekind概形；利用Serre判别法刻画超概形的正规与既约性质，结合平坦性与纤维化技术分析满足性质的纤维对应的底点集合。

Result: 证明了使纤维X_y为正规、GFRR、正则与Cohen-Macaulay的y∈Y构成Y中的开集。

Conclusion: 在A_C^{1|0}上的退化情形下，上述性质可由特殊纤维提升到一般纤维，为超几何中的退化与形变问题提供了开性工具。

Abstract: Recently, Jankowski introduced the Serre criteria for superschemes to define the super versions of normality and reducedness (called generic fermionic regularity and reducedness). We show that for a flat, proper morphism $f:X\rightarrow Y$, where $X$ is a superscheme and $Y$ is a Dedekind scheme of finite type over a field of characteristic $0$, the set of $y\in Y$ for which the fibers $X_y$ are normal, GFRR, regular, and Cohen-Macaulay is open in $Y$. In particular, if one has a degeneration over $\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{C}}^{1|0}$, this shows that these properties can be lifted from the special fiber to the generic one.

</details>


### [219] [Generic vanishing conjecture for divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11377)
*Fanjun Meng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文受Pareschi–Popa一般消灭猜想启发，证明了其余维数1的类比版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pareschi–Popa的一般消灭猜想探讨主极化阿贝尔簇中代表极小上同调类的余维数大于1子簇的存在性与其理想层扭主极化后正性之间的关系。

Method: 以Pareschi–Popa的表述为出发点，构造并研究该猜想的余维数1类比。

Result: 证明了余维数1情形下的一般消灭猜想类比。

Conclusion: 在余维数1的情形建立了子簇存在性与理想层扭主极化正性之间的联系，验证了原猜想的低维类比。

Abstract: Generic vanishing conjecture proposed by Pareschi and Popa is about the relationship between the existence of subvarieties of codimension $>1$ of principally polarized abelian varieties representing minimal cohomology class and the positivity of their ideal sheaves twisted by the principal polarization. Inspired by their formulation, we prove a codimension $1$ analog of generic vanishing conjecture.

</details>


### [220] [Campana Rational Connectedness and Weak Approximation of Del Pezzo Orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11533)
*Saptarshi Dandapat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了度数小于7的del Pezzo orbifold以及具有不可约边界的更高次del Pezzo orbifold是强Campana单直纹的，并由此推出其Campana有理连通性；附录中分类了度6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Campana的一个猜想在二维提供重要的非环面例子，且该形式满足弱逼近定理所需，从而得到Campana纤维化的弱逼近结果。

Method: 证明度数小于7的del Pezzo orbifold、以及具有不可约边界的更高次del Pezzo orbifold的强Campana单直纹性，并据此推导Campana有理连通性；通过附录对度6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold进行分类。

Result: 上述del Pezzo orbifold被证明是强Campana单直纹且Campana有理连通的；并得到一般纤维为此类orbifold的Campana纤维化在其所有位置的Campana截面的弱逼近；同时对度6、7、8且边界不可约的del Pezzo orbifold完成分类。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了Campana猜想在二维的重要非环面情形，并给出弱逼近的应用，同时补充了相关del Pezzo orbifold的分类结果。

Abstract: We prove that a del Pezzo orbifold of degree less than 7, and del Pezzo orbifolds of higher degree with irreducible boundary are strongly Campana uniruled, and deduce that it is Campana rationally connected. This provides important non-toric examples in dimension two of a conjecture by Campana, in the form required by the weak approximation theorem, and yields weak approximation for Campana sections of Campana fibrations at all places whose general fibre is such an orbifold. In three appendices we classify the del Pezzo orbifolds of degree 6, 7 and 8 with irreducible boundary.

</details>


### [221] [A maximal function field of genus $17$ over $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11679)
*Peter Beelen,Maria Montanucci,Jonathan Niemann*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文构造了有限域 F_{11^2} 上一个新的极大函数域 G，证明它不能作为 Hermitian 函数域的子域得到，并计算了 G 的自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知的 F_{p^2}-极大函数域大多可由 Hermitian 函数域的子域方式获得，因此寻找不满足该性质的反例具有理论意义。该新函数域借助 AI 发现。

Method: 利用 AI 工具搜索得到定义在 F_{11^2} 上的新极大函数域 G，并通过代数方法证明其非 Hermitian 子域性，同时计算其自同构群。

Result: 构造出 F_{11^2} 上的极大函数域 G；证明 G 不能作为 Hermitian 函数域的子域获得，这是首个满足该性质的 F_{p^2}-极大函数域；并给出 G 的自同构群。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了首个非 Hermitian 子域型的 F_{p^2}-极大函数域实例，说明 Hermitian 函数域子域并非产生所有极大函数域的唯一途径，同时展示了 AI 在有限域函数域构造中的辅助作用。

Abstract: In this article, we describe a new maximal function field $G$ over the finite field $\mathbb{F}_{11^2}$ with $11^2$ elements and show that it cannot be obtained as a subfield of the Hermitian function field. This provides the first known $\mathbb{F}_{p^2}$-maximal function field that satisfies this property. Further, we compute the automorphism group of $G$. The new maximal function field was found using AI.

</details>


### [222] [Maximality of the Hodge level for smooth weighted complete intersections of general type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11825)
*Victor Przyjalkowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了光滑良构的一般型加权完全交的Hodge数 $h^{0,n}(X)$ 为正（Hodge level 极大），给出几何亏格 $p_g(X)$ 的显式下界，并据此分类了 $p_g(X)=1$ 的一般型加权完全交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般型加权完全交的Hodge理论与几何亏格，刻画其Hodge level 何时达到最大，并探讨 $p_g(X)=1$ 这类极端情形的存在范围。

Method: 对光滑良构（well formed）的加权完全交进行Hodge数分析，证明 $h^{0,n}(X)>0$，并推导几何亏格 $p_g(X)$ 的显式下界，进而通过下界约束排除 $p_g(X)=1$ 的大多数情形。

Result: 对所有维度 $n>0$ 的光滑良构一般型加权完全交，$h^{0,n}(X)>0$ 成立，即 Hodge level 最大；得到 $p_g(X)$ 的显式正下界；除线性锥的数值完全交外，唯一满足 $p_g(X)=1$ 的一般型加权完全交是 $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$。

Conclusion: 一般型加权完全交的Hodge level 总是最大的，几何亏格有统一正下界；$p_g(X)=1$ 的情形极为稀有，唯一非平凡例外为 $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$。

Abstract: We prove that for every smooth well formed weighted complete intersection of general type of dimension $n>0$, the Hodge number $h^{0,n}(X)$ is positive; in other words, its Hodge level is maximal. We also obtain an explicit lower bound for the geometric genus $p_g(X)$. This implies that the only weighted complete intersection of general type that is not a numerical intersection with a linear cone with $p_g(X)=1$ is $X_{6,6}\subset\mathbb{P}(1,2,2,3,3)$.

</details>
